iṉiyamutu patippakam

to.pē. 2433 9030
patippuri
oḷavi cu.turicāmip piḷḷi
tamatu ōyvaṟiyā uḻippāl  tamiḻ āyvuk kaḷattil uyarntu niṉṟavar. 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku puttuyir ūṭṭiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷuḷ muṉ variciyil niṟpavar. naṟṟiṇi, puṟanāṉūṟu, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu ākiya caṅka nūṟ celvaṅkaḷukku urivaḷam kaṇṭavar. civa peruṅkaṭalil mūḻkit tiḷittavar. urivēntar eṉṟu tamiḻulakam pōṟṟip pukaḻappaṭṭa oḷavi cu.turicāmip piḷḷi 1903il piṟantu 1981il maṟintār. 
 vāḻnta āṇṭukaḷ 78. eḻutiya nūlkaḷ 38. itaṉi  poruḷ vaḻip pirittu “urivēntar tamiḻttoki” eṉum talippil 28 tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm.
illaṟa ēntalākavum, urinayam kaṇṭa uravōrākavum , naṟṟamiḻ nāvalarākavum, civa cittāntac cemmalākavum , niṟipukaḻ eytiya urivēntarākavum, pulamiyilum perum pulamipeṟṟavarākavum tikaḻntu viḷaṅkiya ipperuntamiḻācāṉiṉ nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum oru cēra veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟōm. ivaruṭiya nūlkaḷil  em kikkuk kiṭikkap peṟāta nūlkaḷ 5. maṟṟum ivar eḻutiya tiruvaruṭpā  nūlkaḷum it tokutikaḷil iṭam peṟavilli. 
  “ palvēṟu kālat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, urikaḷ, varalāṟu, kalveṭṭu, camayaṅkaḷ eṉṟiṉṉa tuṟip palavaṟṟil niṟipulami peṟṟavar oḷavi cu .turicāmi avarkaḷ” eṉṟu mūtaṟiñar 
va.cupa. māṇikkam avarkaḷālum,
  “iravupakal tāṉaṟiyāṉ iṉtamiḻi vittu
	 varavu celavaṟiyāṉ vāḻvil - uramuṭiyāṉ
	 taṉkaṭaṉ tāynāṭṭu makkaṭ kuḻippatilē
	 muṉkaṭaṉ eṉṟurikkum ēṟu” 
eṉṟu pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avarkaḷālum pōṟṟip pukaḻap paṭṭa ipperuntakiyiṉ nūlkaḷi aṇikalaṉkaḷākak kōrttu, muttumāliyākak  koṭuttuḷḷōm.   
 avar kālattil vāḻnta camakāla aṟiñarkaḷāl pōṟṟip pukaḻap paṭṭavar.  civa ulakil taṉakkeṉat taṉiyiṭattip peṟṟavar.  ivar eḻutiya aṉittu nūlkaḷ maṟṟum malarkaḷ, itaḻkaḷil veḷivanta kaṭṭurikaḷiyellām tēṭit tēṭi eṭuttu orē vīccil veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. 
ittokutikaḷ aṉittum mikacciṟappāka veḷivaruvataṟku muḻuottuḻippum utaviyum nalkiyavarkaḷ avaruṭiya tirumakaṉ oḷavi tu.naṭarācaṉ, marukar irā.kumaravēlaṉ, makaḷ vayiṟṟup peyartti tirumati vēṉilā sṭāliṉ ākiyōr āvar. ivarkaḷ it tamiḻttokikku takka matippuriyum aḷittu eṅkaḷukkup perumic cērttu uḷḷaṉar. ivarkaḷukku neñcam niṟinta naṉṟi
taṉ matippu iyakkattil pērīṭupāṭu koṇṭu  uḻitta ivvaruntamiḻaṟiñar tamiḻp pakivarit tam pakivarākak koṇṭa uyar maṉattiṉarāka vāḻntavar eṉpati niṉivil koṇṭu it toki  nūlkaḷi  ipperuntamiḻ aṟiñariṉ 
107  ām āṇṭu niṉivāka ulakat tamiḻarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum vakiyil  veḷiyiṭuvatil perumakiḻvu koḷkiṟōm. tamiḻ nūl patippil eṅkaḷiṉ inta ariya muyaṟcikkut tōḷ tantu utavuṅkaḷ.

1. aṟivu
aṟivāvatu colvāratu iyalpu nōkkātu collap peṟum poruḷiṉ payaṉōkkik koḷḷutal oḻital ceyvatām. colvār tām uyarntōr, tāḻntōr, naṭṭōr, pakivar eṉap palvakiyiṉarātalōṭu, mukkuṇa vayattarātalāṉum, ivaruḷ ovvoruvariṭattu ovvoṉṟu orō vaḻik kēṭkap
paṭutalāṉum. avaratu iyalpu nōkkāmi vēṇṭum eṉpatu pulappaṭum. aṉṟiyum, vēṇṭappaṭuvatu poruḷēyaṉṟip piṟitaṉṟu. ākavē  "epporuḷ yār yār vāyk kēṭpiṉum apporuḷ - meypporuḷ kāṇpataṟivu" eṉṉum tamiḻmaṟi tuṇiyappaṭṭatu. paṭavē, colvāratu iyalpu nōkkip poruḷiṉ payaṉkāṇātu oḻital aṟiyāmi eṉpatum, ataṉāleytum payaṉ tuṉpamēyeṉpatum peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvuṇmi kīḻkkāṇum poruṭ katiyil viḷaṅkutal kāṇka.
akaliru vicumpiṟ pāyiruḷ parukip pakalkāṉṟu eḻutarum parutiyañ celvaṉ kīḻkkaṭal mukaṭṭil eḻumuṉ, veḷḷi muḷippa viṭiyal vantatu; ōtal antaṇar pāṭiṉar; kūviṉa pūṅkuyil; kūviṉa kōḻi; kurukukaḷ iyampiṉa; iyampiṉa caṅkam; iruñcēṟṟakaṉvayal muṭṭāṭṭāmarittuñci, vikaṟik kaṭkamaḻum neytalūti. eṟpaṭak kaṇpōl malarnta kāmarcuṉi malari añciṟivaṇṭiṉ arikkaṇa molittaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉiṭiyē, mukiyaviḻnta murukkalaroṉṟi vaṇṭiṉañ cūḻvantu muralāniṟpa, "aiyakō! aiyakō!!"  eṉum aḻukuraloṉṟu, avvalarkkīḻ viḷaṅkiya aḻakiya taḷiriṭiyeḻuntu, kēṭpōr cevip
pulampukku, avar tam maṉattiṭi aruḷūṟṟeḻac ceytatu. iṅṅaṉam, evvuyirkkum iṉpak kāṭci nalkum iḷañāyiṟu tōṉṟuṅkālit tōṉṟiya tuṉpak kāṭci āṇṭuṟṟa uyirp poruḷaṉittum maruḷac ceytatu. yāṇṭum amiti nilaviṟṟu. ciṟu curumpun tēṉāṭiṟṟaṉṟu. puṉiyāvōviyam kaṭuppa yāvum pōntaṉavāka, mīṭṭum appulampici kallum pullum kaṉinturuka eḻuntatu. ōciyiyaluṇarntāruḷarāyiṉ, atu, iṟantupaṭu
nilikkaṇ palpoṟip pūcciyoṉṟiṭum mellici yāmeṉa vuṇarvar. maṟṟu, yāṉ avvaṟivilēṉ ātaliṉ, neṭuṅkālan tāḻttup piṉṉarē yuṇarntēṉ. uṇarntēṉ, avvōciyi nuṉittukkēṭkumavā mīkkoṇṭēṉāka,  āṇṭeḻunta poruṇmoḻi piṉ varumāṟu kēṭṭēṉ. atu,
"irunilampurakkum iṟiva! evvuyirkkum nalam payakkum iyavuḷ!! icciṉikaḷi yāṉ īṇṭu iṭṭiṟattal ollumā! ā! ivi tōṉṟiya nāḻikikūṭa iṉṉum kaḻintatiṉṟē! antō! itaṟkuḷ yāṉ iṟakkiṉṟēṉē! iṉṉātamma vulakam, iṉiya kāṇka itaṉ iyal puṇarntōrē eṉṟellām periyōr kūṟutal uṇmiyē! iṟiva! iṟattaṟkup paṇitta nī eṉakku ippacciḷañciṉikaḷi yaḷittal eṟṟukkō? itu muṟikol! yāṉ iṟantupaṭiṉ, ivvarumanta ciṉikaḷip purakkavallunar yāvar kollō? uyirkkuṇavaḷittaṟku nī uḷiyeṉiṉum, oru paṟṟukkōṭumiṉṟi uyir nīttaṟkaṉṟē eṉ uḷam uḷikiṉṟatu! antō! ē! ulakē! eṉṉē niṉ iyaṟki!! uṉit tuṟakkum nilikkaṇuṟṟa eṉ uḷḷatti iṅṅaṉam taḷiceytalē niṉ paṇpupōlum!! naṉṟu! naṉṟu! ammavō! pulaṉkalaṅka poṟi
kalaṅka neṟimayaṅkukiṉṟatē! aṟivu aḻikiṉṟatē! āviyum alamarukiṉṟatē!! aiyakō! yāroṭu nōkēṉ, yārk
keṭutturippēṉ! ācā karuḷiṉar yārkolō. ā!..." eṉpatu
iṅṅaṉam kūṟip pulampikkoṇṭirukkum palpoṟip pūcci (vaṇṇattuppūcci) oṉṟiṉarukē paccilip puḻuvoṉṟu pōntu, ivvurikēṭṭu eriyiṭiyiḻuteṉa iṉintu niṉṟatu. kaṇṭa appūcci "uṭikalappaṭṭōrkku uṟupuṇi tōṉṟi yāṅku, iṭarppaṭum eṉ muṉṟōṉṟiya aṉṉāy! vāḻi! āruyir alamara aḻutu cāmpuvēṉukku nī ōr utaviyaruḷal kūṭuṅkollō! uṟṟuḻi utavutal uravōr kaṭaṉātaliṉ, uṉṉik kāṇṭalum, utavi nāṭiṟṟu eṉ uḷḷam" eṉṟalum, puḻuvum "piṟaruṟu viḻumam tuṭittal cālpeṉap periyōr kūṟuvar. avar vaḻi niṟṟal kaṟpavi kaṟpatiṉ aḻaku. ātaliṉ, niṉmāṭṭu eytiya tuṉpam yātu?
"	kaṭalpāṭavintu tōṇi nīṅki
	neṭunī riruṅkaḻi kaṭumīṉ kalippiṉum."
iyaṉṟaṉa ceytaṟ kiṭiyēṉ. kūṟuka" eṉṟatu.
ikkūṟṟu pūcciyiṉ cevippulampukkatum, atu oruvāṟu maṉantēṟi, "pintaḻi vāḻkki parittōy! āviyō niliyiṟ kalaṅkiyatu. atu eṉ yākkiyiṉ akattatō puṟattatō aṟiyēṉ. inniliyil iṟivaṉ eṉakku ippacciḷañciṉikaḷi yaruḷi
yuḷḷāṉ avaṟṟiyum aṟiviliyāya yāṉ im murukkiliyi
liṭṭuḷḷēṉ. avaṟṟi eṉakkuppiṉ kaḷikaṇāy niṉṟu purappār oruvariyum kāṇātu maṟukukiṉṟēṉ. ivaṟṟiyaruḷiya iṟivarkku aḷittal kūṭum eṉpatu uṟutiyē eṉiṉum, eṉ uḷḷantaṭumāṟukiṉṟatu. ākaliṉ, uṟṟāriṉṟi yuyaṅkum eṉ muṉ orutaṉittōṉṟiya nīyē kaḷikaṇāy niṟpāykol! aṅṅaṉam niṟṟal uṉakku aritaṉṟē. aṉṟiyum āvi tuṟakkum niliyilirukkum eṉṉuṭiya ippulliya vēṇṭukōḷi maṟātu ēṟṟaruḷka. itu niṉakkup pēraṟamākum," eṉṟatu.
puḻu : ām. pēraṟamē. āyiṉum ivaṟṟi yāṉ purattal yāṅṅaṉam? iviyum eṉ iṉattic cārntaṉa vallavē!
pūcci : cārāmiyāl varum kuṟṟamoṉṟumilli. yāṉ 
kūṟum uṇavimaṭṭil nī aruttiyoṭu aḷittuvariṉ, oruvaki iṭiyūṟum nērātu. itu muṟṟum uṇmi.
puḻu : aṟṟēl, urittaruḷ. niṉ uṭal niliyum kuṉṟi varukiṉṟatu. kālam tāḻttaṟka.
pūcci : kūṟuval. ceṭikaḷiṉ iḷantaḷirum paṉinīrum tām avaṟṟiṟkut takka uṇavām. vēṟu eviyēṉum aḷikkap
peṟiṉ, avi uṇṇātu iṟantupaṭum. ātaliṉ ikkūṟiya uṇavi...yē...a...
eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē uyiri viṭukiṉṟatu.
kaṇṭukalaṅkiya puḻuviṟku acciṉikaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṟ keṉap paṇikkappeṟṟa uṇaviyum niṉittoṟum uḷḷam nekkuruka, niṉivu taṭumāṟaluṟṟatu. itu iyaṟkiyē. cettārikkaṇṭu cāvārum varuntutal iyalpaṉṟē! tāntāmum tam āṟṟalaṟintaṉṟō ovvōr paṇiyi āṟṟa muṉ varal vēṇṭum. eṇṇittuṇika karumam. tuṇintapiṉ eṇṇuvam eṉpatu iḻukkaṉṟō! niṟka, ceyakkaṭampūṇṭa paṇiyiyev
vāṟṟāṉum ceytu muṭittalum vēṇṭumaṉṟō. iṟantataṉi eṇṇutalāl iṉip payaṉ yātēṉum eytumō? meyvaruttam pārttal, pacinōkkal, kaṇṭuñcal, cevviyarumi pārttal, avamatippuk koḷḷal ākiya ivaṟṟik karumamē kaṇṇāyiṉār kaṇṇāraṉṟē. ciṟuciṉi purakkum perumpaṇi mēṟkoṇṭa puḻu oruvāṟu tēṟi, ''aiyakō! ivaṟṟi yāṉ eṅṅaṉam purattal kūṭum? iḷantaḷirum paṉi nīrum evaṇ kiṭikkum? kiṭippiṉum yāṉ evvāṟu koṇarvēṉ? icciṉikkuṭpiṟaṅkum pūccikaḷ paṟakkuntaṉmiya; yāṉ avviṉattic cārēṉ. iyaṟkiyāṉ māṟupaṭṭayāṉ purattaṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭēṉē! eṉṉē eṉ aṟiviruntavāṟu!! ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟeḻum pūccikaḷ ciṉṉāṭkaḷiṟ paṟanteḻumē, aññāṉṟu yāṉ avaṟṟi evvāṟu aṭakkutal kūṭum! ā! eṉṉa kāriyam ceytēṉ! 'āyntiṭum uṇarvoṉṟillār alamāl iyaṟki ammā!' eṉap periyōr kūṟutal ettuṇi yuṇmiyuṭittu," eṉa vīḻntu cōrntu, piṉṉarc ciṟitu teḷintu, ''iṉiyāṉ iṅṅaṉam varuntutal kūṭātu.  itaṉi muṉṉarē naṉku ārāyntiruttal vēṇṭum 'viḻiyāvuḷḷam viḻiyumāyiṉum... taṟṟakavuṭimi nōkki, maṟṟataṉ, piṉṉā kummē muṉṉiyatu muṭittal' eṉpar nallicic cāṉṟōr. kaḻintaṉa paṟṟik kalaṅku tuyaruḻattal eṉa nalattiṟkē kēṭutaru moṉṟākaliṉ,. ceytal mēṟkoṇṭa ippaṇiyi, aṟiviṉ mikkār poruḷuri kēṭṭut takkaṉa tērntu ceyval. ituvē yāṉ iṉic ceyaṟpāṟ ṟaṉṟip piṟitoṉṟu maṉṟu" eṉat tērntu niṉṟatu. akkāli, tēṉūṇ
cuviyiṉum ūṉūṇ cuviyē uyiriṉum virumpivāḻum pūñi oṉṟu āyiṭip pōtantatu. kaṇṭapuḻu ataṉi yaḷavāḷāvit tāṉ mēṟceyaṟkēṟṟa virakiṉiyaṟiya avākkoṇṭatu. koḷḷiṉum, ataṉ uḷattac ciṟitu nāḻikikkuḷ appūñi taṉ vēṇṭukōṭkuc cevi cāykkātu, taṉakkum. taṉṉuḻik kiyaṭiyāt tarappeṟṟa ciṉikaṭkum ūṟiḻikkiṉumiḻikkum eṉṉum ōr eṇṇam eyta atu ammuyaṟciyik kiviṭṭatu.
kiviṭalum, pōṉa veñcuram puḷiyiṭa vantāṅku, maṟin
tirunta varuttam mīṭṭum ataṉ maṉattē tōṉṟa, atu ciṟitutēṟi, taṉ maṉakkiyinta aṟavuri kūṟum āṟṟaluṭi yār iṉiyāvaruḷar eṉṉumōr ārāycciyi p piṉṉarum ceytal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. avvuḻik kumpiṭappōṉa teyvam kuṟukkē vanteṉa, vāṉiṟ paṟantuḻalum vaṉciṟipparun toṉṟu ataṉarukē ceṉṟatu. itaṉi yappuḻukkāṇṭalum, taṉ maṉattiṟ palatiṟappaṭṭa eṇṇaṅkaḷik koṇṭatu. avaṟṟuḷ, "ipparuntiṉum aḵki yakaṉṟavaṟivuṭiyār yāvaruḷar? maṉattāṉum eṇṇaṟkiyalāta cēṇiṟ paṟattalum, āṇṭiruntē, maṇmītiyaṅkum tiṟṟikaḷi (īṇṭut tiṉṉaṟkuriya ciṟṟuyirkaḷi yeṉak koḷka)k kaṇṭu, avaṟṟiṉmēṟ kuṟipiḻiyātu ñerēleṉap pāyntu paṭin
tuṇṭalumāya uyariya ceyalkaḷic ceyalvallār vēṟoru
varumila raṉṟē! ākaliṉ, itaṉiṭi, yāṉ eṉakkāvaṉavaṟṟi yucāviyaṟikiṉṟēṉ" eṉpatu moṉṟeṉka.
iṅṅaṉam eṇṇi muṭivuceytu koḷḷum appuḻu virunta pulattiṟ kayalirunta vayaloṉṟil vēṟu paruntoṉṟu vāḻntu vantatu. ataṟkum appuḻuviṟkum neruṅkiya naṭpu muṇṭu. annaṭpurimi yētuvāka, puḻu, apparuntiṉi varavaḻittut tāṉ acciṉikaḷi vaḷarkku māṟṟit 'terintu kōṭali nāṭi niṟpa, celvakkāli niṟpiṉum allaṟkāli nillā māṭciyaminta kēṇmicāl paruntum kākatāliyamākap puḻuvip pārttuc celvāṉ vantatu. varakkaṇṭa puḻuvum kaḻipēruvakikoṇṭu, ''aṉpu keḻumiya naṇpē! varuka iṉpaṅkaṉiyum niṉmukaṅkaṇṭum yāṉ iṉpamiṉṟiyirut tali yaṟitiyō? iṅṅaṉamirukku meṉakku oru māṟṟukkūṟal valli kollō?" eṉṟatu.
paruntu : aṟival. maṟṟu niṉṉiyuṟṟa tuṉpam oṉṟuṇṭeṉa aṟintaṉaṉēyaṉṟi atu ittaṉmittu, ivvētu
vuṭittu eṉpavaṉa vaṟṟi yaṟiyēṉākaliṉ, māṟṟukkūṟal yāṅṅaṉam iyalum?
puḻu  : naṉṟu. kēṭṭiciṉ. eṉakku niṉṉoppa uyirt tōḻami pūṇṭa cēṭappūcci (vaṇṇattuppūcci) yoṉṟu īṇṭuc ciṉṉāṭkaḷukku muṉ vāḻntu vantatu. ataṉ vaḻiyeccamē īṇṭu nī kāṇkuṟum pacciḷañ ciṉikaḷ. ivaṟṟi atutāṉ iṟakkuṅ kālattu īṉṟu, eṉṉiyum puṟantarumāṟu paṇittatumaṉṟi, ivi kuṭampi taṉit toḻiyap paṟantēku nāḷkāṟum taṟkuṇavaḷit tōmputal ceyyumāṟum kūṟiyakaṉṟatu. avvōm paṭiyēṟṟa yāṉ aṅṅaṉam ceyyumā ṟaṟiyātu alamarukiṉṟēṉ.
paruntu : eṉṉi?
puḻu  : av vāṟṟiṉi yāṉ aṟiyēṉākaliṉ.
paruntu : eṉ aṟiyāy?
puḻu : appūcciyō paṟakkumiyalpiṟṟu; maṟṟu, yāṉō ūrvaṉavaṟṟic cārnta pulliya puḻuvāṉēṉ. paṟappaṉavaṟṟi ūrvaṉa purattal,
''	pāytirip puṇari pāṭavin toḻiyiṉum,
  kāykati riraṇṭuṅ katitiri yōṭiṉum,"
kūṭātu eṉpatu uṇmiyaṉṟō. atu paṟṟiyē im mayakkam eytiyatu.
paruntu : mayaṅkal vēṇṭā. nuṉittu nōkkiṉ, ivaṟṟip 
purattalum, ēṟṟa vuṇavu koṭuttalum niṉṉā leḷitiṟ ceyaṟpālaṉa veṉpatē tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. aṉṟiyum, celvuḻic celvuḻi meynniḻal pōla, niṉit toṟum niṉittoṟum immuṭipē eytukiṉṟatu.
puḻu :  uyntēṉ! uyntēṉ!! kūṟuka.
paruntu : kūṟuval. taṭiyumiṉṟi. maṟṟu, yāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟi nī uḷḷavāṟē yuṭkoḷḷuvāyō veṉṟiyuṟu kiṉṟēṉ. epporuḷ ettaṉmittāyiṉum, aṉṟi yāryārvāyk kēṭpiṉum, apporuḷiṉkaṇ meyp poruḷ kāṇṭal kūṭum eṉṉum uṟuti nōkku (Faith) niṉṉuḻi yuḷatāṉālaṉṟi, eṉ moḻiyiṉ uṇmip poruḷ pulaṉākātu. innōkkiṉṟik kēṭṭal araṅkiṉṟi yāṭal purivāratu nōkkiṉō
ṭokkum.
puḻu : ām. cārntataṉ vaṇṇamātalē maṉaviyalpu. en 
nōkkaṅ koṇṭu epporuṇōkkappaṭukiṉṟatō, annōkkiṟ kamintē apporuḷun tōṉṟum. oruvaṉik kuṟṟamuṭiyaṉē yeṉakkaruti nōkku vārkku avaṉ kuṟṟamuṭiyaṉākavum,  allaṉeṉak karuti nōkkuvārkku allaṉākavum tōṉṟal kaṇ kūṭaṉṟō! niṟka, yāṉ, avvuṟutinōkku uṭiyēṉ; veḷip
paṭiyākak kūṟiṉ, nī kūṟumaṉittiyum kūṟi yāṅkē nampuval. itu uṟuti.
paruntu : uṟutiyāyiṉ, kūṟuval: mutaṟkaṇ nī ivaṟṟiṟ
kēynta vuṇavu yāteṉa niṉikkiṉṟaṉi? niṉ uḷattil tōṉṟuvaṉavaṟṟi maṭṭiṟ kūṟuka.
puḻu : (niṉittu) yātākum? paṉittivaliyum pintēṉumām ! ā!
paruntu : allavē. avaṟṟiṟkēṟku muṇavi nī mikka eḷitiṟ koṇartal kūṭumē. aṉṟiyum, atu ivvaṉittiṉum mikka aṇmiyiṟ kiṭittaṟpāṟṟaṉṟō!
puḻu : (tikittu) eṉṉarukil, eḷitil, peṟakkūṭiyatu immucuk koṭṭiyili yaṉṟip piṟitoṉṟumiliyē.
paruntu : ām! ām!! naṉṟu coṉṉāy! nīyē avvuṇavi naṉku uṇarntu koṇṭaṉi. im mucukkoṭṭiyili kaṭām avaṟṟiṟkut takkavuṇavāvaṉa.
puḻu : (ciṉantu) ē! eṉṉē niṉ maṭami!! ivaṟṟiyaṉṟō atu tarutal kūṭāteṉa vaṉpuṟi ceytatu. mukanaka voṉṟu moḻitalum, piṉṉar maṟṟoṉṟu ceytalum aṟaṉalla. ataṉāl vilakkappaṭṭa vuṇaviyē yaḷikkumāṟu kūṟal niṉ aṟivuṭimik kaḻakaṉṟu; amitiyumaṉṟu.
paruntu : aṉpē! poṟuttaruḷ. iṉiya uḷavāka. iṉṉāta kūṟal kaṉi iruppak kāykavarntaṟṟu eṉpati niṉittaruḷka. niṟka, ivaṟṟiṉ tāyppūcci iv vuṇmiyiṉi yaṟiyātu. atu perumpēti. aṉṟiyum yāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟi yuṟuti nōkkuṭaṉ kēṭpaleṉak kūṟi, kūṟakkēṭṭalum, uṇmi nōkkātoḻital niṉ uyarvukkuk kuṟiyaṉṟō!aṟivum, aminta nampikkiyum amivuṟiṉaṉṟō vuṇmi teḷḷitām.
puḻu : ō! aṟṟaṉṟu. eṉ cevippulaṉām poruḷ pala vaṟṟiṉum amivuṟum uṇmipporuḷ kāṇṭalēeṉṉiyalpu. uṟutinōkkum, uṇmi nampikkiyum uṭiyāriṉ mikkār
tēriṉ, yāṉalatilli ivvulakattāṉē eṉpati mīṭṭum niṉakku vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟēṉ. aṟika!
paruntu : aṟivateṉ! niṉcolliṉ valiyiṉmiyi niṉ coṟkaḷē naṉku kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. yāṉ kūṟum uṇavup poruḷiyē nī mutaṟkaṇ ēlā toḻiyiṉ, piṉṉark kūṟappōvaṉavaṟṟi nī eṅṅaṉam ēṟṟal amiyum? niṟka, ippacciḷañ ciṉikaḷ porittalum eṉṉāmeṉa niṉik kiṉṟaṉi? itaṉiyēṉum uṇmiyākak kūṟuti.
puḻu : eṉṉām! vitiyoṉṟu pōṭap patamoṉṟu viḷiyuṅkol “paṇṭu ceyviṉiyalāṟ paravu teyva
moṉṟuṇṭeṉil, tāṉ payaṉ utava vallatō” eṉṉum cem
poruḷiyum teḷika. ākaliṉ, cēṭattiṉ ciṉi cēṭamā mēyaṉṟik  kīṭamākā.
paruntu : aṟṟaṉṟu. cēṭattiṉ ciṉi cēṭamākātu mutaṟkaṭ kīṭamē yām. uṇmiyik kūṟiṉ, avi porittalum, niṉṉoppap puḻukkaḷāmē yaṉṟi nī niṉittuk kūṟum vaṇṇattup pūcci kaḷākā. nī ivvuṇmiyi ēlātoḻiyiṉum oḻika. uṇmi piṉṉarp pulaṉām. yāṉ ceṉṟu varuval.
	iṅṅaṉam kūṟikkoṇṭē paruntu cēṇōkkic civveṉṟeḻuntu paṟantu ceṉṟatu. aṉṟiyum, āṇṭē yiruntu, puḻuviṟku uṇmiyuṇarttum vakiyāṟ kalāyttaṟku apparuntu virumpiṟṟaṉṟu. 'iruḷpaṭu neñcattu iṭumpi tīrkkum aruḷ naṉku uṭiyarāyiṉum, cāṉṟōr, paḻiyoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkār’ eṉpar periyār. ākavē, paruntum kalāyttup paḻiyeytaṟku añci yakaṉṟateṉka.
iṅṅaṉam viḻumiya naṭpukkaṭampūṇṭa paruntu piritalum. puḻu mikkatōr maruṭcikoṇṭu, ceytoḻilaṟiyātu tikittu arukukiṭakkum ciṉikaḷik kāṇṭalum, kaṇṇīrukuttalum, paṉmuṟi yavaṟṟic cūḻvaralum ceytu koṇṭu varum. iṭiyiṭiyē paruntukūṟiya moḻikaḷi eḻuttaḷavāy āyum; poruṭpayaṉip paṉmuṟiyum cūḻntu nōkkum; taṉ yākkip paṇpiyum, pūcciyiṉ poliviyum maṉattāṉoppu nōkkum. atukālippiṉ varumāṟum niṉikkum. atu;
“māntar iṉattiyalpatākum aṟivu” eṉpatu tamiḻmaṟi. itupaṟṟiyaṉṟē, uyarntōr kūṭṭamē yuṟavāk koṇṭuḻalum ipparuntum mikka aṟivamitiyuṭiyatā meṉṟeṇṇik keṭṭēṉ! orukāl, atu immuṟi neṭitu cēṇiṟ paṟantirukkum, ataṉāṟṟāṉ ikkalakkam! ā! eṉṉa alakit taṉmi! ulakattōr uṇṭeṉpa tilleṉpāṉ viyattu alaki yeṉṟu eṇṇappaṭumaṉṟō! ā! uyirk kūṭṭattuḷ, uyarntārōṭu kūṭi vāḻvaṉavum, kūṭi vāḻvatākat tammiyu
yarttik kūṟik koḷvaṉavumāya pala vuyirkaḷ muṭivil ettuṇip pētimiyum koṭumiyum uṭiyavāyt tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṉṉa ulakam! peṟutaṟkariya yākki tamakku eytiṟ ṟeṉṟāl, atu koṇṭu aṟivum oḻukkamum amiyappeṟṟu, takkaviṉṉa, takātaṉaviṉṉaveṉa okka vuṉṉi, oppaṉavaṟṟik kōṭalaṉṟō yāvarkkum oru paṭittāy vēṇṭappaṭuvatu.
“	kaṉṟu muṇṇātu kalattiṉum paṭātu 
	nallāṉ ṟīmpāl nilattuk kāṅku.”
ākumaṉṟō, iḵtu ōr uyirkku eytāviṭiṉ ataṉ polivu kūr yākki. niṟka. ippaṟavi avvicumpip paṭarntu ceṉṟu ceyyum toḻil yātu kol! neṭuṅ kālamaḷavum iḵtu āṇṭup paṟantu koṭpuṟutaliṉ uṭkiṭiyeṉṉō? itaṉoṭu aṅku uriyāṭi nallaṟivu koḷuttavallār yāvarō? ivvaṉittum, eṇṇun tōṟum mayakkatti yaṉṟō tarukiṉṟaṉa. eṉṉa iṟumpūtu payakkum vāḻvukāṇ itaṉ vāḻvu!” eṉpatu.
iṅṅaṉam, atu, niṉittavaṇṇam paṇṭēpōṟ ṟaṉ ciṉikaḷic cūḻvantu kāval purintu vantatu. atupōtu, paruntiṉ mellicimaṭṭiṟ puḻuviṉ cevippulaṉākalum, atu, muṉṉik kaḷippiṉṟeṉiṉum, ciṟitu tēṟi, nōkkak kuḻiyum eṉpataṟkañci, iṉimi tōṉṟa oruvaṇṇam etirnōkki niṉṟatu. paruntum vantatu. iraṇṭaṉ naṭpum nakutaṟ poruṭṭaṉṟākalāṉum.
“	iṉpamum iṭumpiyum puṇarvum pirivum
	naṉpaka lamiyamum iravum pōla
	vēṟuvē ṟiyala vāki māṟetirn tuḷa.”
eṉṉum poruḷi ivi naṉkuṇarntavi yākalāṉum, muṉṉar nikaḻcciyiṟ puḻuviṉ ciṉatti yaṟavē maṟiya oṉṟōṭoṉṟu ciṟitu aḷavaḷāvip piṉ varumāṟu mīṭṭum avi collāṭal toṭaṅkiṉa.
paruntu :	yāṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟi, muṉṉarē kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, uṟutinōkkum, oppaṉa koḷḷum uḷamum koṇṭu kēṭpiyēṟ kūṟuval.
puḻu : āyiṉ, avi eṉṉiṭat tilaveṉa veṇṟuti pōlum! naṉṟu naṉṟu!! ā! kēḷviyilā teytum evvakip
poruḷiyum uṟutinōkkuk koṇṭa, uḷattiṟkōṭarē eṉṉiyalpeṉa yāṉ kūṟi yirukku matu niṉ niṉiviṉkaṇilli kollō!
paruntu : “uṇarntaṉa maṟakkum, maṟantaṉavuṇarum” eṉpar periyōr. iṉi maṟavēṉ; poṟuttaruḷka. niṟka yāṉ muṉpu kūṟiyaṉa niṉṉiyum, niṉ ciṉikaḷi yum paṟṟip  potuvāka vuṇarntaṉa; iṟṟi ñāṉṟukūṟappōvatu niṉporuṭṭu ṇarnta ciṟappup poruḷākum. atu, curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, nīyum orupakal appūcciyē yātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu.
puḻu : eṉṉi kūṟiṉi? ē, maṭapparuntē! niṟuttuka niṉ coṟ poḻiviṉi! niṉṉoṭu nimirntañēyam ippayaṉip payakkum nīrmittāyiṉ, eṉ kūṟuvatu. pālākit tōṉṟip parukiṉār āvi koḷḷum ālāla nīrmittē niṉṉaṉpu eṉpatē cālap poruntuvatu. polivu, tiṭpam mutaliya villā yākkiyēṉ eṉa eṇṇi iṉṉaṇam eṉṉi iḻittuk kūṟuvāṉ ceyta kūṟṟēyitu. aṟintēṉ. niṉ taṉmiyi yāṉ iṉṟē uṇarntēṉ. nī peruṅkoṭiyi! aṟivili! tirutta viyalāt tīyi! pēti!! nāṇiṉṟi eṉ muṉṉiṟṟal niṉ māṇpiṟkē kuṟivām. iṉi eṉakku niṉ poṅku cōṟum vēṇṭā: pūcārittaṉamum vēṇṭā. pōtum! pōtum!!
ivvaṇṇam kūṟiya puḻuviṉ neñcucuṭu neṭumoḻi vaṉciṟipparuntiṉ maṉattut tiṉittuṇiyum ciṉat tī koḷuttiṟṟiṉṟu. eṉṉi, vaṉmiyuḷ vaṉmi maṭavārp poṟi yākaliṉ. maṟṟu, atu, ataṉmaṉattut “tiṟaṉalla taṟpiṟar ceyyiṉum nōnontu aṟaṉalla ceyyāmi naṉṟu” eṉṉum teyvat tiruvaḷḷuvap payaṉi yeḻac ceytu, uṟuvatu tērāp puḻuviṉmāṭṭu irakkamum, ataṉ aṟiyāmikkuk kavaṟciyumē tōṉṟac ceytatu. vēṟu eṉ ceyum!! atu appuḻuviṉi, “aṉpa,  nī vekuṇṭu kūṟuvaṉayāvum veḷiṟṟurikaḷē. oru
puṭi, avi, nī icciṉi māṭṭup pūṇṭoḻukum pērātaraviṉ uṟutiyip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa veṉiṉum, āṉṟa kēḷviyāṟṟōṭṭa ceviyi yalli yeṉpatik kāṭṭātu kaḻiyavilli. eṉ coṟkaḷi nī ēṟṟuk koḷḷayeṉap paṇṭē yāṉ kūṟiṉēṉaṉṟō,” eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu.
puḻu : ām. yāṉ kēṭpaṉavaṟṟil uṟutip poruḷik kāṇṭal kūṭum eṉṉum nōkkattuṭaṉ ēṟṟuk koḷva
leṉpatip paṇṭu kūṟiyatē yaṉṟi, iṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟēṉ. iṉi eṉṟum kūṟuvēṉ. urippār urippaṉa vaṟṟi yuritta vāṟṟāṉuṭkoḷḷumiyalpiṉi yuṭi yārkku, avar uṇmi kalantavaṟit tāmē yurittal vēṇṭum. oru vāṟṟāṉu miyipillāta voṉṟi yiyittukkūṟiṉ yāvar tām ēṟpar. niliyāta vaṟṟi niliyiṉa veṉṟuṇarum pullaṟivāṇmi kaṭiyātal pōla, iyipillaṉavaṟṟi yiyipuṭiyavākkik kūṟutalum, kūṟakkēṭṭalum pullaṟivāṇmi yāta lōṭu kaṭi
yātalum tōṉṟiṟṟaṉṟē. naṇpa! cēṭap pūccikaḷiṉ ciṉi poriyiṉ kīṭaṅkaḷāka māṟu meṉpatum, kīṭaṅkaḷun tam iyaṅkutaṟ ṟaṉmi nīṅkip paṟattaṟṟaṉmiyiṉi yeytu meṉpatum muṟṟilum poruntāk kūṟṟukkaḷē. ivaṟṟōraṉṉa puṉmoḻi kaḷi nī yāṇṭu, yāṅṅaṉam, kēṭṭaṟkut tuṇintaṉi? ivi yollumō veṉpati nīyē āytu nōkkuka. aṅṅaṉamātal oru kālum muṭiyātaṉṟō!
paruntu : iṉi vēṟu kūṟal eṉakku iyalātu. vēṇṭumēl yāṉ aṟintavaṟṟik kūṟuval; kēḷ; yāṉ vayal vaḻiyum, vāṉāṟum paṭarntu koṭpuṟum pāṉmiyēṉ. aṅṅaṉam, paṭaruṅkāl, iṭiyil nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷik kāṇātu celvatu illi. avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum niṉivuvallār neñcum kaṭantu niṟkuntaṉmittu. avaṟṟik kaṇṇuṟṟut teḷinta yāṉ ivvāṟu nikaḻtaluṅ kūṭumeṉat tuṇintēṉ. ōr kuṟukiya ellikkaṇiyakkamum, tāṉvāḻum ili yaṉṟip piṟa
kaṇṭaṟiyāmiyumuṭiya niṉakkup piṟa vaṉittum ariyavum, iyipillavum, muṭiyātavumākavē tōṉṟum, kiṇaṟṟuttavaḷi puṇariyiyalpi yaṟiyuṅkol!
puḻu : antō! āyntiṭu muṇarvutaṉṉi niṉakku avvayaṉ paṭittilaṉ kol! avaṉik kāṇpaṉēl, aṟṟatali pōka, aṟātatali nāṉkiṉiyum paṟṟittirukip paṟippēṉ! aṟitiyō!! ikkūṟṟu ittuṇiyuṟutiyuṭittu; uṇmi yuṭittu eṉpaṉa
vaṟṟiyumō yāṉ aṟiyēṉ! ā!! ittuṇikkālam ivvulakiṭip piṟantuḻaṉṟa yāṉ ītaṟiyātu oḻivalō? eṉṉē niṉmaṭami! eṉ paciya neṭiya vuṭaliyum, eṇṇiṟanta kāl
kaḷiyum nōkkum ivaṟṟi nēriṟ kaṇṭum, eṉakku ivvuṭaṉīṅku meṉpatum, palvaki vaṇṇamaminta puttuṭaloṉṟu eytu meṉpatum ettuṇi iḻittakka moḻikaḷā kiṉṟaṉa. iḵtaṟiyāmiyē pētami yeṉpatu. kūṟuvōrkku uṇarvu kuṉṟiṉum, kēṭpōrkkum atu kuṉṟuṅ kolō?
paruntu : ciṉantu) eṉṉi kūṟiṉi? “yākāvā rāyiṉum nākākka, kāvākkāṟ cōkāppar colliḻukkup paṭṭu” eṉṉum poyyil moḻiyiṉi maṟattiyō? eṉṉi nī matiyili yeṉpatu uṇmi. kallā eṟivil kayavarpāṟ kaṟṟuṇarnta nallār tamatu kaṉam naṇṇutaluṇṭukol! itaṉic ciṟitēyu maṟintiruntum, yāṉ ivvuṇmiyik kūṟiya vantaṉaṉaṉṟē! niṉ aṟivuk
keṭṭātavaṟṟi vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟaniṉittalum, atu paṟṟi niṉṉuḻi yeytalum eṉṉilikku ēṟpaṉavalla vaṉṟō! vāṉōkki yeḻuṅkāl eṉvāyvaḻi varum iṉṉoli kēṭṭōrkku iṉpam payantu, eṉakku matuvaḻiyēyōr ciṟappiṉiyum nalkutal orutali. itu kiṭakka, nī iṉiyēṉum varuvaṉa vaṟṟiyum, piṟar kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟiyum oṉṟiya vuḷḷamoṭu ēṟṟukkoḷka.
ivvāṟu, puḻuviṉ puṉmoḻikaḷ palavum paruntiṉ ceviyiṟ pukkum atu vekuḷātu, nāṇātu, aṉpuṭi naṉmoḻi kūṟalum, puḻu taṉ aṟiyāmikkum, ataṉāṟ ciṉantu pitaṟṟiya moḻikaṭkum uḷam veḷki, “ataṟku nī kūṟum annampikkiyum uṟuti nōkkum eṉ māṭṭila veṇpāyō? eṉṟatu.
paruntu : uḷavēl, ittuṇi neṭumoḻikaḷ piṟakkuṅ kollō!
puḻu : aṟṟēl, avvuṟuti nōkku eṉṉuḻi yuṇṭātaṟku yāṉ ceyyakkaṭavateṉ? aṉpu kūrntu kūṟuka.
iṅṅaṉam amiticāṉṟa vuriyāṭal iṭinikaḻuṅ
kālattup paruntu cēṭpulam paṭarntatu. puḻuvum taṉṉip paṉmuṟiyum nōkki, acciṉiyirunta murukkiliyic cūḻvantu koṇṭiruppa, avviliyit tuḷittukkoṇṭu ēḻeṭṭup paccilip puḻukkaḷ aṅkumiṅku miyaṅkat toṭaṅkiṉa. avi yāṇṭiruntu pōntaṉa? avi acciṉikkaṇiṉṟu pōntaṉavē! pōtarakkaṇṭa puḻuviṉ puntiyiṟ kalakkamum māṉamuṅkalantu tōṉṟiṉa: kaṇṇakaṉ ñālam koṭpuṟal ceytatu; uṭal cumantu niṟkum taṉṉiyē māyamō veṉṟeṇṇiṟṟu. paruntu kūṟiya
moḻi yovvoṉṟum ataṉ uḷattut tōṉṟiṟṟu. iṉṉaṇam paruntiṉ mutaluri yuṇmiyātal kāṇṭaliṉ, maṟṟatum avvāṟāl kūṭumeṉum tuṇipum uṭaṉeytiya eytalum, atu paṭṭapāṭu yārāṟ kūṟappaṭun takittām!!
piṉṉar nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa; parantaviv vulakiṟṟōṉṟum poruḷiṉāṉām kāṭciyaṟivip paruntu uṭaṉuṭaṉ puḻuviṟ kurittalum, uṇmiyāytalum iraṇṭiṟkum marapāyiṟṟu. puḻuvum taṉṉiṉaṅkaṭkut taṉakku nēravirukkum nikaḻcciyiyum, atuvē yavaṟṟiṟkumā meṉpatiyum aṟivuṟuttum; uṟuttiṉum, ataṉiyavi ēlātē yoḻintaṉa. puḻumaṭṭiṟ ṟāṉṟaṉ meyn
naṭpukkuriya  paruntuḻik kaṇṭa meypporuḷ nikaḻtaṟ kēṟṟa kālam eytiṟṟāka, pakalcey maṇṭilam paṉikkaṭal mukaṭṭeḻa, paṟavi yiṉṉicipāṭa, curumpuntēṉum cūḻntārppa, kaṭikkamalam vāyviḷḷa, pūcciyiṉam poliveyta, puḻuviṉaṅkaḷ kuḻīi maṟuka, ippuḻuvum pūcciyāyiṟṟu; yākkiyum kāloṭumitakkum kaviṉeytiṟṟu; iyaṅkiyavuṭal paṟattal ceytatu; irumaruṅkum ciṟakukaḷeḻuntaṉa; avaṟṟiṉkaṭ palvakiya voḷiteṟikkum vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi yaḻaku ceytaṉa.
ivvaṇṇam aḻakutikaḻap puttuṭal peṟṟup pūntēṉuṇṭu polivukūr cuṟṟamoṭu purintu vāḻnta cēṭap pūcci, piṉṉarc ciṉṉāṭkaḷuṭ ṭaṉ ṉiṉṉuyir nīṅkuṅ kāla meytalum, eṇṇaru miṉpaṅkaṉiya, “im maṟuviṉṟu viḷaṅkum māyiru
ñālattu, aṟivu vaḷarkkumāṟṟā ṉaminta poruḷkaḷ mikap
palavuḷa; avaṟṟi yāyntu kōṭalaṟiviṉ kaṭaṉ: ataṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta vuṟuppāvatu uṟutinōkkē (kuyaviā). ataṉi ataṉaruḷālē yaṭiyappeṟṟēṉ. yāṉ peṟṟa iṉpam peṟuka ivviyakam!! iṉi evi variṉum varuka. yāṉ viṇṇakam paṭarkuval” eṉṟeḻuntu ceṉṟu viṇṇil maṟintatu.
(karantit tamiḻc caṅkak kallūriyiṉiṉṟu veḷivanta pañcāpakēcaṉ eṉṉum kiyeḻuttut tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭṭiṉkaṇ muṉṉar veḷivantatōr moḻipeyarppu. āṅkila nūṟ kaṭṭurikaḷuḷ amiḻntu tiḷitta niliyil ikkaṭṭuri āṅkilam taḻuviya tamiḻākkamāka amintatu)

2. uḻippu
"	viṉiyē āṭavark kuyirē vāṇutal
	maṉiyuṟi makaḷirkku āṭavar uyireṉa
	namakkurit tōrum tāmē
	aḻāaltōḻi avar aḻuṅkuvar celavē..."
- kuṟuntoki : 135
naṟkuṇamum naṟceykiyum ciṟanta naṅkiyorutti, aṟivum āṇmiyum perukiya tōṉṟal oruvaṉi maṇantu illaṟam pūṇṭu vantaṉaḷ. orunāḷ avaṉ, tāṉ ceyvataṟ kuriya viṉiyoṉṟu kuṟittut taṉ maṉiyiṉ nīṅkic cella vēṇṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. maṇantanāḷ toṭṭut taṉ kaṇavaṉip pirintaṟiyāta annaṅkikku itu teriyiṉ, avaḷ peruvaruttam eytuvaḷ eṉpati attōṉṟal eṇṇiṉaṉ. uṇmik kātalvaḻi yoḻukuvārkkup pirivupōlap peruntuṉpam taruvatu piṟitu kiṭiyātu.  "iṉṉātu iṉaṉillūr vāḻtal, ataṉiṉum iṉṉātu iṉiyārp pirivu" eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvarum kūṟiyuḷḷār. ataṉāl taṉ karuttiṉi, avaṉ, avaḷatu tōḻikku urittu, piṉṉar mellat taṉ maṉiyāṭkut terivikkumāṟu paṇittāṉ. avaḷum ataṟku oruvāṟu uṭaṉpaṭṭu, kālam vāytta pōtu avaṉatu karuttiṉip piya vuṇarttiṉāḷ. kēṭṭāḷō illiyō, naṅkikkup peruntuyar uṇṭākiviṭṭatu; kaṇṇīr viṭṭuk kataṟiyaḻat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ. avaḷi āṟṟuvikkat toṭaṅkiya tōḻi,  "naṅkāy, kaṇavaṉ collik kāppatu maṉivikkuk kaṭaṉ. avar orunāḷ coṉṉatu oṉṟuṇṭu. atu niṉakkum terintatē. aḵtāvatu: 'āṭavarkkut tāṅkaḷ ceyyum viṉiyē uyir; maṉiyuṟiyum makaḷirkku avvāṭavarē uyir. avarkku uyirākiya viṉimēṟ ceṉṟāltāṉē, avar niṉakku uyirāka iruttalkūṭum; nī aḻutāl avar viṉimēṟ
celvatik kiviṭuvarē. ataṉāl ētamaṉṟō viḷiyum," eṉṟaṉaḷ. atu kēṭṭa annaṅkiyum tēṟi avaṉ pirivi āṟṟit taṉakkuriya aṟattiṉiyum āṟṟivantaṉaḷ.
icciṟiya pāṭṭiṉip pāṭiyavar pālipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō eṉṉum cāṉṟōrāvar.
ivvāṟu viṉimēṟcella niṉinta talimakaṉ orunāḷ mālippōtu varakkaṇṭu,  "ā, ippōtu nam kātali nam maṉiyakattu viḷakkēṟṟi ataṉ muṉ niṉṟu, 'iṉṉum kātalar vantilarē' eṉṟu niṉikkum pōtu aṉṟō?" eṉṟu taṉakkuḷ niṉittāṉ. niṉittavaṉ, immāli,
"uḷḷiṉēṉ allaṉō yāṉē, uḷḷiya
	viṉimuṭit taṉṉa iṉiyōḷ
	maṉimāṇ cuṭaroṭu paṭarpoḻu teṉavē."
- naṟṟiṇi : 3
eṉat taṉakkuḷ niṉintu iṉintāṉ eṉa iḷaṅkīraṉār kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivviru ceyyuṭkaḷiyum nōkkuvōmāyiṉ, namakku ōr uṇmi pulappaṭum. aḵtāvatu, ōr āṇ makaṉukku avaṉ ceytaṟkuriya viṉitāṉ uyir; ataṉi muṭittavaḻi yuḷatākum iṉpam kātaliṉpam pōlvatu eṉpatām.
viṉiyeṉpatu nām ceyyum ceykiyākum. ovvōruyirum ulakil tōṉṟumpōtē viṉi ceyvataṟkeṉṟē piṟakkiṉṟatu. viṉiyē oruvaṉ eytum iṉpattukkum tuṉpattukkum kāraṇamāvatu. iṉpattiyē evvuyirum peṟa virumpum; tuṉpatti virumpum uyir etuvu milli. tuṉpam eṉṟa collik kēṭkumpōtē nam uḷḷam cuḷikkum; collutaṟkum nākkuk kūcum. iṉpattit tarum viṉiyē yāvarum virumpum toḻilākum. ataṉāltāṉ iṉpam tarakkūṭiyataṉi nalviṉi, naṟceyki eṉṟum, tuṉpam taruvataṉit tīviṉi, tīcceyki eṉṟum collukiṉṟōm.
iṉi ivviṉiyic ceyyumpoḻutu nam uṭalum uyirum peritum pāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivviraṇṭum oruṅkē kūṭic ceyyātavaḻi, entat toḻilum naṭipeṟuvatu illi. ettoḻilic ceyvatāyiṉum ivi uḻikka vēṇṭiyiruk
kiṉṟaṉa. uyir uṭaloṭu kūṭiyiruppatu ivvuḻippukkē; ivvuḻippāl, uyir uṭalpaṭum tuṉpamaṉittiyum tāṉ paṭukiṟateṉṟālum, uyirkku uṭaliṉmēl uḷḷa paṟṟiṉāl uḻikka uḻikka, ataṟku ataṉmēl paṟṟu mikukiṉṟatu; "tuṉpam uḻattoṟum kātaṟṟu uyir" eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟukiṉṟār. eṉavē, uṭalōṭu kūṭi vāḻum ovvōruyirkkum uḻippu iyalpāka amintiruppati aṟikiṟōm. āṉāl, ivvuyirkaḷuḷ makkaḷuyirmaṭṭil, ēṉi uyirkaḷiviṭac ciṟitu mēmpaṭṭatākum. taṉ uḻippāl āṇṭavaṉ paṭippil uḷḷa ēṉi uyirkaḷiyum uyiril poruḷ kaḷiyum muṟiyē aṭimiyākavum uṭimi yākavum ākkik koḷḷaṟku vēṇṭiya urimi makkaḷuyir peṟṟirukkiṟatu. ātalāṟṟāṉ iṉṟu maṉitaṉ nilam, nīr, tī, kāṟṟu, vicumpu ākiya pūtam aintum tāṉ virumpumāṟu amiyac ceykiṉṟāṉ. nilattiṟkuḷ nuḻintu kuṭintu celkiṉṟāṉ; nīriṉ mēlum kīḻum nimirntu celkiṉṟāṉ; kāṟṟi ēval koḷḷukiṉṟāṉ; vicumpil paṟantēku kiṉṟāṉ. curuṅkac coṉṉāl, maṉitaṉ āṇṭavaṉukku aṭuttapaṭiyāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ eṉṉalām.
maṉitaṉ ittuṇi ēṟṟamuṭiyavaṉāka iruttaṟkuriya urimi avaṉpāl nilittirukka vēṇṭumāyiṉ, avaṉ calippiṉṟi uḻittal vēṇṭum. uḻippiṉāl avaṉ uṭal tūymiyaṭikiṟatu. ataṉāl avaṉ uḷḷam oḷipeṟukiṟatu; appōtu āṇṭavaṉ avaṟku aruḷiyuḷḷa urimi katirviṭṭut tikaḻkiṉṟatu. atuvaḻiyāka avaṉpāl uyariya eṇṇaṅkaḷum, ulavāta iṉpamum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷuṭ cilar, avvurimiyik kāttuk koḷḷum valikuṉṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar; urimiyi nilippikkum uḻippiṉi nekiḻttuc cōmpaluṟu kiṉṟaṉar. cōmpal uṭalik keṭukkiṉṟatu; ataṉāl uyir maṭaṅkukiṉṟatu; urimi nilavum uḷḷam maṭikiṉṟatu. ākavē, cōmpal maṭimikku uyiri iriyākki, uḷḷattē iruḷiyum uyirkku iṟutiyiyum viḷivikkiṉṟatu. oru periyāratu uṭaṉpiṟantār iṟantupōṉār eṉpatu kēḷviyuṟṟu, vēṟoru periyār avari nōkkit "taṅkaḷ tampi evvāṟu iṟantār?" eṉṟu kēṭṭataṟku, avar, "avaṉ uḻittaṟku iṉṟi iṟantāṉ" eṉṟārām. ākavē, uḻippiṉṟic cōmpiyiruppavaṉ, virivil taṉ uyiri iḻakkiṉṟāṉ; avaṉ uḷḷattē, uyirkkut tīṅku ceyyum tīya eṇṇaṅkaḷum aṟiyāmiyumē kuṭikoḷḷum eṉa aṟiyalām.
iṉṟu nām aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷiyum, periya periya māṭa māḷikikaḷiyum, pacum kampaḷam virittatupōlat tōṉṟum paranta paṇpaṭṭa vayalkaḷiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. kāṭaḻintu nāṭākiyirukkiṟatu; karuṅkaṭalil peruṅkalam celkiṉṟatu;  kampiyiṉṟiyē tanti pēcukiṟatu; virivil, "kaṇṭēṉ avar tiruppātam, kaṇṭaṟiyātaṉa kaṇṭēṉ" eṉa nam nāvaracar pāṭiyati nāmum pāṭappōkiṉṟōm. iviyāvum uḻippiṉ payaṉaṉṟō! "uḻippiṉ vārā uṟutikaḷ uḷavō?" eṉap paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ urittatu uṇmi moḻiyaṉṟō! uḻippiṉṟi uṭal cōmpi, uyir ōyntu oṭuṅkiyirukkum oruvaṉip pāruṅkaḷ! avaṉ uḷḷattil oḷiyilli. ataṉi avalam, kavali, kiyāṟu, aḻuṅkal, āci, vekuḷi, aḻukkāṟu mutaliyaṉa cūḻntu arikkiṉṟaṉa; avaṉum urukkulintu melikiṉṟāṉ. avaṉi ētēṉum oru toḻilicceyya viṭuvōm: uṭaṉē, avaṉatu uṭalum uyirum oruppaṭṭu attoḻiṟkaṇ uḻikkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ vaṉmi muḻuvatum attoḻili muṭittaṟkaṇ oṉṟiviṭukiṉṟatu. avaṉ uḷḷatti yalitta aḻukkuṇarvukaḷaṉittum akalappōy eṅkēyō maṟintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ maṉitaṉ, maṉitaṉāka vayaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉatu uḷḷam pēroḷi viṭṭu miḷirkiṉṟatu; avvaḻuk
kuṇarvukaḷ avvoḷiyiṉkaṇ pukintu erintu puttoḷi ceykiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, oruvaṉatu uḷḷattit tūymi ceyvataṟku uḻippē ciṟanta maruntāka irukkiṟateṉpatu eḷitil viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
nām vāḻum immaṇṇulaku ātiyil ñāyiṟṟiliruntu pōntatu eṉṟum, aṉṟumutal iṉṟu variyil cuḻaṉṟu koṇṭēyirukkiṟatu eṉṟum nām aṟintirukkiṉṟōm. itaṉi ivvāṟu cuḻalvitta paramaṉ karuttu yātākal vēṇṭum? iṭiyaṟātu cuḻalum cuḻaṟciyiṉāl nām vāḻum maṇṇulaku, viṇṇulakil vaṭṭamāka iyaṉṟu, pala vikāra uruvaṅkaḷip peṟṟu, muṭivil, iṟṟiya uruṇṭi vaṭivattip peṟṟirukkiṟatu. cuḻalalāvatu niṉṟupōmāyiṉ, innilavulakam yātāy muṭiyum? orucār pakalum maṟṟiya pakuti iruḷumāy evvuyirum vāḻtaṟkiṉṟi, epporuḷum nilipeṟuvatiṉṟik keṭṭaḻiyu meṉpataṉic collavum vēṇṭumō! aḷittakka immaṇṇakam, iṭiyaṟa viṉṟic cuḻaṉṟuḻalum uḻippiṉāltāṉ itaṉpāl muṟi tirumputalum, nērmi kōṭutalum piṟa tīmikaḷum ilavāyiṉa; vikāraṅkaḷ nīṅkic cīriya ivvuruvamum amivatāyiṟṟu.
kulālaṉatuḻippum, avaṉatu taṇṭa cakkaramum maṉitaṉ uṇavaṭṭu uṇṇattoṭaṅkiya nāṇmutal aṟintaṉavākum. camaya nūlkaḷum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum, avaṉiyē avaṉ ceyyum viṉiyiyum, viṉikkuriya karuvi karaṇaṅkaḷiyum aṭikkaṭi eṭuttukkāṭṭit tam aṟivup poruḷi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. accakkarattiṉ naṭuvil vikkappaṭum maṇtiraḷip pāruṅkaḷ! ataṉiṭattil ētēṉum pāṇṭavamippukkuriya vaṭivamuḷatō? aḻakiya uruvamum tiruntiya vaṭivamumuṭiya maṭkalaṅkaḷ accakkarattiṉ cuḻaṟciyāl piṟakkiṉṟaṉa. aḵtu illiyāyiṉ akkulālaṉ maṭkalaṉkaḷi yākkutal kūṭumō? ettuṇit toḻiṟciṟappum, uliyāvuḻippu muṭiyaṉāyiṉum avaṉ yātuceyya muṭiyum? avaṉ ceyya muyalum kalaṅkaṭku aḻakum vaṭivum amiyuṅkol! iyaṟkiyulakum, cakkarattuṇiyillāta kulālaṉippōla, ōyntu oṭuṅki niṟkumāyiṉ ataṉ payaṉ eṉṉāy muṭiyum! avvāṟu ceyyātu calippiṉṟi, uḻikkumāṟu aruḷiya āṇṭavaṉi eppōtum vaṇaṅkuvōmāka. uḻitta liṉṟic cōmpik kiṭakkum oruvaṉ ettuṇip poruṇalaṅkaḷ uṭiyaṉāyiṉum, avaṟku avi oru payaṉiyum ceyyā. cakaṭacakkaramiṉṟik kiyaṟṟu niṟkum kulālaṉ
pāl uḷḷa maṇtiraḷ pōla, uḻippuc ciṟappillāta oruvaṉum veṟuntacip potiyum ciṟappil piṇṭamumāvatallatu piṟitilli. kulālaṉi makaṉākavum, avaṉatu cakkaratti uḻippākavum koṇṭu nōkkuka. uḻippu uyarvu tarum; uḻippāḷi ulakāḷvāṉ. ākavē, uḻippup peṟṟavaṉ uṟutipeṟṟavaṉā
kiṉṟāṉ. uyir vāḻkkiyiṉ uṟutippayaṉ avaṉ pālēyuḷatu. uyarvu vēṇṭuvōṉ uḻippu vēṇṭuka. karuvarivīḻ aruvikaḷum kāṉyāṟum kikalantu kalittōṭukiṉṟa. iṭippaṭṭa māṇporuḷi varaṉṟiyēkum uḻippu vaṉmiyāl avi kalittuc cerukkukiṉṟaṉa. aḻukkum, mācum, kuppiyum kūḷamum avaṟṟiṉ neṟiyiṟ paṭṭu alittukoṇ ṭēkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kāl kālāy vayaliṭippaṭintu uramākit tām paravum nilap parappip pacum pullum, naṟumalarum kaṇkavar vaṉappumuṭiya vaṇpoḻilākkum ciṟṟāṟukaḷiṉ māṇṭoḻili eṉṉeṉpatu! avaṟṟiṉ teṇṇīr uṇṇīrākiṟatu. atu cumantu koṇarnta aḻukkukaḷum avaṟṟōraṉṉa mācukaḷum māyntu pōkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉālaṉṟō, "uṇaveṉap paṭuvatu nilattoṭu nīrē; nīrum nilaṉum puṇariyōr, īṇṭu, uṭampum uyirum paṭittici ṉōrē" eṉak kuṭapulaviyaṉārum pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñ ceḻiyaṟkup pāṭiyaṟivuṟuttiṉār. ittakiya nīr yātē uḻippeṉa vaṟika.
palatiṟamāy, palvakip poruḷkaḷiyākki, nāṭukaḷi nāṭā vaḷamuṭiyavākac ceytu, makkaḷi iṉpamum, poruḷum, aṟamum, vīṭum iṉitu eytuvittu, avartam vāḻkkiyiṉ perumpayaṉi nukarvippatu uḻippēyākum. ākavē, uḻippē uyir eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. uyir uṭalōṭu kūṭi vāḻkki naṭattuntōṟum, ataṉpāl pēriṉpam peṟutalpōla, uḻippāḷiyum uḻikkun tōṟum uvaṭṭāta pēriṉpamum ūkka mikutiyum peṟukiṉṟāṉ. āṇṭavaṉ tiruvaruḷ avaṉ uḷḷattē ūṟukiṟatu. uyar nōkkum, oḷḷaṟivum, perun taṉmiyum  avaṉpāl uḷavāki yōṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
nōṉmi, tuṇivu, muyaṟci, aṟivup pēṟṟiṉkaṇ ārvam, taṉkuṟṟam tērntu kaṭital mutaliyaṉa iṉpa vāḻvukku ākkamākum nallaṟaṅkaḷ. uṇartta uṇarvatilum, ivaṟṟi nāmē nam uḻippuvāyilāka uṇarntu naṉṉeṟikaḷiṟ celuttuvatē nam uyir vāḻkkiyākum. uḻippu vāyilāka ivaṟṟi nām uṇartal vēṇṭum. nōṉmi eṉpatu ettuṇi iṭukkaṇkaḷ aṭukkivariṉum  avaṟṟiṟku aḻiyātu, avaṟṟāl varum tuṉpattip poṟuttu mēṟkoḷḷum taṉmiyākum. aṅṅaṉam mēṟ koḷḷutaṟkuriya tuṇiyākum. maṉattiṭpamē tuṇivu eṉpatu. tuṇintavaḻi, iṭiyil vantutakiyum iṭiyūṟukaḷāl uḷḷamuṭiyāmal, ceyviṉiyik kaṭipōka uḻittu veṟṟikāṇum tiṟam muyaṟciyākum ataṉkaṇ aṟiyātaṉavaṟṟi aṟintāṟṟum viraku vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, aṟivaṟiyum ārvam vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu. avvārvattāl, mutaṟkaṇ tāmē tērtalum piṟar kāṭṭak kāṇṭalum ākiya iru neṟikaḷāl tam kuṟṟattik kaṇṭu ataṉik kaṭivatum, ceyta kuṟṟatti iṉic ceyyā vakiyil tammip pātukāttu, aṭutta muṟiyil naṉkāṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṉum naṟpaṇpuṭiyaṉāvatum uḻippāḷikku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavākum. ip paṇpukaḷik kikkoṇṭu uḻippōrmuṉ, vāṉṟōy maliyaṉiyapēriṭarkaḷ vantu niṟkum. "naṟceyaluk kaṉṟē nāṉūṟu iṭiyūṟu." āyiṉum uṇmi yuḻippāḷiyō, ēṉiyavaṟṟāl varum "iṉpam viḻiyāṉ; iṭumpi iyal peṉpāṉ; tuṉpam uṟutal ilaṉ." karumamē kaṇṇāka uḻikkum avaṉ, "mey varuttam pārāṉ; paci nōkkaṉ; kaṇ tuñcāṉ; evvevar tīmiyum mēṟkoḷḷāṉ; cevviyarumiyum pārāṉ; avamatippum koḷḷāṉ." cuṭac cuṭarum poṉpōl, avaṉ uḷḷam tuṉpaṅkaḷ toṭarntu pōntu cuṭaccuṭa, oṭpamum tiṭpamum peṟṟu ōṅki yoḷirum. nilavulakiṉum, avaṉ pukaḻē ōṅkip poṉṟātu niṉṟu nilavum, attuṉpaṅkaḷum "eri muṉṉar vittūṟu pōlak keṭum."
ilaṇṭaṉ nakarattil uḷḷa ceyiṉṭ pāl katīṭral eṉṉum peruṅkōyil uliyā vuḻippiṉ uruvuṭip payaṉākum. ataṉi yeṭuttaṟku muyaṉṟa car kiṟistapar reṉ eṉpār, kaṇitanūl, vāṉanūl, ciṟpanūl mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ perum pulamiyum viṉittiṭpamum uṭiyar. āyiṉum, avar itaṉik kaṭṭiyamittaṟku muyaṉṟa kālattu uḷavākiya iṭiyūṟukaṭku aḷavēyilli. urōm nakarattuc ceyiṉṭ pīṭṭars peruṅ kōyil pōlavē ikkōyiliyum eṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉa eṇṇi muyaṉṟa avartam eṇṇam tūrntu pōmāṟu, araciyaṟ pēratikārikaḷum camayap paṇiyāḷarum piṟarum maṇṇaḷḷi eṟinta vaṇṇamē iruntaṉar; poruḷutavi purivōr iruḷil maṟintaṉar; kiṟistaparukkum iṭukkaṇ pala aṭukki varalāyiṉa. āyiṉum eṉ? "maṭuttavāyellām pakaṭaṉṉāṉ uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ iṭarppāṭuṭittu" eṉṟa tiruvaḷḷuvap perumoḻipōla, avviṭiyūṟukaḷum iṭarppaṭṭut tāmē yaḻintaṉa. katīṭral kaṇkavar vaṉappum maṇmakiḻ tōṟṟamum peṟṟu iṉṟum niṉṟu nilavum peruñ ciṟappuṭaṉ tikaḻum cemmi yeytiṟṟu. iṉṟum ataṉik kāṇpōr, "periyār" eṉap poṟittuḷḷa perum peyarik kāṇuntōṟum, nam kiṟistapariṉ viṉittiṭpam, muyaṟci mutaliya viṉimāṇpukaḷi niṉintu pārāṭṭa niṟkiṉṟaṉar.
tiruvum kalviyum ciṟantu viḷaṅkum nāṭukaḷuḷ ippōtu amerikka nāṭu ettuṟiyilum īṭumeṭuppumiṉṟi viḷaṅku vataṉi nām aṟikiṉṟōm. itaṉ uṇmiyiṉiyuṇarntu kaṇṭu uritta kolampas eṉṉum periyāri mēṉāṭṭavar aṉivarum, nam nāṭṭavaruṭ kaṟṟavarum naṉkaṟivar. nam nāṭṭiṟkup putuneṟi yoṉṟu kāṇappukunta apperiyār avvamerikka nāṭṭiṉik kaṇṭaṟintaṉarāyiṉum, ataṉi mutaliṟ kāṇṭaṟ kaṇ avar uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ urikkun taramuṭiyataṉṟu. putuneṟi kāṇṭal kūṭumeṉṟa eṇṇam avar uḷḷattiṟ pukunta nāḷmutal, putupperu nāṭākiya ataṉit tam kaṇkaḷāṟ kāṇun tuṇiyum, avari, avamatippu, accuṟuttal, aṟiyāmi, ikaḻcci, ēmāṟṟam, vacivu, koli, accam mutaliya pala ceyalkaḷ pallāṟṟālum arittu alittaṉa. nāṭṭiṉ kāṭciyi yeytutaṟku iraṇṭoru nāḻiki yirukkumpōtum, avar ceṉṟa vaṅkattiliruntōr avarip paṟṟik koliceytu viṭavum tuṇintaṉar: malipōl aliyeḻuntu muḻaṅkum maṟikaṭal, muṉṉē kiṭantu, maruṭci viḷivittatu; piṉṉē avaratu nāṭu neṭuntoliviṟ kiṭantu, vaṟitē tirumpiṉ avari ikaḻnteḷḷi iṉṉaṟ paṭuttaṟku etirnōkkiyiruntatu; arukil cūḻa viruntōr maṭitta vāyum, veṭitta collum, kaṭutta nōkkum uṭiyavarāyt tīviṉiyē cūḻntu koṇṭiruntaṉar. kaṇantōṟum, niliyiṉṟip puraḷum alikaḷ avartam kalattic celavoṭṭātu taṭuttaṟ keḻuntaṉa pōla veḻuntu alippa, avaṟṟiṭiyē oṭuṅkā vuḷḷamum kalaṅkāk koḷkiyu mallatu piṟitillāta avar maṉat tiṭpam, avvalikaḷiṉ taliyi mitittēkiyatu; virikaṭalic curukkiṟṟu; neṭumpōtu kuṟumpōtāka, nīrkkāṟṟu nilakkāṟṟāka, pakal māya, irā vaṇuka, viṉi muṭiviṉ tuṉivu mika, amerikkap putu nāṭṭiṉ aṭikari pulaṉāyiṟṟu, aṭart taṟkeḻunta alikaḷ avarit talimēṟ cumantu tālāṭṭiṉa; vaṉkāṟṟāy maṭittaṟku niṉṟa kaṭaṟkāṟṟu, meṉkāṟṟāy avar melivu tīrttu makiḻvittatu. ītaṉṟō viṉimāṇpu! eṉṟum poṉṟāta iṉpavici nalkum viṉiyiṉum, uḻippiṉum ēṟṟamuṭiya toṉṟu uṇṭukol! illi! illi! eññāṉṟum illi!!
iṉi ivvuḻippiṉi nam paṇṭic cāṉṟōr āḷviṉiyeṉṟē vaḻaṅki yirukkiṉṟaṉar. uḷḷattiṉ āṭci vaḻi niṟkum uṭal puriyum viṉi uḻippātalāl, ataṉi āḷviṉi yeṉṟatu ettuṇi aḻakuṭittu! kāṇmiṉ. avvāḷviṉiyē āṭavarkku uyir eṉpataṉāl, āṭavaṉi āṇmakaṉ eṉṟal ettuṇi aṟivuṭi moḻi! kāṇmiṉ āṇmakkaḷē! uṅkaḷatu āṇmi, āḷviṉiyē, ayarā uḻippē, ataṉiyuṭiya nīvir eṉ ceykiṉṟīrkaḷ? ayarntu uṟaṅkukiṉṟīrkol? uṟaṅkaṉmiṉ; uṟaṅkutal uyvaṟṟārkkē uriyatu, ōyvu uliyā uḻippuṭiyārkkē uriyatu. uḻippavar ōyvu kōṭaṟkuriyar, ōyvuṟṟavar uṟaṅkutaṟkuriyar; uṟaṅkuvōr mīṭṭum uḻittaṟku uṭaṟkum uḷḷattiṟkum urantarupavarāvār. uṅkaḷatu uṟakkam mīṭṭum uḻittal vēṇṭi uṅkaṭku ūkkam ceytaṟkamintatu; ataṟkuriyapōtu iravuppōtē, ākavē, pakaṟpōtu uḻippuk kuriyatākum. uḻippukkuriya appakaṟpōtu uṟaṅkutaṟkuriya taṉṟu; ataṉāl pakaṟpōtil naṉku uḻimiṉ.
ulakam virintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu; itaṉpāl uyariya payaṉ uḷatu; uḻippavar appayaṉi nukartaṟkuriyar; ulakiyaḷitta oruvaṉ uṅkaṭku uṭampu nalkiyuḷaṉ; avvuṭampiṉi ulakil iyakki uṟupayaṉ peṟutaṟkuriya uṟukaruviyāka uṅkaṭku, avaṉ uḷḷattiyum utaviyuḷaṉ, uḷḷattāl uḷkiyavaḻi uḻittaṟ kurimiyum, āṟṟalum uṅkaḷpāl uḷa eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. avaṟṟi veḷip paṭuttal uṅkaḷ kaṭaṉ. āṟṟalum urimiyu muṭiya nīvir uḻikkumiṭattu, eṅkeṅku muṟi piṟaḻcci tōṉṟu kiṉṟatō, aḵtu uṅkaḷ uyirkkiṟuti payakkum uṟupaki yātaliṉ, aṅkaṅku muṟimiyi niṟuvutal vēṇṭum; nel viḷiyum vayaliṭattup pul muḷiya viṭuvirkol? turum pāyiṉum virumpi yīṭṭumiṉ. paruttiyiṉ pañcu kāṟṟiṟ paṟakkum puṉmittāyiṉum viṭātu paṟṟumiṉ; īṭṭu miṉ; pāṅkuṟa nūṉmiṉ; aḵtu aṟṟam maṟikkum āṭiyākumē; "aṇiyellām āṭiyiṉ piṉ."
uḻippiṟku "ūṟu ceyvaṉa piṟavum cilavuḷa, avi aṟiyāmiyum, maṭamiyumākum. aṟitaṟkariya amiti peṟṟum, aṟiyavēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟi aṟiyaviṭātu, maruṭciyum accamum payappittuc ceyviṉi citaṟuvittaliṉ, aṟiyāmi uḻippiṟku ūṟu ākum. aṟivuṭiyōr aṟivuṟuppavum, āṟṟaṟkuriya viṉiyum urimiyum arukil iruppavum, āḷ viṉikkaṇ karutti iruttātu, maṭimikkaṇ maṭintu kiṭappittaliṉ, maṭami yiṉit takart teṟitalum āṇmakaṟku aṟamākum ataṉic ceyyavēṇṭu meṉpatu āṇṭavaṉ āṇi. "iṉṟu," "ippoḻutu," "ikkaṇam" eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum kālamē, ataṉi ayarātu āṟṟaṟkaminta arumik kālamākum ayarntavaḻi iravu vantu viṭum; akkālattil evarum uṟaṅkuvarē yaṉṟi uḻittalic ceyyār.
ellā vaki uḻippum ēṟṟamuṭiyavaṉākum; avaṟṟuḷ kiyāl uḻikkum uḻippil kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyuṇṭu eṉak kārlil eṉṉum periyār kaviṉak kūṟukiṉṟār; ikkaṭṭurik kaṇ aṭaṅkiya karuttukaḷi vaḻaṅkiya perum pulavarum avarē. uḻippu ulakēpōl paranta paṇpiṉiyuṭiyatu; itaṉ uyarvu vīṭṭulakiṉ ucciyiluḷatu.. mutaṟkaṇuṭalilum, piṉ mūḷiyilum, muṭivil uḷḷattilum oṉṟiyulavip payaṉ uṟuvikkum uḻippē, kepḷar kaṇṭa kōṇilik kaṇakkum, niyūṭṭaṉ nikaḻttiya neṭum poruḷārāycciyum, aṟiviyaṟkalikaḷum, āṇmic ceyalkaḷum iṉṉuyir vaḻaṅki ici naṭum iyalpum yāvum tōṟṟuvitta ēṟṟamuṭiyatu. ātalāl, āṇ kaṭaṉ iṟukkum māṇpuṭiya makkāḷ! uḻippē āṇṭavaṉ uruveṉa vuṇarmiṉ. ataṟku aḻiyātu āṟṟalē avaṉatu tiruppaṇiyeṉa aṟimiṉ; paranta ulakil ciṟantōṅkiya perumakkaḷ kaṇṭuṇarn turitta mutuporuḷum ituvēyām.
uḻippiṉi veṟuttu nonturikkum oruvaṉ uḷaṉō? uḷaṉāyiṉ, avaṉi atu ceyyavēṇṭāveṉa urimiṉ. oṭuṅkikkiṭakkum avaṉ muṉ ceṉṟu, "aṉpa, oṭuṅkutal oḻika. itō, uṉpōl uḻitta tōḻarkaḷ uyarnili yulakattil iṉpañ ciṟantu iṉitē uṟikiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷaṉṟō āṅkē iṉpuṟukiṉṟavar; piṟar evarum ilarē! in nilavukiṉum, uḻippu nalattāl uyarnta varaṉṟō, makkaḷ maṉanilattil maṉṉi, vāṉavarum muṉivararum pōlak kālaṅkaḻiyiṉum kōlaṅ kaḻiyātu eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavukiṉṟaṉar," eṉat teruṭṭit teḷivuṟuttumiṉ.
					


3. varaṉeṉṉum vippu
uṭampoṭu kalantu tōṉṟum uyirttokiyuṭ ciṟappuṭiyaveṉa uyarntōrāṉ uṇarttappeṟum makkaḷuyirkku uṟutipporuḷeṉap periyōr vakuttuk kūṟum nālvakip poruḷkaḷuḷ vīṭeṉpatum oṉṟu. atu cintiyum moḻiyum cellā nilimittu eṉap periyōr kūṟuvar. kūṟiṉum, atu ivvulakam pōl aḻitaṉmālittāya iṉpat tiṉit tarātu, eññāṉṟum kuṉṟā viṉpam tarum pērulaka meṉavum, ataṉi nāṭiyē uyirkaḷ niṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉavum nam āṉṟōr kūṟuvataṉōṭu amiyātu, avvulakanāṭṭam uyirkaṭku iyaṟkiyilē amintuḷa teṉṟum, atu, āyum uṇarvuṭiyārkkē pulaṉāmeṉṟum kūṟiṉar; kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. akkūṟṟiṉi oruvāṟu ārāyntu kaṇṭa vaḻiyē, īṇṭuk kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu.
puviyiṉukku aṇiyāy āṉṟa poruḷtantu, caviyuṟat teḷintu, taṇṇeṉṟu oḻukkamum taḻuvic cāṉṟōr kaviyeṉak kiṭanta kāṉyā ṟoṉṟiṉ aṭikariyiṉkaṇ, veyiṉuḻi paṟiyāk kuyiṉuḻi potumpa roṉṟiruntatu. ataṉ kaṇ eṇṇiṟanta puḷḷiṉam, maṇṇuṟu vāḻkkikku māṇpuṟuttum ceviccuviyamutam kēṭpōr cevippulam vaḷam peṟa aḷittu vāḻtara, avviṉattu ḷoṉṟāya iṉṉicic *cempuḷoṉṟu peṭiyuṭaṉ kiḷimalka avvaṭiciṉip potumparuḷ, peṟṟatukoṇṭu, pēraṟam nērntu, peṟalarum iṉpamē tuyttu vantatu. ataṉāl, atu, eṅkeḻileṉ ñāyiṟu eṉṉum - iṟivaṉaruḷā leytum - neñciṟumāppum, paḻiyañci yīṭṭum poruḷiṉpa muṭimiyiṉ, vaḻiyeñcā vaṉporuḷum uṭiya tāyiṟṟu. ākavē, ataṟku iṉi vēṇṭaṟpāla teṉṉām? oṉṟumilli yaṉṟō!
niṟka, orunāḷ, ic cempuḷḷiṉ vāḻkkittuṇi yāy, ottuṇarvum pāṉmiyu muṭiyatāy, tuṇic cēvaṟku oruvāṟṟāṉu muyarvutāḻvillāk kaviṉuṅ kāṭciyu muṭiyatāy viḷaṅkiya peṭippuḷ, taṉ kūṭṭakat tamarntu, pārppukkaṭku uṇavāvaṉavaṟṟi yaḷittu uvantirukkiyil, pakalceyyum ceññāyiṟu pāviyiṟ paṭiya, piya iruḷ vantu parava, iṉpatteṇkāl iṭivantulava, arukōṭiya aruvikka ṇeḻunta ciṟutiri potuvīḻtaliṉ eḻunta viḻumiya vōci yāṅkaṇum parava, virikatirmatiyam viṇṇakat teḻuntu, maṇṇaka maṭantiyiṉ mayakkoḻippatupōlat taṉ oṇkatir parappiṟṟu. atu kāli, ap pārppukkaḷu ḷoṉṟu, taṉ ciṟutaliyi nīṭṭi, aṉpukaṉiyum āymuka nōkki, "aṉṉāy! vāḻi! nam paṭappit tēṉmayaṅku pāliṉum iṉiya it teṇṇīr yāṟu celliṭam yātukol" eṉa viṉava; kēṭṭa tāyppuḷ, viṭiyaṟiyāmiyiṉ, viḷampal kūṭātu, kuṟunaki kāṭṭi, "makkāḷ yāṉ! aṟiyēṉ; 'kaliṉ' eṉak kalikkum tūkkaṇaṅ kuruviyiyātal, kaṭpulaṅkatuvā viṭpulam paṭarum vāṉampāṭiyi yātal kēṭṭal vēṇṭum. āṇṭut tōṉṟum kōṭṭeṅkiṉ kulimītu avi orukāl vantamariṉum amarum. atu pōḻtu nīvir maṟavaṟka. nīvir kēṭṭatum, ataṉōraṉṉa piṟavum aṟitalām" eṉṟu kūṟi, vāṉampāṭiyiṉ vaḷḷiciyit taṉ meṉkuralāl, vaṭittum untiyum uṟaḻntum icippa, kurī ikkuñcukaḷ kuḷirppeyti, "vāṉampāṭikol ik kāṉam pāṭiyatu" eṉṟeṇṇalāyiṉa.
maṟṟu, av vāṉampāṭum vaṇkuruku, taṉ mellici koṇṭu, kāṭum, kāvum, kaviṉpeṟu turuttiyum, yāṟum kuḷaṉum, vēṟupal vippum, ūrum nakaramum uṟitarum illamum, ūṟucey vilaṅkum, ūṉūṇ  vēṭṭuvar virikkum kaṇṇiyum, piṟavum meypeṟa vicittal marapu. ataṉi yorukālēṉum arukamarntu naṉku kēṭṭaṟiyā ic cempuḷ, pāṭuṅkāl, av vicipporuḷ muṟiyiṟ ciṟitu piṟaḻtalum kuṟitalumāyiṉa. iḻukkuṭiya pāṭṭāyiṉum, ici naṉṟāyiṉmi koṇṭu pēruvaki mītūrat tāmum, tam aṉṉiyoppa, mellici miḻaṟṟal toṭaṅkiṉa. iṅṅaṉam kuravarāyiṉār ceyyum ceyviṉiyi makkaḷāyiṉār, nuṇuki nōkki, ammuṟiyē ceytal uyirttokiyuṭ ciṟantu viḷaṅkum iyalpukaḷ palaviṉu ḷoṉṟu. ituvē, ēṉiyaṟivu vaḷarccikkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum orutaliyāka vaminta aṭippaṭi yeṉpatu kalviyāḷar kōḷ. itaṉi yīṇṭurippiṟ perukum.
veḷḷi muḷittatu; viṭiyal vantatu; paṟavi pāṭiṉa; palluyirum paravukkaṭaṉ ceytaṉa; viḻunta ñāyiṟum eḻuntatu; meṉkāṟṟu vīcaluṟṟatu; pūnāṉkum potuḷi veṟi tici nāṉkum pōyulava, karumpu tēṉārntaṉa. cēkkiyuḷuṟaṅkik kiṭanta cempuṭkuñcukaḷ, uṟakka nīṅki, orupuṭi vantu, uvaki kūrntaṉa. atupōtu, tāyppuḷ, taṉ celvac ciṟu kuñcukaḷiṉ maruṅka marntu, taṉ melliciyit toṭuttu, yāṟṟunīrccelaviṉmēṟ pāṭiṟṟu. akkāli kariyāk kallum pullum kaṉinturukiṉa. curuṅkakkūṟiṉ, avvici yiṉpattāl, 
"	maruviyakāl vicittaciyā; maraṅkaḷmalarcciṉicaliyā;
	karuvarivīḻ aruvikaḷum kāṉyāṟum kalittōṭā;
	perumukiliṉ kulaṅkaḷpuṭi peyarntoḻiyappuṉalcōrā;
	iruvicumpiṉ iṭimuḻaṅka; eḻukaṭalum iṭituḷumpā."
eṉun tīṅkaviyē amiyum.
iṅṅaṉam ciṟantōṅkiya iciyoṉṟil, ap puḷ vīṟuṟṟatē yoḻiya, ataṉuḷattil, ciṟukuñcu viṉaviya poruḷmāttiram pulaṉāyiṟṟaṉṟu. āyiṉum tāṉum taṉpārppukkaḷum orupakal avvāṟu celliṭam cēṟalvēṇṭu meṉṉum tippiyavuṇar voṉṟu ataṉ neñcat takalātu nilavuvatāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu nāṭkaḷ pala ceṉṟaṉa. appuḷḷum yāṟṟunīrccelavu poruḷākap pāṭi vantamiyi nīkkip piṟitōr iṭamoṉṟaṉi nāṭip pāṭutal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. akkāli avviṭamē, ivvāṟu celliṭamām eṉpatu ataṟkut tuṇipāyiṟṟu. ivviṭattik kēṭkuntoṟum ap pārppukkaḷum tam tāyppaṟaviyi, ataṉik kuṟitta viṉākkaḷ pala nikaḻttātu oḻivatiṉṟu.
niṟka. ivviṭam yātāka virukkalām? cilar itaṉi nām yāṇṭiruntu pōntōmō āṇṭiyatu eṉpar. vēṟu cilar nām iṉic cellumiṭam yātu atu veṉpar. cilar, ittakiya iṭamē illi yeṉpar. vēṟupalar nāmum piṟayum nīṅkiyatum; nīkkamaṟa iṉic ceruvatu māya iṭam eṉpar. kāṭci yoṉṟiyē aḷaviyāk koḷvār tuṇipu. maṟṟu, nām kāṇṭal, karutal, uri yeṉṉu mūṉṟiyum aḷaviyāk koḷḷum pāṉmiyē mākalāṉum, avaṟṟā ṉārāyuṅ kālattu, uḷattiṟ ṟōṉṟum cila vuṇarvukaḷum, uṇarntōr palar kūṟiya urikaḷum appeṟṟit tāya iṭamoṉṟuṇṭeṉpataṉi valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, ataṉi oruvāṟu nām kāṇṭaṟkumuṉ aṟiyāvulakam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvōm. *mutaṉūlārum ivvāṟē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār. aṉṟiyum, ivvulakip paṟṟi, iruṇṭa vuṇarvukaḷē appaṟaviyiṉ uṇarvuṭ kalantu kiṭantaṉa.
iṉi, at tāyppuḷ, tāṉē taṉittamarntu, avvulaku paṟṟiya vuṇarvukaḷ taṉ maṉatteḻuntōṟum, icittuk kuḷirntu nāvil ūṟāta amiḻtūṟa. uṭal puḷakittu uḷḷamellām urukik kaḷikkum. ik kaḷiyāṭṭiṉip piṉṉar atu, taṉ pārppukkaḷ kāṇavum kēṭkavum ceytayartal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam ceytaṟkuk kāraṇam yātākum? appārppukkaḷum itaṉit toṭarvaṉa vallavō? avaṟṟiṟkum avvulaka vuṇarviṉi yuṇarttal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? atu pōlum kāraṇam!
iv vaṇṇam nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa. orunāḷ ak kuñcukaḷu ḷoṉṟu, āymukanōkki, av vulaku yāṇṭuḷa teṉṟu kēṭpa, atu, "avvulakam uṇṭeṉpatu uṇmi; maṟṟu, yāṉ aḵtu uṟumiṭam aṟiyēṉ. ākaliṉ, avviṭatti yāṉ kūṟutal kūṭātu" eṉṟatu. eṉṟalum, avaṟṟuṭṭalik kuñcu muṉṉiṉṟu, "iv yāṟu cellumiṭam yātu, atu av aṟiyā vulakam!" eṉṟatu - itu uṇmi kol? aṉṟu. av yāṟu, maṟuvilmāṉavar malinta mūtūr veṟitu cēṟal viḻuppamataṉṟeṉa malipaṭu poruḷum kāṉpaṭu celvamum talimaṇantukoṇṭu, perunakaraṭintu, kālvaḻic ceṉṟu, vayaliṭip paṭarntu, ataṉkaṇ viḷiyum piṅkūḻ vaḷarttu, vaṇikar marakkalam aṇipeṟa mitappa, maṅkiyar kuṭintu māṇalam puṉiya, purinūlmārpar aruṅkaṭaṉāṟṟa, mīṉuṇkurukum, tūṇṭil vēṭṭuvarum, tiṇṭimilvāḻnarum celvam mīkkoḷap parantu ceṉṟu, kōṉōkki vāḻuṅ kuṭiyaṉittum vāṉōkkaliṉ, ataṟkiṉṟiyamiyāp perumaḻi piṟakkum peruntirip puṇariyip puṇarum! ippuṇariyō aṟiyā ulakeṉap paṭuvatu! pakaliṉ pāṉmiyum iraviṉ eḻilum puḷḷiṉattukkoru polivum māviṉattukorumāṇpum vayaṅkat taruvaṉavāka, iviyaṉittum tanta voruvaṉ, ivi celliṭa moṉṟic ceyyātu viṭuvaṉō?
ituniṟka, "aṉṉāy! aṟiyāvulakiṉ aḻakiṉik kūṟuvatāl ākumpayaṉ yātukol! ataṉip palakālum pāṭip pukaḻtal eṟṟukku?" eṉṟu toṭaṅkiya oru kuṟumpuḷ.  "tēṉūṟṟeṭuppa maṇanāṟum pūmpoḻilakattiviṭṭu nām vēṟiṭam cēṟal eṉṉi? meṉṟaḷirum naṟumalarum mikipaṭac ceṟinta kūṭoṉṟākki, nām ēṉ eṉṟum īṇṭē vāḻtal kūṭātu? vāṉkuyilka ḷicipāṭa varivaṇṭu pāṇmiḻaṟṟa, māṉkaṉṟu payiṉṟuḷḷa, palluyirum poliveyta viḷaṅkum ivviḷamarakkāviṉum iṉitukol av aṟiyāvulakam? āruyiraṉṉāy! nām vēṟiṭañcēṟal vēṇṭā. itupōtu, cintāmaṇi teṇkaṭalamirtam tilliyāṉaruḷāl vantālikaḻap paṭumē! muyaṟciyiṉṟiyē perum payaṉ eytutaṟkuriya iṭam atuvāyiṉum, yāṉ ivviṭam viṭṭup peyarēṉ. oḻika! iṉi vēṟupulañ cēṟal. pintāṭ kōriyum, paciya taḷir niṟinta putalum mikka iv vaṭikariyiṉum iṉitukol av aṟiyāvulakam! ākaliṉ, aṉṉāy! iṉip pāṭutaloḻika! pāṭutaṟku uḷam oruppaṭutalum oḻika!!" eṉṟatu.
piṉṉar attāyppuḷ, taṉmaṉattup palavēṟuvakip paṭṭa eṇṇaṅkaḷ paṭarnteḻun taṭaṅka, veytuyirttu, vēṟukūṟā toḻiya, maṟṟoru ciṟukuruku kūṟum. atu, "iṟutta iruḷ keṭa, yāṇṭum iḷaṅkālum, iṉṉoliyum eḻa, poṉṉurukkeṉṉa iḷaveyil parappi, viṭiyaliṟ kīḻkkaṭaliṉ mukaṭṭeḻum iḷañāyiṟṟiṉik kāṇāy! teṇṇīriṉ tiriyiṭi, acceññāyiṟṟiṉ ceṅkatirkaḷ tavaḻtalum, āṇṭup pal vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi miḷirtal kāṇāy! varikkeṇṭiyum paruvarālum naṇpakaloḷiyil miṉceytu maṟitalum, iḷantaḷirkaḷ oḷiteṟittalum piṟavum kāṇāy! taṇṭeṉṟal taḷiracippa, pārppukkaḷ kiḷipaṭarntu icipāṭa, putumalariṉ maṇam parava, kāliyaṇintu pakalellām oḷiyiṉṟiyirunta kaviṉaṉittum, māliyiṟṟuvara muṭittut tōṉṟum iyalaṇaṅkiṉ eḻiṟ cevviyi maṟattalum kūṭumā? īṇṭāyiṉum, āṇṭāyiṉum, yāṇṭāyiṉum amarka. veṇmatiyiṉ taṇkatirkaḷ pintaḻikaḷiṉmītu paravi, kalittōṭum aruvinīril āṭal payiṉṟu, pūṅkāvi vāṉamākki, kāṉyāṟṟi, attiṅkaṭ puttē ḷūrntu cellun tērvaḻiyākka, viḷaṅkum iravup polivi niṉittoṟum neñcam vēṟōriṭattiyum niṉikkuṅ kol! tiṇkalamum ciṟunāvāyum celvuḻiccelvuḻi yeḻum oyyeṉum ōci nam uṟakkam keṭuppa, neṭuntaruviṉ piṅkiḷi paṭarntu pāṭali nam iṉam payila, iṉpakkāṭciyē yāṇṭum ilaṅkum in nilattiṉum av aṟiyānilam aḻakuṭittō?
"tuñcuvatu pōla iruḷi, viṇpaka
	imippatu pōla miṉṉi, uṟikkoṇ(ṭu)
	ēṟuvatu pōlap pāṭuciṟan(tu) urii
	nilaneñ(cu) uṭka ōvātu cilittāṅ(ku)
	ārtaḷi poḻinta vārpeyaṟ kaṭināḷ."
nām nam paṭiyami cēkkiyip paṭarntu, oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu pulli irukkiṉ kārceypaṉippum kalaṅkutuyar ceyyātāka, eḻunta vemmiyāl iṉpam mikum ivvirum poḻiliṉum, nī kūṟum appūm poḻil ēṟṟamuṭittāmō? ātaliṉ aṉṉāy! vēṟupulam muṉṉutali viṭṭoḻittu, ippulam paṟṟiya iṉṉiciyē miḻaṟṟuti!" eṉṟatu.
piṉṉar, attāyāya cempuḷ, iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiya taṉ celvak kuñciṉ peruñcoṟkaḷik kēṭṭu, oruvāṟu, uḷattit tirutti, "celvaṅkāḷ! iṉi nīvir virumpiya vaṇṇamē vēṟoru pāṭṭē pāṭuval. pōṟṟik kēṇmiṉ eṉṟu oru mellici, poruḷ ceṟiyap pāṭutal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. apporuḷ: 
"paranta ulakiṭit tōṉṟiya yāvum taṉ poṟipulaṉ kaḷiṉ vaḻiyē maṉattiṟkuk kuṉṟāviṉpamum, accamaṟiyā viyalpum nalkat tāṉ peṟṟuvāḻntamiyum, avvāḻkki yil nāṭpala cella, taṉakku uḷḷiruntē, 'iḵtu niṉṉiṭa maṉṟu' eṉa vōr poruṇmoḻi yeḻunticittamiyum; ammoḻi, piṉ palamuṟiyum icippak kēṭṭatāṉ, orukāl uvakiyum, orukāl veṟuppum, palakālum kēṭṭaṟ koruppaṭātu cēṟalum koṇṭiruntamiyum; irukkiyil, tāṉ vāḻumiṭamē mētakka iṭamāvatu, ataṉi nīṅkutal aṟavē kūṭātu eṉṉum uvaki yuṇarvu tōṉṟiṉamiyum; piṉṉart taṉ maṉakkiṉiya tuṇip paṟavi pōtantu, kātal ceytu kaḷittuk kalantamiyum; atu kāli, ammoḻip poruḷ paṉmuṟiyum eḻuntu, ūṉkalantu, uṭalkalantu, uyirkalantu, piṉṉar uṇarvuṅ kalantu orō vōciyāy uruveṅkuṅ kalantuniṉṟamiyum; akkalappē, taṉ tuṇippuḷḷiṟkum nikaḻa, iraṇṭum uḷaṅkalantu ikkāṉyāṟṟaṭikari yaṭintu, ciṟṟiṟ camittuc cevviya vāḻkki naṭātti vantamiyum piṟavumām.
itaṉikkēṭṭa pārppiṉuḷ oṉṟu, "aṟṟēl, aṉṉāy! nī paṇṭē yirunta iṭam yāṇṭiyatu? arukuḷatāyiṉ, yām aṉivarum āṇṭē paṭarntu, iṉpantuyttu iṉitu vāḻalāmē!" eṉṟatu. eṉavum, tāyppuḷ uḷaṅkalaṅki, āruyirc celvamē! ataṉi yeṅkuḷateṉpēṉ! aṟiyāvulaka meṉpēṉō! aṉṟi mikamikac cēymiyiluḷḷatōr viṇṇiṭam eṉpēṉō yātu kūṟuvēṉ! yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. āyiṉum, aṉṟeḻun ticitta poruṇmoḻi iṉṟumeḻuntu īrkiṉṟatu. atu aṉṟiṭṭa āṇiyi mēṟkoṇṭaṭaṅkiya yāṉ iṉṟu viṭuttal kūṭumō? ayarāviṉpattiṟṟiḷittirunta aṉṟē avvāṇiyiyēṟṟa yāṉ, iḷamiyum, ataṉāṉām iṉpamum iḻanta inniliyiṟ koḷḷātoḻital muṟiyō? ākavē, eṉ arumic celvaṅkaḷē! av aṟiyāvulakam yāṇṭamintiruppiṉum irukka. nām aṉivarum ataṉinōkkic celvam. vammiṉ!! yāṉ kūṟuvatu uṇmiyē eṉṉum uṟutinōkkaṅ (Faith)  koṇṭu oruppaṭumiṉ” eṉṟatu.
eṉṟalum, “nīyum emmoṭu varutiyaṉṟē. āyiṉ, yāṉ iṉṉē varuval” eṉṟu mikacciṟiyatōr pārppu viḷampit taṉ tāyicitta taṉiyiciyiṟ ṟāṉuṅ kalantu pāṭal toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
iṉṉaṇam, collāṭalālum iciyālum poḻutu kaḻiya, 'veñcuṭar veppan tīrat taṇṇaṟuñcōli tāḻntu niḻaṟceyyavum, taṇpatampaṭṭa teṇkaḻi mēyntu palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa puḷḷellām kuṭampi nōkki uṭaṅkupeyaravum, puṉṉi mutaliya pūviṉāṟṟam muṉṉiṉṟu kañaṟṟavum, neṭuntiri yaḻuvattu nilākkatir parappavum’ māli vantatu. varakkaṇṭa tāyppuḷ, taṉ pārppukkaḷit tamittuviṭṭu iritēṭuvāṉ ceṉṟiruppa, avaṟṟuḷoṉṟu aṇmiyilōṭiya aruviyiṉ maruṅkuniṉṟa putaṟkaṇamarntu, aruviyiṉ kalippum, aṭiciṉi yacitali ṉeḻu mārppum kēṭṭalum kaḷimīkkūrntu, aṅku miṅku mōṭiyōṭit taṉṉallici koṇṭu iṉṉici miḻaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. akkāli atu icikka muyaṉṟa icikaḷ palavum iyipupaṭātoḻiya, iṟutiyiṟ ṟaṉṟāy pāṭiya aṟiyāvulaka viciyi iyakkippārkka, atu naṉkaṉam kivaravuvantu ataṉiyē eṭuttum paṭuttum iṉitu pāṭik koṇṭiruntatu.
“ā! yāḻō! kuḻalō!! iṉṟēṉ perukkō!!! eṉpelām urukkum iṉṉici miḻaṟṟavallatu yātukol! ā! tarukkaḷum caliyā; munnīrc calatiyum kaliyā; nīṇṭa poruppiṉiṉṟum iḻiyum aruvikkālum natikaḷum puraṇṭutuḷḷā; epporuḷiyum taṉvayamākki, uḷniṟi yuyirum meyyum urukkukiṉṟatē!” eṉap pukaḻvatu pōlum iḻittakka kūṟikkoṇṭē puṟavamoṉṟu āṇṭu ataṉarukē vantatu. itu puṟaṅkuṉṟi kaṇṭaṉiyatēṉum mūkkiṟ karumiyu muṭittu; aṉṟiyum oṇmi yuṭiyam yām eṉṉum veṇmi tali ciṟantatu. ekkuṭikeṭukkavō, etaṉalam tūṟṟavō īṇṭu iḵtu vantuḷatu. itaṉiyalpaṟiyāc ciṟucempuḷ, tāṉ kēṭṭa itaṉ poyyuṟu pukaḻcciyi meyyuṟu pukaḻcciyeṉ ṟeṇṇi, peritum muyaṉṟupāṭalum, immaṭappuṟavam, taṉ ciṟutaliyip piyattuḷakki, “naṉṟu, naṉṟu, yāṉum pala viṭaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟuḷēṉ. pala iṉṉicikaḷāṟ ṟōṭkap peṟṟu muḷēṉ. orukāl, makkaḷakattup pōṟṟi vaḷarkkappeṟum pēṟum peṟṟuḷēṉ. eṉavē, icinalaṅ kāṇṭaliṟ ciṟanta vaṉmi peṟṟuḷēṉ eṉpatu naṉku tōṉṟum. yāṉaṟintavaḷavil, āṇiyiṭṭuk kūṟuvēṉ, niṉṉoppār nallicivallār makkaḷiṉum ilar.  āyiṉum oṉṟu, niṉṉāṟ pāṭappeṟum poruḷkaḷ yāvi? avi yāṉ itu kāṟuṅ kēṭṭaṉavallavē!!” eṉac ciṟitu cuḷittu viṉaviṟṟu.
kuṟumpuḷ	: ā! avi yāvum aṟiyāvulaku paṟṟiyaṉa.
puṟavam : evi? aṟiyā...
kuṟumpuḷ	: avi aṟiyā vulakiṉ aḻaku, iṉpam,  vāḻkkiyiyalpu, ataṉiyaṭiyumāṟu mutaliyavaṟṟik kūṟuvaṉa?
puṟavam	: (nakittu) atu aṟiyāvulakāyiṟṟē! ataṉip  paṟṟi nī aṟintatiyāṅṅaṉam? ammamma! muṟṟāk kāyellām muḻutta kēḷvi vallavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. kāṭum ceṭiyum avā vaṟuttal meyyuṇartal mutaliyaṉa ceykiṉṟaṉavē! meyyāka, yāṉ ivaṟṟi yaṟital vēṇṭum. mūttu mutirnta mutumi mikka yāṉ, avvulakiṉiyaṟintu āvaṉa ceytukōṭal vēṇṭumē! naṉṟu! aṟiyāvulakameṉṟu nī kūṟuvatu yātu? yāṇṭuḷatu? aruḷceytu kūṟuka.
kuṟumpuḷ	: yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. (uṇarvukalaṅki) orunāḷ avvulakiṉukkuc cēṟalkūṭum eṉpatiyaṉṟi vēṟu oṉṟum yāṉ aṟiyēṉ.
puṟavam : naṉṟu. aṟintēṉ. pētiyōr pētiyōr eṉa nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avar yāvar, yāṇṭuḷar eṉa āyntu koṇṭē varutal eṉ iyaṟki. aṉpē! iṉṟu kaṇṭēṉ. niṉmāṭṭē an nūlkaḷiṉ kūṟṟu vāymiyātal kaṇṭēṉ. nīyē ataṟkut takka cāṉṟu. ākavē, nīviṟ aṟiyā vulakiṟkuc cellum celavu mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷīr. āṟṟuppaṭiyiṉṟiyē pōlum! appeṟṟit tāya celavu nī nayantaṉi rāyiṉ, num naṉṉar ceñcattu iṉṉaci vāyppa, muṉṉiya yāviyum iṉṉē peṟuka!! yāṉ varuval eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē paṟantu ciṟitu cella, ak kuṟumpuḷ, virutuppaṭṭikku virintu pōṅ kaliyik kuṟukkiṭṭu uṟuvili koṭuttu vāṅkiyāṅku, “niṟka! ciṟitu tāḻttal vēṇṭum naṉṟu, tāṅkaḷ kūṟiyatu yātu? eṉ aṟivu mayaṅkukiṉṟatu” eṉṟatu.
puṟavam : aṟiyāvulakattiṟku amintuḷḷa nuntam  celavu mētakkatē. āyiṉum, iṭippaṭṭa eṉakkum uṉakkumē ac celavu ceytaṟkēṟṟa neṟi tōṉṟātu pōlum.
kuṟumpuḷ  : ō, ataṉi viṭuka. avvulakupaṟṟiya ummuṭiya kōḷ yātu? ataṉi yēṉum kūṟuka.
puṟavam : eṉṉi? ē turumpē! eṉṉiyō ikaḻ  kiṉṟaṉi. naṉṟu! uṉṉotta cīriya icip pulamiyum, nuṭpamum tiṭpamumamintu aṟivu muṭiya evvuyirēṉum aṟiyāta oṉṟiṉmāṭṭu veṟitē niṉintu cāmpuṅkol! vēṇṭiṉ, nī  niṉṉiyē koḷḷalām. uṉṉippōl, vāḷātu poḻutu kaḻippatāyiṉ eṉṉiyum koḷḷalām. oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ; yāvarukkum olluva tollum eṉpatē ciṟantatu. mikuttuk  kūṟal mikka ētamē tarum.
kuṟumpuḷ	: aṟṟēl, nīr av vaṟiyāvulakiṟkup pōkīr pōlum!
puṟavam :	orukālum illi. mutaṟkaṇ, yāṉuṟiyu miṭam,  muḻaṅkukaṭal ñālam muḻuvatum variṉum, vaḻaṅkattavā vaḷappamum, viḻivu viṭutta viḻumi yōrkkum viḻivu tōṟṟuvikkum viḻuppamum vāyntatu. ākaliṉ yāṉ vēṟiṭam virumpēṉ.  mēlum, peṟṟatu koṇṭu peṟum pēṟē pēṟeṉap paṭuvatu, iraṇṭāvatāka, kēṭkap paṭuvaṉa yāvum vāymiya eṉa eḷitu ārāyātu koḷḷum nummōraṉṉōr pōlum  ciṟṟaṟivuṭiyēṉ yāṉalaṉ. aṉṟiyum, nīr kūṟum ulakamoṉṟu uṇṭeṉpati yēṉum yāṉ eṅṅaṉam aṟivatu?
kuṟumpuḷ	 : eṉṉi īṉṟōr eṉakku aruḷiṉar.
puṟavam : īṉṟōrkaḷō kūṟiṉar? aṅṅaṉamā!aṟṟēl, nī mikamikac cīriyatōr paṟaviyē! niṉ īṉṟōr kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟik kūṟiyavāṟē uḷaṅkoḷum nīrmi vēṇṭaṟpālatē! avarkaḷ orupakal, nī ceññāyiṟṟic ceṉṟu cērntu vāḻtaluṅ kūṭum eṉparāyiṉ, nī āmeṉṟē koḷḷuvāy pōlum!!
kuṟumpuḷ	: (cīṟiyatu pōl) īṉṟu puṟantanta eṉ kuravar  orupoḻutēṉum eṉṉi vañcittatiṉṟē!
puṟavam : 	eṉṉi, yeṉṉi? vekuḷuvateṉṉi? eṉṉi ceyti? niṉpeṟṟōr niṉṉi vañcittaṉar; vañcar eṉṟu oruvarum kūṟiṟṟilarē! avar, niṉṉi vañcittilarāyiṉ,  oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ, avar pētiyarē, aṉṟēl, poruḷaṟiyātārē. nummōraṉṉōr māṭṭu oṉṟukūṟal viḻivōr, uṇmiyil,  uḷanōy kōṭal ulakiyal, 'ēvavum ceykalāṉ, tāṉtēṟāṉ, avvuyirpōomaḷavum ōr nōy.’ niṟka, avar kūṟṟu vañcaṉiyiṉpāṟ paṭuvatō, poruḷaṟiyāmi yiṉpāṟ paṭuvatō, nīyē ārāyka. ulakiyalum uṇmiyaṟivu milarōṭu uḷḷōr uriyāṭal oruvāṟṟāṉum ollātākaliṉ, yāṉ ceṉṟu varuval. vāḻka! vāḻka!! (maṟikiṉṟatu.)
iṅṅaṉam am maṭappuṟavaṅ kūṟiya coṟkaḷāṟ ṟaṉ uṇmiyaṟivu kalakkaṅ koṇṭa kuṟumpuḷ oruvāṟu teḷitaṟkuḷ, atu kaṭpulaṅ kaṭantatu.
maṟupakal, vēṉil veppamum, vevviya kāṟṟum, ilavāka, taṉ taṉmiyilōr tiripuṇmiyik kāṭṭiṟṟu. nīṉiṟa vāṉam māṉiṟaṅ koḷḷak koṇmūviṉam viṇ mūṭiṉa. kārkōḷ mukanta kamañcūl māmaḻi vaḷḷuṟi citaṟit talippeyal peytatāka, kariporu tiraṅkum kāṉyāṟu vāṉ yāṟoppap perukkiṭṭōṭi, kuḷirppu mikutalum, uṟintu paṭa, māmēyal maṟantaṉa; manti kūrntaṉa; paṟavipaṭivaṉa vīḻntaṉa; kaṟavi kaṉṟukō ḷoḻintaṉa; kuṭāvaṭi uḷiyam mutaliyaṉa peruṅkal viṭaraḷiyiṟ ceṟintaṉa; āmā nallēṟu cilittaṉa; kāṟṟuṟ ṟeṟitalāl mayiliṉam vīṟṟuvīṟ ṟōṭiṉa. iṭampeṟa vaminta kuṭampikaḷ, paṟavivum pārppum uṟiyuḷiṉṟoḻiya, nilampaṭa vīḻntaṉa. varaṉeṉum vippil uraṅkoṇṭa aṉpu pūṇṭa cempuḷḷiṉ cēkkimaṭṭil ūṟoṉṟiṉṟi iṉitiruntatu. ataṉuḻi yirunta kuñcukaḷ iv vaṉittuṅ kāṇṭalum, peruviyappuṟṟu, pakalōṉ karantaṉaṉ; paṉi mikuntuḷatu eṉkol! eṉum eṇṇan talikkoṇ ṭiruppa, oṉṟu, “peruvāṉam virikati riṉṟiyum, mukiṟkulam paravappeṟṟum uḷatu! teṇṇīryāṟu maṇṇīrmiyuṟṟu aciviṉṟi yuḷatu! pakalavaṉ oḷiyē yiṉṟip, pārmuṟṟum paciya iruḷ ceṟiyap peṟṟuḷḷatu! eṉṉō!” eṉṟatu.
maṟṟum oṉṟu : nerunal uḷaṉoruvaṉ iṉṟilli yeṉṉum  perumi ivvulaku uṭittaṉṟō!
tāyppuḷ : (nakittu) ām! āyiṉum, virikatirc celvaṉi  nām nāḷi kāṇṭal kūṭum. iḵtuṇmi! niṟka. ikkālattup pakaṟpoḻutu kuṉṟivarutali nīvir aṟintilīr pōlum! iṉṟeḻunta puyalē ippakaṟ poḻutiṉik kuṟittatu;  ātalāṟṟāṉ, ceññāyiṟṟiṉ veṅkatirum, im mukilvaḻi nuḻintu veyileṟittal kūṭātu pōyiṟṟu. itukiṭakka. it  talippeyal nam kuṭampi muṟṟum īrañceytiṟ ṟiṉṟākaliṉ, uḷḷē vammiṉ kuḷirppu nīṅka; yāṉ ap perumpeyarc  celaviṉmēṟ pāṭṭoṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟēṉ. pētiyīr, nīvir ip pakaṟ poḻutē eṉṟum kāṇṭal kūṭu meṉa niṉikkiṉṟīrkollō?
in nilavulakiṉ kāṭcinalaṉit tūkki yuritta ciṟupuḷ muṉpōntu, “aṉṉāy! yāṉ, paṇṭu, eṉṟum kāṇṭal kūṭumeṉṟē eṇṇiṉēṉ, niṉittēṉ; maṟṟu, iṉṟē yāṉ ataṉkaṇṇum tiripuṇmi kaṇṭēṉ. āyiṉum yāṉ ataṟku iṉi añcēṉ! paruti paravi paṭiya, karumukil peru maḻi poḻiya, vāṉaṅ karuka, kāṉyāṟu perukiṉ, varum nōytīr maruntum aṟintuḷēṉ. atu, nām aṭiya virumpum aṟiyāvulakiṉ āynta nallici.
kēṭṭa tāy, uvaki kiḷarnteḻa, nuṉikkompa rēṟi, kēṭkum ōraṟivuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyir uḷḷiṭṭa yāvum meypaṉikkumvaṇṇam, tēmpiḻipōlum iṉṉici miḻaṟṟiṟṟu. atukāli, ataṉōṭu cēvaṟpuḷḷum, ciṟupuḷḷum otticittu uvakittēṉ cuvittaṉa. yāvum otticittaṉa veṉiṉum oṉṟumaṭṭil antō! uḷam oruppaṭātu, uvakiyuṅ kūrātu veṟutta nōkkoṭuniṟpa, icipperukku niṉṟatu. niṟṟalum atu, ēṉic ciṟupuṭkaḷi viḷittu, “ivvici, itaṉiṉum pattaṭuttatām, ataṉuṭ kūṟappeṟum ulakupaṟṟiya yāvum uḷḷa vāṟu uṇarappeṟiṉ” eṉṟatu. ivvāṟē, pirivu kāraṇamākat tuyaruṟ ṟirunta peṭicciṟupuḷḷoṉṟu, nakittu, “ataṉip paṟṟi nām mikipaṭa vuṇarvēmāyiṉ, īṇṭi nalattāṉ namakku maṉavamiti yuṇṭākā taṉṟō!” eṉṟatu. ivaṟṟik kēṭṭa maṟṟoṉṟu, “ām, nām avvulaku cārpāka oṉṟum aṟintilē maṉṟō! uṇmiyil, aṅṅaṉamōr ulaku uṇṭeṉpati yēṉum eṅṅaṉam aṟitalām?” eṉṟalum, maṟṟoru peṭippuḷ, “aṉṟu, aṅṅaṉa moṉṟu uṇṭeṉpati nām naṉku uṇarkiṉṟōm. yātō? nam aṉṉi kūṟiya uḷḷoli eṉṉuḷ eḻunticittatu!yāṉ uṇarntēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu.
ik kūṟṟiṉik kēṭṭa talikkuñcu, “uṉṉuḷ eḻun ticittatāka nī niṉittaṉikollō! atu tāṉē! ā! iviyāvum veṟṟurikaḷē. iṅṅaṉam niṉṉik kūṟumāṟu nam aṉṉi kūṟiṉāḷpōlum! ammamma! nī kūṟuvaṉavum, aṉṉi kūṟuvaṉavum piṟavum vīṇ poruḷil yāppukkaḷēyaṉṟip piṟitilli. nuṇṇiya kēḷviyaṟivu mikiṉum iyaṟkiyaṟivu kūṭiṉaṉṟō atu māṭci peṟum! yāṉum appullaṟivē mēṟkoṇ ṭiruppiṉ, av vuḷḷoli eṉṉuḷḷu meḻun ticitta teṉpēṉ. iṉi yāṉ av vulaku cēṟum celaviyum niṉiyēṉ. cēṟaṟku uṭaṉpaṭalum paṭēṉ. celka” eṉac ciṟitu vekuṇṭurittatu.
“iḵtaṉṟu nī niṟkumiṭam” eṉpatu poruḷāka vaminta nallici yoṉṟi eṭutticittavaṇṇam, kuṭampiyiṉ puṟattē tāy vara, akattirunta pārppukkaḷuṭ cila, tāy pāṭiyatiyē tāmum pāṭat toṭaṅkiṉa. anniliyil, at tāyppuḷḷiṉ uḷḷum av voli yeḻuntatu. eḻavē, maṟumuṟiyum, atu an nalliciyiyē toṭaṅki, “iḻumeṉōtiyiṉ aruvi yiraṅkalum, maruṅku kari koṉṟu kāṉyāṟu cēṟalum, viṇṇakaṅ kaṭantu koṇmūvēkalum, mālvari tuḷaṅkak kālporu tōṭalum, virikatirpparuti paraviyiṉ maṟitalum epporuḷ nāṭic ceykiṉṟaṉa. itaṟkuk kāraṇamāvatu, avaṟṟi ṉakatteḻuntu, “iḵtu aṉṟu nī niṟkumiṭam”eṉ ṟolikkum aruḷoli yaṉṟō! yāṟṟoṭu kūṭuka! mukiloṭu moḻika! kāliṉi viṉavuka! nīvir celliṭam yāteṉṟu! aṟiyāvulakamē! varaṉeṉum vippē yaṉṟō! 'nī celliṭam yā’ teṉa veyilavaṉi viḷittuk kēṇmiṉ. avaṉ cēṟumiṭaṉum appeṟalarum ulakē! kuṟitta nāḷ varuṅkāl nām aṉivarum āṇṭup paṭarkuvam” eṉa muṭittatu.
iv vicivayappaṭṭu maṉantiruntiya attalikkuñcu, “aṉṉāy! aṉṉāy! nī kūṟuvatu vāymikollō! yāṉ koḷḷumāṟu yātu? av vulakam uṟumiṭam yāṇṭiyatu? aruḷ ceyka,” eṉak kacintaḻutu, taṉ tāymuka nōkki, taṉakku am maṭappuṟavaṅ kūṟiya aṉittum muṟiyum poruḷum vaḻātu viḷampiṟṟu. viḷampuṅkāṟum amiti koṇṭēṟṟa ac cempuḷ, “arumanta celvamē! alamaral vēṇṭā; yāṉ vēṟōr nallici yicippal kēṭṭi” eṉṟu toṭaṅkit tāṉ paṇṭē yakaṉṟa ulakiyalippaṟṟik kūṟal toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
aṅṅaṉam, avviṭattiṉiṉṟum atu akaṉṟañāṉṟu, tāṉ ēṉ akaṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉittatuṇṭō eṉiṉ iṉṟu; cellumiṭam iṟṟeṉa aṟiyāmē, paṇivum, uṟutinōkkum, nīṅkuta ṉalattiṟkeṉum tiṇṇiya veṇṇamum āya iviyē ataṉ uḷapporuḷā yamintiruntaṉa! peruṅkaṭalulakiṟ pirintu varunāḷ, ac celavukkuriya kāraṇap poruḷi yuṇarttu vārēṉum, “iḵtaṉṟu, atu niṉ neṟi” eṉak kūṟuvārēṉum, atu kaṇṭatuṇṭō veṉiṉ, aṉṟu. aññāṉṟu, ammaṭappuṟavam kaṇṭiruttal kūṭumāyiṉ, atu tāṉum ivvāṟu kūṟaṟpālatāmō? ivi yaṉittum ārāyātu pōnta accempuḷ tāṉum piṉṉar uriya nalattit tuykkā toḻintatō? ataṉi yaṅṅaṉam cēṟal ceyyumāṟu ūkkiya poruḷāya uḷḷoli yātām? atuvē tiruvaruḷ eṉpatu. appaṟavi, ak kāṉyāṟ ṟaṭikari yaṭinta nāḷē uṇmiyi oruvā ṟuṇarntatu. ēṉeṉiṉ, āṇṭut tāṉ atu ciṉṉāḷvarit taṅkutal viḻintatu. piṉṉarē, ataṉ kātaṟṟuṇippaṟavi pōtantatum. vāḻkki yiṉpam nukarntatum piṟavum nikaḻntaṉa. aṉparkaḷē! atu pōtu, kātaṟ cēvaloṭu kalantu atu kuṭampi yamikkumpōtu, taṉvayiṟ piṉṉart tōṉṟuvaṉavāya pārppukkaṭkeṉa ak kuṭampiyic camikkum pōtu, am maṭappuṟavaṅ kaṇṭirukkumēl, ettuṇi ikaḻcci ceytirukkuṅkollō! piṟavāp pārppuk kaṭkuṅ kūṭamittal pētimi eṉ ṟeḷḷiyirukkuṅkollō! aṉṟi, “etirviṉi yaṟital yāṅṅaṉam? nī iṅṅaṉ ceytal vīṇ poruḷilyāppē, iḵtu aṟivuṭiyār toḻilaṉṟu” eṉṟellām viritturit tirukkumō! yātō? niṟka, ic ceykikkellā māvatōr kāraṇattiyēṉum ārāyātu atu ceytatu vañcaṉiyō? aṉṟu iviyaṉittum at tiruvaruḷiṉ ceyalē yām kūṭamittuc cēvalōṭu kūṭi vāḻkkic cākā ṭukittaṟku, itaṟkup pāṅkā yamintatu avvuḷḷoli yāya tiruvaruḷaṉṟō ataṉvaḻi niṉṟataṉā laṉṟō, arumanta pārppukkaḷ peṟṟu, ayarāviṉpattil atu tiḷittiruntatu! itu kāli atu, taṉ celvaṅkaṭkuc celvak kālattum allaṟ kālattum kōṭaṟpālaṉavāya uṟutipporuḷ kaḷi yuṇarttaṟkēṟṟa aṟivamiti peṟṟatō? iṉṟō? “ivi yaṉittum poruḷil puṇarcci, kaṭṭukkati, pulavar puraṭci.” eṉṟellām pitaṟṟiya am maṭappuṟaviyē viṉavumiṉ, avvuḷḷoli yataṉi vañcittatō veṉṟu. aṉṟiyum accempuḷ “celvaṅkāḷ! eṅku, ēṉ cēṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉum ārāycciyiṉṟi, iṉṉē oruppaṭumiṉ. ikkāli yām aṟintilōmāyiṉum, maṟṟorukāli yaṟital kūṭum” eṉṟum, *“paṇivum (Obedience) *aṟivum (Faith) paṇpaṭṭakālattaṉṟē, meyyuṇarvum, poruṇalamum, viḷaṅkat tōṉṟalum kāṭṭap peṟutalum uḷavām,” eṉṟum kūṟiyataṉi appuṟavam uṇaral muṭiyātu pōlum!
piṉṉar nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa. kārum kūtirum nīṅkiṉa. . maḻiyum paṉippum mikuntamiyiṉ, varaṉeṉṉum vippup paṟṟiya nallici, avaṟṟiṭi, ēnticiyum tūṅkiciyumākip piṉṉarc ceppalōciyāy, icikkuntoṟum kēṭṭavi  meycilirppavum, kaṇṇīr vāravum, ūṉkalantu  uyirkalantu, uḷaṅkalantu uṭalamellām uvaṭṭāniṟkum  tēṉkalantu, tittikkum taṉmiyiṟ ciṟantatu. iḵtuṇṭātaṟkuk kāraṇam, eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avi uṇmi yaṟintaṉa; avvuṇmiyi yuṇarntaṉa; avvuṇmiyi uṟuti yeṉavum koṇṭaṉa. ākalāṟṟāṉ, avi āṇṭi yiṉpattiyum īṇṭē yuṇaralāyiṉa.
niṟka, orunāḷ, ciṟukuñcukaḷ kuṭampiyiṉiṉṟum veḷic ceṉṟiruppa, avaṟṟiṉ uḷaṅ kalaṅkutaṟ kētuvāya pēroli yoṉṟu arukē yeḻuntatu. eḻa, pēraccaṅkoṇṭu, avi tam kūṭaṭintaṉa. kūṭu akkāli, nilikulintu, taḷarntu, nīrāl naṉippuṇ ṭiruntatu. iruppiṉum, avi, oṉṟi yoṉṟiṟu kappullic ciṟitupōtu kiṭappa, āyppuḷ illāmi terintatu. etir nōkkiya kaṇkaḷ pūttuppōyiṉa; maṉam peruntuyar pūṇṭatu. eṉceyyum!
piṉṉar avi, kūṭṭiṉiṉṟum veḷippōntu, nōkku miṭantoṟum tam tāyppaṟaviyit tēṭicceṉṟu, taḷarvum, kaḷippum mītūra, īṉṟārik kāṇātu ēkkuṟṟa neñcam tuyarntu cāmpa, kāṇṭal kūṭumō kūṭātō eṉṉum eṇṇam oru puṭayalippa, perumpētuṟṟakāli cēṟum nīrum kalantu ciṟiya nīrniliyum, cuṟṟu mōṅkuta lillāp putalum uḷḷatōr tuṉpaniliyam avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟiṟṟu. kūrntu nōkkiya kālattu, paṟavic ciṟakum ciṟumayirum cāmpiya vōciyum āṇṭut tōṉṟiṉa. kaṇṭa kuñcukaḷ. vēṇavāmīkkoḷa, aṅkuc ceṉṟu, iṟanta toṉṟum, iṟakkap pōvatoṉṟumāya iraṇṭu puṭkaḷik kaṇṭu, iṟakkappōvatu tam yāyātal uṇarntu, paṭṭati eṉ kūṟuvatu! paṭuvatik kaṇṭa tāyp paṟavi, celvaṅkāḷ! “eḻumiṉ! ceṉmiṉ! tiruvaruḷiṉ kāriyamāya uḷḷoli untam uḷattum iṉṉē eḻunticikkiṉṟatu! ceṉmiṉ! ceṉmiṉ! paṇimiṉ! paṭarumiṉ! tāḻttaṟkuc ciṟitu pōtum iṉṟu! iṉpanāṭṭamē koṇmiṉ!!” eṉṟatu. eṉakkēṭṭa kuṟum puṭkuḻām, “eṉṉi? enti yāṇṭuḷār aiyakō! aṉṉāy! aṉṉāy!!” eṉṟu alaṟik kūyiṉa. “amimiṉ! amimiṉ! yāṅkaḷ āṇṭu ummōṭu cūḻa viruttal muṭiyātu; kūṭātu. iṉi...” eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē taṉ taliyik kīḻē cāyttu, varaṉeṉṉum vippil muḷittatu.
niṟka, itaṉi yīṇṭu urittukkoṇṭē celliṉ perukum.
 tiruvaruḷ kikūṭṭumāṟṟiṉi vāḻkkimukattāṉ uṇartta, vuṇarnta paṟavikkuñcukaḷ, iṉpavulaki yeṇṇit tāmum pēraṟam nērntu, ap pērulakiṉi yaṭital vēṇṭi muṭittaṉavāka, palavum palavēṟu neṟiyiṟ paṭarntu ceṉṟaṉa. avi yāṇṭuc celkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟēṉum, ēṉ celkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟeṉum oruvaṟēṉum kaṭṭurittal kūṭātu. kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭaṉa voppa, tam tāyniṉivum, tam paṇṭik kūṭṭi ṉuṟivum piṉṉar avaṟṟiṉ pulattut tōṉṟalum maṟitalu māyiṉa. peṟṟatukoṇṭu pēriṉpantuykkuṅ kālattut tam tāyniṉivu tōṉṟic ciṟitu mayakkalum, avi piṉṉar teḷitalum āya iṉṉōraṉṉa pala nikaḻuṅkālattu, oṉṟu kūṟiyatu, “itaṉiṉum ciṟantatōr iṉpavulakātal vēṇṭum nam tāy paṭarnta vulakam. aṉṉatēyāka, aṅṅaṉam nam tāy kūṟiyatum uṇmiyē pōlum” eṉpatu.



4. piṟappokkum : ciṟappovvā
vaṭuvilā viyattu maṉṉiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ naṭuvaṇateyta iru taliyumeytum eṉṉum cemporuḷuṇarntu, annaṭuvaṇatāya poḷīṭṭal karutit tirikaṭalvaḻiyum, mivarivaḻiyum pala nāṭukaḷukkuc cellum makkaḷ uḷarātalpōla, ciṟṟuyirkaḷuḷ tēṉ tēṭippala ticikaḷukkuñ celvaṉa palavuḷa. avaṟṟiṟkut tēṉ eṉavum, aḷiyeṉavum, vaṇṭeṉavum, curumpeṉavum palapeyarkaḷ uṇṭu. niṟka, īṇṭut tēṉ tēṭicceṉṟa vaṇṭoṉṟiṉ varalāṟṟiṉiyē yāṉ kūṟattoṭaṅkukiṉṟēṉ.
pututtaḷir tōṉṟa, pūkkaḷ malara, naṟumaṇam eṅkum naṉku parava, maliyānilam maruṅkuvantaciya, yāṇṭum iṉpamē ilakiktēṉāṟum iḷavēṉiṟkālattu mutupakaloṉṟiṉ piṟpakalil, tēṉīyoṉṟu naṟuntēṉ vēṇṭik kāvum, cōliyum, kaviṉpūnturuttiyum pukku, āṇṭāṇṭu malarntu vayaṅkum pūttoṟum ceṉṟu, koḷaṟkariya tēṉ mikippaṭakkoṇṭu, ataṉāleḻunta uvakiyāl virintu eḻuntu, taṉkūṭu nōkkic cellat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. āyiṉum, atupōtu, atu pūviyal naṟavam māntip punti mayakkuṟṟiruntamiyāl, vaḻiyaṟiyātu mēleḻuntu ceṉṟu, arukirunta ōr pērakattiṉ mēṟcāḷarattiṉūṭē ataṉ uṭpuṟam aṭintatu.
akkāli appērakattiṉkaṇ ētō oruciṟappu naṭi peṟṟatu: iṉiyavum maṇamikkavumāya paṇṭaṅkaḷ pala malintiruntaṉa. mikappalamakkaḷum kūṭiyiruntatōṭamiyātu collāṭalālum, icikkaruvikaḷālum piṟavaṟṟāṉum pērōciyum ceyvā rāyiṉar. ivaṟṟikkaṇṭa tēṉīyiṉ karuttil accantōṉṟi oru puṭivaruttiṉum, paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ cuviyum maṇamum ataṉi veḷiyēkaviṭātu orupuṭittakintaṉa. takiyavē, tēṉīyum naṟumaṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷ vittuḷḷa taṭṭoṉṟiṉ puṟattētaṅki, avaṟṟiṉ maṇatti nukarntum cuviyit tērntum ciṟitupōtu kaḻittu niṟka, "ā! itō pār. tēṉī! tēṉī!!" eṉakkūṟik koṇṭē ciṟuvar iruvar ataṉarukē vantaṉar. iccoṟkaḷi kēṭṭa avvīyum, uṭaṉē accattāl uṭal naṭuṅka veḷic celvāṉ muyaṉṟu orucāḷarattiṉ mēṟpāyntatu. pāvam! ataṉ katavu kaṇṇāṭiyālāyatākaliṉ, akkaḷivaṇṭu ataṉiyuṇara viyalātu mayaṅki, āṭiyil mōtuṇṭu kalaṅkik kīḻēvīḻntu, piṉṉarc ciṟitu tēṟi, cāḷaraccaṭṭattiṉ kīḻppuṟattē tāṉ mayakkattālum accattālum iḻanta maṉavamitiyum vaṉmiyiyum mīṭṭum peṟaṟporuṭṭu viḻuntu kiṭantatu.
niṟka, itaṉikkaṇṭa ciṟuvarkaḷiruvarum arukē pōntu, itippaṟṟiya oru collāṭal nikaḻttuvārāyiṉar.
ciṟuvaṉ : 	taṅkāy! itutāṉ tēṉī. itaṉit toḻilpuri  tēṉī eṉpatum vaḻakku. itaṉ irutuṭikaḷukku miṭiyiluḷḷa meḻukkup piyiṉiyum kāṇ. ā! eṉṉa ūkkamum uḻippu muṭiyatu itu teriyumā!
ciṟumi : aṇṇāl! meḻukkiyum tēṉiyum ākkuvatu  ittēṉī tāṉō?
ciṟuvaṉ : 	ām pūkkaḷiṉ uḷḷirukkum iṉiyatēṉi itu  avaṟṟiṉ uṭceṉṟu koṇarkiṟatu. muṉṉoru nāḷ nām nampūmputariṉ akattum puṟattum "kamkam" eṉa eṇṇiṟanta īkkaḷ  moyttu icittatu kaṇṭō maṉṟō! avi aṅkumiṅkum paṟantu tirintatik kaṇṭu nakittōmallavā? iḷantaḷirkaḷiṉ mītu ivi paṭaruṅ kālattu ivaṟṟiṉ meyyaḻaku nam  uḷḷamellām koḷḷi koḷḷa villiyā? niṟka, itu iṉṟu kāliyil tēṉīṭṭuṅ kālattum yāṉ pārttatuṇṭu.  itaṟkut tēṉīṭṭaloṉṟu tāṉ toḻileṉal ākātu. itaṉoṭu vēṟupala kāriyaṅkaḷum itu ceytal vēṇṭum. tēṉ kūṭu kaṭṭutal, ataṉiyaḻaku ceyal mutaliya ellāk kāriyaṅ kaḷiyum itutāṉ ceytal vēṇṭum. itu paṟṟiyētāṉ  itu toḻilpuri tēṉī eṉṟaḻikkap peṟutalum kāṇka. pāpam! pāpam!!
ciṟumi : toḻilpuritēṉī eṉpatu yātu? aṇṇā! tāṅkaḷ "pāpam! pāpam!" eṉṟu itaṉmāṭṭu iraṅkuvatēṉ!
ciṟuvaṉ : 	ēṉ teriyātu? nam attāṉ nēṟṟu namakkuc  coṉṉati maṟantuviṭṭāy pōlum: tammic ciṟitum ōmpātu piṟarnalamē yōmpi yavarkkē āṭpaṭṭuc ciṟu toḻil puriyum makkaḷaṉivarum iraṅkaṟpālarē! avar vakiyiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatē  ittēṉīyumākaliṉ, yāṉ itaṉ māṭṭum maṉamiraṅkal vēṇṭiṟṟu. ivvīkkaḷukku araca ī oṉṟuḷatu. ataṉi īyaraci yeṉpatuvē peru vaḻakku. atu oru ciṟutoḻilum, ceyyātu,  eññāṉṟum toḻilīkkaḷ amitta kūṭṭiṉkaṇṇē iruntu, avi koṇarum tēṉiyuṇṭalum, koṇarnta īkkaṭku  vēṟupaṇi paṇittalum, tāṉpayanta ciṟṟīkkaḷip purattalum, ivaṟṟōraṉṉa piṟavumē ceytu koṇṭuvarum. ataṟku vēṇṭum ciṟupaṇikaḷi, maruṅkiruntu ārkkum vaṇṭukaḷ ceyyāniṟkum. ivaṟṟiṟku vēṟāka veṟṟīkkaḷ palavuḷa. avi  oru toḻilum ceyyātu vīṇē aṅkumiṅkun tirintu koṇṭiruppavi. ēṉiyavi yāvum ivvīyippōṉṟu tēṉīṭṭip piṟavaṟṟiṟ kaḷittatōṭu, avaṟṟiṟkām ciṟu toḻilip puripaviyē yākum. ikkūṟiyavaṟṟi nam attāṉ  aṟivārēl avarāl nakittu aṭaṅkutal muṭiyātu.
ciṟumi : ataṉāl icceytikaḷ attāṉukkut teriyāvō?
ciṟuvaṉ	:-illi. avarukkut teriyum eṉṟu yāṉ  niṉikkavilli. teriyumāyiṉ, avar ivaṟṟip piṭittalum piṭittut tuṉpuṟuttalum ceyyā  raṉṟē, yāṉ evvāṟu aṟintēṉeṉiṉ, eṉakku nam cōlikkāvalaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. niṟka, nerunal avar kūṟiyavaṟṟi nōkkiṉiyā? ivvulakil,  aracar, araciyār eṉa uyirkaḷuṭ cilariruttal iyaṟkikkē māṟāṉatām; ellārum orē accāka iruppatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām; iyaṟkiyiṟ ṟōṉṟum ovvoru poruṭkum piṟappiṉāl oruvaki vēṟupāṭum iṉṟām;  eṉavē, aracar eṉavum araciyareṉavum iruppavar naṭunili varampik kaṭantavarē yāvarām.
ciṟumi :āṉāl, ivaṟṟi uyttuṇarntu kōṭal  ivvīkkaḷukku iyalāteṉa yāṉ niṉikkiṉṟēṉ.
ciṟuvaṉ :niṉippateṉṉa! uṇmiyilē atu avaṟṟiṟkuk  kiṭiyātutāṉ. cōlikkāvalaṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟi maṭṭil ittoḻilīkkaḷ kēṭṭirukkumāyiṉ, ettakiya ciṉaṅ koṇṭirukkum! nīyē niṉittuppār!!
ciṟumi :	avaṉ kūṟiyateṉṉa?
ciṟuvaṉ : 	araciyiyum āṭpaṭṭoḻukum ciṟṟīkkaḷiyum  cīr tūkkuṅkāl piṟappiṉāl avaṟṟiṟkiṭiyil  oruvaki vēṟupāṭum illi. piṟappum tōṟṟamum oṉṟē. eṉiṉum ivvēṟu pāṭṭit taruvaṉa	uṇṭivakiyum, uṟiyuḷiṉ  taṉmiyumēyām. icciṟṟīkkaḷi  vaḷarkkum peṭivaṇṭukaḷ, cilavaṟṟiṟku oruvaki uṇavum, vēṟucilavaṟṟiṟku oruvaki uṇavum; cilavaṟṟiṟku oruvakikkūṭum, vēṟu cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟuvaki yāṉa kūṭum tarukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl cila īyaraci kaḷākavum, cila toḻilīkkaḷākavum māṟukiṉṟaṉa. ikkaruttō nerunal nam attāṉ mēṉmakkaḷiyum toḻil makkaḷiyum paṟṟik kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ  amintu kiṭantatu. "piṟappokkum ellāvu  yirkkum." eṉpatu tamiḻmaṟi. niṟka, uṇṇuṅkālam āyiṟṟu. vā, pōkalām.
ciṟumi :aṇṇāl! ciṟitu nilluṅkaḷ. yāṉ ivvīyi veḷiyē viṭuttuvarukiṟēṉ.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟikkoṇṭē, acciṟumi taṉ kittuṇiyāl ataṉip piyaveṭuttu aruḷoḻuka nōkki, "antō! ē! koṅkutēr vāḻkki añciṟit tumpi! kuḻavip paruvattē uṉṉi vaḷarttōr uṉakku uṇṭiyum uṟiyuḷum uyarntaṉa koṭuttirupparēl, ā! iṉṟuniyum oru īyaraciyāka iruppāyaṉṟō! eṉṉa iḻinta niliyi uṉakku avar tantuḷḷār! aṟitiyō? kiṭakka, nī iṉi veḷic ceṉṟu, uṉvāṇāḷ muṟṟilum meḻuku ceytalilum, tēṉīṭṭalilum celaviṭal vēṇṭum. celka. celka. nī mēṟkoṇṭa ciṟu toḻil cīrttoḻilāmāka!" eṉa vāḻtti, cāḷarattiṉ puṟattē viṭuttaṉaḷ. veḷip pōnta vaṇṭu, kaḻipēruvakikoṇṭu taṉ vaḻiccelki toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
ā! eṉṉa iṉiyamālikkālam! kāṭcikkiṉiyamālik kālam! kātalarkūṭik kalanturiyāṭum mālikkālam! iṅṅaṉam iḷavēṉiliṉ mutumāli, maṉṉuyirkkup pēriṉpam payantu iṉitāyt tōṉṟa, attoḻilīkku maṭṭil attaṉmiyiṉ māṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṉ maṉam ētō ōr poruḷāṟ paṟṟappeṟṟut tuyarpūṇṭiruntatu; aṭikkaṭi atu oruvaki yuṇarcciyāl nāṇi mika varuntat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. ivvāṟu varuttamum tuyaramum mayaṅkiya uḷḷamoṭu atu taṉ kūṭṭi - paṇiceytu kiṭappatē taṉṉaṟamākum eṉṉum koḷki yuṭaṉ aṉṟu kāliyil viṭṭuppirinta taṉ kūṭṭi - aṭintatu. aṭitalum, aṭakkalākā veṟuppum vekuḷiyum ataṉ uḷḷattut tōṉṟa, atu tāṉ koṇarnta tēṉpiyi nekiḻtteṟintu, maṉam calippaṭiya, "ellāvuyiriṉum kaṭippaṭṭēṉ yāṉē kāṇ" eṉa aṟintatu.
akkāli, aṅkuc ciṟutoḻil ceytukoṇṭirunta maṟṟoru toḻilī itaṉik kēṭṭu, "eṉṉi ceyti? eṉṉē niṉ ceyki yiruntavāṟu! veṟimikka vēṅkiyiṉ tēṉ uṇṭaṉikollō? aṉṟi akkoṭiya paccilippuḻu nam kūṭukaḷil taṉ muṭṭiyiṭṭuḷatupaṟṟic ciṉaṅkoṇṭaṉiyō? eṉṉikāraṇam?" eṉa viṉaviṟṟu. ataṟku itu, "nī kūṟiya yātēṉum vēṇṭuṅ kāraṇamākātu. aiyō pāvam! aviyeṉ ceyyum? tāntāmuṉ ceytaviṉiyit tāmē tuyppa tallatu piṟar ceyvateṉ?" eṉaviṭiyiṟuppa, atu, "piṉṉar eṉṉiyō kāraṇam. uritti" eṉa mīṭṭum viṉava, itu kūṟum:- "illi. aṉpa! iṉṟu kāliyiṟ ceṉṟa yāṉ mikka cēṇiṟ ceṉṟēṉ. ceṉṟaviṭattu yāṉ, itukāṟum kēṭṭaṟiyā taṉavellām kēṭṭēṉ. kēṭṭavaṟṟiṉ karupporuḷ "nāmellōrum iḻitoḻilpuriyum iḻittakka uyir kaḷāvōm eṉpatē."
ivvuri ataṉ cevippaṭalum, atu tiṭukkiṭṭu, "nī itukāṟum aṟiyāta itaṉi uṉakku uṇarttiyavar yāvar?" eṉakkēṭpa, itu cālavekuṇṭu, uṇmiyi yuṇarttupavar yāvarāyiṉumeṉ! yāṉ iṉṟu aṟintatu muṟṟilum uṇmiyē. itumaṭṭil uṟutiyē." eṉṟatu.
eṉavē, atu, "oru kālum nī kūṟiyatu uṟutiyuṭit taṉṟu. pēticciṟṟuyiroṉṟu iṅṅaṉam pitaṟṟiyatu koṇṭu, piḻi paṭṭoḻukutal peruntakimi yēyaṉṟu. nī iḻittakkōy eṉa uṇarvuṇarttiṉārā lurikkap paṭṭa acciṟupoḻutu kāṟum nī appeṟṟiyi yalli yeṉpati nīyē naṉkaṟivāy. ākavē, avar urittāreṉa nī kūṟum uri veṟṟuriyeṉpataṉiṉum veḷiṟṟuri yeṉavē yāṉ kūṟuvēṉ. iṉi itip paṟṟik kūṟutalum aṟamākātu." eṉak kūṟikkoṇṭē tāṉ muṉṉark kuṟipaṭaviṭṭa kāriyattic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
eṉiṉum, ivvurimuḻuvatum ceviṭaṉ kātil ūtappeṟṟa tivavoliyē pōlap payaṉiṉṟi yoḻintatu. āṉāl, itu maṭṭil, taṉṉotta īkkaḷ palavaṟṟi oruṅkaḻittut tāṉ tēṉṟēṭic ceṉṟaviṭat taṟintavi yaṉittiyum teḷivākak kūṟiṟṟu. kēṭṭavaṟṟuṭ cila peruviyappum, cila peruṅkalakkamuṅ koṇṭaṉa; muṭivil avvīkkuḻātti ṉiṭiyil oru peruṅkuḻappam uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
piṉṉarc cila īkkaḷ eḻuntu, uṇmiyil tām piṟappiṉāl oppāṉavi yeṉiṉum, tammi araca īkkaḷ naṭāttu muṟi iḻivāṉatē yeṉavum, atu tammiyaṭimip paṭuttuva toṉṟeṉavum, ataṉi viriviṟ pōkkuvatu mutaṟkaṭaṉ eṉavum muṭivu ceytaṉa. iṭiyiṟ cila īkkaḷ muṉvantu tām toṭaṅkiya iyakkattukkut talimi tāṅkit tammi uriya neṟiyil naṭattuvataṟkēṟṟavar acciṟuvar tam attāṉē yāvar eṉavum kūṟiṉa. atupōtu, vēṟu cila eḻuntu, muṉṉark kuṟikkappeṟṟavarē talivarāyiṉ, avar tammi oruṅkē yaracīkka ḷākkutalō ṭamiyātu tam kūṭukaḷiyum cevvaṉam purantu, tamakku oru mētakka ciṟappurimiyum tantaruḷvāreṉavum vaḻimoḻiya, okkum okkum eṉṉum pēroli kammeṉa akkuḻāttiṭiyeḻuntatu."
ā! itu takumā? yāvarum civikiyūrat toṭaṅkiṉ, ataṉik kāvuvār yār! inniliyil, ciṟitu nāḷ mutirnta toḻilīyoṉṟu, akkūṭṭattiṭit taṉ taliyi nīṭṭi, "piṟar nalattukkeṉat toḻilpuriyum īkkaḷaṉittum araca īkkaḷāka māṟiṉ, naṉṟu! naṉṟu!! avaṟṟiṟku āmpaṇiyip puripavi yāvō?" eṉṟatu. kallā aṟiviṟ kayavarpāṟ kaṟṟuṇarnta nallār tamatu kaṉam naṇṇuvarō? naṇṇāraṉṟē! ākaliṉ, avvīkka ḷaṉittum, itaṉik kēṭṭatum cālavekuṇṭu, matiyili eṉavum, taṉṉurimi yaṟiyāttaṟutaliyeṉavum kūṟi, "nām aṉivarum aracīkkaḷākuṅkāl, iṟṟip pōtu aracu pūṇṭiruppaṉavum, avaṟṟiṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷum tām uḷḷaḷavum namakkup paṇiceytu kiṭakkalāmē!" eṉṟaṉa. eṉṟalum, av ī maṟumuṟiyum eḻuntu, "avaṟṟiṟkup piṉṉar attoḻili mēṟkoḷḷuvōr yār" eṉak kēṭpa, kam, kam eṉṉum pēroli āṅku mikkeḻuntu. uṭaṉē, atuvum taṉ nāviyaṭakkik koṇṭatu.
maṟṟoru ī eḻuntu, "aṉpuṭiyīr! eṉakku muṟpēciya īkku nikaḻnta vaci aḻakaṟṟatākum. atu namakkuṭ piḷaviyuṇṭu paṇṇiṉum paṇṇum. paṇṇuṅkāl, nām kikkoṇṭa iyakkam veṟṟipeṟātu. nam koḷkikku māṟākavuḷḷa, paḻiya niliyilē tam vāṇāḷic celavaḻittārkkup putiya koḷki kaḷum, uṇarccikaḷum, iyakkaṅkaḷum, piṟavum veṟuppit taruvaṉavākaliṉ, avveṟuppi yuṭiyavariyum nam iyakkattiṟkuk koṇartal iṉṟiyamiyātu.
"niṟka, tēṉīṭṭalum meḻuku ceytalumāya toḻilkaḷic ceyyum nām aṉivarum īyaracikaḷāka māṟiviṭuvōmāyiṉ, namakkāka attoḻilkaḷip puripavi yilavāka, nam nili mikka tuṉpa niliyāy muṭiyum. itu nīṅkaḷ naṉkaṟinta toṉṟu. itaṉi nīṅkaḷē eṇṇip pāruṅkaḷ.
"eṉṟālum, eṉakku oru eṇṇam tōṉṟukiṟatu; ataṉi acaṭṭi ceyyātu kēṭṭu, amivuṭittāyiṉ ēṟṟuṅ koḷḷuṅkaḷ. atu yāteṉiṉ, nām aṉivarum īyaracikaḷā taliṉum, avvaracurimiyiyē muṟṟilum kaḷintu viṭṭu, aracī mutal veṟṟīvariyuḷḷa aṉittīkkaḷum toḻilīkkaḷākamāṟi, namakkuḷ oruvaki yuyarvu tāḻvumiṉṟi orumittu vāḻtal vēṇṭum" eṉṟōr coṟpoḻivu ceytatu.
iṭiyil, muṉṉar vacipeṟṟuc ceṉṟa toḻilī maṟumuṟiyum eḻuntu, "nām aṉivarum iṉṟuvarit toḻilīkkaḷākavē uḷḷōm. aṟṟākaliṉ, namakku ivviyakkattāl oru vakip payaṉum eytutal muṭiyāteṉa niṉikkiṟēṉ! eṉṟatu.
eṉṟalum eḻuntaṉa īkkaḷ. pukippaṭalam pōlat tiraṇṭeḻuntu kam kam eṉap pēroli ceytu, tam ciṉatti evvemmuṟiyil ataṟkuk kāṭṭutal kūṭumō avvam muṟiyil kāṭṭiṉa. kāṭṭakkaṇṭañciya atu muṉpōlavē taṉ iruppiṭam aṭintatu. aṭitalum, pakalavaṉum maṟintaṉaṉ; paṟavippāṭṭu aṭaṅkiṉa; eṅkum iḷiyiruḷ parantatu; nīlavāṉattu nittilam patittāṟpōlak kōla mīṉiṉam tam ciṟṟoḷi parappiṉa; piṟpakal muṟṟum kuḻappattiṭiyuḻanta īkkaḷ tattam kūṭaṭintu uṟaṅkuvavāyiṉa. piṉṉar avviravu muṟṟum vēṟu kuḻappamiṉṟi yoḻiya yāṇṭum amitiyē taliciṟantu niṉṟatu.
veḷḷi muḷippa, viṭiyal vantatu; tāmariyiṟ ṟuñciya kāmar vaṇṭiṉam, kaṭkamaḻum neytalukkuc ceṉṟu, tātūtip piṉṉar, kaṇpōl malarnta kāmarcuṉimalari yaṭintu oli ceyyā niṟpa, muṉṉāḷ nikaḻnta kuḻappattil īṭupaṭṭa īkkaḷ maṭṭil oruṅku kūṭi mīṭṭum tam nilimiyiyum, ataṟkēṟṟa muṟikaḷiyum paṉṉippaṉṉip pēcattoṭaṅkiṉa. vēṟu cila, kūṭukaḷil, araciyar ēviya paṇikaḷi iṉitu ceytu vantavi, ikkuḻappattip paṟṟiyēṉum, ataṉiṭiyē nikaḻum virivuri kaḷiyēṉum oru poruḷāka niṉiyātu tam toḻililē tam karuttic celutti niṉṟaṉa. niṟka, kuḻappattiṟ kalanta īkkuḻāt tiṉiṭi nikaḻccikaḷ pala nikaḻa, mutirā īkkaḷiṟ cila muṉivu mītūrak koṇṭu, ataṉāl tammiyum, tam maṉattiṇmiyiyum iḻantu, peruṅkalakkatti viḷivikkum nilimiyeytiṉa. atupōtu, ikkuḻappattiṟku mutaṟkāraṇamākavirunta ī avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar, "aṉparkaḷē! nīṅkaḷ kikkoṇṭa iyakkam naṉmi payakkak kūṭiyatē. eṉiṉum, iṭiyilirukkum oru poruḷi nīṅkaḷ eṇṇātu pōyviṭṭīrkaḷē! apporuḷ tāṉ etuveṉiṉ, nīṅkaḷ aṉivarum mutirntuviṭṭamiyaṉṟō? nāṉ mutirnta uṅkaḷi evvāṟu iṉi araca īkkaḷāka māṟṟuvatu, kūṭātaṉṟō. āyiṉum, nāmkoṇṭa iyakkattiṉ veṟṟik kaṭiyāḷamāka yātāṉumoru putiya muṭipi eytutal vēṇṭum. eṉ aṟiviṟ keṭṭiya muṭipoṉṟik kūṟukiṟēṉ kēḷuṅkaḷ. piṟaku uṅkaḷ viruppampōṟ ceyyuṅkaḷ" eṉṟatu. uṭaṉē, avvīk kaḷaṉittum orupaṭiyāka nī kūṟukiṉṟavaṟṟi yāṅkaḷ mikka viruppuṭaṉ ēṟṟuk koḷḷukiṟōm. colliyaruḷka" eṉap pēroliyiṭṭuk kūṟiṉa. kūṟalum, atu puṉṉaki tavaḻum iṉmukaṅkoṇṭu, "aṉparkaḷē! nāḷiṉ mutirnta namakku iṉi araciyar vēṇṭuvatilli. nām aṉivarum iṉṟiruppatu pōlavē toḻilīkkaḷākavē yiruppōm; nām iṉi oruvarukku aṭimippaṭṭu avarkaḷ kālāliṭum ēvalit taliyāṟ ceytal vēṇṭā, nāmārkkum kuṭiyallōm! nalamē vāḻvōm. nammik kēṭpavarkkum, tūya vīrattōṭum "yām yārkkum kuṭiyallōm. yātum añcōm" eṉavē, nām iṉikkūṟuvōm. ituvē eṉ ciṟṟaṟiviṟ keṭṭiya muṭipākum. yāṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟil kuṟṟaṅkaḷiruntāṟ poṟuttuk koḷḷumpaṭik kēṭṭukkoṇṭu, immāṭṭōṭu eṉ ciṟiya virivuriyi niṟuttikkoḷḷukiṟēṉ, eṉṟatu. eṉavē, vaṇṭiṉam muṟṟum kammeṉum ici ceytu "itu takum. takum! takum!!" eṉa uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉa. piṉṉar, avikaḷ oruṅkē tiraṇṭu tām iṉittamittu vāḻtaṟkēṟṟa iṭam niṟuvutaṟ poruṭṭu maṟumuṟiyum oru kūṭṭam kūṭiṉa. itupōtu  ivaṟṟiṟkut talimipūṇṭu iyal neṟiyiṟ celuttavallatāya oṉṟum varavilli. ākavē, akkūṭṭattil oruvakikkaṭṭuppāṭum oḻuṅkum ilavāyiṉa. eṉ ceyvatu! ituvum ulakaviyaṟkiyē!!
niṟka, iṭam niṟuvutaṟ poruṭṭu nikaḻnta ikkūṭṭattiṭi, "oṉṟu eḻuntu, kāṭcikkiṉiyavum kaḷ niṟintavumāṉa malarkaḷ ceṟintuḷatum, itō nammuṉṉart tōṉṟuvatu māya cōliyē nām iṉitu vāḻtaṟkēṟṟa iṭamākum" eṉṟatu; maṟṟoṉṟu eḻuntu, "accōliyiṉum, ataṟkuc ciṟitu cēyttuḷḷa vayalē iṉitākum. cōlikku varuvārum, varuvaṉavum palavātalāṉum, kāṟṟum eññāṉṟum virivuṭaṉ mōtiyalittalāṉum namakkum, nam kūṭukaṭkum tīṅkuviḷiyiṉum viḷiyum." eṉṟatu, vēṟoṉṟu, "naṉkaṉam amittuḷḷa vīṭoṉṟiṉ kūriyē mēṟ kūṟappaṭṭa viraṇṭiṉum naṉṟākum. aṉṟiyum atu nammi maḻikkālattil iṉitu kākkavallatākum" eṉṟu colliṟṟu. nāṉkāvatāka, vēṟoṉṟu, "paḻumaramoṉṟiṉ koḻuviya kiḷiyē nallatu" eṉa, maṟṟoṉṟu, mali muḻiñciṉiyum, vēṟoṉṟu kaṟpāṟiyiyum kuṟittaṉa. iṅṅaṉam, ovvoṉṟum taṉtaṉ karuttiṟ pulaṉāya iṭaṅkaḷik kūṟic ceṉṟaṉavē yoḻiya, uṟutiyāṉa iṭam oṉṟēṉum kuṟikkappeṟavilli. aṉṟiyum, oṉṟu kūṟiyatu maṟṟoṉṟiṉ karuttuk kovvā toḻintatu:
