annāḷil namatu nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷum kirēkka yavaṉa ekiptiyarkaḷip pōlap peṇkaḷi makappeṟuṅ karuviyum kaṇavaṉukku maṉikkaṇ iruntu paṇipuriyum aṭimiyu mākavē karutiṉar. oruvaṉ pala makaḷiri maṇattal vilakkappaṭa villi; aracar aintu makaḷiri maṉiviyarākak koḷḷalām. avarkaṭku mēlum makaḷiri vēntar varintu koḷvar; āyiṉum avarkaḷ maṉiviyarākum takutiyiṉṟi urimi makaḷir eṉṟa peyarāl nilavuvar. mutal maṉiviyirukka vēṟoruttiyi oruvaṉ maṇakka virumpiṉ, mutal maṉiviyiṉ icivip peṟṟut tāṉ maṇantu koḷvāṉ. ivvaḻakku mēli nāṭṭilum iruntatu. āpirakām eṉpavaṉ cārā eṉṟa maṉivi yorutti iruppavum avaḷatu icivu peṟṟu iraṇṭām maṉiviyāka ākar eṉpavaḷi maṇantu koṇṭa varalāṟu itaṟkuc cāṉṟu pakarukiṉṟatu.
oruvaṉ oḻukkamum naṟpaṇpum ettuṇic ciṟitum ilaṉāyiṉum pollāta kāmukaṉāyiṉum avaṉ maṉivi avaṉiyē teyvamākak karutip paṇiyuriya vēṇṭum eṉṟum, makaḷirkkeṉat taṉitta muṟiyil vēḷviyō tavamō viratamō illiyākalāṉ, kaṇavaṉukkuc ceyyum toṇṭum paṇiyumē makaḷirkku mēlulaka iṉpavāḻvu peṟutaṟku vāyilām eṉṟum, maṇamāṉa orutti taṉ peṟṟōr maṉiyil iruntukoṇṭu taṉ kaṇavaṉukkup paṇintu avaṉ viruppiṟkēṟpa naṭavāḷāyiṉ, nāṭāḷum vēntaṉ cāṉṟōr kūṭiya pēraviyil avaḷi niṟutti vēṭṭi nāykaḷ kaṭittut tuṉpuṟuttumāṟu avaḷi avaṟṟiṟku iriyākka vēṇṭumeṉṟum, taṉ kaṇavaṉ naṟkuṇa naṉmāṇpukaḷi iḻantu pollāk kuṭiyaṉāyum nōyuṟṟa vaṉāyum māṟiyatu kāraṇamāka avaṉi veṟuttup puṟakkaṇip pāḷāyiṉ avaḷ mūṉṟu tiṅkaṭku uyariya āṭiyaṇikalaṉiṉṟu vēṟōr taṉiyiṭattē otukkappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟum maṉunūl kūṟukiṟatu. kaṇavaṉ colvaḻi yaṭaṅki yoṭuṅki ōḻukuvatē makaḷir kaṭaṉ; atuvē avarkaṭku uyarnta aṟamām eṉa yāññavalkiyar iyampukiṉṟār. kaṇavaṉ uyirōṭu irukkumpōtē tāṉ pēṟu viratam mēṟkōṭalum vēḷvi ceytalum uṭiyaḷāyiṉ, avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉ taliyi veṭṭiṉavaḷākiṉṟāḷ eṉavum, puṇṇiya nīrāṭum orutti, taṉ kaṇavaṉuṭiya aṭikaḷiyō uṭal muḻuvatumō nīrāṭṭi annīriyē uṭkoḷpavaḷ puttēḷir vāḷum ulakil peruñ ciṟappup peṟuvāḷ eṉavum attiri yeṉpār aṟivikkiṉṟār. maṟumiyil kaṇavaṉ eytum katiyiṉum mēṟkatiyi maṉiviyāvāḷ peṟuvatilli yākalāṉ, kaṇavaṉukku aṭaṅkātoḻukupavaḷ iṟantāl iṉpavulaku eytāḷ; eṉavē, avaḷ eṉṟum taṉ kaṇavaṉ maṉam nōka naṭavāḷātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu vāciṭṭanūl vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟatu. kaṇavaṟku aṭaṅkātu oḻukupavaḷ kiyil evarum uṇavu tarappeṟutal kūṭātu; avaḷ kāmi eṉak karutappaṭuvāḷ eṉa āṅkīracar kaṭikiṉṟār.
mēli nāṭṭavarip pōla nam nāṭṭu vaṭamoḻiyāḷarum makaḷiri uṭimip poruḷākavē karutiṉar. ēṉip poruḷ kaḷip pōla makaḷiriyum cūtāṭu paṇiyamākavum, viṟkavum oṟṟivikkavum kūṭiya poruḷākavum matittoḻukiṉar. itaṟku itikācaṅkaḷum purāṇaṅkaḷum mikap pala ceytikaḷic cāṉṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. uyirkku iṟuti nērum kālattāyiṉ, cāṉṟōr iḻikkattakka ceyalkaḷic ceytāyiṉum uyir uytal vēṇṭum eṉpatu annāḷiya aṟamākaliṉ, an niliyil makaḷir aṟamallaṉa ceyyiṉum avar vilakkappaṭār. maṇamāṉa makaḷirkup pati viratamē ciṟanta aṟam. taṉ kaṇavaṉiyaṉṟip piṟar tammit toṭiṉum tampāṟpaṭiṉum tammi virumpiṉum, maṉivi yāvāḷ avarkaḷi maṉattāl virumpāmiyē pativiratattiṉ paṇpu. kaṇavaṉ, āṇmiyiḻappiṉum aṟivu tiṟampiṉum vēṟu nāṭēkiṉum perunōy eytiṉum maṉiviyāyiṉāḷ vēṟōr āṭavaṉi maṇantu koḷḷalām. koṇṭa koḻunaṉ kuṭivaḻi yeñcum niliyil maṉivi vēṟoruvaṉik kūṭi makap peṟuvatu kuṟṟamiṉṟu eṉṟoru koḷki paṇṭi nāḷil iruntamikku itikācamākiya pāratam ēṟṟa varalāṟoṉṟic cāṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
iṉi mēlināṭṭu āṅkilariṭiyē makaḷir irunta niliyi nōkkiṉ aḵtu ēṉi mēli nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa tākat tōṉṟavilli. araciyal vāḻvil araca makaḷirmaṭṭil aracikaḷāka muṭi cūṭik koḷḷum urimi peṟṟiruntaṉar. kiṟittu camayam paraviya piṉpē annāṭṭil oruvaṉ pala makaḷiri maṇakkum iyalvu nīṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. neṭuṅkālam variyil makaḷirkku annāṭṭavar kalvi nalkavilli. camayattuṟiyil tāpata makaḷirkku lattīṉ moḻi kaṟpikkap peṟṟamiyiṉ, ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum pattām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum iṭiyē tāpata makaḷir cilar lattīṉ moḻiyil ciṟanta pulami peṟṟiruntaṉar. ēṉi yāvarum makaḷirkku maṇamum makappēṟumē vēṇṭappaṭuvaṉa eṉṟa koḷkiyilē ūṉṟi niṉṟaṉar. maṇamākumuṉ oruttik kirukkum urimi muṟṟum maṇattiṟkup piṉ avaḷuṭiya kaṇavaṉ pāl cērntu oṉṟiviṭṭaṉa. makkaḷip pēṇutal kalvi kaṟpittal mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiya urimikaḷ tantiyiṭamē irun toḻintaṉa. varalāṟṟuk kālamtoṭṭu āṅkila naṭṭava riṭiyē kaṇavaṟku vēṟāka makaḷirkku evvakic cotturimiyum illāmalē iruntu vantatu; ki.pi. 1882-il tāṉ makaḷirkkuc cottil urimi vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu. aracāḷum vēntaṉ makkaḷuḷ makaḷirkku araciyal urimi paṇṭiyiruntatu; āyiṉum am makkaḷuḷ āṇ makkaḷ iruppiṉ, avarkkē mutalurimi nilaviṟṟu. ceṉṟa 1918-il tāṉ makaḷirkku vākkurimi vaḻaṅkiṉar; ataṉ piṟakē āṅkila makaḷir pārāḷumaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarākum takuti peṟṟaṉar. amerikkar 1920-il avvurimiyi makaḷirkku vaḻaṅkiṉar. uyariya kalviyum kaliyum peṟutaṟkēṟṟa vāyppum āṅkila nāṭṭu makaḷirkkup pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutip pakutiyiltāṉ uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ivarkaḷiyellām nōkkiṉ paṇṭi nāḷit tamiḻ makaḷiriṉ nili peritum viyattaṟkuriya tāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē āṭavar, peṇṭir eṉṟa irupālārkkum piṟappu, aṟivu, oḻukkam mutaliya kūṟukaḷil vēṟṟumi kāṇappaṭavilli. āṇ makkaḷi yoppavē peṇmakkaḷum pēṇappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. kūrtta aṟivuṭi miyē makkaḷil vēṟṟumi kāṭṭiniṉṟatu. tiruvaḷḷuvarum aṟivaṟinta makkaṭ pēṟṟiyē ciṟanta pēṟāka eṭutturik kiṉṟār. ataṟkuk kāraṇam, āṇāyiṉum peṇṇāyiṉum, makkaḷ tammiṉum mikka aṟivunalam uṭiyarāyiṉum ulakiluḷḷa maṉṉuyirkkellām vāḻvu iṉitām eṉpatu karuttu. ataṉāl āṭavar peṇṭir irupālārum tiruntiya kalvi peṟuvatil vēṟupāṭiṉṟi yoḻukiṉar. peṇ makkaṭkuriya nalam kūṟappukka tolkāppiyar "ceṟivum niṟiyum cemmiyum ceppum, aṟivum arumiyum peṇ pālāṉa" eṉṟu terivikkiṉṟār. kalvi kēḷvikaḷāl uṇṭākum tiṇṇiya aṟivoḻukkam kaṟpu eṉṟu tamiḻc cāṉṟōrāl kuṟikkappaṭum. āṭukōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉ eṉṟa vēntaṉatu nalam kūṟappukunta cāṉṟōr, "ulakam tōṉṟiya mēmpaṭu kaṟpiṉ villōr meymmaṟi" eṉṟum, vētaṅkaḷik kuṟikkak karutiya cāṉṟōr, avaṟṟi "eḻutāk kaṟpu" eṉṟum kuṟippatē ipporuṇmikku ēṟṟa cāṉṟākum. makaḷirkkuk kalviyaṟiviṉ tiṇinili yoḻukkattik kaṟpeṉa vāḷā ōtāmal, "ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpu" eṉṟu tolkāppiyar ciṟappittuk kūṟiyaruḷu kiṉṟār. kaṟpeṉṉum tiṇmiyuṇṭāvatu makaḷirkkuriya takutikaḷuḷ perumi vāyntatu eṉṟu paṇṭit tamiḻar paṇittuḷḷaṉar.
makaḷir makappeṟutal eṉpatu iyaṟkiyaṟam; ataṉāl uṭaṟkūṟu vēṟupaṭṭataṉṟi, āṇukku aṭimiyāyt taṉakkeṉa urimiyum ceyalumaṟṟiruttaṟkaṉṟu eṉpatu tamiḻ marapu. āṇṭavaṉ paṭittaḷitta ivvulakil āṇippōlap peṇṇum vāḻap piṟantamiyiṉ āṇukku urimitantu peṇ aṭimiyāyk kiṭantu maṭiya vēṇṭumeṉpatu aṟamākātu. āṇ makkaḷi pōlātu peṇmakaḷ orutti maṉiyiṉkaṇ ceṟippuṇṭirunta pōtu urimi vēṭkiyāl untap peṟṟamiyiṉ
"	viḷiyāṭu āyamōṭu ōri āṭātu
	iḷiyōr illiṭattu iṟceṟin tiruttal
	aṟaṉum aṉṟu ākkamum tēym."
eṉṟu kūṟuvatu iṅkē niṉivukūrat takuvatām. āṇukkup peṇṇum peṇṇukku āṇum vāḻkkit tuṇiyāvaṉa. vāḻkki irupālārkkum potu; iruvarum kūṭiyē ataṉic ceytal iyaṟkiyātalāl iruvarkkiṭiyē vēṟṟumi kaṇṭu pukuttuvatu iyaṟki vāḻkkiyi iṭiyūṟṟuk kuḷḷākkum eṉpatu kaṇṭē paṇṭic cāṉṟōr maṉiviyi vāḻkkit tuṇi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉar. avaḷatu tuṇimiyiṉ ciṟappuṇarntu mēṟkoṇṭa kaṇavaṉik koṇṭāṉ eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar.
paḻantamiḻar kuṭiyil kalvip pēṟṟil āṇum peṇṇum otta urimi peṟṟuḷḷaṉar. maṇṇāḷum vēntarkkuriya nīti naṉṉūl palavum peṇkaḷum kaṟṟiruntaṉar. piṟanta kuṭiyiṉ perumiyiyum pukunta kuṭiyiṉ tolvaraviyum makaḷir naṉkuṇarntu kuṭipperumi kuṉṟāta vakiyil vāḻkkiyi naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum ulakiyal vāḻkkikku oruvark koruvar iṉṟiyamiyāt tuṇivarātaliṉ, kaṇavaṉ
pāl kāṇappaṭum kuṟiyi makaḷirum makaḷiratu kuṟiyik kaṇavaṉum naṉku uṇarntu kuṇam pēṇi vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. kaṇavaṉ pāl piḻi kāṇumiṭattu aṉpuṭiya naṉmoḻikaḷāl tāy pōl kaḻaṟikkūṟi nīkkiyum, nalam kāṇumiṭattu makiḻcci kūrntum, makaḷir naṭantu koḷvatu ciṟanta aṟamākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. "tāy pōṟ kaḻaṟit taḻī ikkōṭal, āymaṉik kiḻattikkum urittu eṉa moḻipa" eṉat  tolkāppiyaṉār cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟār. koṇṭa kaṇavaṉpāl orukāl piḻi tōṉṟak kaṇṭa avaṉ maṉivi tīviya iṉiya kūriya coṟkaḷāl avaṉik kaḻaṟiṉāḷāka, ataṉāl avaṉ neñcu varuntiṉāṉ; an niliyil avviruvar vāḻkkiyiṉ nalam pēṇi niṉṟa naṅki yoruttipōntu, avaṉit teruṭṭi avaṉik kaḻaṟik kūṟiya maṉiviyiṉ urimic ciṟappi eṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṟṟu,
"oṇṭoṭi maṭamakaḷ ivaḷiṉum, 	nuntiyum yāyum kaṭiyarō niṉṉē" 1
eṉṟu kūṟiyatāka āciriyar ōrampōkiyār urikkiṉṟār.
illiliruntu aṟam purintu oḻukumiṭattuk kaṇavaṉ, poruḷ ceytal, pōrviṉi mutaliya mēṟkoṇṭu cēṟal ākiya kaṭami kāraṇamākap pirintu cellavēṇṭiṉ, kātal vaḻiniṟkum pirivut tuṉpam tōṉṟi maṉiviyiṉ maṉatti varuttuvatu iyaṟki. kātalukkum kaṭamikkum iṭiyē nikaḻum pōril kaṭami vīḻcciyuṟiṉ atu kātal vāḻkkiyil nalkuraviyum veṟuppiyum viḷivittut tuṉpam payakkum eṉpatip paḻantamiḻk kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum naṉku uṇarntiruntaṉar. kaṇavaṉ piriyuṅkāl vaṟpuṟuttum coṟkaḷiyē paṟṟukkōṭākak koṇṭu pirivut tuṉpatti āṟṟi maṉikkaṇṇē tām ceyvaṉavaṟṟi muṭṭiṉṟic ceytoḻukuvatē maṉiyuṟi makaḷirkku māṇpum kaṟpumām eṉpatu tamiḻmarapu.
maṉiyiṉkaṇ vāḻuma makaḷir tām peṟṟamakkaṭku moḻi payiṟṟum mutaṟkalvi nalkumpōtē avarkaḷiṭattē nalloḻukkam vērūṉṟi vaḷarac ceytal tam kaṭaṉ eṉpati uṇarntu oḻuku kiṉṟaṉar. viḷiyāṭum paruvattaṉāṉa taṉ makaṉi nōkkiṉa oru tamiḻttāy, avaṉ tantipāl uḷḷa kuṇanalaṅkaḷiyum kuṟikaḷiyum eṭuttōti, kuṇaṅkaḷik koṇṭu kuṟikaḷik kiviṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kaṟpikkap pukunta,
cemmāl, vaṉappellām nuntiyi oppiṉum nunti nilippāluḷ ottakuṟi eṉ vāykkēṭṭu otti,
eṉṟu muṉmoḻintu, kaṇavaṉ pōrval maṟavaṉ eṉṟum, pōriyum ataṟkuriya aṟam piḻiyātu ceytu veṟṟi koḷvatil vīṟuṭiyaṉ eṉṟum kūṟuvāḷāy,
"	kaṉṟiya tevvark kaṭantu kaḷaṅkoḷḷum 	veṉṟi māṭṭu otti peruma."
eṉṟum, ivvāṟē avaṉuṭiya naṭuvu nilimi, īkic ciṟappu ākiyavaṟṟiyum,
	"pālkoḷa liṉṟip pakalpōl muṟikku olkāk
kōlcemmi yotti peruma."	eṉṟum,
"	vītal aṟiyā viḻupporuḷ nacciyārkku
	ītalmāṭṭu otti peruma"			eṉṟum,
kūṟuvatu iṉṟiya tāyarkku nalla paṭippiṉiyākum. ivviyalpu paṟṟiyē, poṉmuṭiyār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr, orutāy taṉ makaṉukkuk kaṭamiyuṇarvu nalkum kaṭṭuri vaṭivil,
"	īṉṟu puṟantarutal eṉtalik kaṭaṉē
	cāṉṟō ṉākkutal tantikkuk kaṭaṉē
	vēlvaṭittuk koṭuttal kollaṟkuk kaṭaṉē
	naṉṉaṭi nalkal vēntarkkuk kaṭaṉē
	oḷiṟuvāḷ aruñcamam murukkik
	kaḷiṟueṟintu peyartal kāḷikkuk kaṭaṉē."
eṉṟu kaṭṭurikkiṉṟār.
iṅṅaṉam tam kuṭiyil vāḻum makkaḷ kaṭamiyuṇarvum nalloḻukkamum koṇṭu ciṟattal vēṇṭumeṉak karutum tamiḻ makaḷir, tām vāḻum nāṭṭiṉ nalattiyum tam neñcil koṇṭu, vāytta pōtellām, "neṟpala polika poṉ peritu ciṟakka" "viḷika vayalē varuka iravalar" "paci illākuka piṇi cēṉ nīṅkuka" "vēntu paki taṇikayāṇṭu pala nantuka" 'aṟam naṉi ciṟakka allatu keṭuka" "aracu muṟi ceyka kaḷavu illākuka" "naṉṟu peritu ciṟakka tītu illākuka" "māri vāykka vaḷam naṉi ciṟakka" eṉa viḻintu vēṇṭuvar.
makaḷiruṭiya maṉivāḻvil kaṇavaṉatu kātaluḷḷam taṉṉiṉ nīṅki vēṟu - makaḷirpāl cellumāyiṉ, am makaḷirkku ataṉiṉum mikka varuttam taruvatu vēṟu kiṭiyātu. ulaka nāṭukaḷil eṅku nōkkiṉum makaḷirkkuriya iv viyalpu oṉṟākavē uḷḷatu, mēli nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa ekiptu, apicīṉiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum kīḻ nāṭṭuc cīṉam, cappāṉ nāṭukaḷilum uḷḷa makaḷir eppōtum kaṇavaṉuṭiya maṉa niṟiviyē kuṟik
kōḷākak koṇṭu oḻukuvar. cila nāṭṭu makaḷir tām ceyyum
ovvoru ceyalum kaṇavaṟku uvaki tarukiṟatā eṉpati aṭikkaṭi kēṭṭu aṟintu koḷvar. ataṉāl, kaṇavaṉatu kātalaṉpu piṟa makaḷirpāl cellātu. kāttoḻukuvati avarkaḷ kaṇṇum karuttumākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. uruciya nāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum, amerikka nāṭṭuc cevvintiyarum āṭavariṉum makaḷirit tāḻntavarākak karuti oḻukupavarāyiṉum, avar nāṭṭu makaḷirkkuk kaṇavaṉpāl parattatimi tōṉṟiṉ peritum varuntuvati avar nāṭṭu varalāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. cumēriyā nāṭṭil makaḷiriṭiyē parattatimi yoḻukkam peritum paraviyiruntatu; eṉiṉum, maṉiyaṟam pūṇṭa makaḷir kaṇavaṉ parattaṉāvatu kāṇa mikavum añci yoḻukukiṉṟaṉar.
mēlē kāṭṭiya paḻaṅkuṭikaḷōṭu evvakiyilum piṟpaṭāta toṉmi vāynta tamiḻ makaḷir vilakkallarākaliṉ, kaṇavaṉatu parattatimi maṉiyuṟiyum makkaḷukku mikka varuttatti viḷivittatu. taṉṉik koṇṭa kaṇavaṉ taṉṉi
yoḻiyap piṟamakaḷirik kaṉaviṉum karutāta pērāṇmiyuṭi
yaṉākap peṟiṉ peṇṭir puttēḷir vāḻum ulakil peruñ ciṟappup peṟuvar eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷattakka vakiyil, "peṟṟāṟ peṟiṟ peṟuvar peṇṭir peruñciṟappup puttēḷir vāḻum ulaku" eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷ amintirukkiṟatu. kaṇavaṉ parattaṉāyiṉ atu taṉakkuc ciṟumi payappatōṭu taṉ kuṭikkum paḻi tarum eṉṟa karuttāl tamiḻ makaḷir avaṉ uḷḷam anneṟiyiṟ celvati virumpavilli. "pūvōraṉiyar makaḷir, vaṇṭōraṉiyar āṭavar" eṉṟa karuttoṉṟum annāḷil makkaḷiṭiyē nilaviyiruntatu. eṉṟālum makaḷiruḷḷam kaṇavaṉatu kātal piṟamakaḷirpāl paravuvatic ciṟitum uṭaṉpaṭavilli.
ippōtu paḻaṉiyeṉa vaḻaṅkum ūrkku muṉṉāḷil tiruvāvi naṉkuṭi eṉṟum paḻaṅkālattil potiṉi yeṉṟum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ataṉarukē āykkuṭi yeṉṟa ōr ūruḷatu; ataṉ uṇmip peyar āvikuṭi eṉpatu, ataṉit talinakarākak koṇṭa ap pakutiyil āviyar eṉpār vāḻntaṉar. ataṉāl ataṉi viyāvi nāṭu eṉpar; paṇṭi nāḷil aṅkē perum
pēkaṉ eṉṟa aracaṉ āṭci purintāṉ. avaṉi viyāvik kōpperumpēkaṉ eṉpatu vaḻakkam; avaṉuṭiya maṉivi peyar kaṇṇaki eṉpatu. orukāl avaṉukku vēṟoru peṇṇiṉ toṭar puṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl kaṇṇakikku maṉakkavali periyatāyiṟṟu. pēkaṉ aruḷ uḷḷam paṭitta peruntaki; kāṭṭil vāḻum mayil oṉṟukkup pōrvi yītta pukaḻāḷaṉ; "paṭāam maññikku ītta eṅkō, kaṭāa yāṉik kalimāṉ pēkaṉ" eṉṟu cāṉṟōr avaṉip pārāṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. avaṉukku aricil kiḻār, kapilar, paraṇar eṉṟa cāṉṟōr palar iṉiya naṇparāvar. kaṇṇaki avarkaṭkup pēkaṉatu parattimiyic colli avaṉi anneṟiyiṉiṉṟum nīkkumāṟu muyaṉṟāḷ. orukāl avaḷ maṉikkup pōnta paraṇar eṉṟa nallicic cāṉṟōrkku,
"	empōl orutti nalaṉ nayantu eṉṟum
	varūum eṉpa viyaṅku pukaḻp pēkaṉ
	olleṉa olikkum tēroṭu
	mulli vēli nallūrāṉē" 1
eṉṟu muṟiyiṭṭaṉaḷ. avarkaḷum avaḷatu vēṇṭukōṭku iṇaṅki, pēkaṉik kaṇṭu vēntē, nī maṉiyaṟam māṇpuṟac ceytal vēṇṭum; nī iṉṟiravē niṉ maṉiyuṟiyum kaṇṇakipāl ceṉṟu avaḷ eyti varuntum tuyarattip pōkkiyaruḷal vēṇṭum eṉpārāy,
"	aṟañ ceytīmē aruḷvey yōyeṉa
	iḵtuyām iranta paricil; aḵtu iruḷiṉ
	iṉamaṇi neṭuntē rēṟi
	iṉṉātu uṟivi arumpaṭar kaḷimē."
eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉar. avaṉum avvaṇṇamē ceytu aṟavāḻkkiyil nili peṟuvāṉāyiṉāṉ. avaṉatu viyāvi nāṭṭiṉ pukaḻ vicaya nakara vēntar kālam varit tamiḻakattil ciṟantu niṉṟatu; ak kālattil viyāvi viyāpuri eṉac citintu perum piṟitāki maṟintatu. niṟka, kōvalaṉ maṉiviyākiya kaṇṇakiyār tam kaṇavaṉ cilampu tiruṭiya kaḷvaṉ eṉṟu paḻi tūṟṟappaṭṭup pāṇṭi vēntaṉāl koliyuṇṭāṉ eṉpatu kēṭṭu ataṉāl tam celvak kuṭikkuṇṭāṉa pukaḻ mācu paṭuvatu niṉintu vēntaṉ muṉceṉṟu vaḻakkuritta varalāṟu nāṭaṟinta toṉṟu.
varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṉpiruntē tamiḻ makkaḷ pōr maṟattukkup peyarum māṇpum piṟaṅkap peṟṟavar. kirēkka, pāpilōṉiya ekiptiya makaḷirip pōlat tamiḻ makaḷir pōrp pukaḻip peritum virumpiṉar. pōril puṟaṅkoṭuttuk kuṭikkup paḻi viḷittāṉ taṉ vayiṟṟiṟ piṟanta makaṉ eṉiṉ avaḷatu maṟavuḷḷam ciṟitum poṟātu. pōrukkuc ceṉṟirunta iḷiyōṉ oruvaṉip paṟṟi avaṉ tāyiṭam pōntu poyyāka, "aṉṉāy, niṉmakaṉ pakivar toṭutta pōril mutuku tantu ōṭiṉaṉ" eṉṟu ūravar alar kūṟiṉar; atu kēṭṭatum, avaṉ tāyiṉ maṟavuḷḷam kotittatu; "ūravar kūṟuvatu uṇmiyāyiṉ, avaṉ vāy vittup pāluṇṭa eṉ mārpi aṟutteṟivēṉ" eṉṟu vañciṉam kūṟip pōrk kaḷam ceṉṟu aṅkē iṟantu kiṭakkum vayavar uṭalaṅkaḷip puraṭṭik kāṇaluṟṟāḷ avaṟṟiṉ iṭiyē, mutukiṟ puṇpaṭātu mukattilum mārpilum puṇpaṭṭuc citintu vēṟupaṭṭuk kiṭanta avaṉ uṭampiṉik kaṇṭu pēruvaki koṇṭāḷ. itaṉik kākki pāṭiṉiyār eṉṟa perumāṭṭi,
			"mutiyōḷ ciṟuvaṉ 
paṭiyaḻintu māṟiṉaṉ eṉṟupalar kūṟa
maṇṭamarkku uṭintaṉa ṉāyiṉ, uṇṭeṉ
muliyaṟut tiṭuveṉ yāṉeṉac ciṉiik
koṇṭavāḷoṭu paṭupiṇam peyarāc
ceṅkaḷam tuḻavuvōḷ citintuvē ṟākiya
paṭumakaṉ kiṭakki kāṇūu
īṉṟa ñāṉṟiṉum perituvan taṉaḷē."
eṉṟu pāṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟār. vēṟoruttiyiṉ tanti muṉporukāl naṭanta tumpip pōril uyir koṭuttup pukaḻ koṇṭāṉ; avaḷ kaṇavaṉ muṉṉāḷ naṭanta karantip pōril nirikāvalil uyir koṭuttāṉ. maṟunāḷ pōr nikaḻcci terivikkum paṟiyōci kēṭṭatum taṉ oru makaṉiyum pōrk kōlam ceytu vēṟpaṭiyik kiyil tantu cerumukam celka eṉa viṭuttāḷ; itaṉi,
"	iṉṟum, ceruppaṟi kēṭṭu viruppuṟṟu mayaṅki
	vēlkik koṭuttu veḷitu virittuṭīip
	pāṟumayirk kuṭumi eṇṇey nīvi
	orumaka ṉallatu illōḷ
	cerumuka nōkkic celkeṉa viṭumē."
eṉṟu cāṉṟōr eṭutturikkiṉṟaṉar.
ivvāṟu tammuṭiya maṉikkaṇ uḷḷa āṭavar pōril maṟam kuṉṟātu porutu pukaḻ peṟutali virumpum tamiḻmakaḷir pōriṟ puṇpaṭṭuvarum vayavarkku maruttuvam ceyvatil mikka māṇpuṟukiṉṟaṉar. kiḻinta puṇkaḷit tittalum maruntiṭutalum iṉiya paṇṇil icipāṭutalum maruttuvat tuṟiyil kiyāḷap
paṭukiṉṟaṉa. puṇṇuṟṟa maṟavarkku maruttuvam ceyyappeṟum maṉikaḷippēy mutaliya tīkkōḷ vantu tākkāvaṇṇam maṉiyiṟippil īra iliyum vēppiliyum cerukappaṭṭirukkum. perum puṇ uṟṟavar uḷḷam ulakiyal toṭarpil viṭutali peṟṟut tuṟakkaviṉpattil nāṭṭam koḷḷumāṟu kāñcip
pāṭṭukaḷ iṉiya muṟiyil pāṭappaṭum.
pōrttuṟiyilum piṟavaṟṟilum pukaḻmikku viḷaṅkiya kātaṟ kaṇavaṉ iṟantupaṭiṉ, avaṉ pirivāṟṟāta tamiḻmakaḷir avaṉuṭaṉē uyir tuṟappatuṇṭu. kaṇavaṉōṭē tāmum uyiriḻantāl maṟupiṟappil ak kaṇavaṉukkē maṉiviyāyp piṟattal kūṭum eṉṟu paṇṭit tamiḻmakaḷir niṉittoḻukiṉar. "immi māṟi maṟumiyāyiṉum, nīyākiyar emkaṇavaṉi; yāṉākiyar niṉ neñcu nērpavaḷē" eṉṟu makaḷir kūṟuvatākac cāṉṟōr pāṭuvatu itaṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟātal kāṇka. itaṉāl iṟanta kaṇavaṉi erikkum īmattil kātalaṉpuṭiya makaḷir uṭaṉ vīḻntu uyirkoṭukkum ceyali mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. uyirkoṭuppōruḷ mikka iḷami nalam paṭittavar tīppāytali virumpāta cāṉṟōrum uṇṭu. pūtap pāṇṭiyaṉ iṟantatu kaṇṭa avaṉ uyirkkātaliyāṉa peruṅkōp peṇṭu avaṉuṭiya īmattīyil tāṉum vīḻntu cātalukkut tuṇintāḷāka, āṅkirunta cāṉṟōr avaḷi vilakki aṟivuri pala kūṟuvarāyiṉar. avarkaḷi vekuṇṭu,
"	peruṅkāṭṭup paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭṭu īmam
	numakkuari tākuka tilla emakkuem
	peruntōḷ kaṇavaṉ māynteṉa arumpaṟa
	vaḷḷitaḻ aviḻnta tāmari
	naḷḷirum poykiyum tīyum ōraṟṟē."
eṉṟu peruṅkōp peṇṭu avalittu araṟṟiṉāḷ. uyir
koṭukkum āṟṟal illāmiyālō cāṉṟōr palar vilakkiyataṉālō kaṇavaṉi iḻantu uyir tāṅki yiruppōr kimmi nōṉpu mēṟkoṇṭu kaṇavaṉukku vaḻipāṭu ceytu oḻukuvar. itaṉit tumpiyūrc cokiṉaṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr,
"	aṭaṅkiya kaṟpiṉ āynutal maṭanti
	uyarnili yulakam avaṉpuka āra
	nīṟāṭu cuḷakiṉ cīṟiṭam nīkki
	aḻuta lāṉāk kaṇṇaḷ
	meḻukum āppikaṇ kaluḻnī rāṉē."
eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟār.
koḻunaṉi yiḻanta kulamakaḷir mēlē kuṟittavāṟu uṭaṉuyir viṭutal, kimmi mēṟkoḷal eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟaṉi mēṟkoḷvarēyaṉṟi maṟumaṇam ceytukoḷvatu ilar. paḻantamiḻ makaḷir maṟumaṇam ceytukoḷḷaṟkup paṇṭip poruḷilakkaṇa nūlār vitikkavum illi! maṟumaṇam ceytu koṇṭa varalāṟum tamiḻ nūlkaḷil itu kāṟum kāṇappaṭa
villi. immiyiṟ pirinta kaṇavaṉi maṟupiṟappil peṟalām eṉṟa karuttunilavum camutāyamātaliṉ, paḻantamiḻiriṭiyē maṟumaṇam nikaḻtaṟku vāyppu ētu?
"	alarnta virinī ruṭukki yulakam peṟiṉum
	aruneṟi yāyar makaḷirkku
	irumaṇam kūṭutal illiyal paṉṟē."
eṉṟa mullip pāṭṭu itaṟkut takka cāṉṟākum. kaḷavu neṟiyil kātaluṟṟuk kaṟpu neṟiyil maṇam ceytu koṇṭu kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum oruvarkkoruvar vāḻkkit tuṇiyāy oḻukum makkaḷātaliṉ maṟumaṇa nikaḻcci paḻantamiḻar vāḻvil iṭam peṟātāyiṟṟu.
maṇantu koṇṭa kaṇavaṉ pōr mutaliyavaṟṟāl iṟuti eytātu mutumi yeytuṅkāl, avaṉ uḷḷattē itukāṟum nukarntu pōnta nukarccikaḷil uvarppum veṟuppum piṟantu tuṟavu mēṟkoḷḷum vēṭkiyi yuṇṭupaṇṇum. ikkaruttiyum ceyaliyum kāṇum cāṉṟōr avarkku vīṭu pēṟṟukkuriya meyyuṇarviyum tavattiṉ ceyal muṟiyiyum aṟivuṟuttuvar. ataṉāl, avarkaḷ tamakkut tuṇiyākiya maṉiviyuṭaṉ tuṟavu mēṟkoṇṭu meyyuṇarntu, avā aṟuttu, yāṉ eṉatu eṉṟa cerukkaṟṟu, aṟivu nūlkaḷik kaṇṭu cemporuḷākiya kaṭavuḷiṉpa vāḻvukku uriyarāvar. itu paṟṟiyē tolkāppiyar, "aṟampuri cuṟṟamoṭu kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum, ciṟantatu payiṟṟal iṟantataṉ payaṉē" eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉar. iṟattal eṉṟatu vayatu mutirtal.
oruvaṉukku orutti yeṉṟa muṟiyil tamiḻ makkaḷ ulaku iṇippuṇṭiruntami paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ urikkum uṇmi
yākum. āyiṉum, annāḷil nāṭukaṭkiṭiyē aṭikkaṭi nikaḻnta pōrkaḷāl āṇ makkaḷ toki kuṟintatu; pōril īṭupaṭāmiyum pātukākkap paṭutalumākiya ceyalkaḷāl makaḷir toki mikuntatu. peṇṭirik kollalākātu eṉpatu paḻan
tamiḻar pōraṟam. itē nili mēlināṭukaḷilum nilaviyatāl, mikuntu niṉṟamakaḷiri maṉiviyākavum urimi makaḷirākavum koḷvatu avarkaḷ nāṭṭu vēntarkaṭkum, celvarkaṭkum ōrāṟṟāl kaṭamiyum marapum āyiṉa. antac cūḻniliyē tamiḻ nilattum iruntamiyiṉ, miki makaḷirip parattiyareṉak kuṟippārāyiṉar. param eṉṟa col mikutiyeṉṟu poruḷ taruvatu. immakaḷir ulakil vāḻtaṟkuriyarātalāl, avaratu vāḻvukku vēṇṭuvaṉa utavuvatu āṇmiyuṭiya āṭavarkku aṟamākum. avvāṟu utavumiṭattum utavi peṟumiṭattum irutiṟattārkkum toṭarpuṇṭāka, ataṉ vaḻiyākak tōṉṟiya makaḷir parattiyarāyp perukiṉar. oruvaṉ oruttiyi maṇantu vāḻntu varuṅkāl avaḷpāl makappēṟu illiyāyiṉ vēṟoruttiyi maṇantu koḷvatu avarkku amivatāyiṟṟu. iraṇṭām maṉiviyip "piṉ muṟiyākkiya perum poruḷ vatuvi" eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kuṟikkiṉṟār. iruvarkku mēlum maṉiviyar uḷarāyiṉ avarik kāmak kiḻattiyareṉa vakuttu muṟi ceytaṉar paṇṭit tamiḻar. nāḷum perukivanta miki makaḷir tām iṉitu vāḻtaṟkuriya poruḷ vēṇṭi āṭal pāṭal aḻaku eṉṟa tuṟikaḷil payiṟci peṟattoṭaṅkiṉar. avaruḷ poruḷ oṉṟē karuti ataṉit taruvōr yāvarāyiṉum variyaṟiyiṉṟit tamatu peṇmiyi viṟṟu oḻukiṉōr varivil makaḷirākak karutappaṭṭaṉar. eñciya parattiyar tammi nayanta āṭavaṉatu kātalaṉpu oṉṟē kuṟikkoṇṭu oḻukiṉamiyiṉ avarik kātaṟparattiyareṉap piṟkālattār peyar kuṟittaṉar. avaric cila āṭavar tām uṟiyum  ūrakattē irutti maṉiviyarip pōlap pēṇiṉar. avvāṟu pēṇappaṭṭōr iṟparattiyar eṉappaṭuvarāyiṉar. ēṉiyōr parattiyar cērikkaṇ uṟintamiyiṉ avaric cērip parattiyareṉavum parattiyar eṉavum urittaṉar. iṟparattiyar kāppiyak kālattil urimi makaḷireṉavum kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉar. ivvakiyil tamiḻ makaḷir paṇṭi nāḷil kulamakaḷireṉavum parattiyareṉavum irukūṟāy iruntami teḷiyappaṭum.
oruvaṉ maṉiviyāṉa kulamakaḷ vayiṟṟil piṟakkum makkaḷukkē avaṉatu uṭimiyil urimiyuṇṭu; parattiyarkkup piṟakkum makkaṭku avvurimi illi. itaṉi,
"	yāṇar ūraniṉ māṇiḻi makaḷiri
	emmaṉit tantunī taḻīiyiṉum avartam
	puṉmaṉat tuṇmiyō aritē; avarum
	pintoṭi makaḷiroṭu ciṟuvarp payantu
	naṉṟi cāṉṟa kaṟpōṭu
	empā ṭātal ataṉiṉum ilamē."1
eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuvatāl iṉitaṟiyalām.
maṇavāḻkki iyalil makaḷir ivvāṟu iru vēṟu vakiyil iyaṉṟu vāḻntaṉarāyiṉum, iyal ici kūttu mutaliyavaṟṟilum, aṟivu araciyal mutaliya tuṟikaḷilum otta urimi koṇṭiruntaṉar. ataṉāl araciyaṟ kuṟippukaḷum aṟivu nilipeṟṟa ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, tamiḻ makaḷiriṉ cālpiṉi oru vēṟṟumiyum tōṉṟātavāṟu kuṟittuḷḷaṉa. ōḷaviyār, kākkipāṭiṉiyār, nacceḷḷiyār, veṇṇik kuyattiyar/ nappacaliyār eṉap pala makaḷir nallicip pulamit tuṟiyil ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. oḷaviyārum, nacceḷḷiyārum aracarkaṭku araciyaṟ cuṟṟamāyiruntu aṟivuriyum aṟavuriyum nalkiyuḷḷaṉar.
makaḷirkkuriya ic ciṟappum urimiyum ki.pi. patiṉāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil iruntuḷḷaṉa. ak kālattē vaṭārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattup paṭivīṭṭut talinakarākak koṇṭatoru ciṟṟaracu ciṟantu niṉṟatu. avvaraciṉar tammic campuvarāyar eṉṟu kuṟippar; avarkaḷ cōḻap pēraraciṉ kīḻp perumi peṟṟavar. avaruḷ etirilic cōḻa campuvarāyaṉ eṉpavaṉ āṭcik kālattil vantavācikku aṇmiyil uḷḷa teḷḷāṟu eṉṟa ūril kaṇṭaṉ iḷaṅkaṇṇaṉ eṉpavaṉ vāḻntuvantāṉ. avaṉuṭiya maṉivi karuṇiyāṭṭi kaṟpāl tāṉ vāḻum ūravar taṉ kuṭiyip pukaḻumāṟu vāḻntu vantāḷ. iḷaṅkaṇṇaṉ oru nāḷ kaṭkuṭiyāl aṟivu mayaṅki ūravar palari vitāṉ eṉṟu kuṟṟaṅkaṇṭa "makācapiyār" ūrvāriyat talimipūṇṭa karuṇiyāṭṭikku muṟiyiṭa, avar "kaṇavaṉeṉṟum pārātu ēḻunāḷ tirumūla nāyaṉārkku viḷakkēṟṟumāṟu" paṇittu, ataṉi "avaṉ peṇṭāṭṭi eṉṟa paricu muṭṭāmē viḷakkēṟṟi"t tamatu kaṭamiyi āṟṟiṉār eṉpatu varalāṟu. itaṉāl, ūrvāriyaṅkaḷil talimi tāṅkum urimi makaḷirkkum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭiruntatu teḷivākiṟataṉṟō?
inniliyil mēlināṭṭu āṅkilēyarum piṟarum amerikka nāṭṭavarum inta nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ makaḷirkku araciyal urimi tantaṉar. iti nōkkiṉ paḻantamiḻ makaḷir ēṉi ulaka nāṭukaḷil vāḻnta makaḷiri nōkka - mika uyarnta niliyil vittut tamiḻarāl matikkap peṟṟami, itaṉāl iṉitu viḷaṅkum. iṉṉōraṉṉa ceytikaḷi maṟantamiyāl iṭikkālat tamiḻmakkaḷ makaḷiri aṭimiyākki iruḷil kiṭattit tammiyum kuruṭarākavum ūmiyākavum ceviṭarākavum keṭuttuk koṇṭaṉar. tamatu nāṭu, moḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu tuṟantu maṟantu keṭum oru camutāyam nāḷaṭivil keṭṭu aṭimic cēṟṟil aḻuntum eṉpatataṟku iṉṟiya tamiḻc camutāyam takkatoru eṭuttukkāṭṭāka viḷaṅkuvatit tamiḻakam naṉku uṇartal vēṇṭum. avvuṇarvu tamiḻ vāḻvu taḻippataṟku ākkamum araṇumām eṉpatu uṟuti.



10. ēṭṭil illāta ilakkiyam
- paḻamoḻikaḷ -
nam tamiḻakattil ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭāmal makkaḷ pēccu vaḻakkilēyē cila ilakkiyaṅkaḷ nilavukiṉṟaṉa. avi poruḷ ceṟinta ciṟu ciṟu coṟkaḷāl iyaṉṟuḷḷa paḻamoḻikaḷākum. avi ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭāviṭṭālum 'ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭuk kaṟṟavarkaḷāl pārāṭṭappaṭṭum, ilakkiya vakikaḷil oṉṟākak karutappaṭṭum, tamiḻp periyōrkaḷāl pōṟṟappeṟṟum varukiṉṟaṉa.' avikaḷip paḻamoḻi eṉpatōṭu mutumoḻi eṉṟum mūturi eṉṟum cāṉṟōr vaḻaṅkuvar.
'appaṉippōlap piḷḷi', eṉpatu nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻikaḷil oṉṟu. itaṉit tolkāppiyarē tam tolkāppiyattil 'tantiya roppar makkaḷ eṉpatāṉal antamil ciṟappiṉ makappaḻittu neruṅkalum," eṉṟu kaṟpiyal cūttiram āṟil kuṟittuḷḷār. "kumpiṭṭa teyvam koṭumi ceyyumā," eṉpatoru paḻamoḻi. itaṉi nallantuvaṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr "vaḻipaṭṭa teyvantāṉ valiyeṉac cārntārkaṭku aḻiyumnōy kimmika aṇaṅkākiyatu pōla," (kali) eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. "iṟikkiṟa ūṟṟut tāṉ curakkum," eṉpatu maṟṟoru mutumoḻi. iti nam tiruvaḷḷuvar "maṟippēṉmaṉ yāṉ iḵtōr nōyi iṟippavarkku ūṟṟunīr pōla mikum," eṉṟu tamatu tirukkuṟaḷil amittu vaḻaṅki yirukkiṉṟār. ivvaṇṇam tamiḻilakkiya ulakil pukaḻpeṟṟa cāṉṟōr aṉivarum eḻutā ilakkiyamākiya immutumoḻikaḷi uyarnta maṇikaḷākak karuti tāṅkaḷ eḻutiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷākiya aṇikalaṉkaḷil vittu iḻittu aḻaku ceytirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
ippaḻamoḻikaḷ nuṭpamum curukkamum eḷimiyumākiya iṉiya nalaṅkaḷi yuṭiyavākum. ivikaḷik kaṇṭa tolkāppiyar ivikaḷiyum ceyyuḷ vakiyil oṉṟāka eṭuttu nuṇmiyum curukkamum oḷiyuṭimiyum eṉṟivi viḷaṅkattōṉṟik kuṟitta poruḷi muṭittaṟku "varūum ētu nutaliya mutumoḻi eṉpa," eṉṟu ilakkaṇam kūṟiyuḷḷār. mēlum avar "mutumoḻi eṉpa," eṉṟataṉāl tolkāppiyar kālattukku muṉpiruntē cāṉṟōrkaḷ inta mutumoḻikaḷik kaṇṭu pārāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu aṟiyalām.
intap paḻamoḻikaḷ ellām uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷik kuṟippavi. ivaṟṟil poyyō, puṉinturiyō kiṭiyā. aḻakiya collāṭciyum piṟitumoḻital mutaliya aṇinalamum ivaṟṟiṉ taṉicciṟappākum. oruvar vēṟoruvarik kēṭṭu yātēṉum oṉṟik kaṭaṉāka vāṅkukiṟapōtu mukam oruvāṟu makiḻcciyākavum, atiyē koṭuttavar kēṭṭu vāṅkumpōtu tarupavar mukam oruvāṟu vēṟupaṭṭum iruppatum uṇmi nikaḻcciyām. ituvē "kēṭkiṟapōtu pacappu, koṭukkiṟapōtu kacappō," eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉi ippōtum kāṇkiṉṟōm. 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu viḷaṅkiyirunta pāli pāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō eṉpavar "uṇkaṭaṉ vaḻimoḻintu irakkuṅkāl mukaṉumtām, koṇṭatu koṭukkuṅkāl mukaṉum vēṟākutal, paṇṭum ivvulakat tiyaṟki aḵtu iṉṟum, putuvataṉṟē pulaṉuṭi māntīr," (kali) eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷār.
inta mutumoḻikaḷiṭattē uṇmiyum teḷivum iruppatu
koṇṭē cumār 1500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu muṉṟuriyariyaṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr oruvar 400 paḻamoḻikaḷi eṭuttu ovvoṉṟiṉ karuttiyum ovvoru veṇpāvil vittup paḻamoḻi nāṉūṟu eṉṟa peyarvittu oru nūl pāṭiyirukkiṉṟār. atu patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku eṉṟa nītinūl tokiyil oṉṟāy irukkiṟatu. mēlum, ippaḻamoḻikaḷ makkaḷuṭiya camutāyam, poruḷātāram, vāṇikam, kittoḻil, camayam, kalvi, araciyal mutaliya pala tuṟikaḷilum amintirukkiṉṟaṉa.  "kulaṅkeṭṭavarōṭu campantam ceytālum kuṇam  keṭṭa varōṭu campantam ceyyakkūṭātu." "cetta piṇattiyum cīriṭṭaḻu," mutaliyaṉa camutāyattil vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
"paṇamillātavaṉ piṇam" "paṇam pattum ceyyum," mutaliyaṉa poruḷ paṟṟi vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻi. "kappalēṟippaṭṭa kaṭaṉ koṭṭi nūṟṟut tīrumā?"  "tirikaṭa lōṭiyum tiraviyam tēṭu," mutaliyaṉa vāṇikattil vaḻaṅkum mutumoḻikaḷ. "vallavaṉukkup pullum āyutam," "uḻippāḷikku oruttar cōṟupōṭa vēṇṭiyatilli," eṉpavi mutaliyaṉa toḻil cārpāka nilavum mūturikaḷ. "vitip paḻutu mutaṟpaḻutu" "pārkkāta payirum kēṭkāta kaṭaṉum pāḻ," eṉpavi mutaliyaṉa uḻavupaṟṟi vaḻaṅkum ulakuri. "avaṉē avaṉē, eṉpativiṭac 'civaṉē civaṉē' eṉpatu mēl", "iravellām irāmāyaṇam kēṭṭuviṭṭu viṭintapiṉ cītikku irāmaṉ ciṟṟappaṉ eṉṟār pōla," eṉpaṉa camayam paṟṟi vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻi. "tōṭṭimutal toṇṭi māṉ vari," eṉpatu iṭikkālac cōḻapāṇṭiyar kāla araciyal paṟṟiyum  "ṭillikku rācāvāṉālum tallikkup piḷḷi," yeṉpatu vicayanakara vēntar kālattum mukammatiya vēntar kālattum nilaviya araciyal paṟṟiyum "nāṭṭukku nallaturi vantālum tōṭṭikkup pulcumi pōkātu," "paraṅkikkut teriyumā caṭaṅkum cāttiramum," eṉpavi mutaliyaṉa mēṉāṭṭavar kālattu araciyal paṟṟiyum uṇṭāki nilavum paḻamoḻikaḷ. araciyal cīrtiruttam vanta piṉ tērtalukku niṉṟu ōṭṭup peṟupavarkaḷip paṟṟi "ōṭṭukkup pōṉāyō ōṭuvāṅkap pōṉāyō!" eṉpatu mutaliya paḻamoḻikaḷ uṇṭākiyiruk
kiṉṟaṉa.
inta paḻamoḻikaḷi nōkkuvōmāyiṉ, paḻaṅkālak kuṟippukaḷil pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṭikkālap pāṇṭivēntaruḷ 12, 13-nūṟṟāṇṭil aracupurinta vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil tirukkoṭuṅkuṉṟattup pakutiyil vāṇātirāyar eṉpavar vantu kuṟumpuceytu ūrkaḷic cūṟiyāṭic ceṉṟaṉar. kōyil cottukkaḷip pātukāppataṟku akkālattil taṉittaṉik kaḻakaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟil kōyiṟ paṇiceyyum civappirāmaṇarkaḷum vayirākikaḷumiruppar. appōtu tirukkoṭuṅkuṉṟattuk kōyilil poṉṉappa aṇukka vaṉṟoṇṭa vayirāki eṉpavaṉ iruntu vantāṉ. cūṟiyāṭa vanta vāṇātirāyarkkum vaṉṟoṇṭa vayirākikkum caṇṭi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kōyil cottukkaḷik kākkum vakiyil vaṉṟoṇṭa vayirāki mikka valiyuṭaṉ pōrceytu vāṇātirāyarit tōṟṟōṭac ceytu tāṉum puṇpaṭṭu veṟṟi peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ūravar avaṉ puṇṇiyāṟṟi avaṉukkuc ciṟappuc ceytārkaḷ eṉṟu iṭiyāttūrk kōyil kalveṭṭeṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. (P.S.Ins.380) inta vayirākikaḷukku vīramuṣṭi eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkum. kōyil paṇiceypavaruḷ vayirākikaḷ kuttukkum caṇṭikkum muṉṉiṉṟu kātta ceytikaḷ pala kalveṭṭuk kaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvaralāṟṟi “uṇpāṉ tiṉpāṉ civappirāmaṇaṉ, kuttukku niṟpāṉ vīramuṣṭi,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻi kuṟittu niṟkiṟatu.
paṇṭināḷil vīrarkaḷ pōrceytu puṇpaṭṭu iṟantu pōvarāyiṉ, avarkaḷuṭiya peyariyum avar peṟṟa perumikaḷiyum oru kallil eḻuti, ati nīrāṭṭip palapēr cellakūṭiya vaḻikaḷilum āṟṟaṅkariyilum niṟutti vippatu vaḻakkam. antak kallik kal niṉṟa vīraṉuṭiya maṉivi nīrāṭṭi nellum pūvum corintu atit taṉ kaṇavaṉākavē eṇṇi vaḻipaṭuvāḷ. iti “olimeṉ kūntal oṇṇuta larivi, naṭukal toḻutuparavum,” eṉap puṟanāṉūṟu mutaliya caṅka nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itupaṟṟiyē “kalleṉṟālum kaṇavaṉ,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. mēlum, kollikaḷilum vayalkaḷilum katirmuṟṟi
varum payirkaḷukkuk kāvalākap pullum vikkōlum koṇṭu āḷpōla uruvam ceytu vayalvarappukaḷil niṟuttuvatu vaḻakkam. ataṉip pullāḷ eṉpar. ataṉiṭattil vīrac ceyki oṉṟum illiyāyiṉum tuliyil varupavarkkum iravil varupavarkkum āḷpōlat tōṉṟi ōraḷavu kāvalāvatupaṟṟi appullāḷi niṟuttivippar. ivvāṟē pōrkkuriya āṇmiyillāmal vaḻippaṟiceytu vāḻpavariyum pullāḷ eṉpatu vaḻakkam. itic cāṉṟōr “pullāḷ vaḻaṅkum puḷḷali vippiṉ pulam,” eṉṟu patiṟṟuppattil kuṟittiruppatik kāṇalām. taṉṉik koṇṭavaṉ nēriya vīramillāta pullāḷāyiṉum avaṉiyum kaṇavaṉākak koṇṭu oḻukavēṇṭiyatu peṇkaṭku vakutturikkum muṟi, immuṟi iraṇṭiyum cērttuk “kalleṉṟālum kaṇavaṉ, pulleṉṟālum puruṭaṉ,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻitōṉṟi vaḻaṅki varuvatāyiṟṟu.
“āvatum peṇṇālē aḻivatum peṇṇālē,” eṉpatu oru paḻamoḻi. maturiyil kaṇṇakiyiṉ kāṟcilampi viṟkac ceṉṟa avaḷ kaṇavaṉāṉa kōvalaṉik kaḷvaṉ eṉṟu kolittaṇṭaṉik koṭuttāṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ. ataṉiyaṟinta kaṇṇaki, pāṇṭivēntaṉ avikkaḷam ceṉṟu taṉ cilampik kāṭṭit taṉ kaṇavaṉ “kaḷvaṉallaṉ,” eṉpati nili nāṭṭiṉāḷ. kōvalaṉ kaḷvaṉeṉṟa paḻiyillātavaṉāṉāṉ. pāṇṭiyaṉ tāṉ ceytatu kuṟṟameṉṟu uṭaṉpaṭṭuk kīḻē vīḻntu uyirviṭṭāṉ. avaṉuṭiya maturi nakar tīppaṭṭaḻintatu. pāṇṭiyaṉ kulamum vēraṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. itaṉiyaṟinta vēntaṉāṉa cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟu kalkoṇṭu vantu kaṇṇakikku vaṭivam ceytu, kōyilamittu vaḻipāṭu ceytāṉ. itaṉi aṭip paṭiyākak koṇṭē “āvatum peṇṇālē aḻivatum peṇṇālē,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻi tōṉṟuva tāyiṟṟu. itaṉi vaṟpuṟuttuvataṟku vēṟoru ceytiyuṇṭu. koṅku nāṭṭil mēṟku malittoṭariṉ aṭippakutiyil naṉṉaṉ eṉṟoru talivaṉ vāḻntu vantāṉ.  poḷḷāccit tālukāviluḷḷa āṉimali eṉṉum ūrkkup paḻaṅkālattil naṉṉaṉūr eṉṟē peyar vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉa avvūrk kalveṭṭoṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. (Annual Report on South Indian epi. Madras No. 214 of 27-28) avvūril avaṉoru māmaratti vittu vaḷarttu vantāṉ atu kāyttup paḻukkat toṭaṅkiyatum ataṟkuk kāvalum ēṟpaṭuttiṉāṉ. anta maram iruntatu āṟṟaṅkari. orunāḷ oru celvaṉuṭiya makaḷ āṟṟil kuḷippataṟkuc ceṉṟāḷ. avaḷ kuḷittuk koṇṭirukkiyil naṉṉaṉuṭiya mā marattiliruntu naṉṟāyp paḻukkāta māṅkāy oṉṟu tāṉē utirntu taṇṇīril mitantukoṇṭu appeṇṇarukē vantatu.  ataṉi avaḷ eṭuttut tiṉṟāḷ. atik kaṇṭa kāvaṟkāraṉ ceṉṟu naṉṉaṉukkut terivikkak koṭiya vaṉāṉa naṉṉaṉ avaḷik koṉṟuviṭumāṟu kaṭṭaḷi yiṭṭāṉ. atiyaṟinta appeṇṇiṉ tanti avaḷ niṟiyaḷavu poṉṉum 81-yāṉikaḷum tantu avaḷik koliceyyātu viṭṭuviṭumāṟu vaṇaṅki vēṇṭiṉāṉ. naṉṉaṉ ciṟitum irakkamiṉṟik koṉṟuviṭṭāṉ. avaṉi aṉṟumutal cāṉṟōr “peṇkolipurinta naṉṉaṉ,” eṉṟu puṟam paḻittaṉar. avaṉ marapiṉariyum ikaḻntaṉar. anta naṉṉaṉ vaḻiyum piṉpu aḻintupōyiṟṟu. itu mutal “āvatum peṇṇālē aḻivatum peṇṇālē,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻi nili peṟṟu varuvatāyiṟṟu.
“āriyak kūttāṭiṉālum kāriyattilē kaṇ,” eṉpatoru mūturi. iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṟa pirivil nāṭakam eṉpatu kūttu. itu tamiḻkkūttu āriyakkūttu eṉa iru vakippaṭum. “āriyam tamiḻeṉum cīrnaṭam iraṇṭiṉum,” eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟuvar. āriyakkūttāṭu vōr āriyanāṭṭiliruntu vantu kaḻikkūttarpōl kayiṟṟiṉmēlniṉṟu pakkattē paṟi koṭṭa aḻakāka āṭuvar eṉak kuṟuntokiyil perumpatumaṉār pāṭiya pāṭṭil aṟikiṉṟōm. avarkaṭku vēntarum celvarum poṉṉum poruḷum paricilākat taruvar, avaṟṟip peṟṟuk
koṇṭu avarkaḷ tam āriyanāṭṭiṟkuc celvar. tamiḻkkūttarkku nilam viṭuvatu vēntarkkum celvarkkum marapu. piṉpu tamiḻkkūttarum āriyak kūttāṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. ataṟkuriya paricilākap poṉṉum poruḷum peṟuvatiyē viṭuttu nilam peṟuvatiyē tamiḻkkūttar virumpiṉar. tamiḻkkūttar ivvaṇṇamē paricil peṟṟu vāḻnta tiṟattik kumpakōṇattu nākanāta cāmikōyil kalveṭṭum, tiruvāvaṭutuṟik kōyil kalveṭṭum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. (Annual Report No. 90 of 32 and No. 120 of 1925) ataṉāl tamiḻkkūttar āriyakkūttāṭiṉālum kāriyamākiya kūttāṭṭuk kāṇipeṟum nāṭṭamē yuṭiyarāyiṉar. atu paṟṟiyē “āriyakkūttāṭiṉālum kāriyattil kaṇ,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻi vaḻaṅkivaruvatāyiṟṟu.
tiruñāṉacampantar cīkāḻit tirukkuḷakkariyil niṉṟu civañāṉap pāluṇṭatu mutal “aḻutapiḷḷi pāl kuṭikkum,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum, avar varalāṟu maṭṭil periyapurāṇattil tiruñāṉacampantar purāṇattilum tirunāvukkaracu purāṇattilum kalantu pātiyaḷavukkumēl iruppatāl “piḷḷi pāti periyavar pāti” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum, ciṟuvayatil kaṟkappaṭṭu, āyuḷ muḻutum payaṉpaṭṭuvarum ciṟappupaṟṟit tirukkuṟaḷiyum nālaṭiyāriyum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, “nālum iraṇṭum collukkuṟuti” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum, kaṟṟavar, kallātavar eṉṟa irutiṟattārukkum kamparāmāyaṇam ilakkiya iṉpam taruvatupaṟṟi “kalviyiṟ periyavar kampar,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum kamparāmāyaṇam tavira vēṟu nūlkaḷik kallātavarum kavipāṭum pāvalarāka viḷaṅkuvatupaṟṟik “kampar vīṭṭuk kaṟṟuttaṟiyum kavipāṭum,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum tōṉṟi nilavukiṉṟaṉa. kaṟṟuttaṟi = kaṟṟuvarum māṇavaṉ. itu piṉpu kaṭṭuttariyeṉa maruviviṭṭatu; ēṭukaḷilum kaṭṭut tariyeṉṟē kāṇap paṭuvatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl, “eḻutiṉavaṉ ēṭṭik keṭuttāṉ paṭittavaṉ pāṭṭik keṭuttāṉ,” eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyum meyyuriyāy nilavuvatāyiṟṟu.
ivvāṟu ilakkiyattuṟi paṟṟiyum, ici nāṭakam paṟṟiyum, “virutta vittiyam pāliya cōtiṭam,” eṉa maruttuvam, cōtiṭam mutaliya tuṟi paṟṟiyum paḻamoḻikaḷ pala nūṟṟukkaṇakkāka nāṭṭavar nāvilum nayampeṟṟut tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟikkaṇṭa mēṉāṭṭu ārāycci yāḷaruḷ oruvarāṉa Charles. E. Gover. M.R.A.S. eṉpavar “uyarnta karuttukkaḷ niṟinta paḻamoḻikaḷum nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷum potuvākat tamiḻ teluṅku moḻikaḷilum ciṟappākat tamiḻil mikuntum uḷḷaṉa. ittuṟiyil uḻittu ivikaḷi veḷiyiṭṭup potumakkaḷ kaṇṭu matikkavum tirāviṭar maṉappāṉmiyiṉ viḷivi aṟintukoḷḷavum ceyvatu paḻuttapulavarkaṭkum ciṟanta nākarikam paṭitta araciyalārukkum takutiyām” eṉṟa karuttamiya,
"There is a great mass of (thoughts) ready to hand in Tamil and Telegu folk-literature, especially in the former. To rise these to public estimation, to exhibit the true products of the Dravidian mind would be a task worthy of the ripest scholar and the most enlightened government (Introduction to folk songs of southern India printed in 1871) eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu aṟiñar karutat takkatu.



11. camaṇa muṉivar tamiḻt toṇṭu
nam tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa ilakkiya neṟiyiṉ vaḷarcciyiṉi varalāṟṟu muṟiyāl ārāykiṉṟavar, ivvaḷarcci kuṟittuc camaṇa muṉivar ceytuḷḷa toṇṭukaḷik kāṇātupōka muṭiyātu. tamiḻ vaḷarccikkeṉac ciṟanta toṇṭu purinta tamiḻ makkaḷiviṭac camaṇa muṉivarē tali ciṟantu niṟkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu palarum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avar ceyta toṇṭiṉi oru ciṟitu ārāyiṉ, avar toṇṭu muṟṟum tam camaya neṟiyiṉit tamiḻnāṭṭil nilipeṟuvittaṟkuc ceyta camayat toṇṭākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, ataṉip pulappaṭukkum kuṟippāl ikkaṭṭuri eḻutappeṟukiṉṟatu.
1. camaṇa muṉivar varalāṟu
camaṇa muṉivar eṉpavar ciṉa camaya voḻukkatti mēṟkoṇṭu, tuṟavikaḷāy, naṉñāṉam, naṟkāṭci, nalloḻukkam ciṟantu, viṉittoṭarpu keṭumāṟu muyalum cāṉṟōrāvar. itu paṇṭik kālattil vaṭanāṭṭil tōṉṟiya putuc camayam eṉṟu cilar kūṟuvar. camayak kaliyil valluṉarākiya irātā
kiruṭṭiṉaṉ eṉṉum pērāciriyar, "iḵtu intiya nāṭṭiṟkē urittākiya vitika camayattiliruntu putitāy kiḷitta camayam" eṉkiṉṟār. ic camayam vaṭanāṭṭil ciṉa camayam eṉṟum, teṉṉāṭṭil camaṇa camayam eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. ciṉaruḷ, illaṟattōr cirāvakar eṉṟum, tuṟavu pūṇṭu iṉpattu viruppum, tuṉpattu veṟuppum ilarāy iraṇṭaṉiyum oppa matittoḻukum cāṉṟōr camaṇar eṉavum kūṟappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ, putta camayatti nāṭeṅkum parapputaṟkuriya peru muyaṟciyiṉic ceyta pēraracap peruntakiyākiya acōka maṉṉarālum naṉku matikkap peṟṟavarkaḷ. ic camaṇarkaḷ nam tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukuntu tam camaya neṟiyiṉip parappiyataṉāl, ivarkaḷatu camayamākiya ciṉa camayam ivarkaḷ peyarāl camaṇa camayam eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
2. camaṇa muṉivar varaviṉkaṇ
tamiḻ irunta nili
tamiḻnāṭṭil, ātiyil tamiḻ makkaḷē vāḻntu vantaṉar; avarkaḷpāl taṉit tamiḻē nilaviyiruntatu. piṉṉar, vaṭanāṭṭu makkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ kuṭi pukuntaṉar. avar vaḻaṅkiya vaṭamoḻiyum avarōṭu pōntu tamiḻnāṭṭil iṭam peṟṟatu. vaṭavarum tamiḻarum vāḻkkiyil oruṅku iyintu ulavat toṭaṅkiyatum, vaṭamoḻiyum tamiḻiṭik kalantu vaḻaṅkap
peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. camaṇar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukunta kālattut tamiḻariṭi vaṭavar vaḻakkum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkum nilaviyiruntaṉa. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ maṉappāṉmiyum, vaṭavar vaḻakkiṉiyum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkiṉiyum mēṟkoṇṭoḻukutaṟkaṇ pērārvamum, pērūkkamum koṇṭu iruntatu.
nam nāṭṭiṟkup putiyarāy, putu vaḻakkum, putu voḻukkum, putu moḻiyum uṭiyarāyp pōnta mēṉāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl, nam nāṭṭavarkku avar moḻi vaḻakkiṉum, naṭiyoḻukkiṉum vēṭkiyum viḻivum peruka, ataṉāl nam nāṭṭu moḻi nili tāḻvuṟṟataṉṟō? aṅṅaṉamē, vaṭavar koṇarnta moḻi vaḻakkiṉiyum, naṭiyoḻukkiṉiyum kaṇṭa tamiḻar avaṟṟiṉpāl eḻunta vēṭkiyāl, tamiḻ vaḷarcciyiṟ karuttūṉṟātu tāḻntaṉar. vaṭavaratu vitika camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. vaṭavaratu vaṭamoḻi uyarntōrkkallatu uṇarttappaṭāteṉṉum vaṭavar vakutta muṟiyāl, ikkālatu āṅkilam pōlat tamiḻariṭam mutaliṭam peṟātāyiṟṟu. āṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyit tamiḻoṭu viravip pēcum neṟiyiṉi maṭṭum kiviṭātu pēṇivantaṉar. ataṉāl, vaṭacol viravāta taṉit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ arukiṉa; vaṭacol viraviya nūlkaḷ kūṭiya aḷaviṟ perukiṉa. ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum vaṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻoṭu naṭipeṟṟu iyalutaṟku iṭam vakuttu amippavāyiṉa. "vaṭacoṟkiḷavi vaṭaveḻuttorīi, eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collākummē" eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
ivvaṟu vaṭavar kūṭṭuṟavāl, tamiḻar tam tāymoḻi vaḷarccikkaṇ ūkkam kuṟintiruppatiyum, vaṭamoḻik kaṇ viruppum, vitika camayak katikaḷil vēṭkiyum mikkuṟṟiruppatiyum, camaṇa muṉivar teḷiya vuṇarntaṉar. tamiḻar, vitika camayappaṟṟu mikuntu atupaṟṟiya katikaḷik kēṭṭu, avaṟṟiṟku vēṟāka varum camaṇa camayak karuttukkaḷiyum katikaḷiyum mēṟkoḷḷutaṟkut tāḻttaṉar. ivvāṟu tampāl vēṟupaṭṭu niṟkum tamiḻar kūṭṭuṟavip peṟutaṟkuriya neṟiyil camaṇa muṉivar muyalvārāyiṉar. tamiḻarkku vaṭanūṟ katikaḷil viruppu mikkiruntamiyiṉ, tāmum vaṭanūṟkaḷiyum, vaṭanūl marapukaḷiyum tamiḻiṉkaṭ koṇarumukattāl tamiḻar ātaravip peṟalāyiṉar. inneṟikkup peruṅ karuviyāka viḷaṅkuvatu iḷañ ciṟārkkuk kalvi payiṟṟuvikkum perun toṇṭēyākum eṉṟu uṇarntaṉar. ataṉāl tām taṅkiyirunta paḷḷikaḷil kūṭaṅkaḷ amittu, avaṟṟuḷ iruntu ciṟuvarkkuk kalvik koṭi puriyalāyiṉar. annāḷ toṭṭē kalvi payilum iṭaṅkaḷ paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭuvavāyiṉa.
3. tamiḻit toṇṭu
camaṇa muṉivarkaḷiṉ toṇṭu tēvāra kālattukku muṉṉiyatum, piṉṉiyatum eṉa iruvakippaṭum, tēvāra kālattum, ataṟku muṉṉarum irunta camaṇa muṉivar peruṅ kalviyum, aṟam urikkum amitiyum nirampit tuṟavaṟamē cīritāka mēṟkoṇṭu iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ neṟiyiṉik kaṭintu vātupurinta civap periyōrkaḷāṉa ñāṉacampantar mutaliyōr ivarkaḷiṉ peruṅ kalviyiṉiyum, aṟavurikaḷiyum cuṭṭi irukkiṉṟaṉar. "amaṇarum kuṇṭarum cākkiyarum nayamuka uriyiṉar," "ōtiyum kēṭṭum uṇar viṉiyilātār uḷkalākātōr iyalpiṉiyuṭiyār" eṉṟum, ivarkaḷ, aṟam uritta perumiyiṉi, "pāṭuṭik kuṇṭar cākkiyar camaṇar payiltarum aṟavuri", "aṟaṅkāṭṭum amaṇarum cākkiyarum", "kaṭumali uṭaluṭi amaṇarum kañciyuṇ cākkiyarum, iṭum aṟavuri" eṉa ñāṉacampantap perumāṉ kūṟiyaruḷiyirukkiṉṟār. ivaṟṟāl tēvāra kālattiṟku muṉṉar irunta camaṇa muṉivar tam camaya aṟaṅkaḷi makkaḷukku uṇarttuvatē tamatu kaṭaṉākak koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷiya viḷaṅkukiṟatu. akkālattiṟku muṉṉar irunta tamiḻ nūlkaḷi nōkkiṉ, avaṟṟuṭ camaṇa muṉivar ceytaṉavākak kāṇappaṭuvaṉa yāvum aṟanūlkaḷēyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. nālaṭiyār, aṟaneṟiccāram, ciṟupañcamūlam mutaliya palavum aṟam kūṟuvaṉavēyākum.
akkālattē avarkaḷ, varalāṟu kūṟum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mikac ceytilar; oṉṟiraṇṭu ceytārāyiṉum avi tamiḻ vaḷaṅ keḻumiyavāyt tamiḻar maṉattik kavvuvaṉavāy illi. avarkaḷ ulaka vāḻkkiyilveṟuppum, tuṟavu neṟiyil viruppum
mikku iruntamiyiṉ tamiḻar vāḻkkiyil nilaviya akapporuḷ nalaṅkaḷum, puṟapporuḷ tuṟikaḷum neṭunāḷkāṟum avarkaḷ maṉattil iṭampeṟṟu, avarkaḷ aṟivi ēvalkoḷḷavilli. ataṉāl, avarkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ilakṇat tuṟiyiṟ pukuntu, ataṉ pakutikaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya ceyyuḷiyalil peruntoṇṭu purivārāyiṉar.
kaṟṟavar kallātavar eṉṟa irutiṟattārkkum aṟavuri vaḻaṅkumiṭattum uriyum pāṭṭumē mika vēṇṭaṟpālaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉativiṭap piṉṉiyatē kallātavariyum eḷitiṟ piṇikkum vāyppuṭiyatākum. ākavē, ataṟkuriya ceyyuḷiyalil perumpāṭupaṭṭup pala putuneṟikaḷiyum iyalkaḷiyum kāṇpārāyiṉar. yāpparuṅkala viruttiyuriyiṟ kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷa ceyyuḷiyal nūlkaḷuṭ pala, camaṇa muṉivarāṟ ceyyap peṟṟiruppataṟku ituvē kāraṇamākum. ici nūlum nāṭaka nūlum kāmam payakkum ciṟumiyuṭiyavi eṉpatu ammuṉivar kaḷiṉ karuttu, ataṉāl attuṟiyi nekiḻppatōṭu avvici nāṭakaṅkaḷil paṟṟuṇṭākāmiyum vaḷarttu vantaṉar. tamiḻar tāmum, tam tamiḻiyē maṟantu, vaṭanūṟ katikaḷilum, vaṭavar vaḻakka voḻukkaṅkaḷilum vēṭki mītūrntu niṉṟamiyiṉ, avi pēṇaṟpāṭiṉṟi iṟantaṉa.
tolkāppiya nūluṭ kāṇappaṭāta pāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷ ceyta ceyyuḷiyalkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu paḻiya tamiḻ neṟikaḷip putuneṟiyiṟ koṇartaṟku muyaṉṟu peruntoṇṭu purinta ip periyōrkaḷ ici, nāṭakaṅ kaḷilum tam karuttic celuttiyiruppiṉ, nam tamiḻ moḻiyil ettuṇiyō ici nūlkaḷum nāṭaka nūlkaḷum tōṉṟi yirukkumē! ici nūlkaḷuṭ cila ivarkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, ataṉi uṇmiyeṉat tuṇitaṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭittila.
toṭakkattil tam camaya neṟikaḷi makkaṭku urippatē poruḷākkik koṇṭu tamiḻnāṭeṅkum tirintu camaṇ paḷḷi kaḷiyum, camaṇ pāḻikaḷiyum ivarkaḷ niṟuvi vantaṉar. avi yāvum  aṟaniliyaṅkaḷāka iruntaṉavēyaṉṟi, aṟivuc cuṭar vaḻaṅkum tamiḻ nūl niliyaṅkaḷāka illāmiyāl, tēvārap periyōrkaḷ kālattil, ivaratu toṇṭu ivarkaṭkup pōtiya araṇ ceyyātāyiṟṟu. camayappōr nikaḻccikaḷil, ivarkaṭkup pōtiya paṭiyākak kūṭiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ illi. ataṉāl ivarkaḷ eḷitil vellap peṟṟaṉar. tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷāka, ivarkaḷ ceyta avinayam, intirakāḷīyam, parimāṇam mutaliyavi camaya nūlkaḷākavō camaya aṟanūlkaḷākavō payaṉpaṭavilli.
tēvāra kālattil ic camaṇa muṉivarkaḷuk kuṇṭākiya tōlvi ivarkaṭkup piṟkālattil aḻiyāp pukaḻip payappatāyiṟṟu. ivarkaṭkut tam toṇṭil irunta kuṟipāṭukaḷ naṉku pulaṉāyiṉa. tamiḻar kūṭṭattil nilaviya camaya nūlkaḷip payiṉṟu, avaṟṟippōla ilakkiyaṅ kaḷiyum, ciṟunūlkaḷiyum ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉar. iḷañ ciṟār mutal periyōr īṟākap palarkkum payaṉpaṭattakka tamiḻ nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟat talip paṭṭaṉar. tamiḻt toṇṭiṉit tam camayat toṇṭiṟkuk karuviyākak koḷḷum karuttiṉarākaliṉ, iḷiñarkkeṉa iyaṟṟiya ciṉṉūlkaḷilun tam camayak karuttukaḷip pukuttu miṭamaṟintu pukuttit tam karuttu muṟṟa niṉintaṉar.
ciṟuvar kaṟkum kaṇakkiyal vāypāṭukaḷil, tam camayak karutti nuḻittaṟku iṭamillāmiyāl, avaṟṟiṟkuk kāppuc ceyyuḷ yāttu, ataṉkaṇ camayavuṇarvip pukaṭṭiṉar. "vaṉmatimukkuṭiyāṉmalaraṭitoḻa, nellaṇi lakkam neñciṉil varumē" eṉpatu nellilakkavāypāṭu. "arukaṉi amalaṉi acalaṉiyaṭitoḻa, ciṟukuḻi muppatum cintiyil varumē" eṉpatu ciṟukuḻi vāypāṭu. ivvāṟu kalvi payiṉṟu varum ciṟuvarkaṭkut tamiḻ nūlkaḷik kaṟpittaṟkut tōṟṟu vāyāka nikaṇṭukaḷ ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉar. ivar kālattukku muṉpiruntavi nūṟpā vaṭivil perunūlkaḷāka iruntaṉa. avi, iciyoṭu viravi, putuppāviṉattiṟ ceyyappaṭiṉ kaṟpārkku ūkkam mikum eṉṟu karutiṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷ cūṭāmaṇi nikaṇṭu mutaliya nūlkaḷic ceytaṉar. itaṉkaṇṇum tam camayak karuttukkaḷi vēṇṭumiṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. kāppuc ceyyuḷ. "muṭivil iṉpattu mūvā mutalvaṉip pōṟṟi"c ceytatu ulakaṟintatu. pakavaṉ eṉṉum collukkup poruḷ kūṟumiṭattu, "pakavaṉē īcaṉmāyōṉ paṅkayaṉ ciṉaṉē puttaṉ" eṉat tokutturittaṉar. paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu tokutik kaṇ, "colvaki eḻutteṇṇellām tollināḷ elliyāka, nalvakiyākkum piṇṭi nāṉmukaṉ nāḷum tīmi, velviṉi toṭaṅkacceytu vīṭaruḷvōṉ tāḷ pōṟṟi" ceykiṉṟār. piṉṉar, nāṉilaṅkaḷ, nālvakiyaṅkaṅkaḷi virittuk kūṟaluṟuvār, iṭiyē, camaṇa camayak karuttākiya nāṟkati vakiyiṉi nuḻittu, "nāṟkati umpar makkaḷ colliya vilaṅkiṉoṭu narakar" eṉak kūṟiṉar. ivvāṟē civarkaḷ kūṟum, civaṉukkuriya eṇvakik kuṇaṅkaḷik kūṟumiṭattu, arukaṉukkuriya eṇvakik kuṇamum, iṟivaṉukku ākāveṉat tam camaya nūlkaḷ vilakkiya eṇvakik kuṟṟaṅkaḷiyum kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻnāṭṭil nilaviya tolkāppiyam mika virintu paranta nūlāyk kiṭantamiyiṉ, ataṉic ciṟuvarkaḷ eḷitil kaṟṟaṟkuriya niliyil ciṉṉūlum naṉṉūlum iyaṟṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ciṉṉūl ceyta kuṇavīrapaṇṭitar eṉṉum periyār vaṭanūṉ marapukaḷi oru ciṟitē taḻuvi, tam camayap poruṭkuriya vaṇakkaṅ kūṟic cellukiṉṟār. naṉṉūl ceyta pavaṇanti muṉivar curuṅkiya cilavāya coṟkaḷāl mika aḻakiyatāka itaṉic ceytuḷḷār. itaṉkaṇ, ivar tam camayak karuttik kāppuc ceyyuḷiṟ kāṭṭiyatōṭu nillātu, eḻuttiṉ ilakkaṇattilum pukuttiyiruk kiṉṟār. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār " eḻutteṉappaṭupa akara mutal ṉakara viṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa" eṉat toṭaṅkiṉarēyaṉṟi, eḻuttāvatu  iḵtu eṉa ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṟṟilar. avvilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappukunta pavaṇantiyār, "moḻimutaṟ kāraṇamām aṇuttiraḷ oli eḻuttu" eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟār. itaṉkaṇ "aṇuttiraḷ oli" eṉṟatu camaṇa camayak karuttu. itaṉiyē eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappuk kūṟumiṭattum, "niṟiyuyir muyaṟciyiṉ uḷvaḷi turappa eḻum aṇuttiraḷ" eṉac ciṟuvar maṉattil naṉkupatiyak kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. ittuṟik kaṇ āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār, "akatteḻu vaḷiyici ariltapa nāṭi, aḷapiṟkōṭal antaṇar maṟittē; aḵtivaṇnuvalātu eḻuntu puṟatticikkum, meyteri vaḷiyici aḷavu nuvaṉṟiciṉē" eṉṟē kūṟiṉar. tolkāppiyar kūṟiya taṉiyē avinayaṉārum, kuṇavīrapaṇṭitarum, puttamittiraṉārum kūṟiṉar. itaṉālum piṟkālac camaṇa muṉivarkaḷiṉ karuttut tam tamiḻt toṇṭu camayat toṇṭiṟkuk karuviyātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivākum. itaṉiyaṟintē ilakkaṇa viḷakkam iyaṟṟiya vittiyanāta tēcikar, "moḻik kāraṇamām nātakāriyavoli eḻuttu" eṉak kūṟiṉārātal vēṇṭum.
 iṉi, ivarkaḷ ceytaruḷiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ciṟṟilakkiyam, pērilakkiyam eṉa iruvakippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ. ciṟṟilakkiyaṅkaḷ palavum antāti, kalampakam mutalākap palatiṟappaṭum. avi yāvum camayap poruḷkaḷiyum, camaṇa camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷiyum pukaḻntu pāṭum ciṉṉūlkaḷākum. tirukkalampakam, tirunūṟṟantāti mutaliyavi camaṇ camayattiṉar peritum pōṟṟip paṭikkum tīviya centamiḻ nūlkaḷ. nariviruttam pōlum katic ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷum, mērumantara purāṇam, yacōtara kāviyam pōlvaṉavum camaṇa camayap poruḷkaḷiyum varalāṟukaḷiyum viḷakkum naṟṟamiḻ nūlkaḷ.
cintāmaṇi mutalākak kūṟappaṭum peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷum, cūḷāmaṇi mutalākak kūṟappaṭum ciṟukāppiyaṅkaḷum tamiḻ moḻikkup potuvāka vittuk kūṟappaṭuvaṉa. ciṟu kāppiyamintaṉuḷ, mēlē kāṭṭiya yacōtara kāviyamum oṉṟeṉak kūṟappaṭum.
ciṟukāppiyamintum tamiḻmoḻikkuriya potuk kāppiyaṅ kaḷākak karutappaṭum centamiḻ nalam ciṟantaviyāyiṉum, ivi yāvum camaṇa camayak karuttukkaḷiyē muṟṟum kūṟic celkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cūḷāmaṇi eṉpatu taliyāyatu. itaṉi iyaṟṟiya periyār tōlāmoḻittēvar. ivar peyarum, cīvaka cintāmaṇi ceyta tiruttaktēvar peyarum iyaṟpeyaralla eṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ. ivarkaḷippōlat tamiḻt toṇṭu purinta camaṇa muṉivar peyarellām vaṭamoḻip peyarākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. kuṇavīrapaṇṭitar, amutacākarar, maṇṭalapuruṭar, vāmatēvar eṉpaṉa palavum vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷē. ākavē, ivarkaḷ tamiḻ nūl payiṉṟu, tamiḻ vēṭki mikuntu, tamiḻarpōlat tāmum tamiḻarākavē iyintu niṟkum tamiḻ nala mikutiyāl, tammit tamiḻp peyarāl tiruttakkatēvar. tōlāmoḻittēvar eṉpavarākak kūṟiṉā rātal vēṇṭum. ivarkaṭkup palavāṇṭukaṭkup piṟpōnta kāṉsṭāṉṭiṉas pescci eṉpārum, tamiḻt toṇṭu puriyum cālpu eytiya vaḻit tammi vīramāmuṉivar eṉak kūṟikkoṇṭatu, eṉ karuttiṟku ātaravu tarukiṉṟatu. iv vīramāmuṉivarum tēmpāvaṇi eṉṉum kāviyamum, toṉṉūl eṉṉum ilakkaṇamum piṟavum ceytu tamiḻt toṇṭu purintirukkiṉṟār. cīvakacintāmaṇi yeṉpatu peruṅkāppiya mintaṉuḷ taliyāyatu. ēṉi nāṉkaṉuḷ cilappatikāratti iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eṉṉum civarum, maṇimēkaliyic cīttalic cāttaṉār eṉṉum tamiḻarum  eḻutiṉar. ēṉi iraṇṭum iṟantuviṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ maṇimēkali putta camayak karuttukkaḷit taṉpāṟ koṇṭatu. cilappatikārattum camaṇa camayak karuttukkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. āyiṉum camayat toṇṭiṟkum tamiḻt toṇṭiṟkum otta ilakkiyamāy viḷaṅkuvatu cīvakacintāmaṇiyē yākum.
cūḷāmaṇi, camaṇa camayattavarum ēṉic camayattavarum oppappōṟṟum takutipuṭiyatāy irukkiṟatu. itaṉi eḻutiya āciriyar, tamiḻnāṭṭil nilaviya puṟaccamayap poruḷkaḷiyum camayap poruḷiyum kaṟṟu, tam camaya nūlkaḷilum, puṟaccamaya nūlkaḷilum ottavāy uḷḷa katip pakutikaḷik kaṇṭu, avaṟṟit tamiḻ makkaṭku uṇartti avar tam ātaravu peṟuvati nāṭiya nallaṟiñarāvar. itaṉāl, ivar eḻutiya innūl iṉṟukāṟum naṉku pōṟṟap peṟṟu varukiṟatu. cīvakacintāmaṇiyum ippeṟṟit tāy civar oruvarāl urikāṇavum peṟṟirukkiṟatu.
tōlāmoḻit tēvar eḻutiya cūḷāmaṇiyiṉkaṇ, tiviṭṭaṉ, vicayaṉ eṉṟa iraṇṭu araca kumārarkaḷ varalāṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷ varalāṟu vitika camayanūṟ katikaḷuḷ varum varalāṟṟip peritum ottirukkiṟatu. iṭiyiṭiyē camaṇa camayak karuttu viravi niṟkiṟatu. eṉiṉum, cintāmaṇi pōla, vitika camayaneṟi niṟpavarum virumpattakka attuṇi uvamanalam uṭiyataṉṟu eṉṟē kūṟavēṇṭum.
kaṇṇaṉ kaṭalvaṇṇaṉ eṉṟum, palarāmaṉ vaḷi vaṇṇaṉ eṉṟum tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟum. "pālniṟa uruviṟ paṉik koṭi yōṉum, nīlniṟa uruviṉ nēmiyōṉum eṉṟiru perun teyvamum uṭaṉiṉṟāṅku" (puṟam 508) eṉa varum tiviṭṭaṉik kaṇṭa makaḷir, "kaṭaloḷi maṇivaṇṇaṉ kaṉaviṉil vantematu, uṭalakam veṟuvitāy uḷḷam vavviṉāṉ" (kumāra 32) eṉṟum, vicayaṉik kaṇṭavar "vārvaḷi vaṇṇaṉ em maṉattuḷāṉ piṟar, ērvaḷar neṭuṅ kaṇukku ilakkamallāṉāl" (33) eṉṟum kūṟiṉār eṉpar. itaṉāl niṟamottal kūṟiyavāṟām.
kaṇṇaṉ kañcaṉ viṭutta kuvalayā pīṭatti aṭarttāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉ accuvakaṇṭaṉ viṭutta ciṅkatti vāypiḷantāṉ. kaṇṇaṉ peruki vanta maḻiyit taṭuppatu kuṟittuk kuṉṟiṉi yeṭuttuk kuṭiyākap piṭittāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉum pulavar ōtiya varalāṟṟiṟkēṟpak koṭimā cili eṉṉum kuṉṟatti yeṭuttup pukaḻ niṟuviṉāṉ.
"	erimaṇi kaṭakakki iraṇṭu mūṉṟiyap
	perumaṇi nilampila mākak kīḻnuḻit
	tarumaṇi neṭuvari yataṉi ēntiṉāṉ
	tirumaṇi neṭumuṭic celvaṉ eṉpavē" 
eṉac cūḷāmaṇi kūṟukiṟatu.
kaṇṇaṉ piṉṉip pirāṭṭiyi maṇantāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉ cuyampirapi eṉṉum vittiyātara makaḷi maṇantāṉ. kaṇṇaṉ cicupālaṉi āḻi yeṟintu veṉṟāṉ; tiviṭṭaṉ ayakkirīvaṉi āḻi yeṟintu veṉṟāṉ. ivvāṟu tiviṭṭaṉ ceyal palavum kaṇṇaṉ ceyalkaḷōṭu ottiruttali, 
"	kōṭicili yeṭuttāṉ kōḷarimāvāy pōḻntāṉ
	āṭiyal yāṉi ayakkirīvaṉi aṭittāṉ
	vīṭiṉ maṇiyaruvi veṇmaliyum kippiṭittāṉ
	vāṭalil pūṅkaṇṇi māmēka vaṇṇaṉē."
"	valampurivāy vittāṉ vārcilikik koṇṭāṉ,
	calampuri caṇṭi talipaṇippuk kaṇṭāṉ,
	polampuri tāmariyāḷ poṉṉākam tōyntāṉ,
	kalampurivaṇṭaṭakkik kārmēka vaṇṇaṉē" 
eṉṟa pāṭṭukkaḷāl iṉitu kāṇalām.
iṉi, ipperiyōrkaḷuḷ tiruttakkatēvar ēṉi yōrik kāṭṭilum ulakiyalil mikka tērcci yuṭiyavarāka viḷaṅkiyirukkiṟār. tamiḻnāṭṭil tām parappak karutum camayap poruḷ kuṟitta inta nūl, tamiḻnāṭṭil paraviyirukkum camayap poruḷip puṟaṅkūṟuvatāyiruppiṉum, tam camayap poruḷiyē niralpaṭak kūṟuvatāyiruppiṉum, tamiḻarkaḷāl ātarikkappaṭuvatu illiyām eṉpati naṉku uṇarntiruntaṉar. am mati nuṭpattāl, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ payiṉṟirunta vitika camayak kati nikaḻccikaḷi innūṟkaṇ uvamamukattāṟ payila vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. ataṉāl innūl camayak koḷkiyāl vēṟupaṭṭa tamiḻar palarālum ātarikkappeṟṟa teṉpatu nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum kati kaḷāl nām aṟikiṉṟōm. ivviyalpiṉik kaṭippiṭiyāmi yāṟṟāṉ. tēmpāvaṇi, cīṟāppurāṇam mutaliya tamiḻariṭiyē naṉku vaḻaṅkātirukkiṉṟaṉa pōlum. ivviyalpiṉi mūṉṟu iyalkaḷāṟ kāṭṭi ikkaṭṭuriyi muṭikkiṉṟōm.
tēvar kālattē, tamiḻar tēvarkaḷ imiyā nāṭṭam uṭiyareṉṟum, avarkaṭkum acurarkaṭkum pakimiyiruntu vantateṉṟum, ataṉāl tēvarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi tuṉpuṟṟaṉareṉṟum, tēvarkaḷ tirumāliṉ utaviyāl kaṭal kaṭintu iṉiya amutam peṟṟu makiḻntaṉareṉṟum kēṭṭiruntaṉar. an nikaḻccikaḷit tiruttakkatēvar tām camayat toṇṭu kuṟittup pāṭiya cīvakacintāmaṇiyiṉkaṇ kuṟittirukkiṉṟār. kaṭṭiyaṅ kāraṉ caccantaṉik kollak karuti amiccarōṭu cūḻnta kālattil amiccaruḷ tarumatattaṉ eṉpāṉ kūṟṟil vittu, "iṟaṅku kaṇṇimiyār viḻittēyiruntu, aṟaṅkaḷ vavva ataṉpuṟam kākkalār" (20408) eṉṟum, tēvarkaḷ kaṭal kaṭintu amutu peṟṟa ceytiyi, patumukaṉ kōvintiyi maṇantu kūṭik kūṟum nayappuriyil, “tīmpāṟ kaṭalit tiripoṅkak kaṭintu tēvar, tāmpāṟ paṭutta amutō” eṉṟum, akkaṭal nākam kayiṟākak koṇṭu kaṭiyap paṭṭateṉpati cīvakaṉ kāntaruvatattiyi maṇantakāliyil, kaṭṭiyaṅkāraṉatu ēvalāl, accīvakaṉi etirtta vēntar avaṉāl alippuṇṭa ceytiyi viḷakkutaṟku uvamiyāka niṟutti, “aruvari nākañcuṟṟi āḻiyāṉ kaṭiyavaṉṟu, karuvari kuṭiyappaṭṭa kaṭaleṉak kalaṅki vēntar...uṭiyaluṟṟār” eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
iraṇṭāvatāka, murukaṉ tāmarippūvil tōṉṟiyavaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ malināṭṭukkuriya kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum, avaṉ ēntiya vēl valiciṟantatu eṉṟum, avaṉ āṭavariṟ pērāṟṟalilum  pēraḻakilum mēmpaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum tamiḻar camayavulaku kūṟip pārāṭṭi vantatu. ic ceyalkaḷit tiruttakka tēvar, “pūviṉuṭ piṟanta tōṉṟal puṇṇiyaṉ” eṉṟum, “tāmari amarar mēvarat tōṉṟiya aṇṇal” eṉṟum, “maṇaṅkoḷ pūmici mivari mintaṉō” eṉṟum, “amaruḷ āṉātu, okkiya murukaṉ eḵkam ōriraṇṭaṉiya kaṇṇāḷ” eṉṟum, “cempoṟ kaṭampaṉ cevvēlum” eṉṟum, “katampaṉē murukaṉ vēṟpōr” eṉṟum, “tiruvari mārpaṉ tiṇtēr maññiyē murukaṉtāṉ” eṉṟum,  “vaḷḷal māt taṭintāṉ aṉṉa māṇpiṉāṉ”  eṉṟum, “muntu cūrtaṭinta murukaṉ nampi eṉpār” eṉṟum, ivi pōlap piṟavāṟum kūṟic ciṟappittirukkiṉṟār. ivaṟṟi iṭaṅkūṟi viḷakkaluṟiṉ kaṭṭuri viriyum.
iṉi, civaperumāṉik kūṟumiṭattup “pōkamīṉṟa puṇṇiyaṉ” eṉṟum, “kāriyuṇṭik kaṭavuḷ”  eṉṟum, “aṟavāḻi yaṇṇal” eṉṟum, piṟavum kūṟiyum pārāṭṭuvar. paramaṉ kaṇṇutalāl kāmaṉik kāynta ceyti “kaṇṇutaṟ kaṭavuḷ cīṟak kaṉalerippaṭṭa kāmaṉ” eṉpataṉālum, purameritta varalāṟṟiṉi,  “kaṭimatil mūṉṟumeyta kaṭavuḷ” eṉpataṉālum, “kaṇiyeri yaḻalam peyta kaṇṇutal mūrtti yottāṉ” eṉpataṉālum, maṅki paṅkiṉaṉāy niṟkum niliyiṉi, “piṟiyaṇi yaṇṇal koṇṭa peṇṇorpāl koṇṭa tottār” eṉpataṉā lum piṟavāṟṟālum pukaḻntuḷḷār.
iṉi, tirumāl, paramaṉ purameritta kālattil ampāyiṉa ceytiyi, “pōkamīṉṟa puṇṇiya ṉeyta kaṇiyēpōl, mākamīṉṟa māmatiyaṉṉāṉ vaḷarkiṉṟāṉ” eṉṟum, avaṟkuk karuṭappuḷ ūrtiyeṉpati, “āḻiyāṉ ūrtippuḷ” eṉṟum, avaṉ cakkarappaṭiyuṭiyaṉ eṉpataṉi itaṉālum peṟa vittum, tirumāl kaṇṇaṉāyt tōṉṟiya kālattu, pāratap pōril caṅkōci eḻuppiya ceytiyi, “ceṅkaṇmāl teḻikkappaṭṭa valampurit turuvaṅ koṇṭa, caṅku” eṉṟum, kaṇṇaṉik kōṟaṟkup pōnta kuvalayāpīṭameṉṟa yāṉiyi avaṉ aṭartta nikaḻcciyiṉi, “mallal nīrmaṇi vaṇṇaṉip paṇṭornāḷ, kolla vōṭiya kuñcaram pōṉṟatac, celvaṉ pōṉṟaṉaṉ cīvakaṉ” eṉṟum, tirumāl irāmaṉākiya kālattu, avaṉ marāmara mēḻum eyta vil vaṉmiyi, “marāmara mēḻum eyta vāṅkuvil taṭakki valvil, irāmaṉi vallaṉ eṉpatu iciyalāṟ kaṇṭatilli” eṉa uvamittum makiḻkiṉṟār.
mēlum, īṇṭuk kūṟiya civaperumāṉum, tirumālum, tām paravum arukamūrttiyē eṉavum, avviruvariyum paravuvōr, arukaṉip paravutal vēṇṭum eṉpār,
“	malarēntu cēvaṭiya māleṉpa mālāl
	alarēnti añcali ceytañcap paṭuvār
	alarēnti añcali ceytañcap paṭumēl
	ilarē malareṉiṉum ēttāvā ṟeṉṉē
“	kaḷicēr kaṇiyuṭiya kāmaṉiyum kāynta
	aḷicēr aṟavāḻi aṇṇal ivaṉeṉpa,
	aḷicēr aṟavāḻi aṇṇal ivaṉēl,
	viḷiyāk kuṇattuti nām vittālāṟeṉṉē.”
eṉṟu moḻintirukkiṉṟār.
iṉi, ivar kāṭṭi makiḻvikkum tamiḻ nūṟ collāṭci kaḷum, tamiḻar vaḻakkavoḻukkaṅkaḷum īṇṭu eṭuttuk kāṭṭaluṟiṉ, iḵtōr perum parappiṉatākum. ivvāṟu, tam camayap poruḷi muṟṟa viritturikkum peruṅkāppiyam pāṭat toṭaṅkiyavar, ataṉit tamiḻ nāṭṭavar peritum virumpiyēṟṟup pēṇattakka viraku aṟintu amitta amiti, avaratu tamiḻ māṇpulamiyiṉi naṉku viḷakkukiṉṟatu. mēlum, ivar kāṭṭiya neṟiyiṉi ivarkkup piṉ vantōr kaṭippiṭittirupparēl, camayap pūcalkaḷum, piṟa tīmikaḷum uḷavātaṟ kētuvilliyākiyirukkum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, camaṇamuṉivar ciṉa camayattuc cāṉṟōr eṉpatum, avarkaḷ nam tamiḻnāṭṭil tam camaya neṟiyi uṇarttap pukuntapōtu, tamiḻar, ipperiyārkaṭkup pala āṇṭukaḷ muṉpē vanta vaṭavar kūṭṭuṟavāl tam tamiḻccamaya neṟi maṟantu, vaṭavar camayak koḷkiyilum, vaṭanūṉ marapu kaḷilum vēṭkiyuṟṟut tam tamiḻ vaḷarcciyik karutātu niṉṟaṉar eṉpatum, āyiṉum camaṇa muṉivarkaḷ tēvāra kālam variyil tamiḻt toṇṭu puritaṟkaṇ ūkkaṅ koḷḷātiruntaṉar eṉpatum, piṉpu akkālattu vātaṅkaḷil tōlviyuṟṟataṉ payaṉāl, tamiḻt toṇṭu puriyat toṭaṅki, ciṉṉūlkaḷum perunūlkaḷum ceytaṉar eṉpatum, ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiṉ cila putuneṟik karuttukkaḷip pukuttiyum, tām ceyta nūlkaḷuḷ vēṇṭumiṭaṅkaḷil tam camayak karuttukkaḷ nuḻittum camayat toṇṭu virittaṉar eṉpatum. atuvē nam tamiḻukkup perun toṇṭāyiṟṟeṉpatu piṟavumākum. inneṟiyik kippaṟṟiya piṉ vanta kiṟistavap periyārkaḷum tamiḻt toṇṭu purintaṉar. ikkālattu ēṉic camayattavarkaḷum immuṟiyi mēṟkoḷvār kaḷāyiṉ, taṅkaḷ camayaneṟi vaḷam peṟuvatōṭu nam tamiḻ moḻiyum ciṟappeytum eṉpatu uṟuti.
12. intiya nāṭṭil iculām 
ceyta iṉiya toṇṭu
nām vāḻum ivviṉiyanāṭu intiya nāṭu eṉpatil namakku iṉpam eḻukiṉṟatu. itaṉkaṇ vāḻum nām aṉivarum intiyar eṉṟu eṇṇumpōtu, nam uḷḷattil iṉpak kiḷarcci eḻumpiyarumpukiṟatu. namatu camayam, vāḻkki muṟi, oḻukkam eṉpaṉavaṟṟi niṉintu eḻutumpōtu nammiyaṟiyāta uvaki kaṉintu ūṟukiṟatu. ittuṟic ciṟappukku mutaliṭamāy, nām iruntu vāḻtaṟkut tāyakamāy viḷaṅkum nam intiya nāṭu oruvakiyil varuttattiyum nalkukiṉṟatu, nam kūṭṭattil camayak kāyccal, vakuppup pūcal uyarvu tāḻvu, tīṇṭutal tīṇṭāmi mutaliya tīkkōḷkaḷ niṉṟu nām peṟum vāḻkki yiṉpattic citikkiṉṟaṉa. piṟacamayaṅkaḷum, piṟacamayattavarkaḷum ceytuḷḷa naṉmikaḷik kāytal, uvattal iṉṟi naṭuniliyilniṉṟu kāṇavoṭṭātu, ivikaḷ maṟikkiṉṟaṉa; maṟittaṉavar eṉpatip paṟṟi iṉi niṉippatu payaṉil ceykiyākum, maṟikkātirukkumāṟu ceytalē ceyyaṟpālatākum.
nallataṉ nalaṉum tīyataṉ tīmiyum kāṇpatē nam aṟiviṉ kaṭamiyākum. uyariyapoṉ mikattāḻnta maṇṇoṭu kalantu kāṇappaṭukiṟataṉṟō? mikavuyarnta pālum orō vaḻit tīmipayakkak kāṇkiṉṟōmallamō? naṉporuḷ iḻintōriṭattum, iḻi poruḷ uyarntōriṭattum peṟukiṉṟōm; keṭuporuḷ naṇpar vāyilum, naṉ poruḷ pakivarpālum kiṭik kiṉṟaṉavē! ivaṟṟiyuṭkoṇṭu, nam tiruvaḷḷuvap peruntaki, “epporuḷ yār yār vāykkēṭpiṉum apporuḷ meyp poruḷ kāṇpatu aṟivu” eṉa moḻintatu poyyā moḻi yaṉṟō! ivvuṇmiyik kaṟpatum, piṟarkkuk kaṟpippatum ceyyum nām, piṟa camayattavar ceyta nalaṅkaḷiyaṟintu pārāṭṭuvatu namakkuk kaṭamiyākum. nammilum, “cīṟāppurāṇam” eṉṉum tīviya centamiḻk kāviyamum, “nākūrppurāṇam” eṉṉum nalañ ceṟi purāṇamum peṟṟa tamiḻaṟiñar, iculām camayattavar nam tamiḻkkāṟṟiya peruntoṇṭiṉi yaṟintu makiḻum peruntakikaḷātaliṉ, avarkaḷ, iccamayattavar nam intiya nāṭṭiṟkē ceytuḷḷa perum paṇikaḷit terintu pōṟṟum ciṟappuṭiyavar eṉak karutukiṉṟēṉ.
iculām camayattavar mucalmāṉkaḷ eṉavum, makamatiyar eṉavum nam nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkappeṟuvar. avarkaḷ mēṟ koṇṭirukkum iculām camayamum mukamatiyamatam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. iccamayam, puttacamayam, camaṇacamayam pōla, intu camayattiṉiṉṟu muḷitta kiḷiccamayamaṉṟu; itu, ciṟitu kālattukku muṉpu nam nāṭṭiṟkup pōntu paraviyirukkum kiṟittava camayattukkum muṉṉar nam nāṭṭiṟkup pōntu ciṟappeytiya camayamākum. nam nāṭṭuc camaya nūlkaḷuṭ kūṟappaṭum aṟuvakippaṭṭa akaccamayam, puṟaccamayam, akappuṟac camayam, puṟappuṟaccamayam eṉṟa camayat tokivaki virikaḷil, iḵtu iṭampeṟāmikkum ituvē kāraṇamākum. itaṟkum kiṟittava camayattukkum mutaṟcamayam yūtacamayam eṉṟu kūṟupa. avyūtacamayam, intiyanāṭṭiṟ piṟantu taḻitta camayamaṉṟu; ataṉāl, ataṉip paṟṟi nam nāṭṭuc camayak kaṇakkarkaḷ ārāycci ceytilar. piṟkālattup pōnta camayap pulavarkaḷum avvarāycci yiṉic ceyyātoḻintaṉar. ceytiruppā rāyiṉ, nam camūkattil pirippiṉṟi vāḻum ciṉar, puttar mutaliyavarkaḷippōla, iccamayattavarkaḷum nammoṭu pirippaṟak kalantukoṇṭiruppar. āṉāl, itaṟku vēṟu kāraṇam kūṟupavarum uṇṭu. āyiṉum aḵtu attuṇic ciṟappāka illi. ataṉiyum piṉṉark kūṟuvōm.
iculām eṉpatu oruteyvak kōṭpāṭṭik kaṭip piṭiyākavuṭiya peruñ camayamākum. puṟaccamayaṅkaḷiyum palateyvak kōṭpāṭukaḷiyum evvakiyilum ēlātatu; param poruḷ oṉṟit tavirap piṟa evaṟṟiyum vaṇaṅkutaṟku makkaḷip paṇiyāta paṇpu mēmpaṭṭatu; kiṟittava camayamum inniliyil itaṉōṭu otta iyalpuṭiyatu. ivi yiraṇṭaṟkum tāyc camayamāka irunta yūtacamayampōla, iviyuṇarttum iyavuḷ, ēṉit teyvaṅkaḷ uḷaveṉum eṇṇutaṟkum iṭantarāta uyarkaṭavuḷ eṉṉiṉ atu mikiyākātu. “pallōrum paramporuḷ oṉṟē uṇṭu; ataṉpāl parivukoṭu paracivāḻtal ellōrum ceykaṭaṉām” eṉṟē meymmineṟiyi ulakil vaṟpuṟuttiya peruneṟi itaṟkē uṇṭu. ettiṟattu makkaḷiyum uyarvu, tāḻvu iṉṟi oppa eṇṇiyoḻuka vēṇṭum eṉṟa uyarneṟiyil oppaṟṟa camayam iculām camayamē. kaṭavuḷiṉ aruṭkaṇṇiṉmuṉ makkaḷ vēṟṟumi yilli eṉṟa cen neṟiyi ulakil niṟuvi, yāvarum ataṉik kaṭippiṭittoḻukac ceyta mutaṟperumi itaṟkē uṇṭu.
iccamayam, paṇṭik kālattil, kiṟittu piṟanta cumār aṟunūṟu yāṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ, arapiya nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta makamatu eṉṟa perumakaṉārāl mutaṉmutalākak kāṇap peṟṟatu. akkālattu, avvarapiya nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭiyē poruntāc ceykikaḷum, tiruttāk koḷkikaḷum palavāy malintu, uruva vaḻipāṭṭiṉ peyarāl, an nāṭṭiṟkup pēriḻiviṉic ceytu vantaṉa. aruḷ vaḷḷalākiya ipperiyār avaṟṟik kaṇṭu uḷam poṟātu iccamayavuṇmiyiṉik kaṇṭu, am makkaḷi nalvaḻiyil teruṭṭit tiruppaveṇṇiṉār. avar muyaṟci toṭakkattil avaratu arumiyuyirkkē iṉṉali viḷivikkat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. avarkaḷ viḷitta tīmi yiṉip poṟāta avar, muṭivil, annāṭṭiṉ cīriya nakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya mekkā nakariviṭṭu nīṅki metiṉā eṉṉum nakarukkuc ceṉṟār. ataṟkuḷ annāṭṭavaruṭpalar, avar teruṭṭiyacamayak koḷkiyi mēṟkoṇṭu, avar kāṭṭiya neṟiyil oḻukattalippaṭṭaṉar. avarkaṭku makamatiyar eṉṟum, apputiya camayattiṟku makamatiya camayam eṉṟum peyarkaḷ muṟiyē vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa. mekkāvi nīṅki avar, metiṉāvukkuc ceṉṟa nikaḻcci, iṉṉum. ijirā eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. avar vaḻaṅkiya arumi moḻikaḷ, “kurāṉ” eṉṉum peyaruṭiya arumaṟiyil poṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. makamatiyarkaḷ, ammoḻikaḷ paramporuḷ, makamatu napiyiṉ vāyvaḻiyāka vaḻaṅkiyavi eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. intukkaḷ, taṅkaḷ nāṉmaṟikaḷi āṇṭavaṉē aruḷiṉāṉ eṉṟum, ataṉāl avaṉukku “vētaṅkaḷ moḻinta pirāṉ” eṉappeyarum kūṟupavarākaliṉ, avarkaṭku itaṉkaṇ putumiyoṉṟum tōṉṟātu. “aruḷneṟi” yākiya accamayamum, aruḷ neṟiyi uṇarttum iyalpu paṟṟi “iculām” camayam eṉṟē vaḻaṅkappeṟṟatu. ic camayam vaḷara varaḷa, arapiyar iṭiyil irunta tīneṟikaḷum tīyoḻukkaṅkaḷum nīṅkiṉa; avarkaḷ aṉivarum uṭaṉ piṟantāreṉavuṇarntu tammuḷ oṟṟumiyuṇṭu paṇṇik koṇṭaṉar. taṅkaṭkup perunalam ceyta iccamayavuṇarvi nilavulakil yāṇṭum parappa vēṇṭu meṉṟa vēṭkimikku, oru nūṟu yāṇṭukaḷukkuḷ, pāracīkam, turukkittāṉam, āpkaṉittāṉam eṉṟa iṉṉōraṉṉa nāṭṭu makkaḷ aṉivariyum makamati camayattavarākkik koṇṭaṉar. pāracīkarum, nam intiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil akkālattu malkiyirunta āriyarkaḷip pōla, tīyiṉik kaṭavuḷākak karuti vaḻipaṭum vaḻakkuṭiyarāyiruntavarkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭ cilar immakamatiya camayattimēṟ koḷḷātavarāy, intiyanāṭṭuṭ pukuntu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. makamatiyarum neṭuṅkālam variyil, ip pāracīkaruṭaṉum, tārttaruṭaṉum, ēṉiya vaṭapulatta varuṭaṉum pōr nikaḻttiya vaṇṇamēyiruntamiyiṉ, nam intiya nāṭṭil, ippoḻutikkuc cumār toḷḷāyiramāṇṭu kaṭkumuṉ varamāṭṭātu niṉṟaṉar; piṉṉarē, avarkaḷ nam intiya nāṭṭiṟku varalāyiṉar.
ivarkaḷ, innāṭṭiṟku varutaṟku muṉpē pāracīkaruṭ cilar pōntu kuṭiyēṟi viṭṭaṉar eṉṟu muṉpu kūṟiyuḷḷōm. avarkaṭku muṉ vēṟupala nāṭṭiṉarum vantu iṅkē kuṭipukuntiruntaṉar. avarkaṭkum, makamatiyarkaṭkum periyatoru vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. muṉṉē vantavarkaḷ tam camaya vuṇarvi nāṭṭiṟ parappavēṇṭu meṉṟa nōkkamuṭiya varallar; ivarkaṭkup piṉṉarp pōnta airōppiyarum avvāṟu vantavarallar; ivarkaḷ maṭṭil camayat toṇṭiṉik kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭu vantaṉar eṉpatu pala cāṉṟukaḷāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. mēlum ivarkaṭku muṉṉē pōntavar, nam nāṭṭiṟ kuṭi pukuntu vāḻntirunta ēṉi makkaḷōṭu kūṭi, orumiyuṟṟu, avarkaṭkuriya oḻukkam, vaḻipāṭu mutaliya neṟikaḷit tāmum ēṟṟu, intiyarōṭu intiyarāy iyintiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ yavaṉar, skitiyar, maṅkōliyar, pārttiyar mutaliya palarāvar. avarkaḷ nāṭcellac cella, intukkaḷuḷ intukkaḷāy, tam peyar, moḻi, camayam, oḻukkam, uṭi, maṉappāṉmi eṉṟa palavaṟṟiṉum oṉṟiviṭṭaṉar. kiṟittu piṟappataṟku iru nūṟuyāṇṭukaṭku muṉ innāṭṭiṟkup pōnta kirēkka tūtar oruvar, innāṭṭiṟ palaviṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟār. avar tanti (Dion) eṉṟum, avar peyar hīliyō ṭōras (Helio dorus) eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avar oru camayam vaṭanāṭṭiluḷḷa tirumāl kōyil oṉṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu, avari vaḻipaṭṭu, ammaṭṭil nillātu, oru “kōpuramum” eṭuppittār. piṉṉar, avar oru viṇava pākavatarāy, tirumāli vaḻipaṭṭu vantār. ivarip piṉpaṟṟip pōntavar palarum, ivvaṇṇamē vaḻipāṭu ceyvatu vēṟṟunāṭṭu makkaṭkum iyintatākum eṉṟu karutiṉar. icceyti pēs nakari (Bas nagar) luḷḷa tūṇiṟ cetukkap peṟṟirukkum kalveṭṭiṉāl namakkut terikiṉṟatu.
makamatiya camayattiṉar, nam nāṭṭiṟ pukuntapōtu, ikkaruttu nam nāṭṭil nilaviyiruntatāyiṉum, ivarkaḷ ataṉi mēṟ koḷḷavilli. iculām camayam kūṟum “allā” tirumāliṉ palvēṟu piṟappukkaḷuḷ oṉṟām eṉṟum, ic camaya vuṇmikaḷi yulakiṟku vaḻaṅkiyaruḷiya makamatu perumāṉit tirumālaṭiyāruḷ oruvareṉṟum kūṟi, intukkaḷ ivarkaḷit tammuḷ oruvarāka iyittuk koḷḷa muyaṉṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyuḷ vallunar, “allōpa niṣat” eṉṟoru nūli yeḻuti, makamatiya vēntaruṭ ciṟantavarāṉa akpar eṉṟa peyar koṇṭa aracar perumāṉi, “avatāra mūrtti” eṉṟum collat toṭaṅkiṉar. āyiṉum, makamatiyar, ivi, tam camayak karuttukaṭku muraṇātal kaṇṭu, ivaṟṟi aṟavē ēlārāyiṉar. avarkaḷ, intukkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭum “caṭaṅku” kaḷiyum uṭaṉ paṭṭilar. “pala teyva vaḻipāṭu ceypavar tūyarallar; tūyatallāta yātum kāpāvil  pukuvatum tītu” eṉṟu kurāṉ moḻivati avar viṭātu pōṟṟiṉar.
ivvāṟu, tiṇṇiya camayavuṇarviṉik koṇṭirunta taṉāl, ivarkaḷ, nam nāṭṭiṟ pukuntu kuṭiyēṟiya piṉpum, tamatu vaḻakkam, oḻukkam, peyar, moḻi eṉṟa palavakiyilum intuk kaḷiṟ pirintē yiruntu varalāyiṉar. orē nāṭṭiṟ piṟantu vaḷarntu vāḻntu vanta pōtum, avar innāṭṭavarōṭu kalappiṉṟiyē iruntaṉar. iv vēṟupāṭṭi nīkkik kalappiyuṇṭu paṇṇum tuṟiyil emmuyaṟciyum payaṉpaṭātu pōyiṟṟu. intiya nāṭṭiṟ pukuntu kuṭiyēṟi vāḻntuvarum intiya makamatiyar aṉivarum, tam camayavuṇarvu nekiḻātu pōṟṟi vantaṉar. ataṉāl, avar tam uḷḷam, avaratu camayam piṟanta nāṭṭiyē nāṭi niṟpatāyiṟṟu; nāṭōṟum, avarkaḷ ceyyum kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭilum, toḻukiyilum avartam mukam arapiyanāṭṭu mekkāviyē nōkki niṟkiṟatu; avartam maṉappāṉmi, oḻukkamuṟi, āṭci muṟi, camayak kalvi ākiya yāvum arēpiyā, ciriyā, pāracīkam, ekiptu eṉṟa innāṭṭu iyalpiṉavākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivar kaḷuṭiya camaya moḻi, camaya cakāptam, ilakkiyam, āciriyaṉmār, aṭikaṇmār, kōyilkaḷ eṉṟayāvum orē vakiyākavē irukkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivarkaḷ, innilavulakil eppuṟattil vāḻiṉum, iviyellām oru peṟṟiyavāy iruppatu kāṇappaṭumē tavira avvannāṭṭu ellikku akappaṭṭu, āṅkē aṭaṅki oṭuṅkiyiruttalik kāṇṭal aritu.
ivartam varukikkum, ivarkaṭku muṉpōnta ēṉiyōr varukikkum irunta vēṟupāṭukaḷuḷ mēlē kāṭṭiyatu tali ciṟantatākum. itaṉōṭu oppap piṟitoru vēṟupāṭum uṇṭu. nam nāṭṭiṟku vanta makamatiyarkaḷ, āyirattu irunūṟu mutal āyirattu aṟunūṟām āṇṭuvariyil, araciyal, camūkaviyal eṉṟa ellāvaṟṟilum, pōrttiṟamum nāṭōṭikaḷāy nili yiṉṟit tiriyum tiṟamumē nirampap koṇṭiruntaṉar. pācaṟiyiṟ ṟaṅkum aracar tāṉipōlavē, makamatiya araciyaṟ ṟalivarkaḷum āṅkāṅku iruntuvantaṉar. oru niliyāyt taṅki, nāṭu nakaraṅ
kaḷic cīrceytu, araciyaliṉ nilimiyi niṟuvi, nāṭukāttal, tīyari yoṟuttal ākiya ceyalkaḷōṭu camūkattuṟiyilum nalam pala ceyyat toṭaṅkiyatu piṟkālattiltāṉ nikaḻvatāyiṟṟu. kalappu maṇam, camayakkāyccaliṉmi, ottavurimi mutaliya pala naṉmikaḷum piṉṉarē uṇṭāyiṉa.
uyarntōrkaḷiṭattil orukuṟi uṇṭākumāyiṉ, atu virivil ulakaṟiyap parantuviṭum. avvaṇṇamē, oruvaṉ ceyta kuṟṟam ulakaṟiyat tōṉṟi nilipeṟu māyiṉ, avaṉ - pāl oruvakic ciṟappuṇṭeṉpatu tuṇintukoḷḷalām. ammuṟiyē, makamatiya maṉṉarkaḷ kālattuc ciṟuceyalkaḷ cila, intiya nāṭṭu varalāṟṟuc ciṟu nūlkaḷuṭ kāṇappaṭumāyiṉ, avaṟṟik koṇṭē, avarpāl amintirunta perumi iṉitu uṇarappaṭukiṉṟatu. taṉ uyirik kollutaṟkup pōnta araca puttiraṉ oruvaṉip poṟuttu vīraṉākkic ciṟappitta āṇṭaki pāpar vēntari aṟiyātār yāvar? intu camayattavariyum makamatiya camayattavariyum oppamatittu uyarnalam purinta aracar peruntakiyāṉa akpari aṟiyātavar aṟivu peṟātavar allarō? apperun takikaḷ āṟṟiyuḷḷa nalam palavum eḻutappukiṉ, ikkaṭṭuri varampiṉṟi virivatallatu avartam pukaḻnalam aṭaṅkiviṭum eṉa eṇṇuvatu ciṟumiyaṉṟō? innalamaṉittiṟkum aṭippaṭiyākavuḷḷatu iculām camayattiṉ iyalpē eṉpati maṟattal ākātu.
intiyanāṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyākiya nam tamiḻnāṭṭi āṇṭa muṭiyuṭivēntar mūvaruḷ cōḻarkaḷ kālattil, nilappaṭiyuṭaṉ kaṭaṟpaṭiyum iruntu nāṭukāttuvanta ceyti nām aṉivarum aṟintatākum. “kāntaḷūrccāli kalamaṟut taruḷiya” irācarācaṉ mutaliya cōḻamaṉṉarkaḷ, kaṭaṟpaṭi koṇṭu ceṉṟu pakippulaṅkaḷi veṉṟaṉar. avarkaṭkup piṉ vanta cōḻamaṉṉarkaḷ vīḻcciyaṭiyavē, kaṭaṟpaṭiyum keṭṭaḻintatu. piṟanāṭṭu makkaḷuṭiya kūṭṭuṟavum avarkaḷ kālattiṟkuppiṉ kiṭippatu aritākiyatu. āyiṉum ikkaṭaṟ paṭiyum, piṟanāṭṭu makkaḷuṭiya kūṭṭuṟavum im makamatiya maṉṉar perumakkaḷāl maṟuvalum uṇṭāyiṉa.
nam intiyanāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ, putta camaya kālattil ēṉi nāṭukaḷiṟ paravi avaṟṟōṭu cīriya toṭarpu peṟṟiruntaṉar. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil attoṭarpi nekiḻttu, tam nāṭṭellikkuḷḷēyē aṭaṅkiyamintu viṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, innāṭu taṉṉiliyil aṭaṅki, piṟavaṟṟōṭu toṭarpiṉṟit taṉippatāyiṟṟu. paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil, iculām camayatvariṉ peruntuṇiyāl, attoṭarpiṉi mīṭṭum nam nāṭu peṟṟatu; āyiṉum attoṭarpu paṇṭē yiruntatu pōṉṟilli. putta camaya, kālattil nam nāṭṭavar vēṟu nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟatupōla, ikkālattil celvatu oḻintaṉar. pala āyirakkaṇak kiṉarāṉa vēṟṟu nāṭṭumakkaḷ nam nāṭṭiṟku varattoṭaṅkiṉar. makamatiya camayattavar māttiram vēṟu nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟu varuvati mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar. ivar tam araciyal, patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil taḷarcci yaṭintu vīḻvuṟṟatu nām aṟivō maṉṟō? atuvariyil, vaṭamēṟkuk kaṇavāykaḷiṉ vaḻiyākap pokārā, cāmalkaṇṭu, pālku, kurācāṉ, kivāricum, pāracīkam eṉṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu makkaṭ kūṭṭam nam nāṭṭiṟku vantu koṇṭiruntatu. annāḷil āpkāṉittāṉamum ṭilli vēntariṉ āṭciyiltāṉ aṭaṅkiyiruntatu. patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil, jahāṅkīr eṉṉum maṉṉaratu āṭcikkālattil, nam intiya nāṭṭiliruntu pōlaṉ kaṇavāy vaḻiyāka āṇṭu tōṟum 14000  oṭṭakac cumiyaḷaviṉavāya poruḷ, kantakār, pāracīkam eṉṟa nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟaṉa. “mēlikkaṭaṟ kariyil irunta paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ (Sea ports) veḷināṭṭuk kaṭaṟ pōkku varavukku vāyil tiṟantiruntaṉa. kīḻikkaṭaṟkarip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷuḷ, macūlippaṭṭiṉam āyirattu aṟunūṟṟēḻām āṇṭuvarik kōl koṇṭāvi yāṇṭa cultāṉkaḷukku uriyatāyiruntatu. ataṟkup piṉpē atu mukalāya vēntarkaṭku uriyatāyiṟṟu. appaṭṭiṉattiliruntu, ilaṅki, cumittiri, cāvakam, cīyam, cīṉam eṉṟa nāṭukaṭku vāṇipam pēṇi, vaṅkaṅkaḷ pala ceṉṟa vaṇṇamiruntaṉa eṉa mukalāyarāṭci (Moghul administration) eṉṟa nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu.
vaṭa nāṭṭil mukalāya muṭimaṉṉarkaḷiṉ āṭci nilaviya kālam irunūṟuyāṇṭukaḷēyākum; āyiṉum, akkālattil nam nāṭṭiṟku uṇṭākiya nalaṅkaḷ palavākum. pala vēṟu vaki moḻi vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭiṟku araciyal moḻi yoṉṟu potuvāka vēṇṭappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. aḵtu annāṭṭu moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāyiṉ, piṟamoḻi pēcuvōr kūṭṭattu amiti uṇṭākātu eṉavuṇarntu, immaṉṉarkaḷ tamatu pāracīka moḻiyiṉiyē araciyal moḻiyāka vakuttaṉar. piṉṉar atu palarkkum tāy moḻiyām nilimiyiṉiyum aṭivatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl āṭcimuṟiyum nāṇayac celāvaṇiyum orumuṟiyāy naṭi peṟalāyiṉa. iṉṟum, kaccēri, jillā, tālukā, pirkkā, tāciltār, ucūr eṉa vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ palavum avartam āṭcineṟiyiṉ orumi muṟiyiṉi viḷakkum aṭiyāḷaṅ kaḷākum. nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭi rekṭā eṉappaṭum intuttāṉi moḻi vaḻaṅki vantatu.
ivarkaḷ kālattil, vintavarikku vaṭakkiluḷḷanāṭu, pala ciṟṟaracark kurimiyuṟṟu, pala ciṟu nāṭukaḷākap pirintu, eppoḻutum pōrum pūcalum nikaḻum amarkkaḷamā yiruntatu, avarkaḷiyaṭakki, araciṟkuriya amiti peṟuvittatōṭu. avar tam nāṭṭil amitiyum iṉpamum nilavuvittaṉar. tam camayatti nāṭṭil parapputal vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttu ivartam maṉattil mikkiruppiṉum, aracu kaṭṭil ēṟi makkaṭku nalam ceyvatum ciṟappuṭippaṇi yākaliṉ, ivarkaḷ attuṟiyiṉum cīriya aṟamē purintu pōntaṉar. annāṭṭil, perumpālum oru neṟippaṭṭa āṭcimuṟiyum, potu moḻiyum nilavacceyta ipperumakkaḷiṉ tiruppaṇiyāl, iṭantōṟum moḻi, uṭi mutaliyavaṟṟil vēṟupaṭṭuk kiṭanta intiya makkaḷ, tava camūka voḻukkam, uṭi mutaliya avaṟṟuḷḷum orumi neṟiyi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. nāṭṭil amitiyum iṉpamum nilavavē, makkaḷ tam tāymoḻiyilum mikka payiṟci koṇṭu ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ceytaṉar. iṉpamum, amitiyum urimiyum nilavum nāṭṭiltāṉ uyarnta ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, ariya karuttuk kaḷum piṟakkum eṉa aṟiñar kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷ kālattil ivi mikap peruki nāṭṭiṟkuc ciṟappu nalkiṉa. tāṉ aṭinta mutumiyiṉit tāṉpeṟṟa makaṉukku nalki, avaṉatu kaṭṭiḷamiyit tāṉ peṟṟu makiḻnta aracarkaḷ intukkaḷil iruntaṉar. tam varalāṟṟiṉit tāmē varinta aracar pāpar perumāṉ. nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya nāṇayaṅkaḷ, cakaṭakkāl pōl makkaḷ kiyakattu nillātu  peyarum nīrmiyavāyiṉum, avarkaḷāl kātalittup pōṟṟappaṭum iyalpaṟintu avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyāka aṟattip parappi niṟuviya aṟivunalam ivarkaḷpāl iyalpākavē amintu kiṭantatu. ‘nallatu ceyvatu āṇṭavaṉukku aḷaviṟanta uvappit taruvatu; naṭunili tiṟampātu naṉṉeṟiyiṟ ceṉṟōr keṭuvatilar; keṭṭār eṉa yām kēṭṭatumilli” eṉap poṟitta nāṇayaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷ kālattil aṟaneṟi vaḻaṅki makkaḷi aṟivuṭiyarākkiṉa. “ayiṉ akpari” eṉṟa araciyal nūl akpar eṉṉum aracar peruntaki kālattil piṟanta teṉpatu ciṟumakārum aṟinta ceyti yākum.
pōrttuṟiyil palatiṟamāṉapayiṟcikaḷum, nākarikattil ciṟappum, kalviyil perumāṇpum nalkiya ivar tam kālattil, kittoḻilum, ciṟpamum piṟavum ulaku pukaḻ uyarvu peṟṟaṉa. intiya cīṉakkivēli yamippukkalanta intucāracaṉ kit tiṟaṅkaḷ nāṭṭil vaḷam peṟṟaṉa. putiya putiya ciṟpat toḻilkaḷ aracar ātaravu peṟṟaṉa. “puki mukantaṉṉa mācil tūvuṭi” eṉavum, “nōkku nuḻikallā nuṇmiyapūk kaṉintu, aravuriyaṉṉa aṟuvi” eṉavum paṇṭit tamiḻc cāṉṟōr ceytutaviyatu pōṉṟa nuṇ viṉiyuṭikaḷum, melliya paṭṭāṭikaḷum ivarkaḷ kālattē vaḷampeṟṟu ulaku pukaḻum oṇporuḷāyc ciṟantaṉa. kampaḷikaḷum, piṉṉalkaḷum, kiṉkāp (Kin khab)  eṉpaṉavum, “nurikavarntaṉṉa meṉpūṅkampala”ṅkaḷum pukaḻvōr pulavariyiṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa.
inta iculām camayap perumakkaḷāl nam nāṭṭil viḷinta nalaṅkaḷ palavum eḷitil kaṇṭukōṭaṟkeṉa oruvāṟu tiraṭṭit tantu ikkaṭṭuriyiṉi muṭittuk koḷḷukiṉṟōm.
1.	cōḻa vēntarkaḷiṉ vīḻccikkup piṉ keṭṭaḻintirunta kaṭaṟpaṭiyum piṟanāṭṭuk kūṭṭuṟavum paṇṭēpōl  mīṭṭum tōṟṟam peṟṟuc ciṟantaṉa.
2.	vintiyavarikku vaṭakkil uḷḷa nāṭṭil, ivartam viṉit tiṭpam, āṭci neṟi, naṉmaṉap paṇpu eṉpavaṟṟāl amitiyum iṉpamum nilavuvavāyiṉa.
3.	tām veṉṟu aṭippaṭutti nāṭeṅkum oru taṉmiyum, oruneṟiyum poruntiya āṭcimuṟi ivarkaḷ kālattē nāṭṭil naṭipeṟuvatāyiṟṟu.
4.	uyar vakuppiṉaruḷ camaya vēṟṟumi yiruntateṉiṉum, camūkavoḻukkam, uṭimutaliyavaṟṟil makkaṭkiṭiyē oṟṟumi yeyti yiruntatu. camaya vēṟṟumiyum kāyccalum pūcalumāy māṟiyatu pēraracap peruntakikaḷ, kālattil illi.
5.	intuttāṉiyeṉṟum, rekṭā eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum moḻi potumoḻiyāyiṟṟu; araciyaṟ potumoḻi pāracika moḻi.
6.	avvannāṭṭut tāymoḻikaḷ, amitiyum iṉpamum nilaviyataṉ payaṉāy vaḷampeṟṟaṉa. varalāṟu kūṟum nūlkaḷum, araciyal nūlkaḷum nalliṭam peṟṟut tikaḻntaṉa.
7.	pala teyvak koḷkiyum vaḻipāṭum kūṟum camayaneṟi kaṭkiṭiyē oru teyvak koḷkiyum vaḻipāṭum aṟiñar maṉattuḷ vērūṉṟi viḷaṅkiṉa. ataṉāl, cila camayaṅkaḷ cīriya tiruttam aṭintaṉa.
8.	pōrmuṟiyilum, nākarikavaḷarcciyilum palvaki nalaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷāl nam nāṭu peṟṟuc ciṟappaṭintatu.
9.	intucāracaṉ kittiṟaṅkaḷum, ciṟpat tiṟaṅkaḷum peru makkaḷiṉ ātaravu peṟṟaṉa. poṉṉum maṇiyum ceṟinta nuṇmāṇkiviṉikaḷ, ivarkaḷ kālattiṟ peṟṟatu pōl piṟa ekkālattum mēmpāṭu peṟavilli yeṉiṉ, aḵtu iṟappap pukaḻum pukaḻcciyākātu.
10.	paṭṭiṉum paruttiyiṉum āya uṭivakikaḷ neytalum, kampala necavum piṟavum inta islām camayattavar nam intiya nāṭṭil ceyta iṉiya toṇṭiṉāl mēṉmiyaṭintaṉa.
ikkūṟiya nalaṅkaḷ aṉittiṟkum uriyarāy viḷaṅkum ipperumakkaḷiṉ naṟpukaḻ innilavulakil eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavuka eṉa vāḻtti ikkaṭṭuriyi ivvaḷavil niṟuttukiṉṟōm.

13. tamiḻakam - vaṭavelli
'nam tamiḻakattukkut 'tamiḻ ulakam' eṉṟa peyar tolkāp piyattukkup paṉampāraṉār eṉpavar eḻutiya pāyirattil kāṇap paṭukiṟatu. avar, "vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari āyiṭit tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakam" eṉṟu urikkiṉṟār. ulakam eṉpatu potuvāka nām vāḻum nilavulakam muḻutum kuṟikkum;  āyiṉum "mivari yulakam," "kāṭuṟi yulakam," "perumaṇal ulakam" eṉṟāṟ pōla, atu nilavulakattiṉ palvēṟu pakutikaḷiyum kuṟippa tuṇṭu. atuvē nāṭu eṉṟa peyarāl peritum vaḻaṅkum. ivvakiyil tamiḻ ulakam eṉpatu tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. tamiḻ nāṭṭit tamiḻ ulakam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyatōṭu tamiḻakam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvatuṇṭu. itaṉāl, tamiḻar vāḻum teṉṉāṭu, tamiḻakam, tamiḻ ulakam, tamiḻ nāṭu eṉṟa peyarkaḷiyuṭiyatu eṉpatu teḷivām.
tamiḻakattukku vaṭavelli vēṅkaṭamum, teṉ elli kumariyum kiḻakkum mēṟkum kaṭalum ellikaḷāka uḷḷaṉa. elli paṟṟiya pēccu nikaḻum pōtellām aṟiñarkaḷ teṉ kumariyip paṟṟiyē peritum eṭuttup pēcuvar. vaṭavēṅkaṭam tamiḻar vāḻiṭamāyiṉamiyiṉ, ataṉip paṟṟi oṉṟum kūṟuva tilli. orukāl ētēṉum pēca vēṇṭiṉ, vēṅkaṭamaliyil vīṟṟirukkum teyvam tirumālā, murukaṉā eṉṟu ārāyntu pēcuvatu vaḻakkam. malikaḷ murukaṉ uṟiyum iṭamākaliṉ, vēṅkaṭat teyvam murukaṉē; atuvē piṟkālattē tirumālāki viṭṭatu eṉpar cilar; iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kālattēyē vēṅkaṭat teyvam tirumāl eṉpatu teḷivāka iruttalāl vēṅkaṭam tirumālukkē uriyatu eṉpar cilar. vēṅkaṭam tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭavelli eṉpatil yārum aiyuṟṟatu milli; ārāycci mēṟkoṇṭatu milli.
tamiḻakattil tamiḻaratu paṇpaṭṭa vāḻvu tōṉṟiya aṉṟu mutal, mēṉāṭṭavar āṭci tōṉṟi maṟinta nāḷ vari, nam tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭavelliyip paṟṟit tamiḻar eṭuttup pēcavēṇṭiya iṭam illāmal iruntatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyil vāḻnta āntirarkaḷum teṉ pakutiyil vāḻnta tamiḻarkaḷum ceṉṉi mānila āṭci muṟiyil orucēra vāḻntu vantatē itaṟkuk kāraṇamākum. ippōtu vēṟṟu nāṭṭavaratu āṭci nīṅki viṭṭatu. nam nāṭṭu nammavarē āṭci ceyyum nalam peṟṟu viṭṭaṉar. inta āṭci iṉitē iyaṅki makkaṭku vēṇṭum naṉmikaḷic ceytaṟku moḻi vaḻiyāka nāṭukaḷ tammuṭ pirintu iyala vēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷki uṇarappaṭṭatu. makkaḷiṉ vāḻvu karuti makkaḷē muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattum makkaḷāṭci ammakkaḷ aṟiyāta moḻiyil naṭappatu eṉpatu pōliyaracām eṉṟu araciyal aṟivu vaṟpuṟuttuva tāyiṟṟu. moḻivaḻiyākap pirintu iyaṅkum iyakkamē makkaḷiṭiyē orumik karuttu vaḷarvataṟku ēṟṟatām eṉṟa karutti muṉkūṭṭi yuṇarnta kāṅkiracu iyakkat talivarkaḷ, pallāṇṭukaṭku muṉpē moḻivaḻiyāka āntira mākāṇak kāṅkiracu, tamiḻnāṭu kāṅkiracu, kēraḷa mākāṇak kāṅkiracu eṉap pirittut tamakkuriya paṇiyi āṟṟi veṟṟiyum vīṟum eytak kaṇṭaṉar. ituvum, nāṭukaḷ moḻivaḻiyākap pirintuniṉṟu araciyali naṭattuvatu nallatu eṉṟa karutti valuppaṭuttuvatāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟṟāl āntirariṭiyē āntira nāṭṭit taṉiyākap pirikka vēṇṭumeṉṟa kiḷarcci tōṉṟiṟṟu. evvakik kiḷarccikkum vaḻakkam pōl tōṉṟum taṭikaḷ itaṟkum eḻuntaṉa. muṭivil āntiramānāṭu piriya vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmi piṟantē pōyiṟṟu.
āntiranāṭu pirivateṉṟāl ataṟkum tamiḻ nāṭṭukkum iṭiyil niṟuva vēṇṭiya elliyip paṟṟiya ceyti muṉṉaṇiyil vantu niṉṟatu. āntira mākāṇa araciyal talivarkaḷum avarvaḻi niṉṟa toṇṭarkaḷum avarnāṭṭut teṉṉellayip paṟṟip pēcit tirivārāyiṉar. avarkaḷ pēccil vīṇvampu viḷiyat takka karuttukkaḷum iṭiyiṭiyē piṟanta vaṇṇam iruntaṉa. tamiḻakattiṉ muṭimaṇiyāy mēṉāṭṭavar kālattil putitu tōṉṟit tikaḻum ceṉṉimānakar āntirarukkuriyatā, tamiḻaruk kuriyatā eṉṟa viṉā voṉṟi eḻuppi makkaḷiṭiyē maṉak kuḻappattic cila aṟiñarkaḷ uṇṭākkat talippaṭṭārkaḷ. avaruṭ cilar, nāṭṭu varalāṟṟiṉ peyari iḻuttuk koṇarntu tamatu pēccukku ātaravu kāṭṭuvārāyiṉar. neṭunāḷkāṟum maṭiyuṟṟirunta tamiḻ nāṭṭut talivarkaḷ. atu kēṭṭatum maṭimi nīṅki viḻitteḻuntu, "ceṉṉimānakar āntirarkaṭkuriya teṉṟu colluvatu poruntātu; atu tamiḻarukkē uriyatu" eṉṟu collat toṭaṅkiṉar. ataṉ piṉ āntiraratu ceṉṉi mānakar paṟṟiya āravāram aṭaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu.
ulaka nāṭukaḷil vāḻum makkaḷuḷ tamatu nāṭṭut tamatu varalāṟu aṟiyāta maṭavōr kūṭṭam oṉṟu inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum uḷatu eṉṟāl, atu tamiḻar iṉattiṉ vēṟāka irukka muṭiyātu. "kaltōṉṟi maṇ tōṉṟāk kālattē vāḷoṭu muṉtōṉṟi mūtta kuṭi" tamiḻar kuṭi eṉṟu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ pāritturikkiṉum ceviyiṟ koḷḷār; ēṉi nāṭṭavaratu varalāṟṟi ēṭu ēṭāyp puraṭṭip paṭittu neṭṭuruc ceyvar; tamatu nāṭṭu varalāṟṟik kaṇṇeṭuttum pārār; mēṉāṭṭu varalāṟṟup perumiyiyum ēṉik kīḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟuk kiḷarcciyiyum tēṉum pālum pōṉṟa ceñcoṟ kaḷāl ciṟakkap pēcip piṟaṅkuvar; tamatu nāṭṭu varalāṟṟi eṭutturikka vāyiṉṟi ūmiyarāy niṟpar. ivarkaḷatu ic ceyalik kaṇṭē tēciya kavi pāratiyār, "pāmararāy vilaṅku kaḷāy," "ūmiyarāyc ceviṭarkaḷāyk kuruṭarkaḷāy," "nāmamatu tamiḻar eṉak koṇṭu iṅku vāḻntiṭutal naṉṟō?" eṉṟu vayiṟerintu pāṭiṉār. iṭik kālattiltāṉ itu nilimi eṉṟāl, iṉṟum atuvē nīṭittu niṟkiṟatu. nāṭṭu varalāṟṟaṟivu nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ araciyal vāḻvi ettuṇi uyarnta nilikkuk koṇarntirukkiṟatu eṉpati mēlināṭṭu varalāṟṟaṟivu nirampap peṟṟa tamiḻar uṇarāmal illi. eṉṟālum, piṟarkku añci añci yoṭuṅkum kīḻmip paṇpu eṅṅaṉamō ivarkaḷip paṟṟikkoṇṭuḷatu.
teṉṉintiya varalāṟu kāṇpōrkku mikap paḻamiyāṉa varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟākat tolkāppiyam muṟpaṭṭu niṟkiṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ ellik kuṟippāka, annūliṉkaṇ paṉampāraṉār eṉpār, "vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari āyiṭit tamiḻkūṟum nallulakam" eṉṟu kūṟiyati muṉpē kāṭṭiyuḷḷōm. itaṉ karuttu, tamiḻakattukku vaṭavelli vaṭavēṅkaṭamum teṉṉelli teṉkumariyumām eṉpatu. mēṟkum kiḻakkum elli kaṭalātalāl avi paṉampāraṉārāṟ kūṟappaṭa villi. vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari eṉṟa elli iraṇṭaṉuḷ teṉkumariyip paṟṟi ārāycciyāḷar pala ceytikaḷi viriyak kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. teṉkumari yeṉpatu kumarikkōṭu eṉṟa maliyiyum ataṉic cārntu ōṭiya kumariyāṟṟiyum kuṟikkiṟateṉat teḷivupaṭutti yuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻ payilum māṇavar aṉivarkkum itu naṉku terinta ceytiyākum. ivvāṟē vaṭavēṅkaṭattip paṟṟik kūṟiyōr, atu tirumāl eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum tirumalit tiruppatiyākum eṉpatōṭu amintu pōyiṉar.
