tamiḻk kuṭiyil piṟanta naṉmakkaḷ tampāl ētēṉum māṉakkuṟivu nikaḻumāyiṉ ataṟkuk kaḻuvāyāka uṇṇā nōṉpu mēṟkoṇṭu uyirviṭuvatu marapu. ataṉi vaṭakkiruttal eṉpatu vaḻakkam. itaṉi mēṟkoṇṭavar, makkaḷ pōkkuvaravu illāta ōr iṭatti varintu koṇṭu tariyil pullip parappi ataṉmēl vaṭakku nōkkit taṇṇīrō paḻaccāṟō etaṉiyum uṇṇātiruntu uyir tuṟappar. appōtu avaruṭiya neruṅkiya naṇparum cuṟṟattārum cūḻa iruppar; cilar avarōṭu tāmum uṭaṉuyir viṭutalumuṇṭu. vaṭakkirukkak karutupavaṉ taṉ maṉivi karuvuṟṟirukkum pōtum ataṉi mēṟkōṭalākātu eṉpar. orukāṟ karikāṟ peruvaḷat tāṉukkum cēramāṉ peruñcēralātaṉukkum veṇṇil eṉṉumiṭattu oru pōr nikaḻnta pōtu, karikālaṉ eṟinta vēlāl cēramāṉ mutukiṭattē puṇṇuṟṟāṉāka, "taṉpōl vēntaṉ muṉpu kuṟittu eṟinta, puṟappuṇ nāṇi maṟattaki maṉṉaṉ vāḷ vaṭakkiruntaṉaṉ" eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kuṟippatu īṇṭu niṉivukūrattakkatu. vāḷ vaṭakkiruttal eṉpatu, pullip parappuvati viṭuttu vāḷip parappi ataṉmēl iruntu uṇṇānōṉpu mēṟkoḷvatu. uṟiyūriliruntu aracu purinta kōpperuñ cōḻaṉ eṉpāṉ orukāl tāṉpeṟṟa makkaḷē taṉṉoṭu māṟupaṭṭup pōrkku eḻuntatu kaṇṭu māṉampoṟātu kāviriyāṟṟiṉ turuttiyil iruntu vaṭakkiruntatu tamiḻaṟiñar naṉkaṟintatu. akkālattē avvēntaṉuṭiya uyirt tōḻarāṉa picir āntiyārum vaṭakkiruntu uyir tuṟantār. pāriyiṉ tōḻarāṉa kapilar, pāri iṟantapiṉ, avaṉ makkaḷit takkārkku maṇam purivittu naṭpukkaṭaṉ kaḻittapiṉ, tirukkōva lūrkku aṇmiyil teṉ peṇṇiyāṟṟut turuttiyil vaṭakkiruntu uyirtuṟantār. itu naṇpar pirivāṟṟāmiyāl mēṟkoṇṭatu.
paḻantamiḻ makkaḷ nāṭṭuppaṟṟu moḻippaṟṟukkaḷil mikka ciṟappuṟṟavar. vēḷpāriyatu nāṭu paṟampu eṉa vaḻaṅkum. atu teṉpaṟampu, vaṭapaṟampu eṉa irupirivukaḷāki iṭikkālac cōḻa pāṇṭiyar kālam variyiṟ peyarkoṇṭu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. teṉ paṟampu nāṭṭu vātavūril piṟantavar kapilar eṉṟu palarum vaḻivaḻi yākak kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉāl, avarukkum vēḷpārikkum uyiroṉṟiya kēṇmi tōṉṟiyiruntatu. vēḷpāri iṟantapiṉ, kapilar paṟampu nāṭṭi nīṅka vēṇṭiya varāṉār. akkāli avaratu nāṭṭup paṟṟu avar uḷḷatti varuttat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. nīṅkuṅkāl, avar kaṇṇukku iṉiya kāṭci tanta paṟampumaliyi nōkki,
"	peṭṭāṅku īyum peruvaḷam paḻuṉi
 	naṭṭaṉi maṉṉō muṉṉē, iṉiyē,
 	pāri māynteṉak kalaṅkik kiyaṟṟu
 	nīrvār kaṇṇēm toḻutuniṟ paḻiccic
 	cēṟum vāḻiyō perumpeyarp paṟampē" 1
eṉṟu pāṭi varuntukiṉṟār.
tamiḻ makkaḷatu moḻippaṟṟu mikka tūymiyāṉatu. moḻi yeṉpatu makkaḷuṭiya maṉavuṇarvi veḷippaṭukkum karuviyeṉa aṟiñar kūṟuvar. uṭaloṭu kūṭi vāḻum makkaḷukku, vāḻkkik kuriya karuvikaḷāka avartam maṉamum moḻiyum uṭalum vāyppa amintuḷḷaṉa. maṉam, kaṇ, kātu mutaliya karuvikaḷāl uṇarum ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷi vāṅki vakuttum tokuttum vakip paṭuttum muṟippaṭuttum, nalam kaṇṭu makiḻvatum tīmikaṇṭu veṟuttu otukkuvatum ceyyum. moḻiyeṉpatu maṉaṅkaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷiyum koṇṭakaruttuk kaḷiyum colluruvil veḷippaṭukkum karuviyeṉak karutuvar. maṉam tuṇinta uṇarvu kaḷi ulakiyaluruviṟ ceyaṟpaṭuttup payaṉ eytuvikkum ki kāl mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷōṭu iyinta uṭampu ceyaṟkaruviyāka iyalukiṉṟatu. im mūṉṟu karuvikaṭkum uriyaniliyil tamiḻmoḻiyi iyal ici nāṭakameṉa mūṉṟākakkoṇṭu pēṇuvatu tamiḻar marapu. iyal maṉattiyum, icimaṉamoḻi kaḷiyum, nāṭakam maṉamoḻi meykaḷiyum paṇi koḷḷum iyalpuṭiyaṉa. itaṉāl uyirvāḻkkikku uṭalpōla ulaka vāḻkkikkut tamiḻi iṉṟiyamiyāta uṟuppākak koṇṭaṉar tamiḻar. tamiḻi iḻappatu tamiḻkkuṭip paṇpuṭiya tamiḻarkkut tam uyiriṉiṉṟum uṭalit tuṟappatu - iṟappatu - pōlak karutti avala muṟuvikkum ceyalākum. maṉa mutaliya uṭaṟkūṟukaḷippōla, iyalum iciyum nāṭakamum ulakiyal vāḻkkikkuriya aṟivuk kūṟākak koṇṭu avaṟṟi vaḷarppatu vāḻviṉ ūtiyamākak koṇṭaṉar. pulavarkaḷip pēṇi iyaliyum, pāṇar mutalāyiṉāri ōmpi iciyiyum, kūttar porunar mutalāyiṉāri ūkki nāṭakattiyum paḻantamiḻ makkaḷ vaḷarttaṉar. pulavar vakiyuḷ, eṇṇum eḻuttumākiya iyaṟṟuṟikkaṇakkarum kālakkaṇakkarum camayak kaṇakkarum aṭaṅkuvar. icivakiyil, pāṇar viṟaliyar vayiriyar yāḻōr eṉap pala tiṟattar uṇṭu. kūttaruḷḷum porunar kōṭiyar, kaṇṇuḷar, viṟaliyar, tōriyar eṉap palar uḷar. ivarkaḷuṭiya tamiḻ nalaṅkaḷil muṭivēntar mutal ciṟu kāṇiyāḷar īṟāka aṉivarum īṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar. avarkaṭku avarkaḷ paricil vaḻaṅkiya tiṟatti, "oṇṇutal viṟaliyar pūvili peṟuka eṉa māṭamaturiyum tarukuvaṉ"1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟuva toṉṟē naṉku kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
yāḻ icippōr māliyiṟ cevvaḻip paṇṇum kāliyil marutappaṇṇum icippar. celvar tarum paricil mikutiyāl cerukkik kiyaṟṟu maṭinta icivāṇar, "māli marutam paṇṇik kālik kivaḻimaruṅkiṟ cevvaḻi paṇṇi"2 icineṟiyi maṟantu pōyiṉar; itaṟkuk kāraṇam celvak kaḷippē eṉṟa poruḷpaṭa vaṉparaṇar valvil ōriyeṉpāṉukkuk kūṟuvataṉāl aṟiyalām. icivāṇar, maṇmuḻā, paṇyāḻ, kaṇviṭutūmpu, kaḷiṟṟuyir, ellari, ākuḷi, patali mutalākap palavēṟu karuvikaḷik koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟi icikkum tuṟi irupattoṉṟu eṉpar; "mūvēḻ tuṟiyum muṟiyuḷik kaḻippi"1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōr attuṟiyiṉ tokiyic cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.
puṟappāṭṭukkaḷi nōkkiṉ paḻiya tamiḻ nilattiṉkaṇ nilaviya viḻākkaḷ pala kāṭcitarukiṉṟaṉa. pāṇṭiyaṉ palyāka cāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻutiyic ciṟappittup pāṭiya neṭṭimiyār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr, apperuvaḻutiyiṉ muṉṉōṉ oruvaṉ kaṭaṟ ṟeyvattukku munnīr viḻā eṉa oṉṟicceytu paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṉik kaṇṭāṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. itaṉi "kuṭumitaṅkō,..... munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ, naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi"2 eṉak kuṟippataṉāl aṟiyalām. periya periya māṭamāḷiki kaḷilum celvarkaḷ taṉittaṉiyē viḻāk koṇṭāṭu kiṉṟārkaḷ. viḻākkālattē teyvaṅkaḷukku uyirp paliyākac cemmaṟikkiṭāykaḷ veṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.3 pōr nikaḻntapiṉ ataṉkaṇ uṇṭāṉa veṟṟi kuṟittu veṟṟi viḻā ayartalum inta naṉmakkaṭku iyalpu.4 viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil, kaḷamar, ariyal eṉṉum kaḷḷi āmi yiṟicciyuṭaṉ uṇṭu āralmīṉic cuṭṭuk katuppil aṭakkik koṇṭu uṟakkamiṉṟi iravu muḻutum viḷiyāṭṭayarvar. tirumaṇaṅkaṭku vatuvi viḻā eṉṟu peyarkūṟuvar. akkālattē putiyarāy vantōric ciṟappikkum ceykiyi, "vatuvi viḻāviṟ putuvōrk kellām, vevvāyp peyta pūtanīr cālka"5 eṉṟu māṅkuṭikiḻār urikkiṉṟār. ivvāṟu ciṟucōṟṟu viḻā, peruñcōṟṟuviḻā, vilviḻā eṉap palavēṟu viḻākkaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. āṟukaḷil putu veḷḷam varuṅkāl putunīr viḻāvum, tittiṅkaḷil tinnīr viḻāvum, mācit tiṅkaḷil kaṭalāṭṭu viḻāvum, paṅkuṉiyil uḷḷiviḻāvum, cittirit tiṅkaḷil intiraviḻāvum koṇṭāṭi yuḷḷaṉar.
viḻākkālaṅkaḷilum piṟakālaṅkaḷilum makkaḷ palvēṟu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷi mēṟkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. kuṟiñcinilac ciṟuvar ūkampulliṉ nuṉiyil uṭi muḷḷic ceruki, vaḷārāl ceyta villiṭivittu ampu eṉat toṭuttu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉar. mulli marutam mutaliya nilaṅkaḷil vāḻvōr ciṟuvar "neṭu nīrk kuṭṭattut tuṭum eṉap pāyntu, kuḷittu maṇal koṇṭu" makiḻkiṉṟaṉar. iḷamakaḷir nīrnilikaḷil ōriyāṭuvar; maṉi yiṭattē iruntu kaḻaṅku ammaṉipantu mutaliyaṉa viḷiyāṭu kiṉṟārkaḷ. neytal nilattuc ciṟuvar kaṭalāṭu kiṉṟārkaḷ. makaḷir kaṭaṟkariyil mēyum ciṟuciṟu naṇṭukaḷi alippatum, vaṇṭaṟ pāvi vittu viḷiyāṭuvatum uṇṭu. pālip pakutiyil vēṭṭuvar ciṟuvar, nellik kāykaḷik koṇṭu vaṭṭāṭu vārkaḷ. mulli nilattavar kollēṟu taḻuvum viḷiyāṭṭic ciṟappākak karutu kiṉṟaṉar. yāṉi mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu poraviṭṭu vēṭikki pārppatum, kōḻi, kuṟumpūḻ, āṭu mutaliyavaṟṟip pōr ceyvittu veṟṟikaṇṭu iṉpuṟuvatum iyalpākavuḷḷaṉa.
paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē nilaviya paḻamoḻikaḷum paḻavaḻakkukaḷum puṟanāṉūṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṇiccēri muṭamōciyār āy aṇṭiraṉik kaṇṭu pāṭip paricil peṟumāṟu viṟali oruttikkuk kūṟumiṭattu, "viṟali, nī aṇṭiraṉip paṟṟip piṟar kūṟak kēṭṭiruppāyē yaṉṟik kaṇṭiruppāyalli; kāṇavēṇṭiṉiyāyiṉ niṉ koṇṭi mēl kāṟṟaṭikkac celka" eṉṟa poruḷ paṭumāṟu, "niṉvirivaḷar kūntal varivaḷi yuḷarak, kalava maññiyiṉ kāṇvara iyali..... ceṉmē"1 eṉṟu icikkiṉṟār. ivvāṟē iṭikkālattuc cāṉṟōr "illārkkuk kiḻiyīṭu vāyttāṟpōl" eṉṟu eḷiyōṉ oruvaṉ perumuyaṟci yiṉṟi ariyaporuḷ peṟum tiṟattik kūṟuvar. ivvaḻakkup paṇṭic cāṉṟōr iṭiyē, "kiḷiyīṭu vāyttāṟpōla" eṉa vaḻaṅki vantuḷatu. taṉṉi nāṭivarum. iravalarkku nāñcil vaḷḷuvaṉ, taṉpāl uḷḷa perum poruḷi variyātu vaḻaṅkum tiṟatti marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār ciṟappikkak karutiṉavar, "kiḷimarīiya viyaṉpuṉattu maraṉ aṇi peruṅkural aṉiyaṉ"1 eṉṟu ivvaḻakkāṟu viḷaṅkak kūṟuvatik kāṇalām. eṟumpukaḷ muṭṭikoṇṭu tiṭṭikaḷi nōkkic cellak kāṇiṉ virivil maḻivarum eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu ikkālattum uṇṭu. ivvaḻakkum karuttum caṅka kālattum iruntuḷatu. cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ, "poyyā eḻili peyyiṭam nōkki, muṭṭi koṇṭu vaṟpulam cērum, ciṟu nuṇṇeṟumpu"2 eṉṟu kūṟuvataṉāl teriyalām.
namatu nāṭṭut teṉkaṭaṟ pakuti muttukkup peyarpeṟṟatu. kaṭaṟkuḷ mūḻki muttukkaḷikkoṇarvatu tamiḻar mēṟkoṇṭirunta ariya toḻilkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. āyiṉum caṅkukaḷiṉ vayiṟṟiṟ kāṇappaṭum muttukkaḷiṉ tōṟṟampaṟṟi innāḷi uyir nūlārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvatu vēṟu: caṅkukaḷiṉ vayiṟṟil cikkik koḷḷum maṇaṟpoṭi, ataṉ vayiṟṟil ūṟum oruvaki nīrāl cūḻappeṟṟu uṟintu mutirntu muttākiṟatu eṉpar. āṉāl, paṇṭiyōr maḻi peyyum nīrttuḷiyāl muttu uṇṭākiṟa teṉak karutiṉar. "micippeyta nīr kaṭaṟ parantu muttākuntu"3 eṉa varum cāṉṟōr uri itaṉi nām aṟiya vurikkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟē naṇṭu ciṉiyīṉumkālattu iṟakkum eṉpatum, mutalitāṉ īṉṟa kuñcukaḷit tiṉṟuviṭum eṉpatum piṟavum annāḷiya karuttukkaḷākum.
nilattukku nellum karumpum aṇi eṉpar. karumpiṉ vakikaḷ pala uṇṭeṉṟu nām aṟikiṉṟōm. ivaṟṟuḷ cāvakak karumpu, paṉṟik karumpu, nāṇaṟ karumpu eṉpaṉa ciṟantavi. cāvakak karumpu cāvakattiliruntu vantateṉavum, paṉṟikkarumpu paṉṟi nāṭṭiliruntu vanta teṉavum makkaḷ kūṟuvar. eṅkaḷ oḷaviyār kuppattukku aṇmiyil uḷḷa ūr ālik kirāmam eṉpatu. avvūril neṭuṅkāla mākak karumpē mikutiyākap payir ceyyap paṭṭatupaṟṟi ataṟku appeyaruṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉṟu mutiyavarkaḷ kūṟuvar. avarkaḷ ciṟappittuk kūṟum cāvakak karumpum paṉṟik karumpum ekkālattuc cāvakam mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantaṉa eṉpatu teriyavilli. puṟanāṉūṟṟuk kālattē karumpu vaki yoṉṟu vēṟṟu nāṭṭiliruntu koṇarap paṭṭuḷatu. itaṉi, eṅkaḷ ūrkkuriya mutalviyākiya oḷaviyār, atikamāṉip pāṭumiṭattu avaṉ muṉṉōr karumpiṉi vēṟṟu nāṭṭiliruntu koṇarnta ceytiyi, "amararp pēṇiyum āvuti aruttiyum, arumpeṟal marapiṉ karumpu ivaṇ tantum, nīrakavirukki āḻi cūṭṭiya toṉṉili marapiṉ niṉ muṉṉōr"1 eṉṟum, "antarattu arum peṟal amiḻtam aṉṉa, karumpu ivaṇ tantōṉ perum piṟaṅkaṭiyē"2 eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ippuṟanāṉūṟṟup pakutikku urikaṇṭa cāṉṟōr, "arum peṟal marapiṉ karumpu ivaṇtantum" eṉṟa toṭarkkup "peṟutaṟkariya muṟimiyiyuṭiya karumpi viṇṇulakat tiṉiṉṟu ivvulakattiṉkaṭ koṭuvantu tantum" eṉṟu urikūṟu kiṉṟār. "antarattu arumpeṟal amiḻtam" eṉṟatu koṇṭu viṇṇulakam eṉpatu varuvittu urikkappaṭukiṟatu pōlum. viṇṇulakam pōkavulakamē yaṉṟit toḻil ceyyum karuma pūmi yaṉṟu eṉpatu nūlōr koḷki. ākavē aṅkē karumpu viḷivikkum toḻil illāmiyāl viṇṇulaka varalāṟu kūṟuvatu poruttamākātu; karumpiṉ vakiyoṉṟu atiyamāṉiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāl orukālattē vēṟṟu nāṭṭiliruntu koṇarap paṭṭatu eṉṟu koḷvatu nēritu.
atikarkaḷ kutirimaliyic cūḻnta mēlik kaṭaṟkarip pakutiyil vāḻntavarkaḷātaliṉ, antap pakutiyil irunta paḻiya nāṭukaḷ oṉṟiliruntu karumpik koṇṭu vantiruppar; ataṉi urikārar ivvāṟu kuṟittaṉar pōlum eṉṟum koḷḷalām.
viyāvik kōpperum pēkaṉi avaṉ maṉivi kaṇṇaki kāraṇamākap pāṭic ceṉṟa kapilar, mali nāṭṭavar vaḻakkāṟu oṉṟik kuṟikkiṉṟār. maḻi vēṇṭiyapōtum mikka maḻi peytapōtum maliyil vāḻum kuṉṟavar, kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ceyvarām; "malivāṉ koḷkeṉa vuyarpali tūuy, māriyāṉṟu maḻimēk kuyarkeṉak, kaṭavuṭ pēṇiya kuṟavar"1 eṉpatu avar kūṟuvatu. ivvaḻakku ippōtum parumiya nāṭṭu mali vāṇariṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. parumā nāṭṭip paṇṭiyōr arumaṇam eṉṟu kuṟittalāl, parumāvukkum tamiḻakattukkum muṉṉāḷil toṭar piruntamiyum terikiṟatu.

18. paḻantamiḻar camayanili
paḻantamiḻar eṉṟa coṟṟoṭar nam kaṇṇilum kātilum paṭṭavuṭaṉ namatu niṉivu tolkāppiyam caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ākiya nūlkaḷ eḻunta kālattukku nammik koṇṭucelkiṟatu. akkāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkik kūṟukaḷ nam maṉakkaṇṇil tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷiṉ camaya vuṇarvu oḻukkaṅkaḷi uṇarntu kōṭaṟkeṉac cila coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum ciṟanta cāṉṟukaḷāka akkāla nūlkaḷil niṉṟu nam aṟivip paṇikoḷḷu kiṉṟaṉa. annāḷiya kalveṭṭukkaḷō ōvac ceytikaḷō piṟavō kiṭikkavilli. nallicip pulamic cāṉṟōr cilar avvakkālattil pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ cilavaṟṟip pattuppāṭṭu eṭṭuttoki eṉap paṇṭiyōr tokut tuḷḷaṉar. avi palavum tērntu koḷḷappaṭṭa oru cila cāṉṟōrkaḷiṉ cilavum palavumākiya pāṭṭukkaḷātalāl, avaṟṟik koṇṭu varalāṟṟu muṟiyil vittuc camaya niliyik kāṇpatu aritākavuḷatu. āyiṉum, iṉṟu kiṭikkum ilakkiyak kuṟippukkaḷik koṇṭu kāṇappaṭum paḻantamiḻar camayanili uṇmikku māṟupaṭṭataṉṟu eṉpatu mutaṟkaṇ maṉaṅkoḷḷāṟpālatu.
mēlē kuṟitta tolkāppiya mutaliya ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷum, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, akkālattē nilaviya ulaka vaḻakkum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkumākiya iruvaki vaḻakkukaḷiyum mutalākak koṇṭu eḻuntaṉavākum. "tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum āyiru mutaliṉ" eṉṟē tolkāppiyattukkup pāyiram vaḻaṅkiya paṉampāraṉār kuṟippatu itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟu. ākavē, ivviruvaki vaḻakkukaḷil nilaviya camayak koḷkikaḷik kāṇpatē namatu muṉṉiṟkum paṇiyākiṟatu.
camaya vāḻvukku iṉṟiyamiyāta aṭippaṭi kaṭavuṭ koḷkiyākum. kaṭavuḷ oruvar uṇṭu eṉṟa uṇarvum, avari vāḻtti vaḻipaṭṭu iyaluvatu camaya oḻukkam eṉṟa karuttum camayattiṉ uṭkiṭi. ivaṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭu, ulakiyal vāḻvil aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉṟa tuṟikaḷil ceyvaṉavum tavirvaṉavum vakuttu, niṉivu col ceyalkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ vaḻi niṟutti yoḻukuvatu camaya vāḻvākum eṉpatu nāṭaṟinta ceyti.
camayattiṉ tōṟṟam mikamikat toṉmiyāṉa makkaḷ, iyaṟkiyil kaṇṭa peru nikaḻccikaḷāl uḷḷattē koṇṭa accamum avalamum kāraṇamākattōṉṟiyatu eṉpar varalāṟṟaṟiñar. accattiṟkaruvuṟṟeḻunta camayam, iyaṟkiyiṉ kūṟukaḷāṉa nilam, nīr, tī, kāṟṟu, vāṉ eṉṟa aintukkum appālāy, avaṟṟi ākkip paṭittu āṭṭivikkum mutaṟ poruḷ oṉṟu uṇṭeṉa uṇarntu, ataṟkum ulaka vāḻkkikkum toṭarpu amittu, ataṉi niṉiviṟkoṇṭu iṉitoḻukac ceyyum muṟiyil makkaṭku nalla toṇṭāṟṟiyirukkiṟatu. anta mutaṟ poruḷip paḻantamiḻar kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum iyavuḷ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum iraṇṭu coṟkaḷāl kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. "kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu" eṉat tolkāppiyattum, "periyōr ēttum perum peyar iyavuḷ" eṉap pattuppāṭṭilum kāṇappaṭum toṭar moḻikaḷ yāvarum naṉkaṟintaṉa.
kaṭavuḷ, iyavuḷ eṉṟa coṟkaḷ kaṭattal iyakkutal eṉṟa poruṭkuriyaṉa. ivi uḷḷeṉṟa īṟukoṇṭu muṭivatāl, akkuḷ vikkuḷ eṉṟāṟpōla, uḷḷum puṟamum kaṭantu niṟpatu, uṇṇiṉṟu iyakki niṟpatu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭuk kaṭavuḷ eṉappaṭum mutaṟporuḷ ellāp poruṭkum akattum puṟattum kalantu niṉṟu iyakkuvatum, avaṟṟik kaṭantu niṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ceyal muṟikaṭku āṇi celuttuvatum ceytoḻukuvatu eṉṟa karutti uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.
ikkaṭavuṭ koḷkikku nilikkaḷam uyirum ulakamu mākum. ulakamāvatu, "nilam tī nīr vaḷi vicum pōṭu aintum kalanta mayakkam" eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. camayac cūḻniliyil "kālam ulakam uyirē uṭampē, pālvari teyvam viṉiyē pūtam, ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ colleṉa varūum āyīrintum" ciṟanta poruḷkaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. uyirkaḷ, pul pūṭukaḷākiya ōraṟivuyir mutal makkaḷ īṟāka āṟuvakiyākap pirikiṉṟaṉa. ivvuyirkaḷ yāvum maṉṉuyir eṉavum, tolluyir eṉavum ciṟappikkap paṭum; ataṉāl, avi ceyappaṭu poruḷākāmal kaṭavuṭ poruḷpōla eṉṟum nilipēṟu uṭiya iyaṟki uḷporuḷ ākum. ēṉi ulakamum ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷum tōṉṟiniṉṟu maṟiyum kāraṇattāl ceyappaṭu poruḷākiṉṟaṉa. ceyappaṭu poruḷ eṉavē, ceyvōṉ oruvaṉ uṇṭeṉṟum, avaṉē kaṭavuḷākiya mutaṟporuḷ eṉṟum paḻantamiḻar mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl avaṉi "ulaku iyaṟṟiyāṉ" eṉṟum, "ulaku paṭittōṉ" eṉṟum kuṟitturippārāyiṉar.
uyirkaḷ iṉṉa vuru iṉṉa niṟam eṉṟu colla voṇṇāta aṟivup poruḷākum. āyiṉum, ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷāṉa uṭampiyum ēṉip poruḷkaḷiyum iyakkuvatum avaṟṟōṭu kūṭi niṉṟu iyaṅkuvatum ceyvaṉa. avviyakkattāl avaṟṟiṭiyē viṉikaḷ tōṉṟi nilavukiṉṟaṉa. uṭampōṭukūṭi yuṟiyum makkaḷuyirkku maṉam eṉṟa nuṇṇiyatoru karuvi yuṇṭu eṉap paṇṭiyōr kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar; aḵtu uṭampu tōṉṟutaṟkuriya mūlap poruḷāl ākiyatu eṉṟum, eṉiṉum aḵtu uṭampu mākātu uyirumākātu iraṇṭiṟkum vēṟāy uṭaṉāy niṟpatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iḷamik kālattu uyirit taṉvaḻi niṟutti uṭaliṉ vaḷarccikkut tuṇiyākiyum, atu kaḻinta vaḻi, uyiriṉ vaḻi niṉṟu aṟivup pēṟṟukkum, ulakiyal iṉpap pēṟṟukku muriya paṇikaḷil uṭali iyakki nalameytuvikkum nuṇ karuviyākiyum iyalum iyalpu immaṉattukku uṇṭu. kāṇappaṭum poruḷit taṉkaṇ oṭukki niṟuttik kāṭṭum kaṇṇāṭipōla, ulakaṉitti yum kaṇ kātu mutaliya karuvikaḷāl vāṅkit taṉkaṇ oṭukki niṟuttik kāṭṭuvatum immaṉattukku vāytta ceyal. mēlum, kāṭṭum vakiyil kaṇṇāṭi pōlvatāyiṉum, kaṇṇāṭikkillāta oru ciṟappu maṉattuk kuṇṭu. taṉṉil oṭuṅki niṟkum ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷi vakuttum tokuttum virittum iyipupaṭuttum urumāṟṟiyum tirittum pirittum kūṭṭiyum perukkiyum curukkiyum kāṭṭavallatu maṉam. maṉattāl kāṭṭap paṭuvaṉa aṉittum ulakiyaṟ poruḷ kaḷēyām; ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟāya uyiriyō ataṟku appālākiya mutaṟ poruḷiyō ataṉāl itukāṟum kāṇappaṭṭatilli. ātalāl, uyirkaḷ uṭampoṭukūṭi ulakil vāḻkki naṭattuvataṟkeṉṟē aminta nuṇkaruvi immaṉam eṉṟu paḻantamiḻar karutiṉar; atupaṟṟiyē, "cuvi yoḷi ūṟu ōci nāṟṟam eṉṟa aintiṉvaki terivāṉ kaṇṇatē ulaku" eṉṟum "maṉameṉṉum poccāvāk karuvi" eṉṟum tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiṉar. maṉattiṉ ceyalellām niṉivu eṉappaṭum. niṉikkappaṭum karuttukkaḷukku vaṭivu taruvatu col. col nikaḻccikkuk karuvi vāy; avaṟṟic ceyaṟpaṭuttuvatu ki kālkaḷōṭukūṭiya uṭampu. uyirkaḷ uṭampōṭukūṭic ceyyum viṉikaḷ yāvum inta maṉam, vāy, uṭampu eṉṟa mūṉṟu karuvikaḷiyumē vāyilākak koṇṭaṉa eṉpar.
viṉiyiṉ iyalpi ārāynta paḻan tamiḻar, ovvoru viṉiyilum, viṉi, viṉimutal, ceyappaṭu poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, karuvi, kāraṇam, payaṉ eṉa eṭṭukkūṟukaḷik kaṇṭaṉar. ivaṟṟi,
"	viṉiyē ceyvatu ceyappaṭu poruḷē
 	nilaṉē kālam karuvi yeṉṟā
 	iṉṉataṟ kitupayaṉāka eṉṉum
 	aṉṉamarapiṉ iraṇṭoṭum tokii
 	āyeṭṭeṉpa toḻiṉ mutaṉiliyē"
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟukiṟatu.
ulakil ceyyappaṭum toḻilukkēṟpa ūtiyamum uṟiyuḷum amivatu pōla uyirkaḷ ceyyum viṉikkēṟpa avvuyirkaṭku nukarcciyum uṭampum amikiṉṟaṉa. uṭampiyum nukar
poruḷiyum paṭit taḷikkum vakiyil mutaṟporuḷākiya kaṭavuḷ mēṉmi yuṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṟār. kaṭavuḷpōla uyirkaḷum nili pēṟuṭiya uḷporuḷāyiṉum, aṟivu vakiyil aṟiyāmi kalantu iruḷ kalappuṇṭa tiriviḷakkiṉ cuṭarpōlvaṉa. kaṭavuṭ
poruḷō tūya aṟivākavēyuḷatu. uyir aṟivu taṉpāṟ kalantirukkum aṟiyāmiyākiya iruḷ nīṅkutaṟkākavē ulakil uṭampiṭik kalantu tōṉṟum paṭippiṉip peṟukiṟatu; uṭampoṭukūṭi ulakil viṉi ceytu aṟiyāmiyiṉ nīṅki viḷakkam eytukiṟatu. aṟiyāmi viraviya kuṟipāṭṭāl uyirpporuḷ kaṭavuṭ poruḷi nōkkak kīḻppaṭṭuviṭukiṟatu eṉpatu paḻantamiḻk koḷki.
itaṉāl, kaṭavuṭkum ulakattukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu paṭippōṉukkum paṭikkappaṭṭa poruṭkum uḷḷa toṭarpāyk kaṭavuḷi, ulakit taṉakkuriyatākak koṇṭa uṭiyavaṉākavum, ulakam avaṉatu uṭimi yākavum niṟuttu kiṟatu. uyirkaḷ ulakiṭittōṉṟum uṭampi iṭamākak koṇṭu viṉipala ceytu aṟivup payaṉ peṟuvatāl, ivvula kuṭampukaḷ uyirkaḷiṉ poruṭṭē paṭikkappaṭṭu avaṟṟiṟku utavap paṭṭiruppatu iṉitu viḷaṅkum. itaṉāl, kaṭavuṭ poruṭkum uyirkaṭkum uḷḷa toṭarpu utavi ceytōṉukkum ceyyappaṭṭōrkkum uḷḷa toṭarpāy, utavi peṟṟōṉ ataṉic ceytōṉukkuk kaṭamippaṭṭu avaṉ ceyta naṉṟiyiṉi niṉin toḻukuvatu pōla uyirkaḷ kaṭavuḷatu aruḷiniṉintu vaḻipaṭṭoḻukuvatu kaṭaṉākum eṉpati vaṟpuṟuttukiṟatu. itaṉāltāṉ tolkāppiyar kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pakutikkuc ciṟappāka ilakkaṇam vakuttuk kāṭṭiṉār.
ulakuṭampukaḷum ēṉi nukarporuḷkaḷum uyirkaḷiṉ poruṭṭē paṭikkappaṭṭaṉavātaliṉ, ivvulakil vāḻum uyirvaki ellāvaṟṟiṟkum itaṉ kaṇ vāḻa urimiyuṇṭu eṉpatu paḻan tamiḻar koḷkiyāyiṟṟu. tamiḻakattē ikkoḷki tōṉṟi nilipeṟṟa kālattil ēṉi nāṭṭavariṭiyum kaṭavuṭ koḷki tōṉṟi yiruntatu; āyiṉum annāṭṭavar makkaḷuyir oṉṟiṟkē ivvulaku paṭikkappaṭṭatu; piṟavaṟṟiṟku aṉṟu eṉṟum, piṟavellām makkaḷuyiriṉ poruṭṭu amintaṉa eṉṟum karutiṉar. makkaḷuyiruḷḷum oru cilavē ulakil vāḻalām; piṟavellām vāḻtaṟkuriyaṉa alla eṉṟu karuti yoḻukiya nāṭṭavarum annāḷil piṟanāṭukaḷil iruntaṉar. paḻantamiḻar avaṟṟi ēlātu, "yātum ūrē yāvarum kēḷir" eṉṟa tam paḻaṅkoḷkiyi eṭuttōti niṟuviṉar.
uyirvakiyuḷ makkaḷuyirit taṉippaṭa niṟuttik kāṇpatil paḻantamiḻar tavaṟavilli. makkaḷi uyartiṇi eṉṟum, ēṉi uyir vakikaḷi aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟum paṇṭiyōr koṇṭaṉar. "uyartiṇi eṉmaṉār makkaṭcuṭṭē, aḵṟiṇi eṉmaṉār avarala piṟavē" eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. makkaḷiṉattuḷ aṭaṅkāmal kaṭavuḷākiya mutaṟporuḷumākāmal iraṇṭaṟkum iṭippaṭṭa niliyil cilavuyirkaḷip paḻantamiḻar kaṇṭārkaḷ. avaṟṟit teyvam eṉa niṟutti, avi makkaḷuyirpōla maṉa vuṇarvum viṉi ceytalum iṉpattuṉpa nukarcciyum uṭiya vāyp piṟappatum iṟappatumāy iruppavi eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ ceyal muṟiyum vāḻkkiyum makkaḷulakiṟku mēṉiliyil iruppaṉa eṉṟum urittaṉar. "teyvam cuṭṭiya peyarnilik kiḷavi" makkaḷiṉattaṉa allavākaliṉ "ivveṉa aṟiyum antam tamakkila" eṉṟu vilakki, colvaḻakkil vēṇṭumāṉāl, "uyartiṇi maruṅkil pālpirinticikkum" eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟiṉār.
paḻantamiḻar collulaki uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉak koṇṭāṟpōlap poruḷulakik kuṟiñci mulli marutam neytal pāli eṉa aintu vakiyāka vakuttuk koṇṭaṉar. toṭakkattil pālikku nilam kāṇappaṭa villi; āyiṉum mulliyum kuṟiñciyum muṟimiyil tirintu nalliyalpu aḻintu nilimi vēṟupaṭak kaṇṭu ataṉip pāliyeṉak koṇṭārkaḷ. ippakutiyuḷ vāḻnta makkaḷ muṟiyē kuṉṟavar āyar vēḷāḷar paratavar eṉak kuṟikkap peṟṟaṉar. avarkaṭkut tiṇinilimakkaḷ eṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅkiṉar. avaravar vāḻkkikkuriya uṇavu toḻil mutaliyaṉa taṉittaṉiyāka uṇṭu. avaṟṟōṭu camaya voḻukkattikkūṭṭi mēlē kuṟitta teyvaṅkaḷiyum ovvoru tiṇikkum urimi ceytaṉar. kuṟiñcikku murukaṉum mullikku māyavaṉum marutattukku intiraṉum neytalukku varuṇaṉum pālikkuk koṟṟaviyum teyvamāvar. makkaḷuḷ uyarntu nāṭāḷum taṉivurimi peṟṟa vēntaṉi marutattukkum, kāliyil kaṭalil tōṉṟi māliyil akkaṭaliṭattē maṟiyum aruṇaṉi neytalukkum orukālattē teyvamākak kuṟit tiruntaṉar; vitika vētiyar toṭarpu peṟṟa piṟkālattil vēntaṉiṭattē intiraṉiyum, aruṇaṉiṭattē varuṇaṉiyum pukuttiṉar eṉṟum, ataṉāṟṟāṉ vēḷāḷar intiraṉiyum paratavar varuṇaṉiyum vaḻipaṭṭa pāṭṭukkaḷ caṅkattoki nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭavilli eṉṟum ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar. itteyvaṅkaṭku nāṭpūcaṉiyum viḻāc ciṟappum ceyvatu camayavoḻukkam. kaṭavuḷ eṉpatu itteyva vakikaṭku mēṟpaṭṭu niṉṟu ellām valla muḻumutaṟ poruḷ eṉa nilaviṟṟu. ellā nilappakutikaṭkum nīr vaḷi tī vicumpu ākiya aṉittukkum vēṟāy oṉṟāy uṭaṉāy mēlāy ellām taṉ āṇi vaḻiyoḻukap paṇṇuvatu paṟṟik kaṭavuṭ poruṭkut taṉiyē nilam vakukkappaṭavilli. ellā nilaṅkaḷilum ikkaṭavuṭ kuṟippup potuvākak kāṇappaṭuvatoṉṟē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟu.
mēlum, iṭikkālattē tōṉṟiya civaṉ kōyilkaḷi nōkkiṉ ivvuṇmi naṉku pulaṉākum. iṉṉavuru iṉṉa iyal eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭāta civakkuṟiyi naṭuvaṇvittu, kuṟiñci mutaliya nilaṅkaṭkuriya murukaṉ māyavaṉ koṟṟavi mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷic cuṟṟup puṟattē vittu amittirukkiṉṟaṉar; naṭuniliyāyt talimi tāṅki viḷaṅkum civattukkuḷḷa ciṟappi ēṉic cūḻpuṟattuḷḷa teyvaṅkaṭku nalkuvatum vaḻakkamaṉṟu. itaṉālum paṇṭiyōr koṇṭirunta kaṭavuṭ koḷki naṉku pulaṉākum.
kuṟiñci nilattu murukaṉ, mullikkuriya māyavaṉ mutaliya teyvavaki ellāvaṟṟiṟkum, tōṟṟamum nilipēṟum kūṟap paṭuvatupōla, muḻumutal eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum kaṭavuṭ poruṭkut tōṟṟamum īṟum kūṟappaṭuvatilli. apporuḷik kuṟikkum pōtu, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, piṟi muṭiyum mukkaṇṇum kaṟi miṭaṟum uṭiyatākak kūṟuvatuṇṭu. itaṉāl iṉṟu civam eṉṟa collāl kuṟikkappaṭum mutaṟ poruḷ ivvaṭiyāḷaṅkaḷit taṉpāl koṇṭiruppatu paṟṟic civamē paḻantamiḻar camayaneṟiyil nilaviya kaṭavuḷ eṉpatu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. civaṉ kōyilkaḷil civavaṭivu mukam kikāl ākiya uṟuppukkaḷiṉṟik kantu vaṭivil iruppatum itaṟkuc ciṟanta cāṉṟu pakarukiṉṟatu. teyvaṅkaṭkup piṟappu, tōṟṟam mutaliya varalāṟu kūṟappaṭuvatum kaṭavuṭku atu kūṟappaṭāmiyum nōkkiṉ, piṟavā vāḻkkip perumi yuṭiyatu kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum, piṟantu nilipēṟu koṇṭu iyaluvaṉa teyvaṅkaḷeṉṟum paḻantamiḻar karutiya koḷkikaḷā kiṉṟaṉa. mukam kikāl mutaliya uṟuppuṭiya poruḷkaḷ tōṉṟi niṉṟu maṟivatu kaṇṭa paḻantamiḻar, avviyalpiṉavāya teyvaṅkaṭkut tali mukam ki kāl koṇṭa uruvaṅkaḷip paṭittu niṟuvi vaḻipaṭuvatum, kaṭavuṭku avvuruvi amikkātu kantu vaṭivil niṟutti vaḻipaṭuvatum marapākak koṇṭaṉar. tuṟavu neṟiyil niṉṟu uruvuṭiya teyvaṅkaḷi nīkki, uruvamillāta kaṭavuṭ poruḷi niṉintu vaḻipaṭṭamiyiṉ attuṟavu neṟic cāṉṟōrkaḷik kaṭavuḷar eṉṟu kuṟittup pārāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar.
makkaḷ vāḻvil viṉiyuṇarvum maṟupiṟappum vīṭupēṟṟiṉ pamum nirayat tuṉpamum col vaḻakkil nilavukiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷuṭiya maṉam moḻi uṭampu eṉṟa mūṉṟiṉ acivu aciviṉmikaḷāl viṉikaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. viṉiyillāta uyirē ulakil illi. ovvoru viṉikkum payaṉ uṇṭu. iṉpam payappatu nalviṉi; tuṉpam taruvatu tīviṉi. nalviṉi ceytōr iṉpattukkum tīviṉi ceytōr tuṉpattukkum uriyarāvar. viṉiyeṉpatu taṉṉiliyil uyirō uṇarvō illātatākaliṉ, atutāṉē ceṉṟu viṉi ceytavaṉukku uriya payaṉi nalkuvatu illi. viṉiyic ceytavaṉpāl viṉip payaṉ ceṉṟu aṭitaṟkuriya ceyalic ceyyum vakiyil teyvaṅkaḷ paṇipurikiṉṟaṉa. atupaṟṟiyē teyvattip pālvari teyvam eṉṟu tolkāppiyar ciṟappittuḷḷār ceyyappaṭum. viṉip payaṉkaḷukku ēṟpa uyirkaṭku immi maṟumi vīṭu eṉṟu mūṉṟu vaki ulakukaḷ kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. viṉiceytaṟ kuriyatu nām vāḻum maṇṇulakam; nalviṉip payaṉākiya iṉpa nukarccikkuriya ulakam tuṟakkameṉṟum, tīviṉip payaṉākiya tuṉpatti nukarum ulakam nirayam eṉṟum kūṟuvar. viṉit toṭarpiṉiṉṟum aṟavē nīṅki viḷaṅkum tūya aṟivuṭiya cāṉṟōrkku vīṭṭulakam uriyateṉap paḻantamiḻar karutiṉar.
viṉi tōṉṟumpōtē ataṉ payaṉum uṭaṉ tōṉṟukiṟatu. viṉi muṭivil ataṉ muḻutta payaṉ uruvāki viṉi ceyta mutalvaṉiyaṭiyum niliyi eytukiṟatu. aḵtu avaṉ appayaṉi nukarum vari avaṉukku aḵtu ūḻkaṉipōl niṉṟu nukarvukkuriya kālam vantatum teyvattiṉ āṇiyāl evvakiyālēṉum avaṉi aṭintu viṭukiṟatu. ataṉāl avviṉiyi ūḻviṉi yeṉat tamiḻc cāṉṟōr kaṇṭu urittaṉar. ūḻviṉiyiṉ payaṉi ūṭṭuvikkum paṇi, kaṭavuṭ poruḷiṉ āṇivaḻi iyaṅkum teyvaṅkaḷiṉ ceyalātalāl, teyva vaḻi pāṭṭukku makkaḷiṭiyē iṉṟiyamiyāmi tōṉṟu vatāyiṟṟu. teyva vaḻipāṭṭāl viṉippayaṉatu nukarcci kāla vakiyil vēṟupaṭum eṉṟa karuttip paṇṭiyōr koṇṭiruntaṉar. kuṉṟavar murukaṉukku vaḻipāṭāṟṟi vēṭṭam celvatum, āyarmāyōṉukkuk kuraviyāṭi vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟu vatum, vēḷāḷar irantiraviḻā, ātiri viḻā mutaliyaṉa ceyvatum, paratavar kaṟavukkōṭu naṭṭuk kaṭal teyvattukkup paravukkaṭaṉ āṟṟi mīṉvēṭṭam purivatum, koṟṟaviyi vaḻipaṭṭu eyiṉar maṟattoḻil ceyvatum caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭuvatu itupaṟṟiyē yākum.
ulakil vāḻum uyirkaḷ aṉittukkum iṉpam peṟuvatu oṉṟē kuṟikkōḷ. "ellā vuyirkkum iṉpam eṉpatu tāṉ amarntu varūum mēvaṟkum" eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. inta iṉpappayaṉip peṟuvatu kuṟittē ulaka vāḻvu naṭipeṟukiṟatu. uyirkaḷāl ceyyappaṭum viṉikaḷyāvum ik kuṟikkōḷiyē nāṭi niṟkiṉṟaṉa. iṉpa muyaṟcikkaṇ tōṉṟum viṉi iṭiyīṭu paṭumiṭattum iṭiyūṟu eytumiṭattum avaṟṟi nīkkum ceyal vakikaḷil makkaḷiṉ aṟivum muyaṟciyum peritum īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avvakiyil camaya vuṇarvum vaḻipāṭum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. perumaḻi, maḻiyiṉmi, kaṭuṅkāṟṟu, koṭu vilaṅku, perumpaki mutaliyavaṟṟāl iṭiyūṟu uṇṭāku māyiṉ, makkaḷ teyva vaḻipāṭṭil ciṟantu niṟkiṉṟaṉar. peru maḻikku varuntiya kuṉṟavar.
"	malivāṉ koḷkeṉa vāṉpalitūuy
 	māriyāṉṟu maḻi mēkkuyarka eṉak
 	kaṭavuṭ pēṇiya kuṟavar mākkaḷ
 	peyalkaṇ māṟiya uvakiyar" 1
eṉac cāṉṟōr kuṟippatu kāṇalām. ceyyum viṉi kāraṇamākat tōṉṟum camaya vaḻipāṭukaḷ viṉit toṭakkattil iṭiyūṟu vārāmi karutiyum, iṭiyil vanta iṭiyūṟu nīṅkuvatu karutiyum, muṭivil viṉippayaṉ nalkum iṉpam kuṟittum naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
makkaḷuṭiya vāḻvi akam puṟam eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttu muṟi ceytu koṇṭavar paḻantamiḻar. akam eṉpatu taṉiyē iruntu vāḻkki naṭattutaṟku vēṇṭum uṭal vaḷarcciyum maṉa vaḷarcciyum oruṅku peṟṟa oruvaṉum oruttiyum oruvari yoruvar kaṇṭu kātaluṟavu koṇṭu uḷḷattāl oṉṟupaṭṭu maṇam ceytu koṇṭu maṉiyaṟam puriyum oḻukalāṟākum. akkālattē piṟakkum kātaluṟavu iṭiyīṭum iṭiyūṟum eytumiṭattuk kātalar mēṉikkaṇ tōṉṟum vāṭṭamum melivum kuṟittut teyva vaḻipāṭu naṭipeṟukiṟatu. kuṉṟavar naṭattum veṟiyāṭṭu itaṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟākum.
puṟavāḻvu eṉpatu maṉiyaṟam purintoḻukum makkaḷiṭiyē poruḷum pukaḻum pōr veṉṟiyum piṟavum paṟṟi nikaḻum ceyal vakikaḷum oḻukalāṟumākum. akavāḻvu maṉiyaṟattukku ākkam tantu immiyiṟ pukaḻum iṉpamum peṟuvataṟkuc camintāṟ pōlap puṟavāḻvu immikkuriya poruḷākiya celvamum kalviyum tantu maṟumiyil iṉpamum vīṭupēṟum eytutaṟku amintatu. akattuṟi iṉpam nukarntum puṟattuṟip pukaḻum veṉṟiyum peṟṟum uvarpput tōṉṟum mutumic cevviyil "ciṟantatu payiṟṟal" eṉṉum tuṟavu vāḻkkiyi mēṟkoḷvatu paṇṭit tamiḻar camaya neṟiyākum.
"	kāmam cāṉṟa kaṭikkōḷ kāli
 	ēmam cāṉṟa makkaḷōṭu tuvaṉṟi
 	aṟampuri cuṟṟamoṭu kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum
 	ciṟantatu payiṟṟal iṟantataṉ payaṉē"
eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟuvatik kāṇka. itaṉkaṇ ciṟantatu payiṟṟal eṉpatu piṟavāp peruvāḻ vākiya vīṭu pēṟṟukkuriya tuṟavu neṟiyi mēṟkoṇ ṭoḻukutal eṉpatu. iṟattal eṉpatu vayatu mutirtal. mutumiyiṉ payaṉākiya ciṟantatu payiṟṟaṟkuk kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum uriyar eṉpataṉāl tuṟavum vīṭupēṟṟukkakuriya meyyuṇarvu oḻukkaṅkaḷum peṇkaḷukkum uriyaṉa eṉpatu tamiḻar paḻamik koḷki. piṟkālattiltāṉ peṇkaḷ vīṭu pēṟṟukkaminta aṟivum oḻukkamum peṟutaṟku ākāreṉa vilakkappaṭṭaṉar. immiyil oruvaṉukku vāḻkkit tuṇiyākiya peṇ makaḷ avaṉ pukaḻum iṉpamum peṟutaṟkut tuṇiceytāṟpōla maṟumiyil iṉpamum vīṭu pēṟum eytutaṟkut tuṇi yātal iyalpum muṟiyumām eṉpatu karuttu,
poruḷ iṉpaṅkaḷiṉ cuvikāṇāta oruvaṉ tuṟavumēṟ kōṭal eṉpatu tamiḻar camayak koḷkikkup puṟampāṉatu. tuṟavōr iṉpamum tuṉpamumākiya iraṇṭiṉiyum oru cēra vuvarttu viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkupavarātaliṉ, viṉiccārpu paṟṟittōṉṟum teyva vaḻipāṭu avarkaṭkuk kiṭiyātu. avarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭap paṭuvatu kaṭavuḷākiya muḻumutaṟ poruḷ oṉṟēyām.
caṭimuṭittup palakālum nīrāṭit tōl uṭuttuk kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅka ilikaḷi uṇṭu, kāṭum kuṉṟumākiya iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻvatu tuṟavōr iyalpu. ivarkaḷ, ēṉiyōrpōla nalviṉi ceytu tuṟakka viṉpam peṟa viḻiyāmal, uṟṟa nōy poṟuttalum ēṉi evvuyirkkum ūṟuceyyāmalum oḻukum tavam mēṟkoṇṭu, meyyuṇarntu avā aṟuttu vāḻvar. ipperumakkaḷatu vāḻvu muḻutumē camayaneṟiyil iyaṅkuvatāl ivarkaṭkeṉat taṉi niliyil camaya vāḻvum vaḻipāṭum illi. ivarkaḷ urippaṉavum ceyvaṉavum ēṉi akavāḻvu puṟavāḻvukaḷik koṇṭoḻukum makkaḷukku ētuvum eṭuttuk kāṭṭum iyalpuṭiya aṟamumākum. ceyvaṉa ivi eṉac cilavaṟṟi vitittalum, tavir vaṉa ivi yeṉac cilavaṟṟi maṟuttalum ivarkaḷuṭiya uriyiyalpu; allaṉa maṟuttal, āvaṉa vitittaliṉum ciṟantu niṟṟaliṉ, avar urippaṉa maṟi eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ammaṟi moḻikaḷ maṉi vāḻvārkku aṟamum aṟivuriyumātaliṉ, avaṟṟi mantiram eṉṟalum marapu. niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa" eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. piṟar aṟiyā vakiyil maṟittuk kūṟuvatu vañcamoḻi eṉpati niṉiyāmal piṟkālattār ataṉi mantiram eṉṟum maṟimoḻiyeṉṟum tavaṟākak kūṟiviṭṭaṉar. maṟimoḻiyākiya mantiram kūṟaliṉ, ipperiyōrkaḷi maṟiyōr eṉṟum, aṟam kūṟutaliṉ aṟavōr eṉṟum, evvuyirkkum ūṟu niṉiyāmiyiṉ antaṇar eṉṟum paḻantamiḻar pārāṭṭi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. iṉpattiyum tuṉpattiyum ivaṟṟiṟku muṟiyē uriya nalviṉi tīviṉi kaḷiyum uvarttu veṟuttamiyiṉ muṉivar eṉṟum muṉivar eṉṟum moḻintaṉar. muṉivu, muṉivu eṉpaṉa veṟuppu eṉṉum poruḷ taruvaṉa.
im muṉivarkaḷiṉ maṉamum moḻiyum meyyum yāvumē kaṭavuṭperum poruḷōṭu kalantu pirippiṉṟip piṟaṅkum. ivarkaḷuṭiya neñcil nilavum kaṭavuḷ, "pālpuri piṟinutal polinta ceṉṉi, nīlamaṇi miṭaṟṟu"p perumpeyark kaṭavuḷ; avaruṭiya tiruvuruvil āṇum peṇṇumākiya irukūṟum kalantirukkum; ulakavuyirkaḷuḷ mikamika nuṭpamāṉavi āṇkūṟum peṇkūṟum oṉṟāyk kalantu viḷaṅkuvatu kaṇṭē iccāṉṟōr uyirkaṭkum ulakukkum oru mutalāka viḷaṅkum kaṭavuḷ, āṇum peṇṇumākiya irukūṟum oṉṟiya ōruruviṉi yuṭiyatu eṉṟaṉar. "peṇṇuru orutiṟaṉākiṉṟu" eṉṟum, "nīlamēṉi vāliḻi pākattu oruvaṉ" eṉṟum varuvaṉa kāṇka. muṟṟat tuṟanta muṉivararum muṭiyuṭiya vēntarum talicāyttu vaṇaṅkiyatu ik kaṭavuḷiyē yākum.
"	paṇīiyar attiniṉkuṭiyē muṉivar  	mukkaṭcelvar nakarvalam ceyaṟkē"
eṉṟu cāṉṟōr teruṭṭik kūṟuvatu iṅkē niṉivu kūrtaṟkuriyatu.
muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuṭkum maṇṇulaka vuyirkaṭkum iṭiyē nilavum teyvaṅkaḷ makkaḷuṭiya vaḻipāṭṭi ēṟṟu avarkaḷ nalam peṟat tuṇipuriyum eṉṟa karuttāl pakip poruḷi velvataṟkum tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum uyirkaḷik kāttaṟkum atteyvaṅ kaḷiṉ toṭarpu camaya vāḻvil ciṟantu niṉṟatu. makkaḷuyirkaḷil allaṉa kaṭintu nallaṉavē purintavi maṟumiyulakil teyvaṅkaḷāyiṉa. ātalāl itteyvaṅkaḷ iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaṭ kuriyavāyp pakiyuyirkaḷāl tuṉpuṟuttappaṭṭa varalāṟukaḷum, tuṉpa nīkkamum iṉpap pēṟum karutik kaṭavuṭ poruḷi vaḻipaṭṭa ceytikaḷum, paṇṭiyōrāl kuṟikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa; māṇpuṭiya makkaḷippōla itteyvaṅ kaḷum kaṭavuḷiṉ aruḷi vēṇṭiyiruttalāl itteyvaṅ kaṭku maṇṇulakattu vēntarum piṟa naṉmakkaḷum tuṇipurintu paki kaṭinta ceytikaḷ pala kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. icceytikaḷi innāḷil purāṇa varalāṟukaḷ eṉpatu vaḻakkam. makkaḷ vāḻkkiyip poruḷākak koṇṭu eḻunta paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil teyvaṅkaḷ cārpāṉa purāṇa varalāṟukaḷ pala vaḻaṅki vantuḷḷaṉa.
kuṟiñcinilat teyvamāṉa murukaṉippaṟṟiya varalāṟukaḷil ammurukaṉ imayamalic cuṉiyoṉṟil tāmarip pūvil piṟantāṉ eṉpati, "nivantōṅku imayattu nīlap piñcuṉip, payantōr eṉpa patumattup pāyal" eṉṟu paripāṭal mutaliya nūlkaḷ kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. kaṭaliṭattē yiruntu kuṟumpu ceytu uyirkaḷi varuttit tirinta cūravaṉmāviṉ kāval maramāṉa māmarattit taṭintu avaṉiyum koṉṟu murukaṉ mēṉmiyuṟṟāṉ eṉpati,
"	naḷiyirum parappiṉ mākkaṭal muṉṉi
 	aṇaṅkuṭi avuṇar ēmampuṇarkkum
 	cūruṭi muḻumutal taṭinta pēricik
 	kaṭuñciṉa viṟalvēḷ"
eṉṟu patiṟṟuppattum,
"	avuṇarnal valamaṭaṅkak kaviḻiṇar
 	māmutal taṭinta maṟuvil koṟṟattu
 	eyyā nallicic cevvēl cēey"
eṉṟu murukāṟṟup paṭiyum piṟavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. murukaṉukku maṉivi vaḷḷi; avaḷ kuṟavar kuṭiyil piṟanta kuṟak kōmakaḷ. "kuṟavarmaṭamakaḷ koṭi pōl nucuppiṉ, maṭavaral vaḷḷiyoṭu nakiyamarnta" murukaṉic ciṟappikkum cāṉṟōr, vaḷḷiyi, "murukupuṇarntiyaṉṟa vaḷḷi" eṉṟum, avaḷuṭiya peṟṟōri, "kuṟiñcik kuṉṟavar maṟaṅkeḻu vaḷḷi tamar" eṉṟum eṭutturippar.
mullinilat teyvamāṉa māyōṉik kaṇṇaṉ eṉpatum vaḻakku. avaṉ iḷiyaṉāy iruntapōtu mulli nilattu āyaruṭaṉ yamuṉik kariyil viḷiyāṭiya varalāṟu
"	vaṭāatu
 	vaṇpuṉal toḻuni vārmaṇal akaṉṟuṟi
 	aṇṭar makaḷir taṇṭaḻi uṭīiyar
 	marañcela mititta māal"
eṉṟum, kañcaṉ viṭuppak kutiri vaṭivilvanta acuraṉi veṉṟa ceytiyi,
"	kāyām pūṅkaṇṇip potuvaṉ takikaṇṭi
 	mēvār viṭuttanta kūntaṟkutiriyi
 	vāypakut tiṭṭup puṭitta ñāṉṟu iṉṉaṉkol
 	mayōṉ eṉṟu uṭkiṟṟu eṉneñcu"
eṉṟum caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟē maruta nilattil vittu urikkappaṭum teyvamāṉa intiraṉ, murukaṉ mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷukkup paṭiyutaviya paṇpum, akaliki poruṭṭu mēṉi muḻutum kaṇpeṟṟa katiyum paḻantamiḻariṭiyē nilaviyuḷḷaṉa. pālikkuriya teyvamāṉa koṟṟaviyi murukaṉukkut tāy eṉṟum, avaḷē ellāt teyvaṅkaṭkum paḻiyavaḷ eṉṟum paṇṭiyōr pakarukiṉṟaṉar. tārukaṉ uraṉkiḻitta taṟukaṇmiyiyum erumi vaṭivilvanta avuṇaṉi veṉṟa vīṟupāṭṭiyum avaḷuṭiya varalāṟu vaṟpuṟuttu kiṉṟatu. neytal nilattukku uriyaṉāka vurikkappaṭum varuṇaṉippaṟṟi oru ceytiyum paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil evarālum evviṭattum kūṟappaṭavilli.
ivvavaralāṟukaḷi viḻāk kālaṅkaḷila makkaṭkuk kāviyam kūṟiyum ōviyam kāṭṭiyum urippōr annāḷil iruntiruk kiṉṟaṉar. itaṉi,
"	intiraṉpūci ivaḷ akaliki; ivaṉ
 	ceṉṟa kavutamaṉ; ciṉaṉuṟak kalluru
 	oṉṟiyapaṭi yitu eṉṟu uriceyvōrum"
uḷar eṉap paripāṭal kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itteyvaṅkaṭkellām mēlāy, ulaku uyirkaḷiṉ, vēṟāy, oṉṟāy uṭaṉāy nilavum kaṭavuḷ niliyil, civaperumāṉi vittu, avaṉ vaṭivi,
	pālpuri piṟinutal polinta ceṉṉi
 	nīla maṇimiṭaṟṟu oruvaṉ
eṉṟum,
pērici naviram mēey uṟiyum
kāriyuṇṭik kaṭavuḷ
eṉṟum kūṟuvar. avaṉ mummatil eritta varalāṟṟip palaviṭaṅkaḷil paṉmuṟiyum paṇṭic cāṉṟōr pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar.
"	ōṅkumalip peruvil pāmpu ñāṇkoḷīi
 	orukaṇikoṇṭu mūveyil uṭaṟṟip
 	peruviṟal amararkku veṉṟitanta
 	kaṟimiṭaṟṟu aṇṇal"
eṉavum,
"	toṭaṅkaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiya mutiyavaṉ mutalāka
 	aṭaṅkātār miṭalcāya amarar vantirattaliṉ
	maṭaṅkalpōl ciṉii māyamcey avuṇarik
 	kaṭantaṭumuṉpoṭu mukkaṇṇāl mūveyilum
	uṭaṉṟāṉ"
eṉavum,
"	umiyamarntu viḷaṅkum imiyāmukkaṇ
 	mūveyil murukkiya muraṇmiku celvaṉ"
eṉavum paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟuvatu ivvuṇmiyi naṉku vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
paḻantamiḻaratu camaya vāḻvil kārttiki viḷakkīṭum, mārkaḻi ampā āṭalum, mācik kaṭalāṭṭum, paṅkuṉi uḷḷiviḻāvum piṟavum ciṟantaṉavākum. kārttiki viḷakkīṭu, iṉṟiya tīpāvaḷic ciṟappum poṅkaṟ putunāḷ ciṟappum ākiya iraṇṭiṉiyum taṉkaṇkoṇṭu muṉṉāḷil viḷaṅkiṟṟu. itaṉi,
"	maḻikāl nīṅkiya māka vicumpiṉ
 	kuṟumuyal maṟuniṟam kiḷara matiniṟintu
	aṟumīṉ cērum akaliruḷ naṭunāḷ
 	maṟukuviḷakkuṟuttu māli tūkkip
 	paḻaviṟal mūtūrp palaruṭaṉ tuvaṉṟiya
 	viḻavuṭaṉayara varukatil amma"
eṉṟu pārāṭṭik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ikkālattē putu maṇamakaḷir putunelkoṇṭu aval iṭittup pāṟ poṅkaliṭṭu makiḻvar. icceytiyi intap pāṭṭilē
"	putumaṇamakaṭūu ayiṉiya kaṭinakarp
 	palkōṭṭaṭuppiṟa pāl uli irīik
 	kūḻik kūntal kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir
 	peruñcey nelliṉ vāṅkukatir muṟittup
 	pācaval iṭikkum iruṅkāḻ ulakki"
eṉṟu nakkīrar navilkiṉṟār. mārkaḻit tiruvātiri nāḷil toṭaṅkum ampāvāṭal tittiṅkaḷ muḻumati nāḷaṉṟu tin nīrāṭṭeṉṟa peyarāl muṭivuṟukiṟatu. vicayanakara vēntar kālattil tōṉṟiya moḻiniliyuṇarvu vēṟupāṭṭāl kārttiki polivu kuṉṟiṟṟu; tīpāvaḷi putitu tōṉṟip pukaḻ peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. tin nīrāṭṭut talitaṭumāṟip pōyiṟṟu. vēṉil viḻā, kāmaṉ paṇṭiki yāyiṟṟu. ikkālattiltāṉ teyva niliyaṅkaḷil vaḻipāṭu vaṭamoḻiyil naṭipeṟu vatāyiṟṟu. ūrppeyarkaḷum teyvappeyarkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyil moḻi peyarntaṉa. maṉṉar makkaṭ peyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyāka māṟiṉa.
paḻantamiḻariṭiyē camaya vaḻipāṭṭil avaratu paḻamiyāṉa tamiḻ moḻiyē vaḻaṅki vantatu. teyvaṅkaṭkuk kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟi nakar eṉṟum tēvakulam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉar. perumaṉikaḷil teyva vaḻipāṭṭuk keṉa orupakuti otukkap paṭṭut tēvāram eṉṟa peyarāl ciṟappikkap peṟṟatu. mali
muṭiyum kaṭaṟkariyum teyvaṅkaṭkuriya kōyil eṭuttaṟkuc ciṟappuṭiya iṭaṅkaḷāyiṉa. naviramaliyiṉ muṭiyil mukkaṭ celvaṉukku irunta kōyili malipaṭukaṭām eṉṟa caṅka nūl tamiḻ nalam keḻuma urikkiṉṟatu. civaṉukku māttiram nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa kōyilkaḷ ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉpē tōṉṟiyiruntaṉa. ikkōyilkaḷ palavum annāḷil maṇṇālum marattālum kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. pallavar kālattiltāṉ kōyilkaḷik karuṅkaṟ kaḷāl amikkum muṟi urukkoṇṭu ciṟappatāyiṟṟu. ellāt teyvaṅkaṭkum mēlāya kaṭavuḷāyc civaṉukku eṭukkum kōyilkaḷil civaṉi naṭuvilvittu ēṉik kuṟiñci mutaliya tiṇi nilit teyvaṅkaḷip "parivāra tēvatikaḷ" eṉap piṟkālattē ōriṭattē vittuc camayavorumi kaṇṭaṉar.
iviyēyaṉṟi ūrtōṟum aṉparkaḷ kūṭip paravutaṟ poruṭṭut teyvaṅkaṭkuk kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟāka,
"	kāṭum kāvum kaviṉpeṟu turuttiyum
	yāṟum kuḷaṉum vēṟupala vippum
 	catukkamum cantiyum putuppūṅ kaṭampum
 	maṉṟamum potiyilum kantuṭi niliyiṉum"
teyva vaḻipāṭukaḷ ciṟappāka naṭipeṟṟaṉa. kantuṭi nili yeṉṟatu piṟkālattē civaliṅkam eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil kuṟikkappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. civavaṭivam kantum ataṉ pīṭam niliyumākum. ivvuṇmi kāṇāta maṭavōr kantiṉi āṇkuṟi yeṉṟum niliyiṉip peṇkuṟi yeṉṟum pitaṟṟiṉar. cilar ippitaṟṟuriyi aṟivuṭik kūṟṟākap piṟaḻa vuṇarntu poykkati puṉintu iliṅkapurāṇam eṉṟa peyarāl vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutip piṟanāṭṭa varum camayattavarum kaṇṭu eḷḷi ikaḻattakka iḻiniliyi uṇṭu paṇṇiṉar.
teyva vaḻipāṭṭil uyirp paliyum kuruti kalanta cōṟum paṇṭi nāḷil teyvaṅkaṭkup paṭippatuṇṭu. eṅkum viḷakku kaḷi ēṟṟit teyvaṅkaṭku nīrāṭṭip pūccūṭṭi maṇi iyakki naṟumpuki eṭuppar. akkāli palavēṟu vācciyaṅkaḷum muḻaṅkum. piṉpu palavēṟu niṟamuṭiya pūkkaḷit tūvi vaḻipāṭṭukkuriya teyvatti vaḻipaṭuvar. coriyappaṭum pūkkaḷip pūppali eṉṟum, cōṟu mutaliyavaṟṟi uṇpali eṉṟum urippar. murukaṉit toḻupavar, paliyiṭṭuk kiyāl toḻutu kāluṟa vaṇaṅki,
"	neṭumperuñ cimiyattu nīlap piñcuṉi
 	aivaruḷ oruvaṉ aṅki ēṟpa
 	aṟuvar payanta āṟamar celva,
 	ālkeḻu kaṭavuṭ putalva, mālvari
 	malimakaḷ makaṉē, māṟṟōr kūṟṟē,
 	veṟṟi velpōrk koṟṟavi ciṟuva
 	iḻiyaṇi ciṟappiṉ paḻiyōḷ kuḻavi,
 	vāṉōr vaṇaṅkuvil tāṉit taliva,
	māli mārpa, nūlaṟi pulava,
 	ceruvil oruva, poruviṟal maḷḷa,
 	antaṇar veṟukki, aṟintōr coṉmali,
 	maṅkiyar kaṇava, mintar ēṟē,
 	vēlkeḻu taṭakkic cālperuñ celva,
 	kuṉṟam koṉṟa kuṉṟāk koṟṟattu
 	viṇporu neṭuvarik kuṟiñcik kiḻava,
 	palarpukaḻ naṉmoḻip pulava rēṟē,
	arumpeṟal marapiṉ perumpeyar muruka,
	naciyunark kārttum icipē rāḷa,
	alantōrk kaḷikkum polampūṇ cēey,
	maṇṭamar kaṭantaniṉ veṉṟā ṭakalattup
	paricilart tāṅkum urukeḻu neṭuvēḷ,
	periyōr ēttum perumpeyar iyavuḷ,
	cūrmaruṅ kaṟutta moympiṉ matavali,
	pōrmiku poruna, kuricil"
eṉap palavāka varum pukaḻurikaḷic colli, muṭivil,
"	niṉ aḷantaṟital maṉṉuyirk karumiyiṉ 
 	niṉṉaṭi uḷḷi vantaṉeṉ, niṉṉoṭu
 	puriyunar illāp pulamiyōy"
eṉṟu kūṟi vaṇaṅkuvar. vēṟu cilar,
"	yāam irappavi
 	poruḷum poṉṉum pōkamumalla; niṉpāl
 	aruḷum aṉpum aṟaṉum mūṉṟum
 	uruḷiṇark kaṭampiṉ olitā rōyē"
eṉṟum, maṟṟum cilar,
"	yāmumem cuṟṟamum paravutum
 	ēma vikal peṟukayām eṉavē"
eṉṟum vaḻipaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
"colliya pāṭṭiṉ poruḷ uṇarntu colluka" eṉpatu tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭuri. iṉṟiya tamiḻar, tām aṟiyāta moḻiyil "muṟam pōṉṟa kātukaḷiyuṭi yavaṉē", "kuṭam pōṉṟa vayiṟṟi uṭiyavaṉē", "peṇṇiṉpattip peritum virumpu pavaṉē", "āṭamparatti viḻipavaṉē" eṉṟu āṇ teyvaṅ
kaḷiyum, "malipōṉṟa koṅkikaḷiyuṭiyavaḷē", "iṭiyaṟāta pōkatti virumpupavaḷē" eṉṟu peṇ teyvaṅkaḷiyum vaḻipāṭu eṉṟa peyarāl vacipāṭu kiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟiya vaḻipāṭṭu aruccaṉikaḷil "teyvatūṣaṇam" perukiyirukkiṟatēyaṉṟip pūcaṉiyum vaṇakkamum illi. "nāṉāvita mantira puṣpāṇi camarppiyāmi" eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷāl illātavaṟṟi ēṟṟip poy kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vaṭamoḻit toṭarkaḷik kēṭkum vaṭamoḻi yaṟiñarkaḷum maṉattil veṟuppum mukattil cuḷippum koṇṭu varuntukiṉṟaṉar. innāḷil makkaḷil cilar moḻi veṟi koṇṭu vaḻipāṭṭil nilavum ikkuṟṟattip pōkkāmal viṭāppiṭi koṇṭu teyvaṅkaḷiṉ tirumuṉ inta nērmiyaṟṟa muṟiyi mēṟkoṇṭiruppatu camaya vaḷarccikku ātaravu taruvatāka illi. vaṭamoḻi ākamaṅkaḷō tamiḻc camaya nūlkaḷō yātum ekkālattum evviṭattum vaṭamoḻiyiltāṉ vaḻipāṭu naṭattavēṇṭum eṉṟu vitittatē kiṭiyātu. vaṭamoḻi yāḷaruḷḷum aṟivuṭiyōr uṇṭu; ataṉāl, avarkaḷ ittakiya kīḻmiyāṉa ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭa māṭṭār kaḷallavā?
iṉṟiya camutāyam mikka virivāka māṟivarukiṟatu. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka iruntuvanta maṉṉaratu muṭiyāṭci maṟintatu; kuṭiyāṭci tōṉṟiviṭṭatu; makkaḷukkut teriyāta moḻiyil naṭanta nāṭṭaracu, makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum avar aṟinta moḻiyil naṭipeṟat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. kalvi, vāṇikam mutaliya aṉittum makkaḷ aṟinta moḻiyil naṭappatē nērmiyum muṟiyumām eṉṟa karuttu nilavukiṟatu. tamiḻakattu araciyal maṉṟam, potumakkaḷ kūṭumiṭam, palvaki aluvalakam eṅkum tamiḻē iṭam peṟṟu ilaṅkuvatu kāṇkiṟōm. ākavē, camaya niliyaṅkaḷāṉa kōyilkaḷil māttiram iṭikkālattil vantu pukunta vaṭamoḻi iruppāṉēṉ? atu nilavuvatāl tamiḻ makkaḷ maṉattil camaya uṇarvō camaya oḻukkamō oṉṟum uṇṭāka villi; ataṟku māṟākac camayattiṉkaṇ veṟuppum camaya nikaḻccikaḷil puṟakkaṇippum vaḷampeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.



19. tamiḻariṭiyē nilaviya 
itikāca purāṇaṅkaḷ
paḻantamiḻaruṭiya camaya vāḻvil teyvaṅkaḷip paṟṟi vaḻaṅkum varalāṟukaḷ pala iruppati nām aṟivōm. avaṟṟip piṟkālattār purāṇaṅkaḷ eṉṟu peyar kuṟittaṉar. appurāṇaṅ kaḷōṭu itikācak katikaḷum nilaviṉa. avi irāmāyaṇam pāratam eṉṟa iraṇṭumākum. ivviraṇṭiyum itikācam eṉpatu vaṭanūl vaḻakku. vaṭamoḻic camayanūl tokutiyil itikācaṅkaḷum purāṇaṅkaḷum cērntavi. ivi eppōtu tamiḻakattiṟ pukuntaṉa eṉpatu teḷivākat teriyavilli. vaṭamoḻiyāḷar toṭarpu uṇṭākiyapiṉ ivi tōṉṟiyiruk kalām eṉpatu potuvākak kūṟappaṭum ceyti. cintuveḷi nākarika kālattum ivvitikāca nikaḻccikaḷ nāṭṭil nilaviyiruntaṉa eṉac cila ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. aḵtu uṇmiyāyiṉ, ivi aiyāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpum nilaviya toṉmiyuṭiyavi eṉṟal nēritu.
irāmāyaṇam pāratam eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ irāmāyaṇatti muṟpaṭavittup pēcuvatu vaḻakkam. cila ārāycciyāḷar pāratamē muntiyatu eṉṟalum uṇṭu. eṉiṉum iviyiraṇṭum otta niliyil nam tamiḻakattil makkaḷiṭiyē paraviyirukkiṉṟaṉa. caṅkakālat tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē itikāca varalāṟukaḷ naṉku payiṉṟiruntamiyiṉ cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷil ivaṟṟiṉ nikaḻccik kūṟukaḷ pala uvamavāyilāka urikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ceruppāḻi eṟinta cōḻaṉ iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉiyi orukāl ūṉpoti pacuṅkuṭiyār eṉṉum cāṉṟōr pāṇar cuṟṟattuṭaṉ ceṉṟu kaṇṭu avaṉ pukaḻi iṉiya pāṭṭukkaḷāṟ pāṭiṉār. atukaṇṭu makiḻnta vēntaṉ pāṇ cuṟṟattiṉ vaṟumikku iraṅki mikka poruḷiyum poṉṉālākiya aṇivakikaḷiyum nirampa nalkiṉāṉ. avaṟṟi avarkaḷ ataṟkumuṉ kaṇṭatilli. iṉṉa aṇiyi ivviṭattē ivvāṟu aṇivatu eṉṟa muṟiyum avarkaṭkut teriyātu. ataṉāl, viralukkiṭuvatik kātilum, kātil aṇivatik kiviralilum, ariyiṟ kaṭṭuvatik kaḻuttilum, kaḻuttil pūṇpati ariyilum aṇintu makiḻntaṉar. avaratu ceyal kaṇṭatum pacuṅkuṭiyāratu pulamik kaṇṇil, kiṭkintiyil cīti kaḻaṟṟi yeṟinta aṇivakikaḷik kaṇṭeṭutta kurakkiṉam tām aṇintu makiḻntaṉa eṉṟa irāmāyaṇa nikaḻcci tōṉṟiṟṟu. avar uvaki mikuntu,
"	mēmpaṭu ciṟappiṉ aruṅkala veṟukki
 	tāṅkātu poḻitantōṉē; atukaṇṭu
 	ilampā ṭuḻanteṉ irumpēr okkal
 	viraṟceṟi marapiṉa cevit toṭakkunarum
 	cevitoṭar marapiṉa viraṟceṟikkunarum
 	arikkami marapiṉa miṭaṟṟiyākkunarum
 	miṭaṟṟami marapiṉa arikku yākkunarum"
eṉṟu naki tōṉṟak kūṟi, irāmāyaṇak katiyi,
"	kaṭunteṟal irāmaṉ uṭaṉpuṇar cītiyi
 	valittaki yarakkaṉ vavviya ñāṉṟi
 	nilañcēr mataraṇi kaṇṭa kuraṅkiṉ
 	cemmukap piṅkiḷi iḻippolin tāaṅku
 	aṟāa arunaki iṉitu peṟṟikumē"
eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷār.
maṇamākāmuṉ iḷiyōṉ oruvaṉukkum iḷamakaḷ oruttikkum maṇañ ceytukoḷḷaṟkuriya toṭar puṇṭāyiruntatu. ataṉi aṟinta ūrmakaḷir avarkaḷip paṟṟi alarkūṟat toṭaṅkiṉar. ciṉṉāṭkaḷil avviḷiyavaṉ cāṉṟōruṭaṉ avaḷ peṟṟōrik kaṇṭu maṇam pēci avaḷit taṉakku vāḻkkit tuṇiyākka vēṇṭumeṉa varintukoṇṭāṉ. ūravar eṭutta alar aṭaṅki yoṭuṅkiviṭṭatu; ataṉi avviḷamakaḷukku avaḷuṭiya tōḻi terivikkalāṉāḷ. avaḷ kūṟṟik kaṭuvaṉ maḷḷaṉār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr aḻakuṟap pāṭak karuti,
			               "tuṟivaṉ,
	nammoṭu puṇarnta kēṇmi, muṉṉē
	alarvāyp peṇṭir ampal tūṟṟap
	palarumāṅ kaṟintaṉar maṉṉē, iṉiyē
	vatuvi kūṭiya piṉṟi.....
	oliyavin taṉṟiv vaḻuṅkal ūrē"
eṉṟu pāṭuvārāyiṉar. muṉṉarp palarum aṟiya alar kūṟiya ūr, vatuvi kūṭiya piṉpu oliyavintatu eṉṟu kūṟak karutiya maḷḷaṉār uḷḷattil irāmāyaṇa nikaḻcci yoṉṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu. irāmaṉ pāṇṭināṭṭukkuriya kōṭik kariyil kurakkup paṭiyuṭaṉ pōntu ōr ālamarattiṉ kīḻē pācaṟiyiṭṭu iruntāṉ. akkālattē ammarattil palvakip paṟavikaḷum uyir vakikaḷum kūṭiyiruntu pēroli yiṭṭukkoṇṭiruntaṉa. ciṟitupōtil irāmaṉ taṉ tāṉit talivaruṭaṉ yātō oru ceytiyi maṟiyākap pēcaluṟṟāṉ. avaṉ pēccukku ālamarattu oli iṭiyūṟu ceytatu; ataṉāl avaṉ avaṟṟi oliyavintirukkumāṟu paṇittāṉ. aviyum oliyavintaṉa. ataṉi niṉinta tamiḻc cāṉṟōr,
"	veṉvēṟ kavuriyar toṉmutu kōṭi
 	muḻaṅkirum puvam iraṅkum muṉṟuṟi
 	velpōr irāmaṉ arumaṟikku avinta
 	palvīḻ ālam pōla
 	oliyavin taṉṟiv vaḻuṅkal ūrē"
eṉṟu eṭuttuk kūṟi iṉpuṟṟār.
orukāl irāvaṇaṉ nāṟṟiciyilum taṉ pukaḻip parappak karuti vaṭavimayap pakutiyil ceṉṟu koṇṭirun tāṉ. vaḻiyil kayilimali niṉṟu avaṉ cellum ūrtikkut taṭi ceytatu. ataṉāl, avaṉ ataṉip peyartteṭuttu oru puṟattē vikkak karuti ammaliyiṉ aṭiyik kuṭintu ataṟkuḷ taṉ taliyi nuḻittut tūkkiṉāṉ. maliyum acintatu. appōtu ammali
mēl civaperumāṉ taṉ maṉivi umiyammiyuṭaṉ vīṟṟirun tāṉ; tēvar palar cūḻa iruntaṉar. maliyacivatu uṇarnta avviṟivaṉ taṉ kāl kaṭṭi viralic ciṟitu ūṉṟiṉāṉ. maliyaḻuntiṟṟu; irāvaṇaṉ taṉ taliyi veḷiyē iḻuttuk koḷḷa muyaṉṟu māṭṭāmiyāl varunti alaṟat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. inta varalāṟu irāmāyaṇattuṭkaṇṭatu. itu caṅkakālattil tamiḻariṭiyē vaḻaṅki vantatu. oru malikkāṭṭil vēṅkimaram oṉṟu pūttu iṉiya kāṭci tantu niṉṟatu. ataṉik kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi oṉṟu cēymiyiṟ kaṇṭu vēṅkip puliyeṉṟu niṉintu maṉattē tiṭukkiṭṭu, maṟamikutiyāl ataṉik koṉṟu aḻikkak karuti neruṅki vantatu. vantatum ataṟku uṇmi pulaṉāyiṟṟu. āyiṉum, taṉakku accam tōṟṟuvittu alittamipaṟṟi ataṟku neñcilē neṭuñciṉamuṇṭāyiṟṟu. uṭaṉē avyāṉi vēṅkiyiṉ aṭippakutiyit taṉ kompiṉāṟ kuttik kīḻē taḷḷiviṭak karuti ataṉmēṟ pāyntatu; ataṉ maruppukkaḷ iraṇṭum marattiṉ aṭiyil naṉku putintaṉavē yoḻiya maram cāyavilli; mīṭṭum atu taṉ kompukaḷi vāṅkit tākkaṟku niṉintu avaṟṟi veḷiyē iḻuttatu; mikka vaṉmiyum parumiyum uṭiya vēṅkimaramātalāl ataṉ aṭiyiṟ putinta maruppukkaḷ veḷivaravilli. kaḷiṟṟiṉ vaṉmiyum matamum ciṟitum payaṉpaṭavilli. ataṉāl atu pēririccaliṭṭup piḷiṟip pulampuvatāyiṟṟu. atu kaṇṭu viyanta kuṟiñcip pulavarāṉa kapilar, ataṉik kalippāṭṭoṉṟil vittup pāṭak karutiṉār. anniliyil avaratu pulamiyuḷḷam irāvaṇaṉ kayilimali yaṭiyil cikkuṇṭu varuntiya ceyali niṉintatu. avaṉ ceykiyi uvamamāka niṟutti,
"	imiyavil vāṅkiya īrñcaṭi yantaṇaṉ
 	umiyamarn tuyarmali iruntaṉa ṉāka
 	aiyiru taliyiṉ arakkar kōmāṉ
 	toṭippoli taṭakkiyiṉ kīḻpukut tammali
 	eṭukkal cellātu uḻappavaṉ pōla
 	uṟupuli yuruvēyppap pūtta vēṅkiyik
 	kaṟuvukoṇṭu ataṉmutal kuttiya matayāṉi
 	nīṭiru viṭarakam cilampak kūyttaṉ
 	kōṭupuyk kallātu uḻakkum"
eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷār.
iṅkē maṟṟoru kuṟippum niṉivukūrtal vēṇṭum. paṇṭit tamiḻar irāvaṇaṉ pattuttaliyum irupatu kikaḷu muṭiya arakkaṉ eṉṟum, kiṭkintiyil kuraṅkukaḷē vāḻntaṉa eṉṟum karutiṉami tuṇiyappaṭum. avaṉ teṉṉāṭṭukkuriyavaṉ eṉṟum, tamiḻiṉat tavaṉeṉṟum avarkaḷ niṉittatilli. irāmaṉ vaṭanāṭṭavaṉeṉavum irāmarāvaṇap pōr vaṭavarukkum teṉṉāṭṭavarukkum naṭanta pōr eṉavum paḻantamiḻar karutavē yilli. āṅkila aṭciyil putitu tōṉṟiya ārāycci aṟivil kāl vitta aṟiñar cilar, mutirāta tamatu ārāycciyaṟivāl ivvāṟu poruḷkūṟi vaṭavaruḷḷat tilum teṉṉāṭṭavar maṉattilum vēṟṟumik karuttukkaḷi vittiviṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl vaṭapulattār irāvaṇaṉ mutaliyōr uruvaṅkaḷic camittu erittu viḻāk koṇṭāṭumukattāl tamatu veṟuppit tōṟṟu vippārāyiṉar. atukaṇṭa teṉṉāṭṭavar irāmāyaṇak kati eḻutiya nūlik koḷuttavum irāmāyaṇattik kūṟiya pulavarkaḷi ikaḻntu veṟukkavum talippaṭṭaṉar. aṇmiyil tōṉṟiya maṭamiyuriyoṉṟu nam nāṭṭil vaṭa pulattukkum teṉ pulattukkum māṟupāṭu viḷivittu araciyal poruḷiyal vāṇikam mutaliya tuṟikaḷil oṟṟumi nalattic citittuc cīraḻittuviṭṭatu! nam matippukkuriya viṉōpāpāvē avarkaḷ itupaṟṟi yuritta aṟivuri nāṭṭil naṉku paravutal vēṇṭum; atutāṉ vēṟṟumi nōykku maruntu.
irāmāyaṇampōlap pāratak katiyum caṅka kālat tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē paraviyiruntirukkiṟatu. pāṇṭavar irunta arakku māḷiki tīppaṭṭapōtu vīmaṉ ēṉi yōri muṉṉaṟintu kāttatum, taṉ tantiyākiya turōṇaṉik koṉṟāṉi acuvattāmaṉ iraviṟ ceṉṟu uyir kavarntatum kalittokiyil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mulli nilattu ūroṉṟil kollēṟu taḻuvum viḻā naṭipeṟukiṟatu; āyar viḻāmaṉṟattil kollēṟu
kaḷi niṟuttip pōrkku viṭukiṉṟaṉar; kaṭṭiḷaṅkāḷiyar palar avaṟṟip pāyntu piṭikkiṉṟaṉar; pala erutukaḷ avarkaḷ piṭikku akappaṭātu etirttup porutu avarkaḷiṟ cilari māykkiṉṟaṉa. veṇṇiṟa eruttoṉṟit taḻuvip piṭippataṟ kākap pāynta oruvaṉi atu taṉ kūriya kompāṟ kuttik koli purikiṉṟatu. ataṉik kāṇpavar kūṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭu pāṭaluṟṟa nallurut tiraṉār, taṉ tanti turōṇaṉik koṉṟa tiṭṭattuymaṉi acuvattāmaṉ iraviṟ koṉṟati niṉintu,
"	cevimaṟi nērmiṉṉu nuṇpoṟi veḷḷik
 	kataṉañcāṉ pāynta potuvaṉic cāṭi
 	nutinuṉik kōṭṭāṟ kulippataṉ tōṟṟamkāṇ:
 	āriruḷ eṉṉāṉ aruṅkaṅkul vantutaṉ
 	tāḷiṟ kaṭantaṭṭut tantiyik koṉṟāṉit
 	tōḷil tirukuvāṉ pōṉm"
eṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟār. arakku māḷiki tīppaṭṭapōtu, tarumaṉ mutaliya taṉ uṭaṉpiṟantōrkaḷi vīmaṉ kātta varalāṟu, kāṭoṉṟil tī yeḻuntu aṅkē mēyntu koṇṭirunta yāṉikaḷi vaḷittuk koḷḷa avaṟṟuḷ valimikka vēḻamoṉṟu tīyaḻaliṟ pukuntu vaḻiyuṇṭākkit taṉ iṉamāṉa piṟa vēḻaṅkaḷik kāppatu kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟup pāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkōviṉ pāṭṭil,
"	vayakkuṟumaṇṭalam vaṭamoḻip peyarpeṟṟa
 	mukattavaṉ makkaḷuḷ mutiyavaṉ puṇarppiṉāl
 	aivareṉṟu ulakēttum aracarkaḷ akattarāk
 	kipuṉi arakkillik kataḻeri cūḻntāṅkuk
 	kaḷitikaḻ kaṭāatta kaṭuṅkaḷiṟu akattavā
 	muḷikaḻi uyarmali muṟṟiya muḻaṅkaḻal
	 oḷḷuru arakkilli vaḷimakaṉ uṭittuttaṉ
 	uḷḷattuk kiḷikaḷōṭu uyappō kuvāṉpōla
 	eḻuvuṟaḻtaṭakkiyiṉ iṉaṅkākkumeḻilvēḻam
 	aḻuvañcūḻ pukiyaḻal atarpaṭa mitittuttam
 	kuḻuvoṭu puṇarntu pōm"
eṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.
orupāl maṟintirunta vayappuli yoṉṟu valimikka kaḷiṟṟiṉmēṟ pāyntatāka ataṉi avyāṉi taṉ kūriya kompiṉāṟ kutti veṟṟiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟa kāṭciyoṉṟu kapilar karuttik kavarntukoṇṭatu. ataṉip pāṭṭiṭi vittup pārāṭṭat toṭaṅkiya pōtu avar maṉattil pāratap pōril pulipōṟ poruta turiyōtaṉaṉ tuṭiyaṟupaṭṭu iṟanta tiṟam tōṉṟavē, ataṉi uvamavāyilākak kuṟippārāy,
"	muṟañcevi maṟippāypu muraṇceyta puliceṟṟu
 	maṟamtalik koṇṭa nūṟṟuvar talivaṉik
 	kuṟaṅkaṟuttiṭuvāṉpōla kūrnuti maṭuttu ataṉ
 	niṟañcāṭi muraṇtīrnta nīṇmarup peḻilyāṉi"
eṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟār. ivvāṟē aruccuṉaṉ kāṇṭava vaṉatti eritta ceytiyik,
"	kāveri yūṭṭiya kavarkaṇit tūṇip
 	pūviri kaccip pukaḻōṉ"
eṉṟu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi ciṟappikkiṟatu. ivviraṇṭu itikācaṅkaḷilum aṭaṅkiya ceytikaḷ cila puṟanāṉūṟu patiṟṟuppattu mutaliya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṉṟu nām peṟṟuḷḷa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil cumār 2400 pāṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa; āyiṉum nām itukāṟum kaṇṭu pōnta purāṇa itikācak kuṟippukkaḷ aṭaṅkiya pāṭṭukkaḷ oru muppatu muppattintukkumēl illi. itaṉāl caṅkakāla ilakkiyac cūḻalil ivvaralāṟukaḷ attuṇic ciṟanta iṭattip peṟavilli eṉpatu tēṟṟam. māvum puḷḷum mākkaḷumākiya uyirpporuḷ nikaḻccikaḷē caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil peritum niṟin tuḷḷaṉa. nūṟṟukku oṉṟu iraṇṭāy arumpit tōṉṟiya ikkuṟippukkaḷ cilappatikāram maṇimēkali eṉṟa peruṅkāppiyaṅ
kaḷil ciṟutu perukit tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. cilappatikāratti nōkka maṇimēkali kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭatē; āyiṉum atu peritum putta camayac cūḻniliyilē tōṉṟi nilaviya kāraṇattāl purāṇa itikācak katikaḷ mikutiyum payilum tiṟampeṟā toḻintatu. eṉṟālum, vāṇaṉuṭiya nakarattil kaṇṇaṉ taṉ makaṉ poruṭṭup pēṭik kōlamkoṇṭu kūttāṭiyatum, intiraṉ akaliki kāraṇamāka āyiraṅkaṇ peṟṟatum, akkiṉitteyvam kāmavēṭki koṇṭu alintatum, tirumāl irāmaṉākat tōṉṟi mayaṅkiyatum, avar pattu avatāram koṇṭatum, vāmaṉāvatāra nikaḻcciyum ākiya ivikaḷē maṇimēkaliyil ciṟuciṟu kuṟippukaḷākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ vāḻnta cēranāṭē ēṉic cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷiviṭa vaṭapulattup purāṇa itikācak katikaḷi mutaṟkaṇ mēṟkoṇṭa teṉpatu varalāṟu kāṭṭum uṇmi. mēlum, cēra nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyavaṉa āciriyarkaḷ kālattil āriyakam (Ariake) eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭukiṟateṉa mākkiriṇṭil mutaliya mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ urittuḷḷaṉar. itaṉālum, purāṇa itikācak katikaḷ peritum payiṟaṟkuc cēranāṭu mikka vāyppuṭiya tātal valiyuṟukiṉṟatu. ataṉāl aṭikaḷ pāṭiya cilappatikāram ikkatikaḷi mikutiyākak kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
caṅka kālattil nilaviya katikaḷōṭu civaṉum koṟṟaviyum murukaṉum kaṇṇaṉum pala vēṟu kūttukkaḷi āṭiyatum, civaṉ kaṇṇi umiyammi maṟittatum, koṟṟavi tārukaṉ uram kiḻittatum, avaḷ makiṭācuraṉik koṉṟatum, intiraṉ malikaḷiṉ ciṟaki arintatum, intiraṉ makaṉāṉa cayantaṉ akattiyaṉuṭiya cāpam peṟṟatum, cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷ intiraṉukkut tuṇipurintu aivaki maṉṟaṅkaḷik koṇarnta ceytiyum, intiraṉōṭu pōr ceytu pāṇṭiyar avaṉatu ārampeṟṟatum, avaṉ muṭimēl avarkaḷ vaḷi eṟintatum, intiraṉ avikkaḷattu nāṭakamakaḷiruḷ oruttiyākiya uruppaci cāpam peṟṟatum, cantiraṉ tirumāl mārpil piṟanta katiyum, tirumāl pāṟkaṭalil araviṉmēl kiṭantu aṟituyil koṇṭatum, avaṉ naraciṅkavuruk koṇṭa katiyum piṟavum iḷaṅkō kuṟikkum purāṇak katikaḷākum. itikācamākiya irāmāyaṇak katikaḷōṭu pāratattiṟ kāṇappaṭum naḷaṉ katiyuṭaṉ kaṇṇaṉ vāṉattu ñāyiṟṟit taṉ āḻiyāl maṟitta katiyum pāṇṭavar poruṭṭuk kaṇṇaṉ nūṟṟuvarpāl tūtupōṉatum, kaṇṇaṉ yāṉiyik koṉṟatum iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. tēvarkaḷ amutam vēṇṭip pāṟkaṭalik kaṭintatum, tēvarkaḷum acurarkaḷum patiṉeṭṭāṇṭukaḷ pōr ceytatum, cōḻaruḷ tūṅkeyil eṟinta cōḻaṉ maṉuccōḻaṉ mutaliyōr varalāṟum, pāṇṭiyaruḷ oruvaṉ taṉ ki kuṟittukkoṇṭa katiyum, kaṭalmēl vēl eṟintatum mēkattip piṇittatumākiya purāṇak katikaḷ perukiyuḷḷaṉa.
cilampiṉ kālattukkuppiṉ kaḷappirar eṉṉum nāṭōṭikaḷ kūṭṭamoṉṟu vaṭapulattiṉiṉṟum pōntu tamiḻakattic cīraḻittatu; tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pala aḻintaṉa. tamiḻar araciyaṟ ciṟappum vāḻkki muṟimiyum nilikulintaṉa. vaṭamoḻikku nilipēṟu tēṭum muṟiyil tamiḻarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟu ilakkiyaṅ
kaḷum ici nāṭaka nūlkaḷum araciyal poruḷiyal araṇ amippiyal paṭiyiyal camayaviyal mutaliya palavaki iyal nūlkaḷum aḻiyavum aḻikkavum peṟṟaṉa. annāḷil ulakil palavēṟu nāṭukaḷil irunta aracarkaḷ pakivar nāṭṭiṟkuḷ pukuntāl annāṭṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum nūl vakikaḷiyum moḻiyiyum aḻippatiyē mutaṟ paṇiyāka mēṟ koṇṭirunta ceyalip potuvāka ulaka varalāṟum ciṟappākac cīṉam, cappāṉ mutaliya nāṭṭu varalāṟum veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. kaḷappirar avviyal piṉarākalāṉ, avarkaḷ mēlē kūṟiya aḻivu vēlikaḷic cemmiyākac ceytaṉar. tamiḻar “nāḷum putuvōr mēvalar” eṉappaṭupavarātalāl, mika virivil tammuṭiya ilakkiyac celvattiyum moḻi nalattiyum kiviṭṭup putuvōr koṇarnta moḻikku ākkamum araṇum ceyyalāyiṉar. ataṉāl, annāḷil, vitikam, camaṇam, puttam mutaliya camayaṅkaḷum vaṭanūṟ cātip piriviṉik koḷkikaḷum pukuntu tamiḻar camaya camutāya nilikaḷiṉ orumip paṇpāṭṭi uruvaḻittaṉa.
caṅkakālattē vaṭapulattu vaṭamoḻi vitikak karuttukkaḷ cilavum tamiḻakattiṟ pukuntu tamiḻariṭiyē ōraḷavu iṭam peṟṟirun taṉavāyiṉum piṟkālattiṟ pōlat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum tamiḻar kali nalaṅkaḷiyum viḻuṅki ēppamiṭum koṭumiyic ceyyavilli; kaḷappirariṉ varavum koṭumiyum tamiḻ vāḻvil iruḷceytami tamiḻ vīḻccikkup peruntuṇiyāyiṟṟu. kaḷappirar koṭumiyiruḷ piṉ vanta pāṇṭi maṉṉarālum pallavarkaḷālum araciyaliṉiṉṟu oruvāṟu nīṅkiyatu. eṉiṉum tamiḻaratu paṇṭiya kuṟiñci mulli mutaliya tiṇinili vāḻvu citintoḻintatu. murukaṉ māyōṉ mutaliya teyvaṅkaṭkuriya vaḻipāṭukaḷ ciṟappiḻantaṉa. antō, tamiḻakamē! eṉṟu tamiḻaṟivu araṟṟukiṉṟatu. civa vaḻipāṭum tirumāl vaḻipāṭum talitūkki niṟka muṟpaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭil cilapala iṭaṅkaḷil civaṉ kōyilkaḷum tirumāl kōyilkaḷum ciṟappuṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷḷum civa vaḻipāṭṭukkup piṉṉarē tirumāl vaḻipāṭu celvākkup peṟṟamiyiṉ, tirumāl kōyilkaḷiṉ tokiyiṉum civaṉ kōyilkaḷiṉ toki mikuntiruntatu. pallavar kālattil tirunāvukkaracar tiruñāṉa campantar mutaliyōrum tirumaṅkiyāḻvār nammāḻvār mutaliyōrum āṅkāṅkuḷḷa civaṉ kōyilkaṭkum tirumāl kōyilkaṭkum ceṉṟu tammuṭiya centamiḻp pākkaḷāl ciṟappittuk kaḷappiraviruḷāl kuṉṟiya taṅkaḷ camaya voḷiyi viḷaṅkac ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ appōtu tammiṭiyē ātaravu peṟṟirunta vitika neṟiyit taḻuvikkoṇṭu tamatu paṇiyi āṟṟivantaṉar. ataṉāl pallavarum pāṇṭiyaru mākiya vēntar cilar civarāyiṉar. putiyavāyp pukuntu araciyal ātaravu peṟṟirunta camaṇamum puttamum valikuṉṟiṉa; camaṇam celvāk kiḻantatu; puttam kaṭal kaṭantu ceṉṟuviṭṭatu. kalivaḷamum poruḷ vaḷamum aṟivu vaḷamum oḻukka vaḷamum niṟinta tamiḻp perunūlkaḷi iḻanta tamiḻarkaṭku im maṟumalarcciyāl civa neṟiyil tēvāramum tirumāl neṟiyil nālāyirap pirapantamum tōṉṟic camayavaraṇum aṟivuk karuvūlamumāyiṉa.
nālāyirappirapantam tōṉṟiya kālattil camayac cūḻniliyil irāmāyaṇamum pāratamum kaṇṇaṉ varalāṟu kūṟum pākavata purāṇamum ciṟanta iṭam peṟṟiruntaṉa. pirapanta muḻutum itikācak katikaḷum pākavatac ceytikaḷum nirampi, iyaṟkic cūḻniliyik kāṭṭum kāṭcikaḷ pōtiya iṭampeṟāvaṇṇam celvākku mikuntaṉa. tēvārat tirumuṟikaḷ caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum piṉvanta cilappatikāram mutaliya vaṟṟilum arumpi niṉṟa purāṇa itikācak katikaḷi mikutiyāka mēṟkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṟkē mutaliṭam tantu ciṟappittaṉa. itaṉāl tēvāra kālattut tamiḻc camutāyam purāṇa itikācak katikaḷil potuvākap peruvēṭkiyum civaṉippaṟṟik kūṟum purāṇaṅkaḷik kēṭpatil mikka perumpaṟṟum koṇṭiruntami terikiṟatu. tēvar poruṭṭuc civap puttēḷ nañcuṇṭatum puramerittatu mākiya paḻañ ceytikaḷuṭaṉ takkaṉ vēḷvi ceṟṟatu, tārukavaṉattu muṉimakaḷiric cinti kalakkiyatu, mārkkaṇṭaṉporuṭṭuk kālaṉi vatittatu, civaṉ kaṇapatiyip peṟṟatu, tirumālukkuc cakkarappaṭi aḷittatu, mālum piramaṉum kāṇāvakiyil civaṉ aḻaluruvāy uyarntu niṉṟatu, caṉakar mutaliya nālvarkkum aṟamurittatu, vicāracarumarkkuc caṇṭīca patam tantatu, muṉimakaṉukkup pāṟkaṭal īntatu mutaliya pala purāṇap putucceytikaḷ ilakkiyavulakil cīrtta iṭampeṟṟaṉa. tiruvācakam mutaliyaṉa tōṉṟiyapōtu, naripariyākkiyatu, piṭṭukku maṇ cumantatu, pāṇṭiyaṉāl civaṉ aṭiyuṇṭatu mutaliya putu varalāṟukaḷ purāṇac cūḻalil pukuntaṉa. ivvāṟṟāl tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē paḻiya caṅkakāla iyaṟkik kāṭciyiṉpamum āḻnta ilakkiya nōkkamum uḷḷataṉ uṇmi niliyi uḷḷavāṟu kāṇum maṉavuraṉum oḷikuṉṟit tēyntaṉa. poyyum puṉivum niṟinta poruntāk kaṟpaṉikaḷum putuppaṭippukkaḷum niṟinta ceytikaḷik kēṭpatil makkaḷ uḷḷam ōṭat talippaṭṭatu.
pallavar kālattil tōṉṟiya civanāyaṉmārkaḷum viṇava āḻvārkaḷum ceyta camayappaṇiyāl nāṭṭil eḻunta kiḷarcciyiṉ payaṉākap puttamum camaṇamum camaḻttup pōyiṉa. āyiṉum tamiḻakattil āṅkāṅkirunta ciṉa caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ muyaṟciyāl camaṇamuṉivarkaḷ tamiḻilakkiyat tuṟiyil paṇi puriyat toṭaṅkic ciṟukāppiyaṅkaḷum peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum eḻutiṉar. pallavar āṭcikkuppiṉ cōḻa pāṇṭiyaratu āṭci tamiḻnāṭu muḻutum ceḻittu niṉṟatu. avarkālattē camaṇ cāṉṟōratu kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ cīvaka cintāmaṇi cūḷāmaṇi peruṅkati eṉṟa perunūlkaḷ ilakkiyac cūḻalil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa. annāḷiya makkaḷiṭiyē civa viṇava camayap purāṇak katikaḷ pērātaravu koṇṭirun taṉavaṉṟō? avvaralāṟukaḷ pala kōyilkaḷil viḻākkaḷ vāyilākavum tirunāṭkaḷ vāyilākavum paravi vantaṉa. purāṇi karuṭiya celvākkum ciṟantu niṉṟatu. ataṉāl, camaṇ cāṉṟōr ceyta kāppiyaṅkaḷiṭiyē intap purāṇa varalāṟukaḷ puka vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmi piṟantatu.
ituniṟka, civaviṇavac cāṉṟōrkaḷ pāṭiya tēvārat tirumuṟikaḷum nālāyirap pirapantamum kaṭavuṭporuḷiṉ muḻumutalām taṉmiyi vaṟpuṟuttiṉa. uyirkaḷ ceyta viṉik kēṟpa uḷavākum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷi avitāmē nukarntu kaḻikkavēṇṭumē yaṉṟi, viṉit toṭakkiṉiṉṟum kaṭavuḷālum avaṟṟi viṭutali ceyya muṭiyātu eṉṟa karutti vitikarāṉa mīmāñcakarum camaṇarum puttarum vaṟpuṟuttivantaṉar. avari maṟuttu muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷ ellām vallavarātalāl avar aruḷ peṟṟōri viṉittoṭakkut taṭi ceyyātu; kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭuvōr viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷakkam peṟuvar eṉṟa caṅkakālat toṉmit tamiḻk karutti eṭuttōti naṉmakkaṭku aṉpu neṟiyil ūkkamuṇṭākkiṉar. iṟivaṉ poruḷcēr pukaḻpurinta naṉmakkaḷi iruviṉiyum cērā eṉṟum, iṟivaṉatu aruṭcārpi yuṇarntu viṉic cārpu keṭa oḻukuvāri, viṉiyāl viḷiyum nōykaḷ cārā eṉṟum tiruvaḷḷuvar mutaliyōr caṅkakālat tamiḻ makkaṭku aṟivuṟuttiyuḷḷami ulakaṟinta ceyti. ataṉāl, viṉi nīkkattukkut tuṇiceyyum teyvaṅkaṭkum atteyvaṅ kaṭku appāl uyarntu oru taṉimutalāka uḷḷa kaṭavuṭkum kōyileṭuttu vaḻipāṭu ceyvatum viḻā ayartalum makkaḷiṭiyē perukiyiruntaṉa. viṉiyi velvateṉpatu muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuṭkum ākāta oṉṟu eṉṟu karutiṉōruḷḷattē makkaḷuṭiya icceyalkaḷ maṉap puḻukkatti viḷivittaṉa. avaruṭ cilar itteyvaṅkaḷ pāl makkaṭkuḷḷa naṉmatippik kuṟittal vēṇṭip pala poyyum vaḻuvum pukuttip purāṇaṅkaḷ pala eḻutuvārāyiṉar. ippurāṇak kēḷviyāl meymmi poymmikaḷit tērntaṟiyum cemminili makkaḷiṭiyē kuṉṟiṉamiyiṉ purāṇa itikācak katikaḷip pukuttāta enta nūlum celvākkup peṟātoḻintatu.
iccūḻniliyil tōṉṟiya nūlkaḷuḷ cīvaka cintāmaṇi taliyāyātākum. civaṉ muppuram erittatum tirumāl ampāṉatum tēvarum acurarum kaṭal kaṭintatum mutaliya paḻañ ceytikaḷōṭu civaṉ maliyariyaṉ makaḷi maṇantatum, kāmaṉik kāyntatum, irāmaṉ marāmaram eytatum kaṇṇaṉ pāratappōril caṅkōci eḻuppiyatum putiyavāyp pukuntaṉa. kaṇṇaṉ palatēvaṉ ākiya iruvarippaṟṟik kūṟum purāṇac ceytiyoppac cūḷāmaṇi yeṉṉum nūl vicaya tiviṭṭar varalāṟukaḷi eṭutturittu iṭiyiṭiyē paḻiya purāṇa varalāṟukaḷ cilavaṟṟi urittu niṉṟatu. peruṅkatiyil, koṅku vēḷir, pattirāpati yeṉṉum teyvamakaḷ yāṉiyākap piṟantatum, intiraṉ avuṇarkaḷi veṉṟatum, tiṅkaḷañ celvaṉ urōkaṇiyi virumpiyatum, cīti nilattiṟ pukuntatum, kaṇṇaṉ apimaṉṉuvāviṉuṭiya viṉṉāṇi yaṟuttatum, vīmaṉ kuravar nūṟṟuvarik koṉṟatum piṟavum putiyavāyp puṇarkkappaṭṭaṉa. ikkati kaṭkiṭiyē evarum maṟantēyum caṅkakāla maṉṉar varalāṟukaḷiyō makkaḷiṭiyē nikaḻnta ceyal vakikaḷiyō uvama vāyilākavum kūṟiyatu kiṭiyātu. itikāca purāṇak katikaḷāl tamiḻ ilakkiyam oruvāṟu vaḷarcci peṟṟateṉiṉum, tamiḻaratu paṇṭi varalāṟum oḻukalāṟum nākarikak kūṟukaḷum mīḷat taliyeṭāvāṟu putikkap paṭṭaṉa. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ niṉivum collum ceyalum ellām ippurāṇa itikācaṅkaḷ nalkiya aṟivuriyāl tirintu vēṟupaṭṭamiyiṉ piṟkālattil tōṉṟiya pulavar perumakkaḷ attuṟiyil karuttilarāyiṉar.
nīr cellum vaḻiyē pul cāyvatupōlap piṉṉarc civavulakil oṭṭakkūttaratu takkayākap paraṇiyum tiruñāṉa campantar varalāṟum, cēkkiḻāratu tiruttoṇṭar purāṇamum tōṉṟip purāṇac cūḻnilikku ātaravu ceytaṉa. inniliyiṟ paḻam purāṇac ceytikaḷ cīrtta iṭam peṟak kaṇṭatum, ilakkiyap pulavarkaḷuḷ ciṟantu niṉṟa ātittaṉ kampaṉ eṉpār itikāca vakiyāṉa irāmāyaṇattit taṉiyākak koṇṭu tamatu pulami nalamellām ciṟakkak kamparāmāyaṇatti eḻutiṉār. piṉ vanta cāṉṟōrāṉa kacciyappar murukaṉ varalāṟṟi varintukoṇṭu kanta purāṇat tiyum, kōṉēriyappar mutaliyōr upatēca kāṇṭattiyum, oppilāmaṇitēcikar mutalāyiṉōr civarakaciyam mutaliya nūlkaḷiyum taṉittaṉiyē pallāyiram pākkaḷil pāritturittaṉar.
civaṉuṭiya tirukkōyilkaḷiyum āṅkāṅku vāḻnta periyōrkaḷiyum paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ cila civat tirumuṟikaḷil kuṟippākac civa nāyaṉmārkaḷāl kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. elli yillāta pala purāṇac ceytikaḷ vaṭamoḻikkaṇ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu koṇṭu avaṟṟit tamiḻppaṭuttiṉatākat talapurāṇaṅkaḷ pala piṟkālattē eḻuntaṉa. āṅkāṅkirunta civa maṭaṅkaḷil vāḻnta cāṉṟōr cilarum talapurāṇaṅkaḷ eḻutiṉar; pāṇṭi nāṭṭut tirupputtūrilirunta civa maṭattuc cāṉṟōr oruvar ōṅku kōyil purāṇam pāṭiṉar eṉa avvūrk kalveṭṭu oṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. talapurāṇaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, vaḷam peṟṟu ōṅkiya kālam cōḻa pāṇṭiyarāṭci vīḻcciyuṟa, vicayanakara vēntaratu āṭci ciṟappuṟṟa kālamākum.
ivvāṟu tōṉṟiyuḷḷa talapurāṇaṅkaḷuḷ civañāṉa muṉivar pāṭiya kāñcip purāṇamum kacciyappa muṉivar pāṭiya taṇikip purāṇamum, parañcōti muṉivar pāṭiya tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇamum, nirampa aḻakiya tēcikar pāṭiya cētuppurāṇamum, civa ellappa nāvalar pāṭiya cevvanti purāṇamum, tirikūṭarācappak kavirāyar pāṭiya kuṟṟālat talapurāṇamum ciṟantu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. talapurāṇattiṉ vaḷarcci ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiya tiruvāvaṭutuṟi yātīṉa makā vittuvāṉ mīṉāṭci cuntaram piḷḷi yavarkaḷāl uyarnili yaṭintatu. avarkaḷāl ceyyap paṭṭa talapurāṇaṅkaḷ pala.
it talapurāṇaṅkaḷ piṟkālattē paḻiyaṉa cilavum putiyaṉa palavum koṇṭu iyaṟki aṟivu varampik kaṭantu cellat talippaṭṭaṉa. kīḻmakkaḷ ceyaliṉum mikak kīḻppaṭṭa ceyal kaḷit teyvaṅkaḷiṉmēl ēṟṟi yurippatum ivaṟṟiṉ ceyal kaḷāyiṉa. teṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭu ūrkaḷippaṟṟiya varalāṟukaḷ vaṭa nāṭṭu vaṭamoḻikkaṇ tōṉṟutaṟku iṭamētu eṉṟukūṭa ivikaḷ karutā toḻintaṉa. ikkāla viññāṉam araciyal poruḷiyal mutaliya nūlaṟivāl kāṭci peṟum makkaṭku ivvaralāṟukaḷ aruvaruppi viḷivikkat talippaṭṭaṉa; iḷami yuḷḷaṅkaḷ ippurāṇac ceytikaḷik kēṭṭum ivaṟṟit tirip paṭaṅkaḷilum nāṭaka mēṭikaḷilum kaṇṭum teyvaṅkaḷpāl nampikki koḷḷā toḻivaṉavāyiṉa. purāṇaṅkaḷik kūṟaluṟṟavar cilar ivaṟṟiṟku vēṟu poruḷ kūṟuvatākap poy pala pukalvārāyiṉar. ivvāṟṟāl ippurāṇa nūlkaḷiṉ ilakkiya nalamum pēṇuvāraṟṟup pōyiṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, ilakkiya vaḷamum aḻakum koṇṭa uriyum pāṭṭum tamiḻil tōṉṟuvatu aritāy viṭṭatu. ilakkiyat tōṟṟam illiyāyiṉ, makkaṭ camutāyattil aṟivut tuṟiyil vaḷarcciyilli yeṉpatu muṭipām.
20. caṅka kālam
intiyap perunilattiṉ pala pakutikaḷilum nam tamiḻakap pakuti varalāṟṟu vakiyil taṉic ciṟappuṭiyatu. teṉṉintiyāvil vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉ kumari yeṉṟa iraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyiluḷḷa nilappakutiyē tamiḻakam. itaṟkuk kiḻakkilum mēṟkilum teṟkilum kaṭalē elliyākum. oru kālattē teṉpakutiyil nilapparap piruntataṉāl, paḻaṅkālac cāṉṟōr teṉṉel liyik kaṭaleṉṟu kūṟāmal teṉkumari yeṉṟu kūṟiṉar. an nilappakuti kaṭalāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭateṉṟu paḻañ ceyyuṭkaḷ cila kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. kaṭalkaḷiṉ iyaṟkiyamippukkaḷi ārāynta mēṉāṭṭaṟiñar palarum teṉkaṭal oru kālattē nilamāka iruntateṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻakattup paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ mikka paḻami vāyntatu tolkāppiyam. caṅka ilakkiya meṉappaṭum toki nūlkaḷuḷ it tolkāppiyattukkum muṟpaṭṭa ceyyuṭkaḷ cila uṇṭu. āyiṉum, nūl vaṭivil uḷḷatu tolkāppiyamē. ataṟkuc ciṟappup pāyiram kūṟiyavar paṉampāraṉār eṉpavarāvar. avar nam tamiḻnāṭṭit tamiḻ kūṟum  nallulakam eṉṟum, itaṟku vaṭakkelli vēṅkaṭamum teṟkelli kumariyumām eṉṟum urittuḷḷār.
tamiḻakattiṉ varalāṟu kāṇpōrkku mikka paḻami yāṉa kālam caṅka ilakkiya kālamākum. atu tolkāppiyattukku muṉṉum piṉṉumākiya kālamākum. iti nām ippōtu vaḻaṅkum muṟiyil iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa kālam eṉpatu porutta mākum . ikkālattē mēli nāṭukaḷil kirēkka nāṭum, ekiptu nāṭum ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉa. vaṭakkē cintuvum kaṅkiyum pāyum nāṭum ataṉi yaṭuttirunta nāṭum āriya nākarikam naṉku paraviyirunta kālamum antak kālantāṉ. tamiḻ nāṭṭil āriya nākarikam paṭarntu ōraḷavu iṭam peṟṟiruntatum ak kālamētāṉ. itu paṟṟiyē caṅka kālam tamiḻaka varalāṟṟil mutaliṭamākak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. mēlum, tamiḻ nāṭṭil itupōtu nilavum tamiḻ nūlkaḷil mikap paḻiyaṉavāka uḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ ikkālattaṉavē.
vēṟṟu nāṭṭu nākarikam oru poruḷākak karutat takka aḷavu paṭarāmal tamiḻ nākarikamē ciṟantu niṟka viḷaṅkuvatu iccaṅka kālamēyākum. nāṭṭil vāḻpavar taṉit tamiḻar; avari āḷpavarum tamiḻar; āṭci muṟiyum tamiḻ; avarkaḷ niṉippatum pēcuvatum ellām tamiḻ. ātalāl akkālam tamiḻakattukkuc ciṟanta toru poṟkālam eṉṉalām. itaṉi yaṭuttu vanta kālaṅkaḷil tamiḻ nākarikam vēṟṟu nāṭṭu nākarikam viravit taṉ taṉittaṉmi kuṉṟiyatu; araciyal, vāṇikam, camutāyam , camayam, moḻi mutaliya vāḻkkik kūṟukaḷum tamiḻp paṇpil kuṟintaṉa.
iṭikkālattil, tamiḻ nākarikam peritum tēyntu viṭṭatu; araciyal, camutāyam, vāṇikam, camayam, moḻi mutaliya palavaṟṟilum vēṟṟu nāṭṭuk kūṟukaṭkē mutaliṭam. tamiḻakattil tamiḻarkkē mutaliṭam  illiyāyiṟṟu. kalit tuṟiyil tamiḻmoḻi iraṇṭāvatu moḻiyē. araciyalil ataṟku iṭamē kiṭiyātu; āṅkilattukkē mutaliṭam vāṇikattiṉ elli nilavulaku muḻutum parantuviṭṭatu; ataṉāl ataṟkum āṅkilamē moḻiyāyviṭṭatu; poruḷ vakiyilum tamiḻar vaṟiyarāyiṉar; ataṉāl vaṭanāṭṭa varkkum piṟanāṭṭavarkkum kaṭaṉpaṭṭaṉar. tamiḻariṟ palar kaṭalkaṭantu vēṟu nāṭaṭintu kūlikaḷāy vayiṟuvaḷarkka luṟṟaṉar. iṉṟum ituvē tāṉ nili.
iṉi varuvatu etirkālam; irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakuti. intiyap perunilam araciyalil mēli nāṭṭukkuk kīḻppaṭṭi rukkum nili māṟi viḷaṅkum naṟkālam; araciyal, poruḷ, vāṇikam mutaliya tuṟikaḷil intiyap perunilam taṉakkuriya urimiyuṭaṉ tikaḻum cīriya kālam. tamiḻakattukkuc ciṟappāka varum cemmik kālam. ik kālattē vaṭa intiyarkku araciyal vakiyil mēli nāṭṭavariṭamiruntu viṭutali kiṭikkiṟatu. tamiḻakattut tamiḻarkku araciyal vakiyil mēli nāṭṭa variṭamiruntum, poruḷ vakiyil vaṭa intiyariṭa miruntum viṭutali yuṇṭākiṟatu. ikkālam teṉ tamiḻarkkuc ciṟanta kālamaṉṟō?
ivvaṇṇam nammi nōkkivarum iṉiya urimik kālattil tamiḻar, araciyal, poruṇili, vāṇikam, kalvi mutaliya palavaṟṟiyum tamiḻp paṇpu viḷaṅka mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya varākiṉṟaṉar. ippaṇpāṭṭi uḷḷavāṟu aṟitaṟkuc caṅka ilakkiyam nilaviya kāla nili viḷaṅkat teriyavēṇṭum. tamiḻakattu araciyal muṟi, poruḷ nili vaki, vāṇika vaḷarcci, kalvip payiṟci mutaliya pala tamiḻ nākarikak kūṟukaḷ ārāyappaṭa vēṇṭum  ivaṟṟiṉ kuṟivu niṟivukaḷik kaṇṭu niṟivip piṟa nāṭṭavarkku uṇarttuvatum, kuṟivip piṟanāṭuka ḷiliruntu koṇarntu pukutti niṟippatum tamiḻarkkuk kaṭaṉākum. mēlik kaṭaṟkarikkum kīḻik kaṭaṟkarikkum paraviyirunta paṇṭit tamiḻ nākarikac ciṟappu mīḷavum paravuvataṟkut tamiḻar taṅkaḷic cemmi ceytu koḷḷum kaṭamiyuṭiyarākiṉṟaṉar. itaṟku ippōtu ceyyavēṇṭuvatu yātu? nām muṉṉē coṉṉa vāṟu, tamiḻaratu taṉit tamiḻ vāḻkkik kālamākiya caṅka kālatti oru muṟi ārāyavēṇṭum. ataṉi iṅkē ciṟitu kāṇalām.
caṅka kālattil tamiḻakam vaṭavēṅkaṭattiyum teṉ kumariyiyum vaṭakkilum teṟkilum elliyākak koṇṭirunta teṉṟu muṉpē kūṟiṉōm. kiḻakkum mēṟ kum kaṭal. ippōtu uḷḷa kēraḷa nāṭum kaṉṉaṭa nāṭum akkālattil illi. appakutiyum tamiḻakattut tamiḻ nāṭukaḷēyākum. innāṭu cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar eṉṟa mūvēntarkkum uriyatāyiruntatu. teṉṉāṭṭiṉ mēlik kaṭaṟkariyil niṟkum mali nilamum ataṉic cārnta uḷnāṭṭup pakutiyum cēranāṭu; vēṅkaṭa mutal ippōtuḷḷa tañci tiruccirāppaḷḷi yeṉṟa mānāṭukaḷiṉ elliyuḷḷiṭṭa pakuti cōḻa nāṭu. itaṟkut teṉpakuti muṟṟum pāṇṭi nāṭu. ivaṟṟiyāṇṭa vēntar muṟiyē cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyareṉa vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉar. cēra nāṭṭukku vañciyum, cōḻanāṭṭukku uṟiyūrum, pāṇṭināṭṭukku maturiyum talinakarkaḷākum.
ivvāṟu tamiḻakam, nāṭṭu vakiyil mūṉṟu perum pirivu peṟṟu mūṉṟu muṭi vēntarāl āḷappeṟṟu vantatāyiṉum araciyal, vāṇikam, moḻi, camayam eṉṟa vakiyil ciṟu vēṟupāṭum iṉṟiyiruntatu. aracar aṉivarum “valiyuṭi yōrkkē nilam uriyatu” eṉṟa karuttuṭiyar. ataṉāl valimikka vēntaṉ, vali kuṟintāṉōṭu pōr ceytu avaṉ nilattik kavarvatu taṉakku aṟamākak karutiṉāṉ. oru vēntaṉkīḻ vāḻum kuṭimakkaḷuḷ valimikkōṉit talivaṉākki, avaṉ kīḻ cilavūrkaḷit tokuttuc ciṟu nāṭākkit taṉakku vari celuttumāṟu ēṟpāṭu ceytāṉ. at talivarkaḷuṭ cilar nāḷaṭivil valimikkuk kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷāyiṉar. iv vakiyil valimik kōrukkum vali kuṟintōrukkum iṭiyaṟātu pōr naṭantavaṇṇam iruntatu. kuṭi makkaḷum tām vāḻum iṭamum viḷikkum viḷi nilamum aracarkkē yuriyaveṉak karutiṉar. taṅkaḷ nalvāḻvukku aracavēli yallatu vēṟu vēli illiyeṉa uṇarntu kuṭikkuk kuṭiyākavum paṭikkup paṭiyākavum vāḻntum payaṉpaṭṭum vantaṉar. avaruḷ valiyāl talivarāyiṉavar tam vēntarkku “viṉi vēṇṭu vaḻi aṟivu utaviyum, paṭivēṇṭu vaḻi vāḷutaviyum” vantaṉar. avar valimuṟṟum aṟivu āṇmi poruḷ paṭi eṉṟa nāṉku kūṟāka amintiruntatu. aṟivu vēṇṭi nalla kalvi kaṟṟalum, āṇmi vēṇṭip paṭippayiṟci peṟuvatum, poruḷ vēṇṭi uḻavu mutaliya toḻilkaḷi vaḷarttalum, paṭivēṇṭi makkaṭku naṉṉaṭi payiṟṟutalum ceytu vantaṉar. aṟivuṭiya makkaḷit tērntu ciṟappittu avar kūṟiya aṟivu neṟi paṟṟiyē aracu celuttiṉar.
nilappakutiyik kuṟiñci mulli marutam neytal eṉṟu nālvakiyākap pakuttu avvannilattu makkaḷ āṅkāṅku viḷiyakkūṭiya viḷiporuḷi viḷittaṉar. innilattu makkaṭkiṭiyē paṇṭamāṟṟutalum atuvē vāyilāka vāṇikam ceytalum nilaviyiruntaṉa. kaṭaṟkarip pakutiyil vāḻntōr kalaṅkaḷil tam nāṭṭu mikka poruḷip piṟanāṭukaṭkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu vāṇikam ceytaṉar. vēṟṟu nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷum  nam nāṭṭil iṟakkumatiyāyiṉa. uḷ nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ kālilum (vaṇṭiyilum) veḷi nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ kalattilum ceṉṟaṉa. iruvakiyālum iyaṅkum vāṇikap poruḷkaṭku araciyal cuṅkam vitippatum uṇṭu. uḷnāṭṭu vaṇikarkkuk kaḷvarāl tīṅkuṇṭākāvāṟu paṭiyamittu viṭuppatum kaṭaṟkoḷḷik kārari yaṭakkutaṟkuk kaṭalil kalañceluttic ceṉṟu porutaḻip patum paṇṭit tamiḻvēntar aracu muṟiyākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. 
poruḷvaḷam mikuvatu kuṟittu nāṭṭiluḷḷa kāṭu kaḷi aḻittu nāṭākkiṉar; nilam paḷḷamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu kuḷam amittaṉar. kaṭalin kalañceluttuvōr iravil kariyaṟintu koḷḷumāṟu kalaṅkari viḷakkam niṟuviṉar; kaṭalil avvak kālantōṟum vīcum kāṟṟiṉ iyalpu kaṇṭu, akkālattē kalañ celuttu muṟiyik kaṇṭiruntaṉar. tam nāṭṭil illāta putup poruḷkaḷi vēṟṟu nāṭukaḷiliruntu koṇarntaṉar. atiyamāṉ neṭumāṉ añciyiṉ muṉṉōr veḷināṭṭiliruntu karumpik koṇarntaṉareṉa oḷaviyār kūṟukiṉṟār. ivvakiyāl cīṉarum yavaṉarum innāṭṭiṟku vantupōvārāyiṉar.
kalvit tuṟiyil aracarkaḷ pērūkkam kāṭṭiṉar. “uṟṟuḻi utaviyum uṟuporuḷ koṭuttum piṟṟi nili muṉiyātu kaṟṟal naṉṟu” eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉar. makkaḷil āṇ, peṇ, uyarntōr tāḻntōr eṉṟu vēṟupāṭiṉṟi ellārkkum kalvi potup poruḷāy amintiruntatu. kaṟṟavaṉ kīḻōṉāyiṉum mēlōṉākac ciṟappikkap paṭṭāṉ. peṇ makkaḷum perum pulami peṟṟu vēntar pārāṭṭum vīṟu peṟṟiruntaṉar. iciyum nāṭakamum mikkaciṟappuṭaṉ vaḷarkkappeṟṟaṉa. vēntarkaḷum celvarkaḷum iyaṟpulavarip pēṇi iyaṟṟamiḻum, pāṇariyā tarittu icit tamiḻum, kūttaric ciṟappittu nāṭakat tamiḻumākiya muttamiḻiyum muṟiyē ciṟappuṟa vaḷarttu vantaṉar. tamiḻ makkaḷ maṇamākā muṉṉum maṇamākiya piṉṉum kalvikuṟittu veḷināṭu kaṭkuc celvatum marapu. ikkalvi ellāk kalikaḷiyum taṉṉakattē koṇṭiruntatu. vāṉa nūl, nila nūl, poruṇūl, aṟa nūl eṉa nūlkaḷ pala uṇṭu. ici nāṭakaṅkaṭkum nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa. kaḷavu nūl kaṉavu nūl eṉavarum nūl vakikaḷ paṇṭit tamiḻariṉ uḷa nūṟ pulamiyi viḷakki niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
uyarvu, akalam, tiṇmi, arumi eṉṟa kūṟupāṭa minta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭuvatum, melliya āṭineyvatum, maṇi yiḻittalum, poṉṉarittalum, mīṉ piṭittalum, muttuk kuḷittalum eṉṟa ivipōṉṟa toḻil vakikaṭkum caṅka kālattil kuṟivilli. kalvipōlat toḻililum āṭavar peṇṭir uyarntōr tāḻntōr eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu kiṭiyātu. pōrkkaḷattil makaḷir paṭiyēntip pōr ceyvatilliyē tavira, pōriṟ puṇpaṭṭōrkku maruttuvam ceyvatum vēṟu pala toṇṭupurivatum uṇṭu.
iṅkē kūṟiya kalvi, celvam, toḻil mutaliyavaṟṟāl tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē vēṟupāṭukaḷ iruntaṉa. āyiṉum, uṇṇutal, peṇ koḷḷumukattāl uṟavu muṟi koḷḷutal mutaliya vakiyil ivvēṟu pāṭukaḷ taṭiceyyavilli. tamiḻar aṉivarum ōriṉamākavē yiruntaṉar. iṉṟu kāṇappaṭum piḷavu kaḷum vēṟṟumikaḷum aṉṟu accaṅka kālattil kāṇappaṭavē yilli. ūrāṭciyum nāṭāṭciyum aṟivu oḻukkaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟanta cāṉṟōr tuṇikoṇṭu naṭi peṟṟu vantaṉa. makkaḷum nīṇṭa vāḻnāḷuṭiyavarākavē iruntiruk kiṉṟaṉar. iṭikkālattu nūlkaḷ makkaṭ kuṇṭākum nōy nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatteṭṭeṉṟu kūṟum. avaṟṟuḷ caṟṟēṟakkuṟiyap pattup paṉiraṇṭu nōy kaṭkuttāṉ tamiḻil peyaruṇṭu. piṟavellām vēṟṟu moḻip peyarākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē  annōykaḷ iruntiruntāl avaṟṟukkut tamatu tamiḻil peyar vittiruppar. itaṉāl tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē akkālattil nōyum kuṟivu eṉpatu collāmalē viḷaṅkum. “nōyiṉ ṟiyaṉṟa yākkiyar” eṉṟu pulavarkaḷ makkaḷ meynnalattip pārāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. picirān tiyār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr oruvar, tām piṟantu vaḷarnta pāṇṭi nāṭṭiviṭṭuc cōḻanāṭṭu uṟiyūrukku vantapōtu, avarik kaṇṭōr, avarkku āṇṭukaḷ mikap pala kaḻintum, mayir nari yeytāmi kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟaṉar. avar, “eṉ kuṭiyil eṉ maṉivi yum makkaḷum aṟivuṭiyavar; ēvalarum eṉ karuttiṉ paṭiyē oḻukuvar; eṅkaḷūril aṟivu oḻukkaṅkaḷil ciṟanta cāṉṟōr iruntu ūrāṭci ceykiṉṟaṉar; eṅkaḷ nāṭṭu vēntaṉum allavi ceyyāṉ; ivvāṟu yāṅkaḷ kavaliyaṟṟa vāḻkki naṭattuvatāl nari tirikaḷ eṅkaḷi nāṭā” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
nāṭṭil ūrkaḷtōṟum avvak kālaṅkaḷil viḻākkaḷ naṭipeṟum. viḻākkaḷ neṭunāṭkaḷ toṭarntu naṭakkum. aracarkaṭkup piṟanta nāḷ viḻākkaḷum, āṇṭiṟ putumaḻi peytaviṭattup putunīr viḻākkaḷum, kaṭalāṭṭu, kuṉṟu viḷiyāṭṭu, ceṇṭāṭṭu mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭu viḻākkaḷum naṭipeṟṟaṉa. ivviḻākkaḷil, viḻā nikaḻcci kāṇavarum makkaṭku ūravar variyātu viruntu ceyvar. pōr ceytu veṟṟipeṟṟa vēntaṉ, taṉ vīrar cūḻa iruntu virun tuṇṭu veṟṟiviḻāk koṇṭāṭuvaṉ. akkālattē kūttum pāṭṭum mikuntu makkaḷ uḷḷattē mikka kiḷarcciyi yuṇṭupaṇṇum. veṟṟi vakiyāl tāṉ īṭṭi vanta poruḷi vēntaṉ palarkkum vaḻaṅkip pāṭṭum uriyum peṟuvaṉ. aṟavaḻiyil pōr ceytu māṇṭa vīrarkkuk kalleṭuttu, ataṉkaṇ avaruṭiya peyarum pīṭum eḻuti, nīrāṭṭi niṟutti viḻāk koṇṭāṭiṉar. curuṅkac colliṉ nāṭukaḷiṉ ciṟappellām, avaṟṟuḷ makkaḷ koṇṭāṭi makiḻum viḻākkaḷiyē poṟuttiruntateṉṟē kūṟalām.
kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṟku akkālattē iṭamillā mal illi. kuṟiñci nāṭṭavar murukaṉiyum, mulli nāṭṭavar tirumāliyum, marutanilatta var intiraṉiyum, neytal nilattavar varuṇaṉiyum, pāli nilattavar koṟṟaviyiyum ciṟappāka vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam vaḻipāṭṭu vakiyil kaṭavuḷar palar kūṟappaṭiṉum, “ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mēlāya kaṭavuḷ oruvarē; avarkku iṉṉa uru, iṉṉa niṟam eṉpatu kiṭiyātu; uyirum uṭampumākiya ellāvaṟṟiṉuṭiya iyakkaṅkaṭkum kāraṇarātaliṉ avar iyavuḷ; aimpoṟikaḷiyum maṉa mutaliya uṭ karuvikaḷiyum kaṭantu niṟṟalāl kaṭavuḷ” eṉṟa koḷki ammakkaḷ maṉattē vērūṉṟiyiruntatu. iṉṉavuru veṉṟum iṉṉa niṟameṉṟum collaṟkiyalāta ak kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭutaṟkuk kuṟiyāka, maṉṟaṅ kaḷil kantu eṉappaṭum taṟikaḷi naṭṭu avaṟṟi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. pakiyaracar nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ciṟi koṇarappaṭṭa makkaḷuḷ makaḷir palarum im maṉṟaṅkaḷil iruntu ikkantuk kuṟikaḷi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. kuṟiyeṉpatu vaṭavāriya moḻiyil liṅkam eṉappaṭum. ik karutti yaṟiyāta piṟa nāṭṭu āriyari yuḷḷiṭṭa makkaḷ, ikkantu vaḻipāṭṭit tavaṟākak koṇṭu poyk karuttukkaḷip pukutti viṭṭaṉar. uyir vāḻvukku immi, maṟumi, vīṭu eṉṟu mūṉṟu nili yuṇṭu. immi vāḻvu ivvulaka vāḻvu; ivvulakil vāḻvāṅku vāḻntu pukaḻ peṟṟōr maṟumi yulakil imiyavarāy iṉpuṟuvar; ivviruvaki vāḻvum iṉitu amivatu vēṇṭi nilantōṟum teyvaṅkaḷ nāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭupēṟu eṉpatu piṟavānili. ataṉi vēṇṭu vōrkkuk kantukkuṟi vaḻipāṭu amintiruntatu. tolkāppiyamum ataṉkaṭ kāṇappaṭum poruṭ kūṟukaṭ kēṟpac cāṉṟōr pāṭiya pala pāṭṭukkaḷiliruntum tērntu tokuttuk koḷḷap paṭṭa toki nūlkaḷumē ippōtu kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. tokuttaṟku nilik kaḷamāyirunta kuṟippukkaḷum piṟa pāṭṭukkaḷum varalāṟu kaḷum nūlkaḷum iṟantu pōyiṉa. ataṉāl ikkaṭavuḷ niliyi viḷaṅka virivāka aṟintu koḷvatu arumiyāka irukkiṟatu.
makkaḷippōla nam nāṭṭil nūlkaḷ iṟappatum iyaṟki. paḻaṅkālattut tamiḻ vēntar mēṟkoṇṭirunta pōr muṟikaḷuḷ pakivarūrit tīkkiri yākkutal oṉṟu. ataṉāl nūlkaḷ aḻitaṟku iṭamuṇṭu. vēṟṟu nāṭṭavar pōntu innāṭṭu makkaḷ ivartam toṉmi yuṇarntu cemmi nili peṟāvāṟu ceyvatu kuṟittu nūlkaḷi yaḻittatumuṇṭu. maturimā nāṭṭukkut talivarāyp pōntirunta vēṟṟu nāṭṭavaroruvar tēṭit tokuttirunta paḻiya tamiḻ ōliyēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷ, iru nūṟu nāṭkaḷ kutirikku vennīr kāyccutaṟkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉpar. immaṭṭō! āṭip patiṉeṭṭām perukkeṉṉum nīrviḻāviṉ peyarāl aḻinta nūlkaḷ ettaṉi ettaṉiyō! yār aṟivār? veḷḷi yarāṭci inta nāṭṭil nilipeṟumuṉ nāyakka maṉṉarum, marāṭṭiya maṉṉarum, caḷukka maṉṉarum, pallava maṉṉarum; cōḻa pāṇṭiya cēra maṉṉarum eṉap palatiṟa vēntar āṭci niliniṉṟu naṭanta teṉak kalveṭṭukkaḷum piṟavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉavē? avvāṭci nikaḻccikaḷik kuṟitta ēṭukaḷuḷ ippōtu cilavēṉum uṇṭā? kalveṭṭukkaḷum āṅkāṅkuc citaṟik kāṇappeṟum ceppēṭukaḷum tavira vēṟuoru kuṟippum kiṭikkiṟatilli. iviyellām kuṟikkoṇṭu tēṭip paṟṟaṟattuṭitteṭuppatupōla aḻippuṇṭum aḻintum pōyiṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟākak kaṭalkōṭkaḷum kaḷaviyaluri kāṭṭumak kālam pōlum maṟṟak kālaṅkaḷum tammāl iyaṉṟa aḻiviṉic ceytuḷḷaṉa.

21. nallicip pulamic cāṉṟōr
caṅka ilakkiyak kālatti yuṇarttaṟku tuṇi ceyyum cāṉṟōr caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum nallicip pulami paṭitta cāṉṟōrāvar. pōrt tuṟiyil naṟpukaḻ peṟuvōriyum cāṉṟōr eṉpatu caṅkakāla marapu. ataṉāl nallicip pulami mikka cāṉṟōr eṉa avarkaḷic ciṟappittal vēṇṭiṟṟu. iccāṉṟōr vāḻnta kālattil tamiḻakattil tamiḻc caṅkam irunta teṉṟum, atu mūṉṟu niliyākavittut taliccaṅkam, iṭiccaṅkam, kaṭiccaṅkameṉa varum eṉṟum, mutalil ataṉkaṇ nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟeṇmarum, iṭiyil nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpat teṇmarum, kaṭiyil nāṟpatteṇmarum iruntaṉareṉṟum, ivaruḷ aracar, celvar, āṭavar, peṇṭir, pārppār, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar mutaliya palarum iruntaṉareṉṟum ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar. itaṉik koḷḷā toḻipavarum uṇṭu. neṭuṅ kālamāka icceyti tamiḻ nāṭṭil toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅki varutalāl, itaṉik koḷḷōmeṉṟu maṟuttal nērmiyākātu. icceytiyāl oru ciṟanta uṇmi pulaṉākiṟatu. oru kālattē nallicip pulami mikka cāṉṟōr mikappalar iruntaṉareṉpatum, varavara avar toki kuṟinta teṉpatum, pulamikkup pālum toḻilumākiya vēṟupāṭu kiṭiyāteṉpatumām. vēṟṟu nāṭṭavarkaḷiṉ moḻi kali araciyal vāṇikam camayam mutaliya kūṟukaḷ viravi makkaḷ toki peruki niṟkum innāḷil piḻiyaṟat taṅkaḷ tāy moḻiyil niṉikkavum pēcavum eḻutavumvallavar tokiyē nūṟṟukku aintu pērukkumēl illi yeṉṟāl, paṇṭi nāḷil pulavar tokimikak kuṟintu pōyiṟṟeṉpatil viyappuṇṭātaṟku iṭameṅkē yirukkiṟatu.
tamiḻ makkaḷē vāḻnta tamiḻakattil tamiḻaracum, tamiḻ moḻiyum tamiḻ vāṇikamumē nilaviyapōtu tamiḻc cāṉṟōr toki kuṟivāṉēṉ? iṭik kālattilum ikkālattilum vēṟṟunāṭṭup paṭarcci yāl orukāl kuṟiyalāmāyiṉum, iraṇṭāyira māṇṭu kaṭku muṉpu cāṉṟōr toki kuṟivatu takātaṉṟō? eṉṟa eṇṇam nammaṉōr maṉattē eḻalām. avvāṟu eḻuvatum muṟiyē. ikkālattē ellā nāṭukaḷilum anta nāṭṭu araciyal, ataṉ kīḻ vāḻum makkaḷ kalviyaṟivu uṭiyarāka vēṇṭumeṉpatit taṉ kaṭaṉāka mēṟkoṇṭiruk kiṟatu. attakiya araciyaṟ cūḻalil nām vāḻkiṉṟōmātalāl, nām ivvāṟu niṉippatu muṟiyākiṟatu. paḻaṅkālattil aracarkaḷ ikkoḷkiyi mēṟkoṇṭilar; ellā nāṭukaḷum paḻiya kālaṅkaḷil itaṉi mēṟkoḷḷāmalē iruntirukkiṉṟaṉa. kalvit tuṟiyil mikka mēṉmi yaṭintirukkum nāṭukaḷilum paḻaṅkālam nam nāṭṭil iruntatu pōlavē iruntata. āyiṉum, ēṉi nāṭṭu aracarkaḷik kāṭṭilum paḻan tamiḻnāṭṭu aracarkaḷ kalviyiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi yuṇarntiruntaṉar; araciyal, aṟivuṭiyōṉ celuttum vaḻiyilē cellum eṉṟum, araciyalik "kāvaṟcākāṭu" (kāvalākiya vaṇṭi) eṉṟum, ataṉic celuttum pākaṉākiya vēntaṉ kalviyaṟivu ṭiyaṉāy neṟiyaṟintu celuttum nīrmiyaṉāka vēṇṭum; avvāṟu illiyāyiṉ nāṭṭil pakimalintu tīṅku viḷi vikkumeṉṟum  tamiḻvēntar naṉkaṟintiruntaṉar. avaṉ cevicuṭat takka coṟkaḷi aṟivuṭiyōr kūṟiṉum, avaṟṟi amitiyuṭaṉ ēṟṟu āvaṉa ceyvatu avaṉukku iyalpākavum iruntirukkiṟatu. ivvāṟiruntum nāṭṭu makkaḷukkum avaṉukkum irunta toṭarpu, kalvit tuṟiyil avaṉ karutti yīrttu niṟuttavilli.
