kuḷir kāṟṟum maḻi mukilum nilapparappeṅkum kuḷircci parappiya appoḻutil mayiṟ cēvaloṉṟu, kāṭṭiluḷḷa oru kuṉṟiṉ maruṅkil perum pāṟiyoṉṟiṉmēl niṉṟu, makiḻcci mītūrntu. taṉ aḻakiya tōkiyi virittu āṭattalippaṭṭatu. taliyiluḷḷa koṇṭi aciyap pāṟiyiṉ mēṟparappil aṅkumiṅkum naṭantu, virinta taṉ tōki nāṟṟiciyum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟa mayil āṭiyatu kāṇum kaṇṇukkuk kaviṉiya kāṭci nalkiṟṟu. ōrorukāl vāṉiṟ paṟantu niṉṟa mukiṟ kūṭṭam kāṟṟāl ciṟitu vilaka, ataṉūṭē māli veliyiṉ poṉṉoḷi, paṉṉiṟam pūttoḷirum tōkiyiṟ paṭumpōtu, miṉṉaṟ koṭipōl mayiṟ cēvaliṉ mēṉiyi viḷakkam ceytatu. appakutiyi āṭci ceyyum peruntakiyāṉa perum pēkaṉ, avvayiṉ vantavaṉ, pāṟimēl niṉṟāṭum paṟavi mayiliṉ āṭaṟ kāṭciyiṉ pattil taṉ karutti iḻantāṉ. pārtta kaṇkaḷ imittal maṟantaṉa. pāṅkar niṉṟu āṭal nalkum ataṉ iṉiya kāṭci nalam avaṉ uḷḷamellām paṭarntu parantatu. āṭukiṉṟa mayil oru paṟavi yeṉpatu maṟantu nāṭaka naṉṉūl naṉku  payiṉṟāṭum vīṟumiku viṟaliyō eṉa vimmuṟṟāṉ. putumiyētuvāka avaṉpāl perumitam meyppaṭṭu niṉṟatu. taṉṉi maṟantāṉ; tākkum kuḷirkkut tāṉ pōrttirunta pōrviyi eṭuttāṉ; aḵtu ammayiṟkup pōrviyākātu eṉpatu vaḷḷaṉmi niṟanta avaṉ uḷḷattil tōṉṟavilli. eṉ ceytāṉ? araṅkākat tikaḻum pāṟikku ōṭiṉāṉ; āṭaṉ mākkaṭkut tūcumpoṉṉum tuḷakkiṉṟi yaḷḷivīcum taṉ vaṇmiyāl pōrviyi ataṉpāl eṟintuviṭṭup poṉ poliyum taṉ naṉmaṉiyi aṭintāṉ.
avaṉ koṭimaṭattik kaṇṭu viyanta kāṉavarum kāvalarum ūravarkku urittaṉar. mayiṟkup pōrviyaḷitta nikaḻcci nāṭumuḻutum parantatu. pulavar perumakkaḷiṉ ceviyakam pukunta icceyti “kāṉamaññikkuk kaliṅkam nalkiya, aruntiṟal aṇaṅkiṉ āviyar perumakaṉ, peruṅkal nāṭaṉ pēkaṉ”1 eṉpatu pōlum pāṭṭāy veḷippaṭṭatu. eṅkum evar pālum uriyum pāṭṭum yāvum pēkaṉ mayiṟkup pōrvi tantatē poruḷāy eḻuntaṉa.
viyāvik kōpperumpēkaṉ  āvi nāṭṭil āṭci purinta nāḷil cēra nāṭṭil kaṭalpiṟak kōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vañci nakarkkaṇṇē iruntu aracu celuttiṉāṉ. avaṉuṭiya maṟap pukaḻ māṇpu, tamiḻakam niṟintu kaṅki pāyum vaṭavāriya nāṭuvariyiṟ parantiruntatu. akkālattil ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōruḷ paraṇar eṉpavar oruvar. cēranāṭu ceṉṟu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉik kaṇṭu avaṉ ceyta ciṟappukkaḷip peṟṟu varukiyil āvi nāṭṭup perumpēkaṉuṭiya pukaḻ avar uḷḷatti īrttatu. āvi kuṭikkup pōntu avaṉi nēriṟkaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟu aḷavaḷāviṉār. avaṉ ciṉṉāḷ avarit taṉṉōṭu uṭaṉirut tiṉāṉ. annāṭkaḷil pēkaṉatu pukaḻ vēṭkiyi naṉku uṇarntu koṇṭār. avaṉuṭiya nāṭṭil vāḻum makkaḷ vaṟumip piṇiyuṟṟu vāṭutal kūṭātu;atu paṟṟiyavaḻi uḷḷattil tīya niṉivukaḷ tōṉṟit tīcceyalāy makkaḷiṉ potunalattukku ūṟu ceyyumākaliṉ, vaṟiyavarkku vēṇṭuvaṉa aḷittu viyattu vāḻac ceytal vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇamē avaṉ maṉattil mītūrntu niṉṟatu, avaṉuṭaṉ mēlum uri yāṭiyaviṭattup paraṇarkkup piṉvarum karuttukkaḷ teḷiyap pulaṉāyiṉa.
pulavar pāṇar mutaliya kalittuṟi mākkaḷi vaṟumi nōy tīṇṭumāyiṉ, ataṉāl viḷiyum tī niṉivum tīccollum tīcceyalum kāṭṭut tīppōl nāṭu muḻutum parantu peruṅkēṭu viḷivikkum eṉpatu pēkaṉ mutaliya tamiḻ vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ uḷḷattil naṉku nilipēṟu koṇṭiruntatu. celvamuṭi yarukku vaṟumi perumpakiyātaliṉ, vaṟiyavartoki peruku māyiṉ, celvattiṉum ataṉiyuṭiyārkku uyirkkēṭu viḷavippatu piṟitu yātum illi. vaṟumip pēyiṉ vāyil, ici mutaliya kalippulami vīḻumāyiṉ, ataṉ vali mikuntu celvarpāl celvam niliyāmikkuriya cūḻccikaḷum ceyal vakikaḷum tōṉṟi ulakiyal vāḻvic cīraḻittuviṭum. ātalāl celvaruṭimikkup payaṉ vaṟiyārkku īntu avaratu aṉpip peṟṟuc celvattukku araṇ ceyvatākum.
celvar cilarum vaṟiyavar palarumātaṟkuriya kāraṇam aṟivu nūlār muṟiyē tavamuṭimiyum iṉmiyumē eṉṟu kūṟuvar. tavam eṉpatu uḻippu eṉpatum poruḷāl oṉṟē; uyiriya payaṉ karuti mēṟkoḷḷum ūkkamum muyaṟciyum tavam eṉappaṭum; ulakiyal vāḻvu voṉṟē karutum ūkkamum muyaṟciyum uḻippu eṉṟu urikkappaṭum. uṟṟa nōy nōṉṟalum uyirkku uṟukaṇ ceyyāmiyum iraṇṭaṟkum uruvākum. makkaḷiṉattuḷ piṟappu vakiyil yāvarum opparāyiṉum aṟivu āṇmi mutaliya ciṟappiyalpukaḷil oruvariṉoruvar vēṟupaṭutaliṉ celvamum makkaḷiṭiyē mikkum tāḻntu mirukkiṉṟatu. cīrtta aṟivum tiṇṇiya āṇmiyu muṭiyāriṉum ēṉiyōr palarātalāl nāṭṭil celvar cilarum vaṟiyavar palarumāka uḷḷaṉar.
celvarum vaṟiyarumākiya makkaḷulakil yāvaruṭiya ārvattiyum eḷitil kavarntu viḻivu tōṟṟuvippatu pukaḻ. pukaḻi virumpātavar ulakil evarumē ilar. ip pukaḻi ulaka vāḻkkiyiṉ ūtiyam eṉavē uyarntōr urippar. “pulavar pāṭum pukaḻuṭiyōr vicumpiṉ, valavaṉ ēvā vāṉavūrti, eytupa eṉpatām ceyviṉi muṭittu” eṉpatu tamiḻulakiṉ taṉippaṭṭa koḷki. mēlum oruvar īṭṭum poruḷiṉum pukaḻ eṉṟum poṉṟāta nili pēṟuṭiyatu. “oṉṟē ulakattu ōṅku pukaḻallāl poṉṟātu niṟpatu oṉṟu il” eṉṟu poyyil pulavarum pukaṉṟu moḻintaṉar.
ippukaḻkku viḷinilam nalviṉi. tīviṉi ceyvōr itaṉi orukālum peṟamuṭiyātu. pukaḻ peṟṟōṉ oruvaṉ pāl tīviṉi tōṉṟumāyiṉ, peṇ koli purinta naṉṉaṉ pōla, pukaḻaṉittum keṭṭup palarum ikaḻntu veṟuttaṟkaminta paḻiyi  yuṇṭākkum. ataṉāl pukaḻ paṭittōr tampāl ataṟku ūṟu ceyyum tīcceyalkaḷik kaṉaviṉum niṉiyāmal nallaṉavē niṉintum ceytum oḻukuvar. celvam mikunta viṭattu,  uḷḷattil kaḷippuc ciṟantu ceyyappaṭum ceyal vakikaḷiṉ tīmi naṉmikaḷi vēṟu paṭuttuṇarāmal celvap pēṟṟilēyē cellumātalāl, ataṉi illārkku īnta vaṇṇamiruttal vēṇṭum. īki pukaḻip perukkum. appukaḻ ivvulaka vāḻvi mēlulaka iṉpa vāḻvākkum. maṟumiyum vēṇṭā veṟuppāy viṭum. ataṉāl, cāṉṟōr, “pukaḻeṉiṉ uyirum koṭukkuvar paḻiyeṉiṉ, ulakuṭaṉ peṟiṉum koḷḷalar” eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē immi yulakattu iciyuṭaṉ  viḷaṅku pavar maṟumiyiṉpatti nōkkuvatilli.
perum pēkaṉuḷḷattil ikkaruttukkaḷ naṉku vērūṉṟi niṉṟu nilavuvatik kaṇṭa āciriyar paraṇar peruviyappuṟṟār.  maṟumiyi matiyātu immi vāḻvu īkiyum iciyum koṇṭu ēṟṟa meytal vēṇṭu meṉṉum avaṉuṭiya eṇṇamum ceykiyum nāṭu muḻutum paravutal naṉṟu eṉṉum tuṇivu paraṇar pulami yuḷḷattil patintatu. mayiṟcēvalukku avaṉ īnta pōrvi, ataṟku uṭiyākavō, pōrviyākavō ciṟitum payaṉpaṭātu eṉpataṉip pēkaṉ aṟiyāmal illi; avaṉ niṉittatellām īkiyoṉṟu tāṉ. atukaṇṭu attōki mayil ōkiyuṭaṉ taṉṉip pukaḻumeṉṟō, ēṟkumeṉṟō, ēlāteṉṟō, oṉṟiyum avaṉ eṇṇavilli. ivvīkiyāl taṉakku maṟumi vāḻvu eytumeṉṟum avaṉ karutavilli. ikkuḷirkāṟṟil tōkiyi viritta mayir cilirttu iyaṅkuvatu nalla pōrvi yillāta vaṟumi pōlum eṉa eṇṇiṉāṉātal vēṇṭum eṉap paraṇar tuṇintār. piṉṉar avaṉpāl viṭi peṟṟuk koṇṭu  puṟappaṭṭār. pēkaṉum avarkkuriya ciṟappuk kaḷic ceytu avaroṭu pōnta pāṇaṉukkum poṉṉāṟ ceyta tāmarip pūvum avaṉ maṉivi yāṉa viṟalikku uyariya poṉṉari māliyum paricaḷittu mēlum avar tam vāḻkkikku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. peruvaḷam peṟṟum perumakiḻcciyuṭaṉ varum pāṇ cuṟṟam cūḻntuvarap paraṇar annāṭṭiṉṟum nīṅkic cellaluṟṟār.
paṇṭi nāḷil innāḷiṟ pōlak kaṟparappit tiṇitta akaṉṟa neṭuvaḻikaḷō makkaḷ iṉitu iyaṅkutaṟkaminta kāvaṟ cālikaḷō iṉmiyiṉ vaḻic celavu mēṟkoṇṭōr taṉittuc cēṟaliṉṟit tuṇivarum cuṟṟamum cūḻappōvatu marapu. atu paṟṟiyē tuṇiyōṭallatu neṭuvaḻi pōkēl’ eṉṟa mūturi piṟantu vaḻaṅkiṟṟu avvāṟu celvōruḷ pulavar pāṇar kūttar porunar mutalāyiṉōrum palvēṟu vaṇikarum kūṭṭam kūṭṭamākavē ceṉṟaṉar. avaruḷ pulavarāyiṉōr pāṇarum viṟaliyarum kūttarum uṭaṉ varac celvar. pulavarpāṭum iyaṟṟamiḻp pāṭṭukkaḷip pāṇar yāḻiliṭṭu icikka viṟaliyarum kūttarum appāṭṭiṉ poruṭkum icikkum ēṟpa āṭuvar. ataṉāl mūvarum oruṅkuṭaṉ kūṭic cella vēṇṭiya iyalpiṉarāyiṉar. ataṉāl, paraṇar kapilar mutaliya cāṉṟōrkaḷ pāṇcuṟṟam uṭaṉ varat tamiḻakattil celvarkaḷi nāṭip pōkku varavu ceytaṉar.
pāṇcuṟṟattōṭu pōnta āciriyar paraṇar āvināṭu kaṭantatum,  etiril kāṇappaṭṭa cura moṉṟil nuḻittaṉar. vēṉiṟ kālamātaliṉ veyiliṉ vemmimikuntu avarkaḷi vetuppiṟṟu. viriniḻal nalkum perumaraṅkaḷ curattiṭi - iruppatu arumiyātalāl yāvarum peritum varuntiṉar. “attam celvōr alaṟat tākkik kipporuḷ vuvum kaḷavēka vāḻkkik koṭiyōr” perum pēkaṉatu āṭci nalattāl ilarāyiṉamiyiṉ, accamiṉṟi iyaṅkum avarkaṭku ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmi tāṉ koṭumi ceytatu. muṭivil ōriṭattē kuṟu marac ceṟivoṉṟu taṇṇiya niḻal payantu viḷaṅkuvatu teriyavē aṅkē ceṉṟu taṅkiṉar. pāṇarum pāṇmakaḷirum viṟaliyarum viyāvikkō aḷitta poṉṉaṇikaḷi aṇintu oruvarkkoruvar poliyum poruttamum kāṭṭi uvaki pūttaṉar. avarkaḷatu akattil curattiṭi irukkum niṉivēyilli; tattam ūriliruppa tākavē iṉpam taḻittiruntaṉar. ciṟitu pōtil avarkaṭku etirē iravalar tiraḷ oṉṟu vantatu. pāṇar cuṟṟam avarkaḷi aṉpumika varavēṟṟu iṉpuṟuttiṟṟu. avarkaḷuḷ talivaṉākiya iravalaṉ muṟpaṭappōntu cāṉṟōṉākiya paraṇari vaṇaṅki. “aiyaṉē. pāṇaṉ poṟṟāmarippūvum viṟaliyar poṉṉari māliyum aṇintu viḷaṅka, ōr ūrakattē iruppatu pōṉṟa maṉakkiḷarcciyum makiḻcciyum poṅka iccurat tiṭiyē vīṟṟirukkiṉṟīrkaḷ. nīvir yāvirō?” eṉa viṉaviṉāṉ. avaṉi iṉitu nōkkiya paraṇarkku avaṉatu uḷḷakkiṭi pulamiyuḷḷattil aḵtu oru pāṭṭāy veḷivantatu; anta iṉiya pāṭṭu,
	pāṇaṉ cūṭiya pacumpoṉ tāmari
	māṇiḻi viṟali māliyoṭu viḷaṅka
	kaṭumpari neṭuntēr pūṭṭuviṭṭu acii
	ūrīr pōlac curattiṭi iruntaṉir
	yārīrō eṉa viṉava lāṉāk
	kāreṉ okkal kaṭumpaci iravala
	veṉvēl aṇṇal kāṇāvūṅkē
	niṉṉiṉum pulliyam maṉṉē iṉiyē
	iṉṉēm āyiṉēm maṉṉē eṉṟum
	uṭāa pōrā ākutal aṟintum
	paṭāam maññikku ītta eṅkō
	kaṭāa yāṉikkalimāṉ pēkaṉ
	ettuṇiyāyiṉum īttal naṉṟu eṉa
	maṟumi nōkkiṉṟō aṉṟō
	piṟar, vaṟumi nōkkiṉṟu avaṉ kivaṇmiyē 
eṉpatu.
iravalar talivaṉ taṉṉik kaṇṭatum colliya collum kēṭṭa viṉāvum ataṉ vaṟumiyāl iṉṉāmiyuṟṟu varum kuṟippiyum, tām pēkaṉ pāl peṟṟu varum ciṟappik kaṇṭu eytiya viyappum paraṇar naṉku teḷiya niṉṟamiyiṉ avaṟṟiyē koṇṭeṭuttu moḻiyaluṟṟu, “pāṇaṉ cūṭiya pacum poṉ tāmari, māṇiḻi viṟaliyoṭu māli viḷaṅkak kaṭumpari neṭuntēr pūṭṭuviṭṭu acii, ūrīr pōlac curattiṭi iruntaṉir, yārīrō eṉa viṉaval āṉākak kāreṉ okkal kaṭumpaci iravala” eṉṟu tamatu pāṭṭukkut toṭakkavuriyākak koṇṭār. iravalaṉ kaṇkaṭkup pāṇarum viṟaliyarum aṇintirunta poṟṟāmarip pūvum poṉṉari māliyum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiṉamiyiṉ, avaṟṟiyē vitantu, pāṇaṉ cūṭiya pacum poṉ tāmariyum viṟaliyaṇinta māṇiḻiyum māliyum oḷitikaḻac curattiṭiyiruntīr” eṉa viṉavukiṉṟāy eṉṟār. pāṇaṉ pāl poṟpūvum viṟalipāl māṇiḻiyum poṉṉari māliyumākap pala kāṇappaṭṭamiyiṉ, pāṇaṉatu tāmari, viṟaliyaṇinta māliyōṭu viḷaṅka eṉṟār āciriyaroṭu māṇavarkaḷ vantaṉar eṉṟāl, māṇavarkaḷiviṭa āciriyarkku varukiyil ciṟappu mikuti eṉpatu karuttu. ataṉāl pāṇaṉ aṇinta pacum poṟ pūviṉum viṟaliaṇintirunta poṉṉari mālikkuc ciṟappu mikuti  eṉṟaṟku māliyoṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. viṟaliyum pāṇmakaḷē; āyiṉum avaḷ viṟal paṭappāṭi āṭum kaliyil vallunaḷākiyapōtu viṟaliyeṉa uyartti kūṟap paṭupavaṉ. avarkaḷip pārtta iravalaṉ, arukē pūṭṭuviṭṭirunta tērkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṟ pūṭṭappaṭum kutiri
kaḷiyum kaṇṭāṉ. tērkaḷ nāṉku uruḷikaḷ koṇṭaṉa; cakaṭam, cākāṭu, paṇṭi eṉṟu nūlkaḷil varuvaṉa iraṇṭu uruḷikaḷ uṭiyaṉa eṉa aṟital vēṇṭum. tērkaḷ  valiyavum neṭiyavākavum kutirikaḷ mika virintu cellum iyalpiṉa
vākavum iruppatu avaṉ maṉattik kavarntatu. ataṉāl avaṟṟik “kaṭumpari neṭuntēr” eṉṟāṉ. paraṇaruṭiya cuṟṟam curattiṉkaṇ iṉitu taṅkiyiruntatu. vaḻiyaccam iṉmiyum, ataṟkuk kāraṇamākap pēkaṉatu kāval nalamum avaṉukkup pulappaṭuttiṉa. ātalāl, “ūrīr pōlac curattiṭi iruntaṉir, yārīrō” eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ. avaṉ palapaṭa viṉaviṉami tōṉṟa “viṉaval āṉā iravala” eṉṟār. illārkaṇkaḷ eppōtum uṭiyār uṭimiyiyē nōkkuvaṉa. pāṇ cuṟṟam paricilākap peṟṟirunta poṉṉiyum poruḷiyum eṭuttu moḻitalāl iravalar talivaṉ, iṉmi iṭumpiyāl varuntutal viḷaṅkiṉamiyiṉ, “kāreṉ okkal kaṭumpaci iravala” eṉṟum, avaṉ maṉatil iṉmi tōṟṟuvikkum aḻukkāṟu mutaliya tīyaṉa eḻāvāṟu taṭuttu avaṉip pēkaṉ pāl āṟṟup
paṭukkaluṟṟu, ‘veṉvēl aṇṇal kāṇāvūṅkē, niṉṉiṉum pulliyēmmaṉṉē, iṉiyē iṉṉē māyiṉēm maṉṉē” eṉṟu paraṇar pāṭiṉār. iravalaṉ karuttil pēkaṉpāl cēraṟku maṉattē eḻucciyuṇṭākum kuṟippiṉāl avaṉ peyarik kuṟikkāmal “veṉvēl aṇṇal” eṉṟum, avaṉik kaṇṭataṉ payaṉē ematu iccelva nili eṉṟum urippārāy, “kāṇāvūṅkē niṉṉiṉum pulliyam maṉṉē” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār iccoṟkaḷ iravalaṉ uḷḷattil paranta amitiyum eḻunta makiḻcciyum avaṉ mukattil malarntu viḷaṅkiṉa. ‘kāṇāvūṅkē niṉṉiṉum pulliyammaṉṉē’ eṉṟatu koṇṭē, “iṉiyē iṉṉēmāyiṉēm maṉṉē” eṉpatu peṟappaṭutaliṉ, paraṇar ataṉik kūṟavēṇṭāvē eṉṟāl, pēkaṉ kāṭṭiya aṉpum aḷitta poruṭkoṭiyum avar niṉivil eḻutaliṉ, naṉṟiyuṇarvu perukiniṉṟu mikiyāyiṉum ataṉic colvittatu.
itu kēṭṭatum iravalaṉ uḷḷattil, paraṇar kuṟitta “veṉvēl aṇṇal” peyarum pīṭum aṟivataṟku ārvam piṟantatu. ataṉik kuṟippāṟ kaṇṭu koṇṭa āciriyar paraṇar, peyariṉum kuṇañ ceyalkaḷ ūkkam mikuvikkum eṉpatu paṟṟi avaṟṟik kūṟa muṟpaṭṭu avaṟṟuḷḷum, iravalarkku vēṇṭuvaṉa koṭinalamēyaṉṟip pēkaṉuṭiya aṟivu āṟṟalkaḷ allavātaliṉ, tāṉ aḷitta poṉṉāṭiyi mayilkaḷ uṭukkavō pōrttuk koḷḷavō māṭṭā eṉpatik terintiruntum, īttuvakkum iṉpak kaḷippāl nalkiyavaṉ eṉpatu uṇartta, “uṭāā pōrā ākutal aṟintum, paṭāam maññikku ītta eṅkō” eṉṟār. ataṉāl iravalaṉ uvakiyum viyappum aṭiyap peṟṟu ārvattōṭu paraṇari nōkkiṉāṉ. avaṟku avar, “kaṭāa yāṉik kalimāṉ pēkaṉ” eṉa veṉ vēl aṇṇaliṉ peyarip pulappaṭuttār. iravalaṉ eytiya makiḻccikku elli yēyilli. iṉpavurikaḷ cila nikaḻttiya avviravalaṉ, “ām; celvarkaḷ immiyiṟ peṟṟa celvattukku muṉṉip piṟappiṟ ceyta tavam kāraṇam eṉpar; ataṉi uṇarntār kaḷāṉāl, maṟumiyilum accelvap pēṟē virumpi īkiyaṟatti mēṟkoḷvatu iyalputāṉē?” eṉṟāṉ. paraṇar, avaṉi nōkki, “nī kūṟuvatu okkum; āṉāl viyāvik kōpperum pēkaṉ attakiya iyalpiṉaṉallaṉ eṉa maṟukka vēṇṭiya niliyiṉarāṉār. ēṉeṉil, avar avaṉ pāl ciṉṉāḷ iruntu uriyāṭi avaṉuṭiya aṟivu nalatti naṉku kaṇṭavaraṉṟō? taṉpāl uḷḷatu ettuṇi yāyiṉum ītal naṉṟu eṉak karutuvatallatu maṟumip payaṉ  nōkkic ceyvatu pēkaṉukku eṇṇamaṉṟu. avaṉ koṭiyāvum piṟarpāl niṉṟu varuttum vaṟumiyip pōkkuvatoṉṟiyē kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭavi eṉpārāy, “ettuṇiyāyiṉum īttal naṉṟu eṉa maṟumi nōkkiṉṟō aṉṟō, piṟar varumi nōkkiṉṟu avaṉ ki vaṇmi” eṉṟār.
ivvāṟu paraṇar kūṟiyavaṟṟik kēṭṭa iravalar talivaṉ nikaḻttiya urikaḷ avaṉ ciṟanta ōr aṟiñaṉātalip pulap paṭuttavum, paraṇar, avaṉōṭu toṭarntu uriyāṭal toṭaṅkiṉār. “īkiyāṟ pukaḻ eytiṉōr ēṉi aṟivu āṇmiyāṟ ciṟakkum pukaḻāḷariṉum ciṟitu melliyarā yuḷḷaṉar; koṭip pukaḻ peṟṟōr palarum, atu nalkum iṉpattukku iriyākit tamatu celvattukkum pukaḻkkum araṇ nāṭātu nekiḻppatu uṇṭu; ataṉāl avarkaḷ paṭi maṭam, paṭṭuk keṭṭuppōṉati varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu; pulami yuṭiya cāṉṟōr uṭaṉiruntu aṟivu koḷuttutal kaṭaṉaṉṟō?” eṉṟu potuvāypāṭṭāl pēkaṉ mayiṟ cēvalukkup paṭām īnta ceytiyik kuṟippākac cuṭṭi yurittāṉ.
cāṉṟōrākiya paraṇar ciṟitum tāḻātu viṭiyiṟukkat toṭaṅki, “aṟiñarē, nīr aṟṟa kuḷattilum akaṉṟa vayalkaḷilum taṉatu maḻi nīric corintu niṟittum, ōrorukāl avaṟṟiṟkup peyyātu payaṉillāta kaḷar nilattiṟ poḻintum, ivvāṟu, vēṇṭuvatu itu vēṇṭātatu itu eṉṟu varitalic ceyyātu, taṉpāluḷḷa nīri nilattiṉ mēṟpoḻivatu kaṭaṉ eṉa ulavum maḻi mukilpōla, emkōvākiya pēkaṉ koṭik kaṇmaṭam paṭuvatuṇṭēyaṉṟip pōriṟ pakivar paṭivantu kalakkumāyiṉ paṭi maṭam paṭuvatu illi” eṉpārāy,
“	aṟukuḷattu kuttum akalvayal poḻintum
 	uṟumiṭattu utavātu uvarnilam ūṭṭiyum
 	variyā marapiṉ māripōlak
 	kaṭāa yāṉik kaḻaṟkāl pēkaṉ
 	koṭi maṭam paṭutal allatu
 	paṭimaṭam paṭāṉpiṟar paṭimayakkuṟiṉē.”
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār. iravalar talivaṉum avaṉ cuṟṟamum appāṭṭi iciyamittu iṉimiyuṟap pāṭi viṭipeṟṟuc ceṉṟaṉar. tamatu cuṟṟam cūḻtarac cāṉṟōrākiya paraṇarum curattik kaṭantu pōvārāyiṉar.
iravalarkkup perum pēkaṉ vaḻaṅkumiṭattu māri pōlak koṭi maṭampaṭutaluṇṭu eṉap paraṇar kūṟiyati uṟṟu nōkku miṭattu, ataṉ pāṟ kāṇappaṭum kuṟṟamoṉṟu appēkaṉiṭattum uṇṭeṉṟu kuṟippatu pulaṉākum. uvamikaḷ, poruḷi viḷakkutaṟkup payaṉpaṭuvatu pōla, ōr uṇmiyit tuṇitaṟku valiyuṭiya ētuvākā. āyiṉum, iṅkē māriyiṉ kuṟṟatti maṟukkāmal ‘aṟu kuḷattu ukuttum akal vayal poḻintum ’ eṉpatu veḷippaṭuttukiṟatu. aṟu kuḷattum akal vayaliṭattum maḻi peytu niṟikkum eṉṟataṟkēṟpa viyāvikkōmāṉ vaṟiyavarkkum virinta pulamiyuṭiyavarkkum peruvaḷam nalkip payaṉpaṭuvaṉ eṉṟa naṉ poruḷ peṟappaṭukiṟatu; “uṟumiṭattu utavātu uvarnilam ūṭṭiyum” eṉṟataṉāl, pēkaṉum taṉṉi yiṉṟiyamiyāta taṉ kātal maṉivikkut taṉ nalatti utavāmal makap payantu payaṉpaṭutal illāta parattiyarkku mikipaṭa aḷikkum melliyaṉ eṉṟoru poruḷ koḷḷak kiṭakkiṟatu.
āvikuṭikkaṇ iruntu aruḷāṭci purintoḻukum pēkaṉukku āṟiya kaṟpum aṭaṅkiya cāyalumuṭiya maṉiviyoruttiyuṇṭu. peruñ celvap peruṅ kuṭiyil tōṉṟip peṇmikkuriya naṟpaṇpukaḷ yāvum tiraṇṭu urukkoṇṭāṟpōlum annaṅ kiyiṉ peyar kaṇṇaki yeṉpatu. maṉivāḻkkikkup poruntiya uḷḷamum tiruntiya ceykiyum avaḷpāl ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa. taṉṉik koṇṭa kaṇavaṉ pāl avaṭkirunta kātal ‘nilattiṉum peritu ; vāṉiṉum uyarntatu; kaṭaliṉum āḻntu viḷaṅkum’ iyalpiṟṟeṉiṉ atu mikiyākātu. illiruntu vāḻvataṉpayaṉ nallaṟac ceyalkaḷāl ōlkāp pukaḻ niṟuvi ōṅkuvatu eṉṉum kuṟikkōḷ avaḷatu uṇarvil ūṟikkiṭantatu. ūṭiṉum iṉiyavē kūṟum uyar paṇpiṉaḷāṉa akkaṇṇaki, evarpālēṉum tavaṟu kāṇiṉ maṉam poṟātu varuntum iyalpiṉum taliciṟantu niṉṟāḷ. ataṉāl, peruvāḻvāl piṟaṅkum perumakkaḷ tamatu perumikkoppap perum pukaḻ viḷivikkum peruñ ceyal kaḷil maṉam uṟātu piṇaṅkuvarāyiṉ, ‘niṇam tīyil iṭṭaṉṉa neñciṉa’ ḷāy iraṅkuvaḷ.
ittakiya māṇkuṇam paṭitta kaṇṇakiyuṭaṉ iṉpa vāḻvu naṭātti vanta viyāvikkō, nallūr oṉṟil iḷami vaḷamum iṉiya vaṉappu muṭiya oruttiyiṉ kaṇvaliyiṟ cikkuṇṭu avaḷōṭu kātaluṟavu koṇṭu, nāḷum avaḷ maṉikkuc ceṉṟu varalāṉāṉ. paṉṉāḷkāṟum pulappaṭā malirunta avaṉatu parattami evvakiyālō kātaṟ kaṇṇakiyiṉ kātukku eṭṭiyatu. kavaliyum kiyaṟavum avaḷ maṉatik kalakkiṉa. poṉṉiyō poruḷi yōtāṉ taṉ vayiṟṟiṟ cumantu peṟṟa makkaḷiyō  iḻappiṉum kaṟpuṭiya maṅki maṉam attuṇip peritākak karuti varuntātu. taṉ kaṇṇik kavarntu taṉatu kātaluḷḷattil iṭampeṟṟa kaṇavaṉatu kātaluṟavu māṟumāyiṉ. avaḷ ciṟitum poṟātu tuṭituṭittup perum pēturuvaḷ. iḵtu iṉṟum evviṭattum māṟāta peṇṇiyalpu.
ivvāṟu iruntuvarum nāḷil, perumpēkaṉatu koṭip pukaḻ nāṭeṅkaṇum parantiruntamiyiṉ, paḻumaram tērntu cellum paṟaviyiṉam pōlap pulavar perumakkaḷum iṉṉicip  pāṇarum naṉṉayak kūttarum avaṉi nāṭi vanta vaṇṇam iruntaṉar. avaṉuṭiya tiruvōlakkattil avaṉ peṟṟa pukaḻiyum avaṉpāluḷḷa kuṉṟāc celvattukku ētuvākiya nāṭṭiṉ maliyiyum kāṉattiyum ciṟappittup pāṭiṉar. appāṭṭukkaḷik kēṭkuntōṟum kaṇṇakiyiṉ maṉam karintu kaṇṇīrāy oḻukum. kaṇavaṉuṭiya kātaliṉpattip peṟāmi yāl, kūntalic cīvi vārip pū muṭittal, neṟṟiyil tilakam tīṭṭal, vēṟu piṟa oppaṉikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟic ceytu kōṭalil kaṇṇakiyiṉ karuttuc cellātāyiṟṟu. avarkaḷuṭiya kātaluṟavik kinnekiḻntamiyiṉ pēkaṉum avaḷ maṉa niliyi aṟiyāṉāyiṉaṉ.
annāḷil ciṟappuṭiya cāṉṟōrkaḷ celvar maṉikaṭkup pōtariṉ, accelvarkaḷum vēntarkaḷum pēraṉpum peruviruppum koṇṭu ciṟappuṭaṉ varavēṟṟu viruntu pēṇuvatu marapu. avarōṭu uṭaṉiruntu maṉimakaḷir uṇavu paṭippa uṇṇuvatum vaḻakku. evarēṉum avarkaḷip puṟattē vittup pōṟṟuvarāyiṉ accāṉṟōruḷ cilar, vekuṇṭu,
“	yāmāyiṉ emmillam kāṭṭutum tāmāyiṉ
 	kāṇavē kaṟpaḻiyum eṉpārpōl-nāṇip
 	puṟaṅkaṭi vittīvar cōṟum ataṉāl
 	maṟantiṭuka celvar toṭarpu” 
eṉap paḻittut tūṟṟiyatum uṇṭu.
orunāḷ cēranāṭu ceṉṟu ciṟappup pala peṟṟa kapilar eṉṟa cāṉṟōr, pēkaṉatu perumaṉikku vantār. avar vēḷ pāriyiṉ uyirt tuṇivarum avaṉatu kuṭumpattiṉaruṭaṉ neruṅkip payilum pāṉmiyarumāvar; vēḷ pāri iṟanta piṉ avaṉ peṟṟa peṇkaḷit tāmpeṟṟa makkaḷākap pēṇiya apperiyār avarkaḷit tirukkōvalūril pārppārakattē aṭikkalap paṭutti avari araciḷaṅ kāḷikaṭku maṇam ceytu koṭuttaṟ poruṭṭu vēṇṭappaṭum cīrum ciṟappum kuṟittuc cēramāṉ celvak kaṭuṅkōviyum piṟa celvarkaḷiyum kaṇṭu vantār. varupavar pēkaṉiyum kāṇavirumpiṉār. avaratu nallicip pulamic cāṉṟāṇmiyi naṉkaṟintavaṉātalāl, perum pēkaṉum avarkku uriya ciṟappukkaḷic ceytu varavēṟṟu makiḻvittāṉ. avaṉuṭiya koṭinalamum pukaḻc ceykikaḷum kapilarkku mikka iṉpam ceytaṉa.
uli vāyi mūṭalām; ūr vāyi mūṭa iyalātaṉṟō! kapilar āvikuṭikku vantapōtu pēkaṉatu puṟattoḻukkatti yārō avarkkuc colliviṭṭārkaḷ. annāḷiya tamiḻ vāḻvil parattiyarik kūṭi yoḻukum puṟattoḻukkam peruṅ kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭavilli. nāṭṭil aṭikkaṭi nikaḻnta pōrkaḷāl peṇ makkaḷ tokiyiṉum kaṭṭāṇmiyuṭiya āṭavar toki kuṟintiruntamiyiṉ, tammi maṇantu kōṭaṟ kuriya āṇ makkaḷ illāta kuṟiyāl maṇṇulakil vāḻtal vēṇṭi miki makaḷir āṭal pāṭal aḻaku eṉṟa kalikaḷip peritum payiṉṟu parattiyarāyiṉar. avarkaṭku vāḻ vaḷippatum makkaḷ kaṭaṉāyiṉamiyāl parattimi yoḻukkam uḷatāyiṟṟu. parattiyaruḷḷam tāṉ paṟṟiya āṭavaṉ oruvaṉōṭē nillātu poruṇaciyāl variyaṟiyiṉṟip palarik kūṭiya parattiyar maṉam īramillāta pāli nilam pōṉṟami paṟṟi avarkaḷi varivil makaḷir eṉa niṟuttit tiruvaḷḷuvar mutaliya cāṉṟōr, avaratu uṟavi veṟuttu vilakkuviti kūṟiṉar. caṅkakāla nallicic cāṉṟōrkaḷum varivil makaḷiri veṟuttu nīkki, oru mip parattiyar uṟaviyē tammuṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷil niṟuttip pāṭiṉar. avaṟṟiyum ūṭuruvi nōkkiṉ parattimiyum viṭaṟpāṟṟu eṉṟa kuṟippu uṇarvuṭiyōrkkup pulaṉākā toḻivatilli. itu niṟka.
tām kēḷviyuṟṟa parattimiyiṉ uṇmi tuṇitaṟku nalla toru vāyppuk kapilarkkuk kiṭittatu. orunāḷ, pēkaṉiyum avaṉatu perumi mikka maliyiyum pāṭṭoṉṟil toṭuttu iṉimi mika avar pāṭiṉar. ataṉip pāṇar yāḻil iṭṭu icikkak kēṭṭōr pēriṉpam eytiṉar. pāṭṭum iciyum paruntum niḻalum pōl iyintu, pēkaṉatu perum pukaḻik kēṭṭōr ceviniṟiyat tēkkiyapōtu, kaṇṇakiyiṉ uḷḷam peritum kiyaṟavupaṭṭuk kavalip perukkil uḻaṉṟatu. ittuṇip pēricic celvak kōmakaṉāṉa pēkaṉuṭiya perum peyar avaṉatu parattimiyāl mācu paṭukiṟatē eṉṟa eṇṇam eḻuntu avaḷi alittatu. avvoḻukkattāl maṉivāḻvu māṇpuṭiya aṟac ceyal kuṉṟi avalameytuvatum, atu vāyilāka avaṉ piṟantu mēmpaṭum āviyar kuṭikku aminta uyarvu, māṟṟār eḷḷi nakiyāṭum aḷaviṟkut tāḻvaṭivatum perun tuyaratti viḷittaṉa. orupāl. ittuṇip perumakaṉi uyirt tuṇik koḻunaṉākap peṟṟum, avaṉatu pukaḻiṉpatti tāṉ nukarvataṟku iyalāmiyāl uḷatākiya tuṉpam avaḷ uṭaliyē meli
vittatu. mēṉi melivum oppiṉiyiṉmiyum kaṇṭu iraṅki ēṉiya tōḻiyarum piṟarum kūṟum taṇṇiya moḻikaḷ avaḷ uḷḷattil vemmi tōṟṟuvittu, vetuppiyatāl, mukaṅkarukik kaṇkaḷ pacantu, aruvipōla iṭiyaṟātu kaṇṇīr corivaḷāyiṉaḷ. ataṉic cāṉṟōrākiya kapilar aṟintu tām kēḷviyuṟṟatu meyyātalit teḷintār.
maṟunāḷ avar pēkaṉōṭu uriyāṭi irunta pōtu ēṟṟa cevvi vāykkavum tamatu maṉattiluḷḷati urikkak karutiṉār. avaṉuṭiya parattimiyiṉ ciṟappiṉmiyi vāyāṟ kūṟāmal oru ceyyuḷāl avaṉ uyttuṇarum vakiyil urikkaluṟṟār. acceyyuḷ,
“	malivāṉ koḷkeṉa uyarpali tūuy
 	māri yāṉṟu maḻimēkku uyarkeṉak
 	kaṭavuṭ pēṇiya kuṟavar mākkaḷ
 	peyalkaṇṟiya uvakiyar cāral
 	puṉattiṉi ayiṟum nāṭa, ciṉappōrk
 	kivaḷ ḷīkik kaṭumāṉ pēka,
 	yārkol aḷiyaḷ tāṉē nerunal
 	curaṉ uḻantu varuntiya okkal pacitteṉak 
 	kuṇilpāy muraciṉ iraṅkum aruvi
 	naḷiyiruñ cilampiṉ cīṟūr āṅkaṇ
 	vāyil tōṉṟi vāḻtti niṉṟu
 	niṉṉum niṉmaliyum pāṭa iṉṉātu
	ikutta kaṇṇīr niṟuttal cellāḷ
 	(mārpakam) naṉippa vimmik
 	kuḻal iṉivatu pōl aḻutaṉaḷ peritē.
eṉa varuvatu,
itaṉkaṇ, āvināṭṭuk kuṟavar iyalpum pēkaṉatu kivaṇmiyum mutaṟkaṇ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉiyaṭuttut tām oru cīṟūr vāyilil niṉṟu pēkaṉiyum avaṉ maliyiyum pāṭiyatum, atu kēṭṭuk kaṇṇaki kaṇṇīr corintu aḻutatum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṭiyil kaṇṇakiyiṉ peyarik kuṟiyāmal, yār koḷ avaḷ? aḷiyaḷ eṉṟu icikku mukattāl kaṇṇakiyiṉ āṟṟāmiyi avaṉ uyttuṇara urikkiṉṟār.
āvināṭṭukkuṟavarkaḷiṉ iyalpukūṟum pakutiyil, maḻi yiṉmiyālum maḻi mikutiyālum kuṟivu nērntāl, avarkaḷ tamatu vaḻipaṭu kaṭavuṭkup palitūvi vaḻipāṭu ceytu kuṟi nīṅkip puṉattil viḷiyum tiṉiyuṇṭu makiḻvār eṉṟatu, niṉatu aruḷiṉmi paṟṟiyum piṟa makaḷirpāl avvaruḷ mikkiruttal paṟṟiyum āṟṟāmi eytiṉ niṉṉic cērntu vaḻipaṭumāṟṟāl akkuṟivu tīrntu iṉpam nukartaṟkuriyavaḷ niṉ maṉivi; ataṉāl nī avaḷpāl aruḷaṭiyaṉātal vēṇṭum eṉṟa karutti uḷḷuṟiyākak koṇṭatu. itaṉi uyttuṇarum pēkaṉ taṉ puṟattoḻukkam kapilarkkut terintuviṭṭateṉa nāṇit tāṉākavē tiruntuvaṉ eṉpatu uṭkōḷ.
aṭuttapaṭiyāka ip pāṭṭil pēkaṉuṭiya pōr vaṇmi yiyum īkiyiyum ciṟappittatu, pakivarpāl vevviya ciṉamum iravalarpāl taṇṇiya aruḷum uṭiyavaṉākiyāl niṉmaṟap pukaḻum īkiyāṟ piṟakkum niṉ aṟap pukaḻum kaṇṇakiyi aruḷāmiyāl mācu eytutal naṉṟaṉṟu eṉpatik kuṟippāyk kūṟukiṟatu. itaṉi uḷkiyuṇarumiṭattu, avaṉ maṉattil kaṇṇaki yāl taṉ parattimi veḷiyākiya teṉac ciṉam piṟavāmal taṭukkavēṇṭi- avaṉuṭiya aruḷiṉmiyāl avaḷ āṟṟāmal varuntiya niliyi ‘eṉṉōṭu vanta pāṇ cuṟṟattār eytiya pacit tuṉpattip pōkka eṇṇi, yām niṉṉiyum niṉ maliyiyum pāṭiṉōmāka, ematu pāṭṭu niṉṉiyum niṉatu aṉpu māṟiya niliyiyum niṉintu avaḷatu neñcit tuṉpuṟuttiyataṉāl, avaḷ peritum aḻutāḷ’ eṉṟār. itaṉ piṉ, cīṟūrp perumaṉik kaṇ uṟiyum avaḷ yāvaḷ? iṉṉē ceṉṟu nī aruḷutal vēṇṭum eṉa uṟikkaluṟṟa kapilar, ‘yār kol, aḷiyaḷ” eṉṟār. mēlum, avaṭkuṇṭāṉa varuttam āṟṟa oṇṇāta aḷavu peruki niṉṟatu eṉpatu viḷaṅka, “iṉṉātu ikutta kaṇṇīr niṟuttal cellāḷ mārpakam naṉippa vimmi aḻutaṉaḷ peritu’ eṉṟu kūṟi iraṅkiṉār. vēṟu iciyiṉ pattāl uḷḷam urukik kaṇṇīr vaṭittatākak koḷḷaṟku iṭamilli eṉpataṟkāka, “iṉṉātu ikutta kaṇṇīr” eṉa viḷakkiṉār.    
ivvāṟu āḻnta karuttukkaḷiyuṭiya coṟkaḷic colliviṭṭuk kapilar, avaṉiṭam viṭipeṟṟuc ceṉṟār. āyiṉum, pēkaṉuṭiya parattimi yoḻukkam nīṅkavē yilli; avaṇ taṉ parattiyuṟiyum nallūrkkuc ceṉṟa vaṇṇamēyiruntāṉ. ciṟitu kālattukkup piṉ cōḻanāṭṭuk kuṭantikku aṇmiyil aricilāṟu kāviriyiṉiṉṟum piriyu miṭattil iruntu, iṭikkālattil maṟintupōṉa aricilūrkku uriyarāṉa aricil kiḻār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr, cēramāṉ takaṭūr eṟinta peruñcēral irumpoṟiyik kāṇṭaṟkuc celpavar āvi nāṭṭukku vantār. avarkku viyāvik kōviṉ koṭip pukaḻ iṉpam ceytamiyiṉ, avaṉik kāṇa virumpiṉār. avar vanta ciṟitu pōtukkellām pēkaṉuṭiya parattimiyum ataṉāl kaṇṇakiyiṉ āṟṟāmiyum tuṉpamum kaṇkāṇat terintaṉa,
avar pēkaṉik kaṇṭu avaṉ kāṭṭiya aṉpiyum ceyta ciṟappiyum viyantu makiḻntār. ittakiya peruntakiyiṉ iṉpa vāḻvil parattimi yoḻukkam tōṉṟumāyiṉ atu vaḷam kuṉṟumeṉa aricil kiḻār añciṉār. ataṉāl avar avaṉ nāṭṭip pukaḻntu pāṭṭoṉṟic ceytu cevvaḻip paṇṇil amittup pāṇari koṇṭu pāṭuvittār. avaṉum mikka uvakiyuṟṟu, avarkkup poṉṉum poruḷumākap perum paricil nalka muṟpaṭṭāṉ. atu kaṇṭatum, aricil kiḻār kikavittu, ‘taliva, niṉ celvam paricilarkku utavum pāṉmiya vākuka; yām pāṭiya cevvaḻippāṭṭik kēṭṭup peritum nayantu perum poruṭparicil nalkuvāy āyiṉi; āṉāl, yām virumpum paricil vēṟu; ātalāl, ivaṟṟip peṟa virumpu kiṉṟilēm; nī niṉ aruḷi nalkāmiyāl, niṉ maṉiviyākiya arivi peritum āṟṟātu varuntukiṉṟāḷ; avaḷ taṉatu kūntali muṭippatumilli; ēṉi oppaṉikaḷi vēṇṭuvatu milli. maṉikku viḷakkamākiya avaḷ, maṅkalap poliviṉṟik kuḻal muṭikkāmal, maṉamum mukamum kūmpiyiruttal maṉiyāṭci yākātu. ippoḻutē virintu kiṭakkum avaḷ kūntal pūccūṭumāṟu, niṉ tēril kutirikaḷip pūṭṭip puṟappaṭuka” eṉṟa poruḷ pulappaṭa,
“	aṉṉa vākaniṉ aruṅkala veṟukki
	avipeṟal vēṇṭām aṭupōrp pēka
 	cīṟiyāḻ cevvaḻi paṇṇiniṉ vaṉpula
 	naṉṉāṭu pāṭeṉṉi nayantu
 	paricil nalkuvi yāyiṉ kuricil nī
 	nalkā miyiṉ nivarac cā ay
	aruntuyar uḻakkum niṉ tiruntiḻi yarivi
 	kalimayiṟ kalāvam kālkuvit taṉṉa
 	olimeṉ kūntal kamaḻpuki koḷīit
 	taṇkamaḻ kōti puṉiya
 	vaṇpari neṭuntēr pūṇkaniṉ māvē”
eṉṟa ippāṭṭip pāṭiṉar.
pēkaṉuṭiya parattimiyit tām uṭaṉ paṭāmi tōṟṟa, “aṉṉavāka niṉ aruṅkala veṟukki, avipeṟal vēṇṭēm aṭupōrp pēka” eṉṟu eṭutta eṭuppilēyē kuṟittār. ‘niṉatu malināṭṭit toṭuttu yāṉ cevvaḻip paṇṇiṟ pāṭiyati nayantu nī paricil nalka muṟpaṭṭāy; ataṉiṉum nī ippoḻutē ceyya vēṇṭiyatu oṉṟu uḷatu, ataṉic ceyka’ eṉṟu kūṟutaṟku, paraṇar mutaliyōruṭiya kuṟippuri payaṉpaṭā toḻintamiyāl, mikavum veḷippaṭi yāka, “nī nalkāmiyiṉ nivaraccāay, arun tuyar uḻakkum niṉ tiruntiḻiyarivi, olimeṉ kūntal kamaḻpuki koḷīit taṇkamaḻ kōti puṉiya, vaṇpari neṭuntēr pūṇka niṉmāvē” eṉa urittu viṭipeṟṟu akaṉṟār. avvaḻiyum perumpēkaṉ taṉpuṟat toḻukki viṭavilli.
aricil kiḻār ceṉṟa ciṟitu kālattukkuppiṉ peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār eṉṟa cāṉṟōr cēranāṭṭiṉiṉṟum pōntār. avar, cēramāṉ kuṭakkō iḷañcēral irumpoṟi eṉṟa vēntaṉāl muppattīrāyiram kāṇam poṉ paricil tarappeṟṟavar. avar pēkaṉik kāṇac ceṉṟār. orupāl tamatu pāṇ cuṟṟattōṭu iruntu pēkaṉuṭiya pukaḻik kūṟum pāṭṭoṉṟi amittuc cevvaḻip paṇṇil pāṭiṉār. avviṭattē taṉittirunta kaṇṇaki ataṉikkēṭṭu kaṇṇīr coṟintu kavaṉṟāḷ. peruṅ kuṉṟūrk kiḻār avaḷik kaṇṭu uṟṟatu viṉavavum, avaḷ pēkaṉatu parattimiyiyum cāṉṟōrkaḷākiya paraṇar kapilar aricil kiḻār ākiyōr aṟivuṟuttiya urikaḷ payaṉpaṭā toḻintamiyiyum paṇivuṭaṉ terivittu vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ. avar pēkaṉik kaṇṭa pōtu, cēramāṉkaḷ perum paricil tantu ciṟappikkum attuṇip perumiyuṭiya avaratu pulami nalatti avaṉ pārāṭṭit tāṉ perum poruḷ paricil tarac camintāṉ. uṭaṉē, avar avaṉi nōkki,‘aracē, niṉ maṉikkaṇ uṟiyum maṅkala maṭantiyākiya niṉ maṉivi kuḻal muṭittup putu pūccūṭi iṉpuṟutaliṉṟi maṉam vāṭi maṭintuṟivatu māṇpaṉṟu; ataṉāl; avaḷ maṅkalam poliya makiḻntu vāḻac ceyvatutāṉ nī emakkut tarattakka paricil” eṉṟa karuttamiya,
“	kaṉmuḻi yaruvip paṉmali  nīntic
 	cīṟiyāḻ cevvaḻi paṇṇi vantatik
 	kārvāṉ iṉṉuṟi tamiyaḷ kēḷā
 	nerunal oruciṟip pulampu koṇṭu uṟiyum  
	arimatar maḻikkaṇ ammā arivi
 	neyyoṭu tuṟanta miyiruṅ kūntal
 	maṇṇuṟu maṇiyiṉ mācaṟa maṇṇip
 	putumalar kañala iṉṟu peyariṉ
 	atumaṉ emparicil āviyar kōvē.
eṉṟu pāṭiṉar. peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār, malināṭu kaṭantu pēkaṉuṭiya ūrkkaṇ pukuntapōtu, avaṉ pukaḻic cevvaḻip paṇṇiṟ pāṭiṉa tiṟattik “kaṇ muḻiyaruvip paṉmali nīntic cīṟiyāḻ cevvaḻi paṇṇi vantati” eṉṟum, appōtu kārmukil iṉiya ōciyuṭaṉ maḻiyip peyya, āṅku uḷḷa perumaṉiyil otuṅkiṉamiyum ataṉuḷ kaṇṇaki orupāl taṉittu maḻi yōciyic ceviyēṟṟu iruntamiyum pulappaṭak “kār vāṉ iṉṉuṟi tamiyaḷ kēḷā, nerunal oru ciṟip pulampukoṇṭu uṟiyum ” eṉṟum, avaḷ iyaṟkiyākavē ariparantu amartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷum māmi viḷaṅkum mēṉiyumuṭiyavaḷāyiṉum, yām kaṇṭapōtu maṇṇutaliṉṟic cīr kulinta veṟuṅ kūntal virintaliya vīṟṟiruntāḷ eṉpārāy, “arimatar maḻikkaṇ ammā arivi” eṉṟum, tamakku iyalpāka aminta ceyyuṇaṭiyiṟ kūṟiṉār. celvar tarum pariciliṉum, avaratu celva  vāḻvu ciṟantu niṟpatiyē pēṇuvatu cīroḻuku cāṉṟōr ciṟappiyalpātalāl pēkaṉatu vāḻvu aṟaneṟippaṭṭa iṉpap payaṉ  eytavēṇṭumeṉṟu karuti, kaṇṇaki taṉakkuriya maṅkala vaṉappum vaciyilā iṉpamum peṟuvittalē emakku nī taraṟkuriya paricilākum eṉṟa tammuṭiya viruppam teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkumāṟu, “ney yoṭu tuṟanta miyiruṅ kūntal, maṇṇūṟu maṇiyiṉ mācaṟa maṇṇip putumalar kañala iṉṟu peyariṉ, atumaṉ emparicil āviyar kōvē” eṉṟu pāṭiṉār.
iṭaṅkaḻi kāmattukku iriyākiya maṉam, ataṟku ākkamum araṇumākiya ceyalkaḷilēyē urintu niṟkum. ataṉāl uṇṭākum kuṟṟatti eṭuttu urippatiṉum. ataṉ viḷivākat toṭarpuṭiyār eytum tuṉpattik kāṭṭi, avar maṉattil irakkap paṇpi eḻuvitta, naṉṉeṟikkaṇ niṟkap paṇṇa vēṇṭum; akkoḷkiyāltāṉ pēkaṉōṭu uriyāṭiya cāṉṟōr palarum, kaṇṇakiyiṉ kiyaṟavum kavaliyum niṟinta avala niliyiyē avaṉukkup pāṭikkāṭṭiṉar. nāṭkaḷum piyap palavāyk kaḻintaṉa. cāṉṟōrkaḷiṉ cālpurikaḷ pēkaṉ ōḻukkatti māṟṟum valiyiṉṟi māṇṭu maṟintaṉa. kaṇṇakiyiṉ kavalik kaṭal nāṭōṟum kariyiṉṟip perukiya vaṇṇam iruntatu. kaṟpuk kāvalum poṟpuṭi oḻukkamumē iṉi vāḻkkikkut tuṇiyeṉṟa nallaṟamē avaḷ akkaṭaluḷ nīntutaṟku ēṟṟa puṇiyāy ilaṅkiṉa. ñāyiṟṟi viḻuṅki ñālattip putitta iruḷiravu, eppaṭiyum pularntu viṭiyat tāṉē vēṇṭum?
celvaratu celvam pallōrkkum payaṉpaṭutal vēṇṭi avaratu pukaḻ paravum ceyaliṉarāṉa paraṇar, maṟupaṭiyum āvināṭṭu vaḻiyākac cella vēṇṭiyavarākip pēkaṉatu pērūrkku vantār. muṉpu vantapōtu, pēkaṉ mayiṟcēvalukkup pōrvi īnta pukaḻ naṉku paraviyiruntatu kaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟup pāṭiṉār aṉṟō? appōtu avar avaṉiṭam perumporuḷ paricil peṟṟuc ceṉṟār. avar ceṉṟa ciṉṉāṭkup piṉpē avaṉukkup puṟattoḻukkam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. avar ippoḻutu avaṉ parattamiyoḻukkam mēṟkoṇṭati ūravar collak
kēṭṭut terintu koṇṭār. avar kaṇṇakiyik kaṇṭār. avarik kaṇṭatum kaṇṇakikkuṇṭāṉa tuyarattukku aḷavilli. oruvāṟu tēṟi, kapilar, aricil kiḻār, peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār mutaliya cāṉṟōr pōntatum, avaṉatu puṟattoḻukkattip pōkka iyalātu avarkaḷiṉ coṟkaḷ payaṉiṉṟi yoḻintatum eṭut turittāḷ. avaṟṟik kēṭṭatum, avar uḷḷattē acivu piṟantatu. kāṉamayilukkup pōrvi nalkum pēraruḷāḷa ṉuṭiya aruḷ, avaṉuṭiya kātal maṉiviyāṉa kaṇṇakipāl cellāmi kaṇṭu avar tamatu uḷḷattē mikka varuttamuṟṟār. puṟattoḻuk kattiṉ puṉmi orupāl niṟka, ataṉ vaṉpiṇippi naṉku aṟinta pulamic celvarātalāl, pēkaṉi avar nontu koḷḷavum illi; ikaḻavum illi. takkāṅku moḻiyiṉ avaṉ uḷḷam teḷikuvaṉ eṉṟa oru tuṇivu avarkku uṇṭāyiṟṟu
cāṉṟōrākiya paraṇar, peṇmakkaḷ pakkal aruḷuḷḷam koḷḷāmal, vaṉkaṇmi ceypavar yārāyiṉum avarkaḷi iḻittukkūṟip paḻittalil avar ciṟitum kaṇṇōṭṭam ceypa varallar. iḷam peṇ orutti āṟṟil mitantu vanta māviṉ pacuṅkāy oṉṟit tiṉṟatu kāraṇamāka irakkamiṉṟi avaḷik kolipurinta naṉṉaṉ eṉṉum kuṟunilat talivaṉip pulavarulakam eṉṟum veṟuttu otukkumāṟu avaṉip paḻittup pāṭiyavar. avaruṭiya paḻitturi nāṭṭil ettuṇiyaḷavu parantiruntatu eṉpatip piṉvarum nikaḻcciyāl aṟiyalām.
tiruccirāppaḷḷi māvaṭṭattiṉ vaṭapakutiyil uḷḷa paccimalit toṭarkkup paraṇar mutaliyōr vāḻnta kālattil viccimali eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ataṉ kīḻppāl aṭiyil vicciyūr eṉṟu ōr ūr uḷatu; ippōtu atuviccūr eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ataṉ kaṇ iruntu viccikkō eṉṟa maṉṉar nāṭu kāval purintu vantaṉar. ataṟku vaṭakkup pakutiyil iḷaṅ kaṇṭīram eṉṟōr ūruṇṭu. aḵtu ippōtu vāḷikaṇṭapuram eṉṟu tirittuk kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉkaṇ kaṇṭīrakkō eṉṟa kuṟunilat talivar aracu purintaṉar. avarkaḷuṭiya iḷaṅkaṇṭīra māṉa vāḷikaṇṭapuram iṉṟum taṉ toṉmik kuṟippat tōṉṟa iṭinta kōṭṭiyoṉṟit taṉṉakattē koṇṭu cirāppaḷḷi yiliruntu ceṉṉikkuc cellum peruvaḻiyil iruntu varukiṟatu. oru camayam iḷaṅkaṇṭīrakkōvum iḷaviccikkōvum oruṅkē naṭpāl kūṭi iṉitiruntaṉar. appōtu koṅkunāṭṭup peruntaliyūravarāṉa peruntalic cāttaṉār eṉṟa perum pulavar aṅku vantu cērntār. avari iruvarum aṉpuṭaṉ varavēṟṟaṉar. āṉāl avar kaṇṭīrakkōvi aṉpōṭu taḻuvi, viccikkōvit tīṇṭārāyiṉar. atu kaṇṭu viyappum varuttamum koṇṭa iḷaviccikkō, avarik kāraṇam viṉava, avar. “aracē, yāṉ niṉṉit tīṇṭip pullikkoḷḷāmikkuk kāraṇam, nī muṉpu orukāl peṇ koli purinta naṉṉaṉ marumakaṉāvāy” eṉṟu colli avaṉit teḷivittār. peṇkoli purinta naṉṉaṉ paḻipaṭṭa tiṟatti pulavar kūṭṭattukku aṟivittu, avar  aṉivarum avaṉiyum avaṉ vaḻivantōriyum ikaḻntu nīkkum vaṇṇam ceytavar ipparaṇar. ataṉāl, tavaṟiḻitta vēntarum celvarum paraṇariṭattē añciyē oḻukuvar.
naṉṉaṉ iruntu peṇ koli purintu perum paḻik kuḷḷāṉatu āvināṭṭi aṭuttu mēṟkilirukkum mīkoṅku nāṭṭup pakutiyātalāl, viyāvikkōpperum pēkaṉukku atu naṉku terintiruntamiyiṉ, avaṉum paraṇarpāl ciṟitu accat tōṭē varavēṟṟuc ciṟappippatu vaḻakkam. ciṟitupōtu avaṉōṭu aṉpuriyāṭiya paraṇar, kapilar mutaliyōr vantu avaṉip pāṭicceṉṟa tiṟattic ciṟitum kūṟāmal, tāṉ varum pōtu nikaḻnta toṉṟik kūṟa muṟpaṭṭār. avaṉum avar collak karutiyatik kēṭpatil ārvamuṭiyaṉāṉāṉ.
uriyāṭaluṟṟa paraṇar, toṭakkattil, “vēntē, aruḷuru vākiya nī ippōtu aruḷillā yāvatu koṭumiyākum” eṉṟār. atu kēṭṭut tikippuṇṭa pēkaṉ, “aruḷillāta ceyaloṉṟum ceytatu illiyē” eṉpāṉpōla avari nōkki muṟuvalittāṉ. “vaḷḷalē, nēṟṟu māli iruḷ mayaṅkum pōtu pāṇcuṟṟattōṭu pōnta yāṅkaḷ kārmaḻiyāl taḻittup pūttuk kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiya niṉ kāṉattiṉ kāṭciyip pāṭṭoṉṟil vittu mālip pōtukkēyuriya cevvaḻip paṇṇil pāṭiṉēṉ. ataṉik kēṭṭu, iḷiya naṅki yorutti nīlamalar pōṉṟu aḻakiya kariya kaṇkaḷ kaluḻntu nīr vaḻintu mārpil aṇinta pūṇkaḷi naṉikka aḻutāḷ. avaḷi nōkki, “aṉṉiyē, ematu kēṇmiyi virumpum peru vaḷḷalākiya pēkaṉukku nī uṟavuṭiyi pōlum” eṉat toḻutu niṉṟu kēṭṭēmāka, taṉ kaṇkaḷit tuṭittuk koṇṭu  “yām appēkaṉukku uṟavallēm,” eṉṟu toṭaṅkiyatum  tuyaram avaḷ nāvi aṭittatu; piṉpu ōraḷavu tēṟi, “ippoḻutu emmip pōṉṟa oruttiyiṉ nalatti virumpi, nāḷum tērēṟi, mulliyiṉ vēli cūḻnta avaḷuṭiya nalla ūrkkuc cel kiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu palarum collukiṉṟārkaḷ.” eṉa iyampiṉāḷ” eṉṟu moḻinta paraṇar, ataṉiyē oru pāṭṭil amittu avaṉiṭam koṭuttār.
“	aruḷi yākalō koṭitē iruḷvarac
 	cīṟiyāḻ cevvaḻi paṇṇi yāḻaniṉ
 	kāretir kāṉam pāṭiṉē māka
 	nīlnaṟu neytaliṉ polinta uṇkaṇ
 	kaluḻntu vār arippaṉi pūṇakam kaṉippa
	iṉita lāṉāḷ āka, iḷiyōy
 	kiḷiyimaṉ emkēḷ veyyōṟku eṉa
 	yāmtaṟ ṟoḻutaṉam viṉavak kāntaḷ
	mukivuri viraliṉ kaṇṇīr tuṭiyā
 	yām avaṉ kiḷiñarēmallēm; kēḷ, iṉi
 	empōl orutti nalaṉnayantu eṉṟum
 	varūum eṉpa vayaṅku pukaḻppēkaṉ
 	olleṉa olikkum tēroṭu
 	mulli vēli nallū rāṉē”
eṉpatu appāṭṭu.
itaṉkaṇ, pēkaṉ mayilukkup pōrvi īntu makiḻnta taṉāl veḷippaṭṭa avaṉatu aruḷ nalattit tamiḻakameṅkum parappiya nallicic cāṉṟōrākiya paraṇarkkē, avaṉuṭiya aruṭpaṇpip pōṟṟik kākkum urimi mikkiruttalāl, ‘aruḷāy ākalō koṭitu’ eṉṟu eṭuttavuṭaṉ iyampukiṉṟār. aruḷiṉmi tōṉṟiṉ avar  col poypaṭuvataṉṟi avaṉ pukaḻum niliyiṉṟik keṭuvatu paṟṟiyē ‘koṭitu’ eṉṟār pēraru ḷāḷaṉātalāl accol avaṉ uḷḷatti acittu accamum avalamum aṭiyat tōṟṟuvittatu. piṉpu, avar colla virukkum aṟivurikaḷik kēṭṭaṟkaṇ uḷḷa ārvamum avar orukālum taṉakkut tītukūṟār eṉṟa naṉṉampikkiyum eḻuntu makiḻvittalāl avari muṟuvalittu nōkkiṉāṉ. ikal maṉṉaṉāyiṉum, avar kūṟiya col piṟpayaṉ viikkum perumi yuṭiya teṉpatu avaṉ aṟintavaṉ; ataṉāl avaṉ maṉattil ciṉamō etirppuṇarcciyō appōtu eḻavilli. aruḷaṟam tirinta aracil maḻivaḷam kuṉṟum; atu māṟāmi tōṉṟa “niṉkār etirkāṉam pāṭiṉēm” eṉṟār.
tāṉ pāṭiya pāṭṭik kēṭṭuk kaṇṇaki varuntiya tiṟatti, “nīlnaṟu neytaliṉ polinta uṇkaṇ kaluḻntu vār arippaṉi pūṇakam naṉippa iṉital āṉāḷāyiṉaḷ” eṉṟum, atu kaṇṭu tāṉ viyappu mikunta  avaḷi aracamātēviyāṉa kaṇṇaki eṉpatu uṇarntu kitoḻutu, oṉṟum aṟiyātār pōla viṉaviya vakiyi “iḷiyōy, kiḷiyimaṉ emkēḷ veyyōṟku eṉat toḻutaṉam ” eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. ataṉāl pēkaṉum puṉṉaki pūttāṉ. piṉpu, avaḷ urittati uḷḷa vāṟē avaṉukkut terivikkat toṭaṅki avaṉ avaḷukkut taṉ aruḷic ceyyāmi iṉitu viḷaṅka, “yām avaṉ kiḷiñarēm allēm” eṉṟu avaḷ urittatum, ataṉāl, paraṇar avaḷōṭu uriyāṭali viṭuttu nīṅkuvatu pōlum kuṟippik kāṭṭa, avaḷ itaṉiyum kēṭṭal vēṇṭum eṉat toṭaṅki “empōl orutti nalam nayantu eṉṟum varūum eṉpa vayaṅku pukaḻppēkaṉ, olleṉa olikkum tēroṭu mulli vēli nallūrāṉē” eṉṟatum eṭutturittār. pēkaṉ talikkoṇṭu oḻukum parattiyi “em pōl orutti” eṉa uyarttik, kūṟiyatu, pēkaṉ takutikku iḻukkuṇṭākāvāṟu kāppatu kuṟittatu. avvuyar makaḷiṉ nalaṉ nayantu eṉṟum varūum eṉa oḻiyātu, eṉpa eṉap piṟarmēl vittuk kaṇṇaki coṉṉatu, avaṉ ceyal ūreṅkum alarāyp parantami pulappaṭuttiṟṟu. kaṟpiṉ kaṇ alarkku nāṇuvatu kulamakaṭku  iyalpu. “īramil kūṟṟu etir ēṟṟu alar  nāṇal” eṉa ataṉit tolkāppiyarum eṭutturippar. aṉṟiyum, avaṉ  pukaḻip pēṇik kākkum maṉikkiḻatti yātalāl, ivvalaruri maṟuvaṟṟa avaṉ pukaḻkku mācu nalkuvati virumpāmiyāl vayaṅku pukaḻppēkaṉ eṉṟu iyampiṉāḷ. mēlum, nallūr eṉṟatu, kaṟpiṉ mikka maṅkiyar vāḻum nallūriṉ kaṇ olleṉa olikkum tērūrntu palaraṟiya avaṉ parattipāl vantu pōvataṟku varuntum niliyi uṇarttukiṟatu. ittuṇik kaṟpuṭip perumaṭantiyi nī aruḷātu oḻukuvatu cemmineṟiyaṉṟu eṉpatu tōṉṟavē, toṭakkattiyē paraṇar, ‘aruḷāyākalō koṭitu’ eṉṟār. pēkaṉum maṉam teḷintu, “cāṉṟīr, namatu nalluriyāl eṉatu tavaṟṟi uṇarntēṉ. ittakiya parattami yoḻukkattukku iṭaṅkoṭēṉ” eṉṟu colli avaṟku viṭinalka muṟpaṭṭāṉ.
pēkaṉuṭiya  moḻiyilum ceyalilum aiyuṟattakka ciṟumi paraṇarkku itu kāṟum kāṇappaṭṭilatāyiṉum, parattami pūṇṭaviṭattu oruvaṉpāl poymmi tōṉṟutaṟku vāyp puṇmiyiṉ, paraṇar pēkaṉi maṟupaṭiyum aṉpoḻuka nōkki, “nī aruḷaṟattip peritum viruppukiṟavaṉ; tōkiyi virittu niṉṟu āṭiya mayil kuḷirāl varuntumeṉak karutip pōrvi nalkiya pēraruḷāḷaṉ. emakkup pacittuṉpamum illi; ataṉi yuṟṟu varuntum cuṟṟamum illi; kēṭpār makiḻcci mikuntu tam taliyi yacittu iṉpuṟumāṟu yāṅkaḷ niṉ pukaḻi yāḻiliṭṭup pāṭuvataṟkēṟpa illiruntu ceyyappaṭum nallaṟatti nī iṭiyī ṭiṉṟi āṟṟutaliyē vēṇṭukiṉṟēm; niṉṉi yāṅkaḷ mikavum irantukēṭkum paricil iḵtu oṉṟē: aḵtāvatu iṉṟiravē nī niṉ neṭiya tērūrntu, niṉatu iṉiya aruḷiṉṟi vāṭi varuntiyuṟiyum kaṇṇakiyiṉ naṉmaṉikkuc ceṉṟu avaḷ maṉakkavaliyi māṟṟavēṇṭum eṉṟa poruḷpaṭa,
“	maṭattaki māmayil paṉikku meṉṟu aruḷip
 	paṭāam ītta keṭāa nallicik
 	kaṭāa yāṉik kalimāṉ pēka
 	pacittum vārēm; pāramum ilamē;
 	kaḷaṅkaṉi yaṉṉa karuṅkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻ
 	nayam purin tuṟiyunar naṭuṅkap paṇṇi
	aṟañ ceytīmō aruḷvey yōyeṉa
	iḵtuyām irantaparicil aḵtuiruḷiṉ
	iṉamaṇi neṭuntēr ēṟi
 	iṉṉātu uṟivi arumpaṭar kaḷimē”
eṉṟu pāṭik kūṟiṉar.
paraṇaruṭiya ippāṭṭip pēkaṉ naṉku eṇṇiṉāṉ. taṉatu parattimi, paraṇar mutaliya cāṉṟōr taṉ pukaḻip pāṭātu tāṉ tarum paricili vilakki otukkutaṟkuk karutum aḷavu tīmi ceytuviṭṭa teṉṟu uṇarntāṉ. kaṇṇakiyiṉ kaṟpum poṟpumē iccāṉṟōrkaḷi iṉṟu vari nammi nayantu aṉpu ceyyum niliyil niṟuttiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatiyum maṉakkaṇṇil māṇak kaṇṭāṉ. kaṇṇakiyiṉ poruṭṭuk kapilar, aracil
kiḻār mutaliya cāṉṟōr paricil peṟātu maṟuttu moḻintatum avaṉ niṉiviṟku vantatu. “nī niṉ maṉiviyuṭaṉ kūṭicceyyum nallaṟamē niliyāṉa pukaḻip payappatu; atuvē emmaṉōr yāḻiliṭṭu  icikkum ciṟappuṭiyatu; illi yāyiṉ, yāmum piṟarum niṉpukaḻ nilipeṟap pāṭuvatik kiyoḻikka vēṇṭiyavarā vōm” eṉṟa karuttukkaḷ palavum pulappaṭa, “karuṅkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻ nayampurintu uṟi nar naṭuṅkap paṇṇi aṟañceytīmō” eṉṟu paraṇar pāṭiyatu, pēkaṉ uḷḷattil iṉṉōraṉṉa niṉivukaḷum eṇṇaṅkaḷum eḻacceytatu. taṉ kaṟpoḻukkattāl cāṉṟōr palariyum taṉ poruṭṭut tamatu pulami nalatti īṭupaṭuttiya kaṇṇakiyiṉ maṉap perumiyi eṇṇiṉāṉ. paraṇari nōkki, “aiyaṉmīr, ippoḻutē eṉ parattimiyi oḻittēṉ; kaṇṇaki vāḻum kaṭimaṉikkē ceṉṟu cērkiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟu colli, paraṇarkku viṭiyaḷittut tāṉum colliya vaṇṇamē ceytāṉ kaṇṇakiyiṉ kavaliyum oḻintatu.



28. vaṇikar makaḷ kaṇṇaki
caṅka kālattil cōḻa maṉṉar tamiḻakattil āṭci niṟuvi āṇṭu varukiyil, iṉṟu cirāp paḷḷi nakarattiṉ oru pakutiyāka vuḷḷa uṟiyūr tāṉ avarkaḷukkut talinakaramāka iruntatu, annāḷil cēraratu vañci nakar vāṇikattukkum, pāṇṭiyaratu maturi centamiḻkkum pukaḻ  koṇṭu viḷaṅkiyatu pōlac cōḻaratu inta uṟiyūr aṟattukkuc cīrtta iṭamām eṉat tikaḻntatu. ataṉāl, viyaka vāḻvukku iṉṟiyamiyāta aṟam poruḷ iṉpameṉṟa mūṉṟiṉuḷ aṟattukku uṟiyūr; poruṭku vañci mānakar tamiḻiṉpattukku maturi ciṟantaṉa eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kuṟittaṉar. "ūreṉappaṭuvatu uṟiyūr" eṉpatu paḻaṅkālap paḻamoḻi. cōḻanāṭu kāviriyāṟu pāytalāl vaḷam mikuntu kaṭaṟku appāluḷḷa nāṭukaḷilum peyar ciṟantu niṉṟatu. annāṭṭavar cōḻanāṭu varuvataṟku ēṟṟa vakiyil kaṭaṟkaṟiyil takuti vāynta nakaram toṭakkattil illi. veḷi nāṭṭavar aṅku varuvataṟku mikavum iṭarppaṭṭaṉar.
cōḻar kuṭiyil karikālaṉ eṉṟa vēntaṉ āṭcikku vantapōtu kaṭal vāṇikattiṉ ciṟappiyum, aḵtu iṉitu naṭattaṟku vāyppuṭiya kaṭaṟṟuṟi iṉmiyum uṇarntu kāviriyāṟu kaṭalōṭu kalakkumiṭattē ciṟṟūrāy irunta pukārp pakutiyik kalaṅkaḷum vaṅkaṅkaḷum vantu taṅkutaṟkuriya paṭṭiṉa mākkik kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉap peyariṭṭu viḷakka muṟuvit tāṉ. ciṉṉāṭkaḷil veḷināṭṭuk kalaṅkaḷ peruvāriyāka vantu vāṇikam ceyyat talippaṭṭaṉa. cōḻanāṭṭu vaṇikar palar kaṭal vāṇikatti mēṟkoṇṭu poṉṉum poruḷum īṭṭip peruñ celvarāyiṉar. uḷ nāṭṭil viḷintavaṟṟuḷ mikiyā yiṉavaṟṟik kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuk koṇarvatil nāṭṭu vaṇikar ūkkam mikuntaṉar. veḷināṭṭu vaṇikar cilar kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattil taṅki vāṇikam ceytaṉar. ivvāṟṟāl ippaṭṭiṉattuk kaṭaṟkariyil vāṉaḷāva uyarnta celva maṉikaḷum paṇṭa cālikaḷum peruka uṇṭāyiṉa. paṭippaṭiyāka makkaḷ tokiyum, celva vāṇika vakikaḷum perukavum cōḻa vēntaruṭ cilar kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattiyum aracirukki nakaramākak koḷḷalāyiṉar. ataṉāl akanāṭṭu veḷināṭṭu vāṇikaṅkaṭku vēṇṭiya araciyaṟ kāppum eḷitiṉ iyaṉṟatu araciyaliṉ ciṟappum makkaḷiṉ matippum kaṭaṟkappāluḷḷa kīḻi nāṭukaṭkum mēli nāṭukaṭkum iṉitu viḷaṅkat tikaḻntaṉa.
nāḷukkunāḷ kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam makkaṭ perukkam ciṟantu nakaramāy vaḷarntu varuvatukaṇṭa cōḻa vēntar ataṉi aḻakiya muṟiyil amikkak karutiṉar. kiḻakkiliruntu mēṟkup pakkamāka nakaram perukat talippaṭṭamiyiṉ, kaṭaṟkarip pakutiyi palvaki vāṇikattukkum, uṭpakutiyi araciruk kikkumāka vakuttu, kīḻp pakutiyi maruvūrp pākkam eṉavum, mēlip pakutiyip paṭṭiṉappākkam eṉavum peyariṭṭaṉar. ivviraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil periya veḷiyiṭam amittu ataṉkaṇ palvēṟu pūñcōlikaḷum pūmpoḻilkaḷum perumarakkākkaḷum maṉṟaṅkaḷum niṟuvi aḻaku ceytaṉar. ataṉāl kāvirip paṭṭiṉam kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉap peyarkoṇṭu viḷaṅkiṟṟu.
nāḷ cellac cella maruvūrp pākkattil vēyā māṭamum, viyaṉkala irukkiyum, yavaṉar irukkiyum, paṭṭiṉum mayiriṉum parutti nūliṉum nuṇviṉi ceyyum kārukar irukkiyum, muttum maṇiyum poṉṉum ākiyavaṟṟāl aṇi pala ceyyum āyviṉiyāḷar iruppum, kūlam kuvitta kūlavītiyum, kañcakārar. cempu ceykunar, maraṅkol taccar, karuṅkik kollar, kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar, maṇṇīṭṭāḷar, poṉcey kollar, tuṉṉakārar mutaliya palvēṟu toḻiliṉar vāḻum terukkaḷum, ici vakiyil valla perumpāṇar iruppum iṭam peṟṟaṉa. paṭṭiṉappākkattil kōviyal vīti, koṭit tēr vīti, pīṭikit teru, peruṅkuṭi vaṇikar piṟaṅkum vīti, maṟiyavar irukki, vēḷāḷar vīti, āyuḷ vētarum kālak kaṇakkarum piṟarum vāḻum perumaṉi maṟukukaḷum, cūtar, mākatar, vētāḷikar, kaṇṇuḷar, kaṇikiyar mutaliyōr irukkikaḷum, kaṭumpariyāḷar, kaḷiṟṟiṉ pākar, neṭuntēr ūrum vayavar, perum paṭi maṟavar mutaliyōr uṟiyum terukkaḷum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa. iraṇṭiṟkum iṭi veḷi yiṭattil nāḷaṅkāṭiyum, pūta pīṭiki, pali pīṭiki, vacciranāṭṭuk koṟṟappantar, makatanāṭṭup paṭṭimaṇṭapam, avanti vēntaṉ tōraṇavāyil, mayaṉ cey maṇṭapam mutali yaṉavum, veḷḷiṭi maṉṟam, ilañci maṉṟam, neṭuṅkal maṉṟam, pūta catukkam, pāvi maṉṟam, eṉṟa aivaki maṉṟaṅkaḷum, piṟavā yākkip periyōṉ kōyil, aṟumukac cevvēḷ aṇitikaḻ kōyil, vāliyōṉ kōyil, nīlamēṉi neṭiyōṉ kōyil mutaliya kōyilkaḷum kōṭṭaṅkaḷum aṟavōr paḷḷikaḷum piṟavum iruntaṉa.
vaṇikar palki vāḻum ikkāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil kaṭal vāṇikarum uḷnāṭṭu vaṇikarum eṉṟu iruperum piriviṉar uṇṭu. avaruṭ kaṭal vāṇikari nāykar eṉavum maṟṟiyōric cāttuvar eṉavum paṇṭiyōr kuṟittaṉar. ivaruḷ celvap perukkāl ciṟantavarkaḷip parata kumarar eṉa vēntarkaḷ tammōṭu oppavittup pēṇiṉar. nāṭāṭci mēṟkoḷḷātu poruḷāṭci yoṉṟiyē virumpi niṉṟamiyiṉ, ivvaṇikariṉatti aracarkku aṭutta niliyil muṟippaṭuttit tamiḻc cāṉṟōr takutip paṭuttiṉar. ivarkaḷi "aracar piṉṉōr" eṉṟu ciṟappippatu nūl marapāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl vaṇikarkkup poruḷum ataṉik kāttaṟ poruṭṭup paṭiyum uriyavāyiṉa. akanāṭṭavarkku vēṇṭap paṭum poruḷ vaki pala vātaliṉ, avaṟṟi viṟpōrum palarāvar. palvakip paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum makkaḷ iṉitu peṟaṟ poruṭṭu, av vaṇikar palarum. oru kūṭṭamāy nāṭṭil ulāvaruvar. ak kūṭṭattukkuc cāttu eṉpatu peyar. cāttu vaṇikar cāttuvar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷil celvamikkōric cāttappaṉ eṉṟum, piṟaric cāttaṉ eṉṟum piṟkālattavar kuṟittu vaḻaṅki ṉar. iṉṟum am marapu maṟavāmiyāl makkaṭkuc cāttappaṉ eṉṟu peyariṭappaṭukiṟatu.
kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kaṭal vaṇikaruḷ ki.pi mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil mānāykaṉ eṉṟu oruvaṉ vāṇikattāl peruñ celvam eytic  "celvattup payaṉē  ītal" eṉṟa karuttāl iravalarkku īntu iṟavāp pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. avaṉi, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, "māka vāṉ nikarvaṇki mānāykaṉ" eṉṟu ciṟappikkiṉṟār. avaṉukkuk kaṇṇaki eṉṟoru makaḷ piṟantu māṇpuṟa vaḷarntu vantāḷ. poṉṉiṟamum koṭi pōṉṟa mēṉiyumuṭiyavaḷāy, uyar kulappeṇ makkaṭkuriya kaṟpum poṟpum oruṅkamintu iṉiya kuṇañ ceyalkaḷāl yāvarum pārāṭṭip paravum mēṉmiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiṉāḷ.
nāḷoru mēṉiyum poḻutoru vaṇṇamumāka vaḷarntu varum kaṇṇakiyik kaṇṭa makaḷir palarum avaḷip pukaḻntu ēttiṉar; ataṉi,
"	pōtilāl tiruviṉāḷ pukaḻuṭi vaṭivu eṉṟum
 	tītilā vaṭamīṉiṉ tiṟam ivaḷ tiṟam eṉṟum
 	mātarār toḻutu  ētta vayaṅkiya peruṅkuṇattuk
 	kātalāḷ peyarmaṉṉum kaṇṇaki eṉpāḷ maṉṉō"
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eṭutturikkiṉṟār.
akkāvirim pūmpaṭṭiṉattil uṇṇāṭṭu vaṇikar talivaṉāy viḷaṅkiya mācāttuvāṉ eṉṟa peruñcelva vaṇikaṉukkuk kōvalaṉ eṉṟa oru makaṉ iruntāṉ. ammā cāttuvāṉi, "perunilam muḻutāḷum perumakaṉ talivitta oru taṉik kuṭikaḷōṭu uyarntōṅku celvattāṉ" eṉṟum, "varuniti piṟarkku ārttum mācāttuvāṉ " eṉṟum, "irunitik kiḻavaṉ" eṉṟum cāṉṟōr pārāṭṭiṉar. avaṉ makaṉāṉa kōvalaṉ uriya paṇpum kaṭṭāṇmiyum, cevvēḷ pōṉṟa mēṉiyaḻakum uṭiyavaṉ; iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṟa mūṉṟilum naṉku payiṉṟu mēmpaṭṭavaṉ; kūttilum iciyilum avaṉ taṉitta pulami vāyntavaṉ. kaṇṇakikku paṉṉiraṇṭu vayatum kōvalaṉukkup patiṉāṟu vayatum nirampiṉami kaṇṭa peṟṟōr, tammiṟ kalantu pēci naṉṉāḷil paṭṭiṉattup perumakkaḷ palarum aṟiyat tirumaṇam ceytaṉar. maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum peruṅ kātalāṟ piṇippuṇṭu oruvark koruvar uyirum uṭampumāy iyintu iṉitiruntaṉar kaṇṇakipāl peruṅkātaluṟṟu oḻukiya kōvalaṉ, avaḷuṭiya kuṇa nalaṅkaḷi viyantu, 
"	mācaṟu poṉṉē valampuri muttē
 	kācaṟu viriyē karumpē tēṉē
 	arum peṟal pāvāy āruyir maruntē
 	peruṅkuṭi vāṇikaṉ perumaṭa makaḷē
 	maliyiṭip piṟavā maṇiyē eṉkō
 	aliyiṭip piṟavā amutē eṉkō
 	tāḻiruṅ kūntal tiyāl niṉṉi"
eṉṟu pārāṭṭi iṉpuṟṟāṉ.
ciṉṉāṭkaḷukkuppiṉ  iruvariyum taṉip perum vīṭoṉṟil iruntu ilvāḻkki naṭattumāṟu peṟṟōr ēṟpāṭu ceytaṉar. kōvalaṉ tāy, avarkaḷ  putuvatiṉ naṭattum ilvāḻvukku vēṇṭum poṉṉum poruḷum ēvalarum vaḻaṅkic celvak kuṟipāṭu tōṉṟāvaṇṇam ellāvaṟṟiyum niṟivuṟac ceytamittāḷ. aṟavōrkku aḷittal, antaṇar ōmpal, tuṟavōrkku etirtal, viruntetir kōṭal eṉṟa aṟam palavum iruvarum iṉitu āṟṟi, okkalum cuṟṟamum oruṅku pārāṭṭat tamatu ilvāḻkkiyi naṭatti vantaṉar,
aṉparatu aṉpu vāḻvu tōṟṟa mutal muṭivu kāṟum oru neṟiyāka iyaluvatilli; iṭiyiṭiyē avalamum kavaliyum kiyaṟavum tōṉṟi avarkaḷip pirittum vēṟupaṭuttum alippatu iyalpu. "iṉpamum iṭumpiyum puṇarvum pirivum naṇpakal amiyamum iravum pōla vēṟu vēṟu iyalavāki māṟetirntuḷa" eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟuvar. immuṟiyil kaṇṇakiyum kōvalaṉum aṉpāl maṉamottu naṭattiya ilvāḻvilum ivvalippukaḷ tōṉṟat talippaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ vāḻnta kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil cittirāpati eṉṟa nāṭaka makaḷ vāḻntu, araciḷaṅkumararum vāṇikac celvarum pārāṭṭum ciṟappup peṟṟiruntāḷ. avaḷukku mātavi yeṉṟa peyaruṭiya makaḷ oruttiyuṇṭu. nāṭaka makaḷirkkuk kūṟappaṭum piṟappu muṟiyil oru kuṟiyumiṉṟi iṉitu iyaṉṟa kuṭiyātalāl mātaviyi,
"	ciṟappil kuṉṟāc ceykiyōṭu poruntiya
 	piṟappiṟ kuṉṟāp peruntōḷ maṭanti,
 	tātaviḻ purikuḻal mātavi"
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ciṟappittuk kūṟuvar. avaḷ aintu vayatiliruntu ēḻu āṇṭukaḷ iyaṟ kalviyum ici nāṭakak kalviyum payiṉṟu, paṉṉiraṇṭām vayatil tāṉ kaṟṟa nāṭakak kalvit tiṟaṉi nakara makkaṭkuk kāṭṭum cevvi eytiṉāḷ.
nāṭaka makaḷātaliṉ, mātavikku nāṭakattukku vēṇṭiya āṭal pāṭal aḻaku eṉṟa muttiṟak kalikaḷum naṉku kaṟpikkap paṭṭaṉa. iyal ici nāṭakam eṉṟa muttamiḻt tuṟikaḷ muṟiyē niṉivup payiṟciyum coṉṉalamum uṭal vaḷarcciyum kuṟittaṉa vātalāl, tamiḻ muṉṉōr āṇ peṇ iru pālarkkum ivaṟṟik kaṟpippatu  kaṭaṉāka koṇṭaṉar. nāṭaka makaḷirkku iciyum kūttum ēṟpuṭiya toḻilātaliṉ, mātavikku nāṭakak kalvi naṉku tarappaṭṭatu. nāṭakattiṉ uṟuppākiya icikku iciyāciriyaṉum, icikku iṉṟiyamiyāta yāḻ, taṇṇumi, kuḻal mutaliya karuvikaḷi iyakkum āciriyarkaḷum, āṭal vaki palavum kaṟpikkum āṭalāciriyarkaḷum annāḷil perukiyiruntaṉar. ivarkaḷi ātarikkum vakiyil celvap perumakkaḷiṉ celvam peritum payaṉpaṭṭamiyiṉ, ici nāṭakak kalikaḷ mikavum parantupaṭṭu viḷaṅkiṉa. mātaviyum iyalpākavē uṭalaḻakum iṉiya kuralum vāykkappeṟṟu, avaṟṟiṟkēṟpa ici nuṭpaṅ
kaḷiyum āṭal vēṟupāṭukaḷiyum  nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarum kūriya aṟivum uṭiya vaḷāyiruntaṉaḷ. ataṉāl avaḷuṭiya āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakum mikac ciṟanta niliyil vaḷarntu ōṅki niṟkak kaṇṭa cittirāpatiyum āciriyarkaḷum, nāṭāḷum vēntar kāṇa avaḷuṭiya tiṟamiyi araṅkēṟṟam ceyya eṇṇiṉar.
nāṭakam araṅkēṟutaṟkeṉac cila viti muṟikaḷ uṇṭu. avvāṟē nāṭaka mēṭi yamittu vēntaṉ icivu peṟṟuk kuṟitta nāḷil mātaviyi araṅkēṟṟiṉar. icik karuvikaḷ avaḷ āṭal pāṭalkaḷukku ēṟpa icittaṉa. curuṅkac colliṉ,
"	kuḻalvaḻi niṉṟatu yāḻē; yāḻvaḻit
 	taṇṇumi niṉṟatu takavē; taṇṇumip
 	piṉvaḻi niṉṟatu muḻavē; muḻavoṭu
 	kūṭiniṉṟu icittatu āmantiriki"
eṉṟu cilappatikāram terivikkiṉṟatu. appōtu, mātavi araṅkiṉ mēl, "poṉṉiyal pūṅkoṭi" pōlat tōṉṟi, "nāṭṭiya naṉṉūl naṉku kaṭip piṭittu" taṉ kalip pulamiyi araṅkiṟ kūṭiyirunta vēntarum celvarum viyakkumāṟu kāṭṭiṉaḷ. avaḷuṭiya pulami nalatti nayantu nōkkiya cāṉṟōr.
"	eṇṇum eḻuttum iyalintum paṇṇāṉkum
 	paṇṇiṉṟa kūttup patiṉoṉṟum -maṇṇiṉmēl
 	pōkkiṉāḷ pūmpukārp poṟṟoṭi mātavitaṉ
 	vākkiṉāl āṭalaraṅkil vantu"
eṉṟu naṟcāṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉar. vēntaṉum avaṭkut talikkōli eṉṉum paṭṭap peyar tantu pārāṭṭi, "ivaḷ virivil makaḷ allaḷ" ātalāl ivaḷi urimi koḷpavar poṉ āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu paricam taral vēṇṭum" eṉa variyaṟi ceytu ataṟku aṟikuṟiyākap poṉ māli yoṉṟu nalkic ciṟappittāṉ.
mātaviyiṉ kalippukaḻ nakaramuṟṟum paraviṟṟu, makaḷ peṟṟa māṇpukaḻ kaṇṭu makiḻcci malinta cittirāpati, am māliyip paṇip peṇkaḷil oruttiyāṉa kūṉiyiṉ kiyil īntu innakara nampiyar tiritarum teruvil niṉṟu ip pacumpoṉ "māli vāṅkunar cālum nam koṭikku" eṉak kūṟumāṟu ēṟpāṭu ceytāḷ. peruṅkuṭi vaṇikar celvap perumakaṉāṉa kōvalaṉ kaṇṭu kūṉi kūṟiya aḷaviṟṟākiya poṉ tantu māliyi vāṅkumāṟṟāl mātaviyit taṉakku uriyaḷākkiṉāṉ. avaḷum avaṉpāl peyarāp peruṅkātal koṇṭāḷ; ataṉāl avaṉum avaḷpāl viṭutal aṟiyā viruppiṉaṉāyiṉaṉ.
nāṭkaḷ pala kaḻintaṉa. kōvalaṉ ceyal avaṉ peṟṟōrkkum maṟṟiyōrkkum mikka varuttatti viḷivittatu. kuṭikku oruperum tali makaṉātaliṉ, uṟṟārum uṟaviṉarum avaṉi neruṅkutaṟku añci vāḷā iruntoḻintaṉar. kaṇṇaki eytiya varuttattukku elliyilli. āyiṉum ataṉi avaṉ peṟṟōrum piṟarum aṟiyā vakiyil naṉku maṟittoḻukiṉāḷ. kaṇavaṉ illāta pōtu kaṟpuṭi makaḷir tammi aṇi ceytu koḷvatu muṟiyaṉmiyiṉ, kaṇkaḷukku mi tīṭṭuvatiyum neṟṟiyil tilakam iṭuvatiyum ki viṭṭāḷ; kūntalukku eṇṇiyiṭṭuc cīvi muṭippatilli; kaṇavaṉatu pirivu taṉ neñciṉ kaṇ niṉṟu peritum varuttiṟṟāyiṉum, atu māmaṉār māmiyār mutalāyi ṉārkkut teriyā vaṇṇam poyyākap puṉ muṟuval ceytu avarkaḷ maṉam makiḻac ceytāḷ. avaḷ maṅkalavaṇi tavirap piṟa aṇikaḷik kaḷintu kālil cilampaṇivatiyum nīkkiviṭṭāḷ. illiruntu  makiḻvōr, aṟavōrkku aḷittal, tuṟavikaḷip pēṇutal, viruntōmputal ākiya nallaṟaṅkaḷic ceyvataṟku uriyar. kōvalaṉākiya taṉ kaṇavar pirinta pōtu avaṉ maṉivi yākiya tāṉ taṉiyē avaṟṟic ceytal ākāmiyāl avaḷ uḷḷam mikavum varuntiṟṟu.
mātavi maṉiyil vāḻnta kōvalaṉukku, maṇimēkali eṉṟoru peṇ ammātavi vayiṟṟiṟ piṟantāḷ. avaṭkup peyariṭum naṉṉāḷil avaṉ vaḻaṅkiya koṭiyip peṟutaṟku vantōruḷ mutiyavaṉāṉa vētiyaṉ oruvaṉi matayāṉi yoṉṟu paṟṟikkoḷḷa, kōvalaṉ carēleṉa ataṉmēṟ pāyntu ataṉ mataveṟiyi yaṭakki avvētiyaṉi uyvittāṉ, avaṉatu aruṭ peruñ ceyali nakaravar pukaḻntu pārāṭṭiṉar. pārppaṉa maṭanti yorutti tāṉ vaḷartta kīrip piḷḷiyi aṟiyātu koṉṟa pāvam nīṅka, kōvalaṉ avaḷpāl iraṅki vēṇṭuvaṉa vutavi, avaḷuṭiya pāvac ceyal kaṇṭu veṟuppuṟṟu nīṅkiya avaḷ kaṇavaṉi avaḷuṭaṉ kūṭṭi maṉi vāḻkkikku vēṇṭiya celvaṅkaḷiyum aḷittu vāḻac ceytāṉ piṟitorukāl pattiṉip peṇṇoruttimēṟ perum paḻi eytuvittal kuṟittup poykkari kūṟic catukkap pūtattāl ōr iḷiñaṉ kollap paṭṭāṉāka, avaṉ tāy varuntiya pulampulik kaṇṭu kōvalaṉ kaḻipērirakka muṟṟu avaṭkum avaḷ cuṟṟattukkum perum poruḷ utavip pallāṇṭu vāḻvittāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam kōvalaṉ ceyta naṟceyalkaḷāl avaṟku mikka pukaḻ eytak kēṭṭuk kaṇṇakiyiṉ uḷḷam kaḷittatu. eṉiṉum avaṉ taṉṉōṭu uṭaṉuṟivatiṉṟi mātaviyiṭamē oḻukiyatu kaṇṇakikku mikunta varuttam payantatu. kaṇṭār ikaḻvaṉa vaṟṟiyē kātalaṉ ceytālum kulamakaḷ avaṉ, taṉṉi maṉiviyākak koṇṭavaṉ eṉṟa oru kāraṇam paṟṟi avaṉiyē niṉintu vāḻvar. ataṉāl kaṇṇakiyum kōvalaṉiyē nāḷum niṉintu nalinturukiṉāḷ.
orukāl kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil intira viḻā naṭipeṟṟatu. aḵtu irupatteṭṭu nāḷ naṭappatu vaḻakkam. orunāḷ kōvalaṉum mātaviyum kaṭaṟkarikkuc ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa kāṉaṟ cōliyil taṅki iṉpuṟṟaṉar. appoḻutu iruvarum yāḻēnti iṉiya pāṭṭukkaḷip pāṭiṉar. appāṭṭukkaḷiṉ poruḷ iruvar uḷḷattilum karuttu vēṟṟumi piṟappittatu. kōvalaṉ uḷḷam kuḻampit taṭumāṟṟa meyti mātaviyip pirintu taṉ maṉiyakam nōkki varuvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
itaṟkiṭiyē orunāḷ kaṇṇaki oru tīkkaṉavu kaṇṭu maṉam kalaṅkit taṉ tōḻiyāṉa tēvanti eṉpavaṭku ataṉik kūṟiṉāḷ. attēvanti nāṭōṟum pukār nakarattuc cāttaṉ kōyiṟkuc ceṉṟu avaṟku vaḻipāṭāṟṟum iyalpiṉaḷāvaḷ. avaḷ cāttaṉār muṉṉiṉṟu "kaṇṇaki nallāḷukku oru kuṟiyuṇṭu; atu virivil nīṅkutal vēṇṭum" eṉa vēṇṭik koṇṭu vantāḷ. vantavaḷ, kaṇṇakiyi aṉpuṭaṉ vāḻtti "peṟuka niṉ kaṇavaṉ" eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ avaṭku kaṇṇaki kaṇkalaṅki nīr tuḷikka, "tēvanti yāṉum eṉ kippiṭitta kaṇavaṉum oru perunakarkkuc ceṉṟēm; aṅkē ūravar avar mēṟ perum paḻi yoṉṟik kūṟiṉar; ataṉāl avaṟkoru tīṅku uṟṟatu kēṭṭu, annakark kāvalaṉ muṉṉē ceṉṟu, avaṉpāl kuṟṟamiṉmi kaṭṭurittēṉ. ataṉpiṉ akkāvalaṉukkum ūrkkum peruntīṅku uṇṭāyiṟṟu; yāṉum tiṭukkiṭṭuk kaṇviḻittuk koṇṭēṉ. 'uriyār iḻittakka kāṇil kaṉā' eṉpatu paṟṟi yāriṭamum uriyātu uṟi vaṉāyiṉēṉ" eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ. avaḷit tēvanti tēṟṟi, "nī paḻampiṟappil uṉ kaṇavaṟkuk ceytaṟkuriya nōṉpu oṉṟil tavaṟiṉāy; kāviri kaṭaloṭu kalakkum tuṟiyil cūriyakuṇṭam cōmakuṇṭam eṉa iru kuḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ mūḻki arukē irukkum kāmavēḷ kōṭṭattukkuc ceṉṟu vaḻipāṭu ceytāl, nīṅkiṉa kaṇavaṉi makaḷīr peṟuvar; maṟumi yulakattum kaṇavaṉoṭu piriviṉṟi uṟivar" eṉṟu iyampiṉāḷ. kaṇavaṉi yallatu piṟa teyvaṅkaḷik kitoḻutal kaṟpuṭiya makaḷirkku ovvāta oṉṟu eṉpatu tamiḻar kaṟpuneṟi. anneṟiyik kippiṭit toḻukupavaḷātaliṉ, kaṇṇaki avaḷ kūṟiyatu kēṭṭu, "atu pīṭaṉṟu" eṉa vāḷā iruntoḻintāḷ.
ciṟitu pōtil kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakiyirunta neṭumaṉikku vantu paḷḷiyaṭintāṉ. avaṉ maṉattil kalakkamum mukattil teḷiviṉmiyum viḷaṅkak kaṇṭa kaṇṇakiyār vēṟu oṉṟu uriyāmal, uḷḷattil aṉpu peruka mukattilum kaṇṇilum muṟiyē malarcciyum kuḷircciyum koṇṭu iṉṉuri vaḻaṅki varavēṟṟāḷ. kōvalaṉ kaṇṇukkuk kaṇṇakiyiṉ pacanta nutalum melinta mēṉiyum poṟpuṭiya ōviyam pukipaṭintu tōṉṟuvatu pōlak kāṭcitantaṉa. avaṉ peritum varuntic 
"	calampuṇar koḷkic calatiyōṭu āṭik
 	kulamtarum vāṉ poruṭkuṉṟam tolitta
	ilampāṭu nāṇut tarum eṉakku"
eṉṟu eṭutturittāṉ. kaṇṇakiyiṉ nalam kuṉṟiyatiyō tāṉ avaḷip piṉṉāl pirintiruntatiyō moḻiyāmal poruṭkuṉṟam tolintatiyē eṭuttuk kūṟiyatu kaṇṇakikku avaṉ poruṭ kuṟi paṟṟi vantuḷḷāṉ pōlum eṉṟa eṇṇam tōṉṟiyatu; uṭaṉē, "eṉpāl cilampukaḷ uḷḷaṉa; eṭuttuk koḷḷum" eṉṟāḷ. avaṉ peritum maṉam nontu, tāṉ teḷivākak kūṟāmikku varunti, "kaṇṇaki, yāṉ kūṟuvatu kēḷ; iccilampi mutalākak koṇṭu itukāṟum yāṉ tolitta aṇi kalaṅkaḷiyum iḻanta celvaṅkaḷiyum maturi mānakarkkuc ceṉṟu īṭṭak karutukiṉṟēṉ; eṉṉōṭu varuka" eṉṟu icittāṉ. tāṉ paṉṉāḷ taṉippap pirintirunta āruyirk kaṇavar taṉpāṟ pōntu taṉṉōṭu piriviṉṟik kūṭiyirukku māṟṟāl varuka  eṉa aḻittatum, kaṇṇakik kuṇṭāṉa kari kaṭanta uvakiyāl maṟumāṟṟam kūṟātu uṭaṉ cella oruppaṭṭu eḻuntāḷ.
aṉṟiravu muṟpakuti nilavum piṟpakuti iruḷum koṇṭatu. nilavu maṟiyak kāriruḷ paranta viṭiyaṟ kāliyil kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakiyik kūṭṭik koṇṭu nakara vāyilik kaṭantu tirumāl kōyili valam ceytu, puttacittiyattu intira vikāram ēḻiyum kaṭantu, cayiṉa cilātalattit toḻutu koṇṭu  ilavantikiyiṉ eyiṟpuṟa mākac ceṉṟa kāviriyiṉ vaṭakari yaṭintu ataṉ vaḻiyāka maturi nōkkip pōvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
oru kāvatam ceṉṟatum oru camaṇpaḷḷi tōṉṟak kaṇṭu ataṉiyaṭuttuḷḷa pūmarap potumpi iruvarum cērntaṉar. anniliyil, kaṇṇaki, vaḻi naṭanta kaḷippāl ciṟitu ayarvu pōkki, kōvalaṉ mukam nōkki, uvaki mikutiyāl nāṇam talikkoṇṭu kuṟumuṟuval pūttu, "maturi mūtūr yātu?" eṉa viṉaviṉaḷ. avaṉum, "namatu nāṭṭiliruntu āṟintukātam uḷatu; cēyttaṉṟu; aṇittē" eṉṟucolli nakittāṉ.
piṉpu appaḷḷiyiṉ ciṟiyakattē irunta kavuntiyaṭikaḷik kaṇṭu iruvarum toḻutaṉar. aṭikaḷ avarkaḷi nōkki,
"	uruvum kulaṉum uyarpēr oḻukkamum
 	perumakaṉ tirumoḻi piṟaḻā nōṉpum
 	uṭiyīr, eṉṉō? uṟukaṇāḷariṉ
 	kaṭikaḻintu iṅṅaṉam karutiyavāṟu"
eṉak kēṭṭār, kōvalaṉ. uṇmiyik kūṟamāṭṭāmiyāl. "aṭikaḷē, yāṉ oṉṟum kūṟuvataṟku illi. maturikkuc ceṉṟu poruḷīṭṭum viruppattāl varukiṉṟēṉ" eṉṟāṉ. atu kēṭṭu kavuntiyaṭikaḷ, cellum vaḻiyiṉ arumiyiyum kaṇṇakiyiṉ meṉmit taṉmiyiyum eṭuttuc colli viḷakkiṉār. kōvalaṉ celvatē karuttuṭiyaṉāy niṉṟāṉ. ciṟitu pōtil kavuntiyaṭikaḷ teḷivuṟṟuk kāṭiṭiyiṭṭa nāṭu cellutal avaḷ cevikku aritu; aṟikunakar yārē? uriyataṉṟu; oḻika eṉa oḻiyīr. "teṉtamiḻ naṉṉāṭṭut tītutīr maturikkup pōka vēṇṭum eṉṉum eṇṇam eṉakkum uṇṭu. yāṉum varukiṉṟēṉ; varuka" eṉac collavum, kōvalaṉ mikka makiḻvuṟṟu, "aṭikaḷ; nīrē aruḷutirāyiṉ, kaṇṇakikku uṟṟa tuyar nīrttēṉāvēṉ" eṉṟu uvaki vurittāṉ. piṉpu, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ tuṇiyāy niṉṟu neṟi kāṭṭak kaṇṇaki kōvalarkaḷ avarip piṉ paṟṟi naṭakkaluṟṟaṉar.
nāḷukkoru kāvatamāka avarkaḷ naṭantu ceṉṟu ciṉṉāṭ kuppiṉ tiruvaraṅkatti aṭintaṉar. kāviriyāṟṟiṉ naṭuvil maram pala ceṟintu viḷaṅkum tiruvaraṅkattup pūkkaḷ malinta cōli yoṉṟil taṅkiṉar. avviṭattē cayiṉacamayattuc cāraṇar iruntu aṟam urikkum cilātalam oṉṟu iṭam peṟṟiruntatu. ciṟitupōtil avaṇ cāraṇar cilar tōṉṟi,
"	kaḻiperuñ ciṟappiṉ kavunti kāṇāy
 	oḻikeṉa oḻiyātu ūṭṭum valviṉi;
	iṭṭa vittiṉ etirvan teyti
 	ōṭṭuṅ kāli oḻikkavum oṇṇā;
 	kaṭuṅkāl neṭuveḷi iṭumcuṭar eṉṉa
 	oruṅkuṭaṉ nillā uṭampiṭi uyirkaḷ" 
eṉṟu colli, avarkaḷ camayattiṉ mutalvaṉāṉa aruka paramēṭṭiyiṉ ciṟappukkaḷip pala paṭiyākap pukaḻntu ōtiṉar. atu kēṭṭut toḻuteḻunta kavuntiyi, "pāvam tarupācam kavunti keṭuka" eṉṟu vāḻttiviṭṭu avarkaḷ ceṉṟaṉar. piṉpu kavunti yaṭikaḷ kōvala kaṇṇakikaḷōṭu nīraṇi māṭam eṉappaṭum paṭakēṟik kāviriyāṟṟik kaṭantu, ataṉ teṉkariyil uḷḷa uṟiyūr eṉṉum pērūric cārnta pūmpoḻil oṉṟil taṅkiṉar.
ciṟitupōtu kaḻintatum, oḻukkak kuṟiviṉarāṉa āṭavaṉ oruvaṉum peṇmakaḷ oruttiyum avviṭattukku vantaṉar. vantavarkaḷ kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakiyākiya iruvariyum pārttuk kavuntiyittoḻutu,"kāmaṉum iratiyum pōla viḷaṅkum ivarkaḷ yāvar?" eṉṟu kēṭka, kāmaṉum iratiyum tēvarāvar; ivviruvarum makkaḷiṉattavar viḷaṅkuvārāya, "makkaḷ kāṇīr māṉiṭayākkiyar" eṉṟu kavuntiyaṭikaḷ viṭi kūṟiṉār. makkaḷ eṉṟatu tāṉ peṟṟa makkaḷ eṉṟum poruḷ paṭumātalāl, avarkaḷ, "orutāy vayiṟṟiṟ piṟanta āṇum peṇṇumākiya iruvar kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumāka iyivatu uṇṭō?" eṉa ikaḻvatu pōlak kēṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl ciṉamuṟṟa kavunti, "muḷḷuṭik kāṭṭil mutunariyākuka?" eṉac capittār; avarkaḷ uṭaṉē nariyāki ūḷiyiṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷuṭiya niliyik kaṇṭatum kaṇṇakikkum kōvalaṉukkum maṉatil mikka irak kamuṇṭāyiṟṟu. avarkaḷ, kavuntiyi nōkki, "neṟiyiṉ nīṅkiya ivarkaḷ nīrmiyallaṉa moḻintaṉar; ataṟkuk kāraṇam avarkaḷatu aṟiyāmi; taṅkaḷi aṟiyātu kuṟṟappaṭṭa ivarkaṭku uytik kālam urittaruḷka" eṉa vēṇṭiṉar. paṉṉiraṇṭu tiṅkaḷ ivvāṟu iruntu varuntip piṉṉar muṉṉiyuruvam peṟuka" eṉac cāpaviṭi kūṟiṉar.
avviṭattiṉiṟum puṟappaṭṭu avvuṟiyūril uḷḷa arukaṉ kōyilit toḻutu vaṇaṅki, aṅkē naṉmakkaṭkut tiruvaraṅkattil cāraṇar tōṉṟiyuritta naṉmoḻikaḷi urittu, aṉṟiravu aṅkē taṅkiṉar. maṟunāḷ viṭiyalil eḻuntu teṉṟici nōkkic celvārāyiṉar. ciṟitu tūram ceṉṟatum kiḻakkil ñāyiṟu tōṉṟi vemmi ceyyavē āṅkē tōṉṟiya iḷamarakkāvil iruntaṉar. avviṭattē kuṭamali nāṭṭu māṅkāṭu eṉṉum ūriliruntu maṟiyōṉ oruvaṉ pāṇṭināṭu kaṭantuvantu koṇṭiruntavaṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ pukaḻi vāḻttik koṇṭu avaṉ vantu cērntāṉ. avaṉik kaṇṭu kōvalaṉ, avaṉ varalāṟu kēṭṭu muṭivil maturi mūtūrkkuc cellum vaḻikūṟumāṟu vēṇṭiṉāṉ. avaṉ maturikkuriya vaḻi mūṉṟu eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu teṉṉavaṟkuriya ciṟumalit toṭari valaṅ koṇṭu celvatu eṉṟum, oṉṟu tirumāliruñ cōlimali vaḻiyākaccelvatu eṉṟum colli avvaḻiyē ceṉṟāl akkuṉṟattil puṇṇiya caravaṇam, pavakāraṇi, iṭṭa cittiyeṉa mūṉṟu poykikaḷ uṇṭu; avaṟṟuḷ mūḻkiṉāl intira viyākaraṇam, muṟpiṟappuṇmi, iṭṭa citti eytalām eṉṟum, mūṉṟāvatu ūriṭiyiṭṭa kāṭupala koṇṭatu; avvaḻiyil uṟiyum teyvam celvōrkkuc ciṟanta tuṇiyāy utavum eṉṟum coṉṉāṉ. avaṟṟik kēṭṭatum, "maṟiyōy, yāṅkaḷ poykikaḷil mūḻka vēṇṭuvatilli. kāmuṟu teyvam kaṇṭu aṭi paṇiya nī pō; yāṅkaḷ ivvaḻiyē celkiṉṟōm" eṉṟu avaṟku viṭikoṭuttu, aṉṟu pakal muṟṟum aṅkē irunta ūriliruntu viṭṭup puṟappaṭṭaṉar.
ivvāṟu mūvarum celkiyil orunāḷ kōvalaṉ oru nīr niliyil taṉittu niṟpakkaṇṭu, oru teyvam, mātaviyiṉ tōḻiyākiya vayantamāli vaṭivil tōṉṟik kōvalaṉi mātaviyi niṉintu varuttutal karuti mayakka muyaṉṟatu; kōvalaṉ pāykalippāvi mantiramoṉṟik kūṟalum atteyvam maṉanaṭuṅkit taṉ kuṟṟattic collit taṉ ceykiyik kavuntiyaṭikaṭkum kaṇṇakikkum terivikka vēṇṭā eṉa vēṇṭikkoṇṭu nīṅkiṟṟu. innikaḻcciyāl kōvalaṟkuk kavuntiyaṭikaḷiṉ puṇṇiyac ciṟappum kaṇṇakiyiṉ kaṟpup perumiyum naṉku viḷaṅkiṉa. piṉpi mūvarum vēṭṭuvar vaḻipaṭum koṟṟaviyirunta kōyiṟ cūḻaliyaṭintaṉar. akkāliyil aṅkē āṇkaḷum, peṇkaḷumāka vēṭṭuvar kūṭṭamoṉṟu akkoṟṟavikku vaḻipāṭu ceytal vēṇṭi vantucērntatu. avarkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭṭu nikaḻcciyil teyvamuṟṟa cāliṉi muṟpōntu kaṇavaṉōṭirunta kaṇṇakiyip pārttu, "ivaḷō, koṅkac celvi, kuṭamaliyāṭṭi, teṉ tamiḻp pāvi ceytatavakkoḻuntu, oru māmaṇiyāy ulakiṟku ōṅkiya tirumāmaṇi" eṉṟu urittāḷ. atu kaṇṭu palarum viyantu nōkka, nāṇam mikunta kaṇṇaki kōvalaṉ mutukiṉ puṟattē maṟintu "pētuṟavu moḻintaṉaḷ mūtaṟivāṭṭi" eṉṟu moḻintu muṟuvalittāḷ. piṉpu vēṭṭuvar tammuṭiya ciṟukuṭikkuc ceṉṟu cērntaṉar.
kavuntiyaṭikaḷ kaṇṇakiyiṉ vāṭṭam kaṇṭu kōvalaṉi nōkki, "ikkaṭuveyiliṉ vemmiyik kaṇṇaki poṟāḷ; kaṟkaḷ niṟinta neṭu vaḻi naṭantu payiṉṟavaḷallaḷ; innāṭu ceṅkōl vaḻātu teṉṉavar kākkum tirunāṭu; ākaliṉ, iṅkē māvum mākkaḷum puḷḷum piṟavum evarkkum tīṅkuceyyā; ataṉāl pakalil taṅki iravuppōtil nilavoḷiyil celvōm; iravil celvataṟku ētam yātum illi; eṉṟāḷ. atu kēṭṭu iruvarum icivu terivikka aṉṟiya pakaṟpōtu kaḻintatu. mālivaravum, "malayattu ōṅki maturiyil vaḷarntu pulavar nāvil poruntiya teṉṟal" maṉṟal kamaḻntu taṇṇilavip poḻiya mūvarum vaḻi naṭakkaluṟṟaṉar. poḻutu viṭiyavum ñāyiṟeḻuntu veyiloḷiyip parappa, iruvariyum pārppaṉar uṟiyum ōr ūrkkaṇ iruttik kōvalaṉ kaṭaṉ kaḻippatu kuṟittu veḷiyē ceṉṟāṉ.
ceṉṟaviṭattil kōcikaṉ eṉṟa ōr antaṇaṉ mātavi koṭutta ōliyoṉṟi avaṉiṭam tantu kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil kōvalaṉik kāṇāta avaṉ peṟṟōr avaṉit tēṭik koṇarumāṟu ēvalari viṭuttu varuntiyatum, uṟaviṉarum ūravarum peruntuyar uḻantatum kūṟiṉāṉ. piṉpu kōvalaṉ avvōliyi nōkki mātavi viṭuttatē eṉat tērntu, avvōliyip paṭittu mātavipāl kuṟṟamiṉmiyum taṉatu tavaṟṟiyum uṇarntu oruvāṟu tēṟi, "inta ōliyāṉ eṉ peṟṟōrkku eḻutum muṟiyil poṟpuṟa amintuḷḷatu; ākavē itaṉi eṉ peṟṟōriṭam cērkka" eṉak kūṟi avaṟku vaḻiviṭṭāṉ.
kōvalaṉ, piṉpu kavuntiyōṭirunta kaṇṇakiyiṭam aṭintu irukkiyil aṅkē pāṭum pāṇar cilar varakkaṇṭāṉ. avarkaḷ pāṭiya iciyil īṭupaṭṭu iṉiya uriyāṭi, "maturi nakar iṉṉum evvaḷavu tūrattil uḷatu" eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ. avarkaḷ, appōtu vīciya teṉṟaliṉ iṉimiyi vitantu, maturiyil vāḻum vēḷāḷar vaṇikar araciyaṟ celvar antaṇar mutaliyōruṭiya maṉikaḷil eḻum palvēṟu pukiyiṉ maṇam kalantu, pāṇṭi vēntaṉ perumaṉiyil pukikkappaṭum kalavik kūṭṭattu naṟumaṇam viravip
"	pulavar cennāp poruntiya nivappiṉ
 	potiyil teṉṟal pōlātu īṅku
 	maturit teṉṟal vantatu kāṇīr"
eṉṟu urittu, "naṉicēyttaṉṟu tirumali mūtūr" eṉṟu colli iṉpuṟutti, viṭipeṟṟu nīṅkiṉar. piṉpu kavuntiyaṭikaḷuṭaṉ kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum vaḻakkam pōl naṭantu maturiyarukē viyi yāṟṟiṉ vaṭakarikkaṇ puṟañcēriyi neruṅkiṉar. iṭiyē palvakip pūkkaḷ mitantu varaccellum viyi yāṟṟik kaṇṭu, 'puṉal yāṟu aṉṟu itu pūmpuṉalyāṟu" eṉac collak kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum avyāṟṟit toḻutu makiḻntārkaḷ. viyiyiṉ vaṭakariyil niṉṟavarkaḷ, kutiri, yāṉi, ciṅkam  mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mukam poṟitta paṭakukaḷ iyaṅkum nīrttuṟiyik kaṇṭu, ataṉi vilakki, vēṟoru puṇi iyaṅkum tuṟiyi yaṭintu ataṉmēl ēṟi mūvarum āṟṟik kaṭantu ataṉ teṉkariyic cērntu pūmpoḻilum teṅkum vāḻiyum kamukum ākiya palvakic cōlikaḷum cūḻnta puṟañcēriyi yaṭintu aṅkē aṟampuri māntar vāḻum tavappaḷḷi ceṉṟu cērntaṉar. anniliyil vikaṟi eytavum kaḻaṉikaḷilum cōlikaḷilum uṟiyum puḷḷiṉam cilikka kōḻic cēval eḻuntu kūva, ñāyiṟu kiḻakkil eḻuntu tōṉṟiṟṟu.
kōvalaṉ kavuntiyaṭikaḷit toḻutu 'yāṉ maturi nakarkkuṭ ceṉṟu maṉṉar piṉṉōrākiya vaṇikarkaḷik kaṇṭu eṉ niliyi uṇartti varak karutukiṟēṉ. yāṉ tirumpuṅkāṟum kaṇṇaki uṅkaḷ pātukāppiṉaḷātal vēṇṭum " eṉṟu kūṟupavaṉ, taṉatu paḻiya varalāṟṟik kuṟippāyc cuṭṭi varuntiṉāṉ. kavuntiyaṭikaḷ avaṉ maṉam teḷiyum vaṇṇam aṟivuri kūṟi irāmaṉ, naḷaṉ mutaliyōr varalā ṟukaḷic cuṭṭik kāṭṭit tēṟ ṟiṉār. kōvalaṉum avaḷpāl viṭipeṟṟuk koṇṭu maturinakarkkuṭ pukuntu palavēṟu vītikaḷiyum vaṇikar iyalpiyum vāḻvōr vāḻkki amitikaḷiyum kaṇṭu koṇṭu puṟañcēri tirumpiṉāṉ.
kōvalaṉ kavuntiyaṭikaḷik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅki maturinakariṉ māṇpiyum maṉṉaṉākiya pāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya veṟṟic ciṟappiyum viḷampiṉāṉ. appōḻtu, talic ceṅkāṉattu maṟiyavaṉāṉa māṭalaṉ teṉ kumari ceṉṟu nīrāṭi varupavaṉ vaḻinaṭi varuttam tīrakkavunti yuṟiyum iṭattukku vantāṉ. avaṉik kōvalaṉ vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ. talic ceṅkāṉa meṉṉum ūr kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattukku aṇmiyiluḷḷa tākaliṉ, ammāṭalaṉ kōvalaṉi naṉku aṟintiruntāṉ. avaṉ kōvalaṉ mātaviyōṭirunta kāliyil ceyta nallaṟaṅkaḷi eṭuttuc colli
"	immic ceytaṉa yāṉ aṟinalviṉi
 	ummip payaṉkol oru taṉi yuḻantu it
 	tiruttaku māmaṉik koḻuntuṭaṉ pōntatu
 	virutta kōpāla nī eṉa"
viḷampiṉāṉ. anniliyil, kōvalaṉ tāṉ kaṇṭatoru tīkkaṉavik kūṟi varuntiṉāṉ.
anniliyil, kavuntiyum māṭalaṉum kōvalaṉit tēṟṟi, "ivviṭam tuṟavikaṭkallatu maṉiyaṟam pūṇṭa makkaḷ iruttaṟ kuriya taṉṟu. ippakaṟ poḻutu kaḻivataṉ muṉ nakaram pukuntu vaṇikar maṉi yoṉṟik kaṇṭu taṅkutaṟku vaḻiceyka" eṉa urittaṉar. anniliyil, puṟañ ciṟiyiluḷḷa iyakki kōyiṟkuc ceṉṟu pāl maṭi koṭuttu vaḻipaṭṭu varum mātari eṉṉum āyar mutumakaḷ orutti avaṇ pōntu kavuntiyaṭikaḷik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ. avaḷik kaṇṭatum, kavunti avaḷpāl kaṇṇakiyik kiyaṭippaṭukkak karuti "ākāttu ōmpi āp payaṉ aḷikkum kōvalar vāḻkkiyil koṭumiyilli; mātariyum tītilaḷ; mutumakaḷ' avaḷpāl kaṇṇakiyi iruttuvatu naṉṟu;' eṉat taṉakkuḷ eṇṇit tuṇintu, avaḷi nōkki, "mātari, kēḷ, ivaḷuṭiya kaṇavaṉāṉa ikkōvalaṉ tanti peyarik kēṭkiṉ, ivvūrp peruvaṇikar ivarkaḷi virun tēṟṟuk kaṭi maṉippaṭittuvar. atuvari, ivaḷi niṉakku aṭikkalam tantēṉ. ivaḷi nīrāṭṭi, kaṇkaṭku mitīṭṭi, kūntaliṟ pūccūṭṭi, tūya āṭi yuṭuppittu, āyamum kāvalu māvatuṭaṉ tāyum nīyēyākit taṅkutal vēṇṭum. ivaḷō taṉ cīṟaṭi maṇmakaḷaṟiyāta celvak kuṭiyiṟ piṟantu vāḻkkippaṭṭavaḷ. iṉtuṇi makaḷirkku iṉṟiyamiyāta kaṟpukkaṭam pūṇṭa poṟpuṭit teyvam; tavattōr tarum aṭikkalam ciṟi tāyiṉum, ataṉāl viḷivatu mikap pēriṉpam. iṉi nīṭittirātu ivarkaḷik koṇṭu celka" eṉṟāḷ. mātariyum mikka makiḻcciyuṭaṉ iruvariyum tāṉ vāḻum iṭiccērikku aḻittuc ceṉṟāḷ.
aṅkē, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ paṇittavāṟē kaṇṇakiyi nīrāṭṭic cīrāṭṭit taṉ makaḷ aiyi eṉpavaḷik kāṭṭi avaḷi aṭit toḻilāṭṭiyākak koḷka eṉa urittu, poḻutu maṟiyumuṉ uṇavukoḷḷum oḻukkamuṭiyareṉa niṉintu, aṭicil ākkutaṟkeṉa ōr iṭam vakuttu, uṇavu camittaṟku vēṇṭum kalaṅkaḷiyum kāykaṟikaḷiyum tantu ciṟappittāḷ. kaṇṇakiyum naṉmuṟiyil camittuk kōvalaṉi uṇpittāḷ.
uṇavu koṇṭa kōvalaṉukkuk kaṇṇaki mikka aṉpuṭaṉ veṟṟilipākkut tantāḷ. kaṇṇakiyiṉ mukamalarcciyum aṉpuk kiḷarcciyum kōvalaṉukkup pēriṉpatti viḷittaṉa. atē camayattil, kaṇṇakiyik kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiliruntu kāl kaṭukka naṭattiyē koṇṭu vanta taṉ ceyaliyum avaṉ niṉittāṉ; avaṉ maṉam peritum varuntiyatu. taṉṉi nontu koṇṭāṉ. kaṇkalaṅkak kaṇṇakiyit taṉ arukē iruttik kūntalik kiyāl nīvi,
"	irumutu kuravar ēvalum piḻittēṉ;
 	ciṟumutuk kuṟivikkuc ciṟumiyum ceytēṉ;
 	vaḻuveṉum pārēṉ; mānar maruṅku īṇṭu
 	eḻuka eṉa eḻuntāy; eṉ ceytaṉi! eṉa"
maṉamāra vurittu maṟukiṉāṉ. kaṇṇakiyum maṉattiṇmi karintu avaṉiyiṉṟit taṉintirunta taṉ vāḻvi niṉittu curuṅkiya coṟkaḷāl, "illiruntu aṟavōrkkaḷittal mutaliya nallaṟaṅkaḷic ceyyum vāyppiḻantu maṉikkaṇ taṉittirunta eṉṉi niṉ tantiyār uḷḷattē mikka aṉpōṭu nōkkiyum aruḷ niṟinta moḻikaḷi vaḻaṅkiyum pārāṭṭiṉar. eṉ maṉanōyum tuṉpamum avarkaṭkup pulaṉākāvāṟu maṟittup poymmuṟuval ceytēṉ; atu kaṇṭu avar peruntuṉpam uḻantu pētuṟṟār. aṅṅaṉam avarkaḷellām varunta, nīṅkaḷ pōṟṟā oḻukkamē pūṇṭiruntīrkaḷ. evvakiyilum uṅkaḷ pāl vitta eṉ uḷḷatti oru ciṟitum māṟṟāta vāḻkkiyil iruntēṉ; ataṉāl, eḻuka eṉṟu col vantavuṭaṉ ataṉi ēṟṟu eḻuntēṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. accoṟkaḷ kōvalaṉ varuttatti ōraḷavu māṟṟiṉa. avaṉukkuk kaṇṇaki pāl kari kāṇāk kātalaṉpu poṅkip perukiṟṟu; avaṉ maṉattil niṟintirunta avalamum kavaliyum aṟavē nīṅkiṉa; aṉpum ārvamum avaṉ uṇarvu muṟṟum kavarntaṉa. ataṉāl, avaṉ kaṇṇakiyuṭaṉ iṉiya uriyāṭi aṉṟu maṇa amaḷimēl iruntu pārāṭṭiya niliyi eyti,
"	nāṇamum maṭaṉum nallōr ēttum
 	pēṇiya kaṟpum peruntuṇiyāka
 	eṉṉōṭu pōntu eṉtuyarkaḷinta 
 	poṉṉē koṭiya puṉipūṅkōtāy
 	nāṇiṉ pāvāy nīṇila viḷakkē
 	kaṟpiṉ koḻuntē poṟpiṉ celvi" 
eṉṟu pārāṭṭi makiḻntāṉ.
piṉpu, maturikkup puṟappaṭuṅkāl, "cilampu mutalākac ceṉṟa kalaṉōṭu ulanta poruḷ īṭṭutal uṟṟēṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiya vāṟē kaṇṇakiyiṉ kāṟcilampukaḷil oṉṟi vāṅkik koṇṭu maturi nakarkku puṟappaṭṭāṉ. celluṅkāl kaṇṇakiyit taṉiyē viṭuttuc celvataṟkum taṉṉiliyiṉi niṉittum varuntiṉa kōvalaṉ, etirē timil ēṟu oṉṟu tīkkuṟiyāka vantataṉi niṉiyātu pōyk koṇṭirukkiyil pīṭikit teruvil palar piṉ toṭara varum poṟ kollaṉik kaṇṭu avaṉpāl taṉ cilampik kāṭṭi vilipiṭṭut tarumāṟu kēṭṭāṉ. iḵtu aracamātēvikkallātu piṟarkku yāppuṟavu illi' eṉṟu colli, avaṉit taṉ maṉikkaṇ irutti vēntaṉ tirumuṉ ceṉṟu kōvalaṉik kaḷvaṉ eṉa avaṉ maṉaṅkoḷḷumāṟu ēṟpaṉa collik koli yāṇi peṟṟu vantu koliñarkku avaṉik kāṭṭik koṭuttāṉ. avarkaḷ avaṉatu uruvam tiruvum kuṟiyum kaṇṭu aiyuṟṟu uriyāṭukiyil avaruḷ kallāk kaḷimakaṉ oruvaṉ kōvalaṉic carēleṉa vāḷāṟ koṉṟoḻittāṉ. uṭaṉē icceyti kāṭṭut tīppōl nakareṅkaṇum parantatu.
kaṇṇakiyirunta āyccēriyil cila turnimittam kaṇṭu āyar kuravik kūttu āṭaluṟṟaṉar. appoḻutu mātari nīrāṭaṟku āṟṟukkuc ceṉṟavaḷ aṅkē maturiyiliruntu vanta orutti kōvalaṉ koliyuṇṭa ceytiyit terivikkak kēṭṭup peritum varunti virintu pōntu kaṇṇakikku ataṉik kuṟippāyt terivittāḷ. kaṇṇaki patipatittut  tuṭittu vīḻntu; alaṟiṉāḷ; kataṟiṉāḷ; kaṇṇīr āṟākap pulampiṉāḷ,
"	iṉpuṟu tamkaṇavar iṭareri yakammūḻkat
 	tuṉpuṟuvaṉa nōṟṟut tuyaruṟum makaḷirippōl
 	maṉpati alartūṟṟa maṉṉavaṉ tavaṟiḻippa
 	aṉpaṉi iḻantēṉyāṉ avalam koṇṭu aḻivalō
 	naṟimali viyaṉmārpiṉ naṇpaṉi iḻantu ēṅkit
 	tuṟipala tiṟamūḻkit tuyaruṟum makaḷirippōl
 	maṟaṉoṭu tiriyuṅkōl maṉṉavaṉ tavaṟiḻippa
 	aṟaṉeṉum maṭavōy yāṉ avalaṅkoṇṭu aḻivalō
 	tammuṟu peruṅkaṇavaṉ taḻaleri yakamūḻkak
 	kimmikūr tuṟimūḻkum kavaliya makaḷirippōl
 	cemmiyiṉ ikantakōl teṉṉavaṉ tavariḻippa
 	immiyum iciyorīi iṉintēṅki yaḻivalō."
eṉṟu vāyviṭṭu araṟṟiṉāḷ. ciṟitu tēṟi, cūḻntu niṉṟa āymakaḷiri nōkki, "āy makaḷirē, ellōrum kēṇmiṉ" eṉṟu colli, ñāyiṟṟi nōkki, "kāy katirc celvaṉē, kaḷvaṉō eṉ kaṇavaṉ?" eṉṟu kaṉaṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ. "niṉ kaṇavaṉ kaḷvaṉ allaṉ; avaṉik kaḷvaṉ eṉṟu koṉṟa ivvūrtīkku iriyākum" eṉṟoru kural akkūṭṭattil eḻuntatu.
piṉpu, kaṇṇaki taṉpāl irunta maṟṟoru cilampik kiyil ēntikkoṇṭu virintu maturi nakar peruvītikkuṭ ceṉṟu, ōveṉak kataṟik kaṇṇīrum kampaliyumāka varum avaḷi aṅkē uṟiyum naṉmakaḷir tattam maṉivāyilil niṉṟu nōkkuvatu kaṇṭāḷ. "maturiyil vāḻum pattiṉip peṇkaḷē, kēṇmiṉ: eṉkāṟcilampik kavarntukoḷḷum poruṭṭu eṉ kaṇavaṉik kaḷvaṉeṉṟu koli ceytu viṭṭaṉar; eṉ kaṇavaṉ kaḷvaṉ allaṉ; itō, ippeṇkaḷ kāṇak kolikkaḷam ceṉṟu eṉ kaṇavaṉik kaṇṭu avaṉ vāyiṉ nalluri kēṭkappōkiṉṟēṉ" eṉṟu kataṟip pulampik kaṭṭurittāḷ. avaḷ nilikaṇṭa makaḷir aṉivarum, "ivaṭku ipperun tuyaram uṇṭākumāṟu namatu maṉṉavaṉ ceṅkōl vaḷintu viṭṭatē! ceṅkiyil poṟcilampu ēntit teruvil tirintu varum ivaḷ teyvamō?" eṉac collit tuyaruṟṟaṉar. cūḻntu varum makkaḷ kāṭṭak kaṇṇaki nērē kolikkaḷam, kaṇṭa ṭintāḷ. poḻutum maṟintatu; māliyiruḷum vantu mayaṅku vatāyiṟṟu.
koliyuṇṭu kiṭakkum kōvalaṉ uṭalik kaṇṭatum kaṇṇaki koṇṭa tuyarattukku elliyilli. avaṉ uṭal mēl viḻuntāḷ: puraṇṭāḷ; ataṉit taḻuvikkoṇṭu kallum pullum kaṇṭu urukumāṟu vāyviṭṭup pulampiṉāḷ. "eṉ tuyar kaṇṭum ivaḷ iṭarppaṭukiṉṟāḷē eṉṟu collu kiṉṟīrilli; uṭalellām puḻuti paṭintiruppatu kāṇkiṉṟēṉ; uṅkaḷ naṟumēṉi poṭiyāṭik kiṭappatō?" eṉṟāḷ. cuṟṟu muṟṟum nōkkiṉāḷ. oruvarum illāmi kaṇṭu varuntiṉāḷ; kōvalaṉ uṭalam kuruti tōyntu maṇpaṭintiruppatu kaṇṭu maṉan tuṭittāḷ. "ceṅkōl tavaṟiyateṉṉavaṉ maturiyil pattiṉip peṇṭirō, cāṉṟōrkaḷō, teyvaṅkaḷō, oṉṟum illiyō?" eṉṟu collik koṇṭu, avaṉ taliyi uṭalōṭu cērttut taḻuvip piṭittāḷ; oruvaki mayakkam talip paṭṭatu. kōvalaṉ oḷi viḷaṅkum mēṉiyuṭaṉ tōṉṟi, avaḷ kaṇṇīrittuṭittu "muḻu mati pōṉṟa niṉmukam kaṉṟiyatu, kāṇ' eṉṟu colli, "iru " eṉak kiyāl amarttit tēvar kūṭṭam cūḻa viṇṇakam ceṉṟāṉ. kaṇṇaki maṉam teḷintu, "māyaṅkol maṟṟu eṉ kol? teyvam maruṭṭiṟṟō? iṉi eṅkē pōy nāṭuvēṉ" eṉṟu vāyveruviṉāḷ. iṉik kōvalaṉ mēl cumattappaṭṭa kuṟṟattit tuṭippatu mutaṟkaṭaṉ eṉat tuṇintāḷ. vēn taṉikkaṇṭu vaḻakkurittu uṇmiyi veḷippaṭuttaṉṟi veṟitē pōkēṉ" eṉṟu nērē vēntaṉ aracu vīṟṟirukkum iṭam nōkkic ceṉṟāḷ.
nāḷum kiḻamiyum nalintōrkku ētu? kaṇṇaki ceṉṟa pōtu, aracamātēvi tīkkaṉā oṉṟu kaṇṭu uḷḷam naṭuṅki vēntaṉukkuc collik koṇṭiruntāḷ. kaṇṇaki ceṉṟu, vāyilōṉik kaṇṭu "kaṇavaṉiyiḻantavaḷ orutti kiyiṟ cilampēntiya vaṇṇam vantirukkiṉṟāḷ eṉṟu muṟi tiṟampiya niṉ vēntarkku moḻika" eṉṟu kaṇkaḷil tīppoṟi paṟakka moḻintāḷ. avaḷuṭiya talivirikōlattiyum kaṉaṟṟum kaṇkaḷiyum veṭikkum coṟkaḷiyum nōkki, koṟṟaviyō eṉa añci vēntaṉukkurittuk kaṇṇakiyiyum vēntaṉ tirumuṉ koṇṭu niṟuttiṉāṉ. tēvi kaṇṭa tīkkaṉavu kēṭṭu maṉam teḷiviṉṟi yiruntāṉāyiṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṇṇakiyip pārttu, "kaṇṇīr oḻuka, eṉ muṉ vanta nīyār? " eṉa avaṉ viṉavavum, eṉatu ūr pūmpukār; avvūriluḷḷa mācāttu vāṇikaṉ makaṉum, vāḻtal vēṇṭi niṉ maturi nakarkkum pukuntu eṉ kāṟcilampi viṟka muyaṉṟu niṉpāl kolikkaḷap paṭṭavaṉumākiya kōvalaṉ maṉiviyāvēṉ; eṉ peyar kaṇṇaki eṉpatu" eṉṟāḷ.uṭaṉē vēntaṉ, "kaḷvaṉikkōṟal kaṭuṅkōl aṉṟu; vēntar kōl muṟi" eṉṟāṉ. "vēntē, pala kūṟuvāṉēṉ; eṉ kāṟcilampiṉ ari māṇikkamaṇi" eṉṟu kaṇṇaki kūṟa, teṉṉavaṉ, ā eṉak kataṟi, "yām uṭic cilampu muttuṭiyatu" eṉṟu, poṟkollaṉ kōvalaṉ kiyakatti liruntatu eṉak kāṭṭit tanta cilampiṉik koṇarvittuk kaṇṇaki muṉvikka, avaḷ ataṉi eṭuttu ōṅki nilattil eṟintu uṭittāḷ; uṭaṉē ataṉuḷ irunta māṇikkamaṇi veḷippaṭṭu maṉṉaṉ vāyil teṟittatu. uṇmi teḷintatum vēntaṉ tali cuḻaṉṟatu; avalam mītūrntatu; uṭal taḷarntāṉ; tāṉ ceyta perumpiḻiyi eṇṇiṉāṉ. amiccar col kēṭṭu aṟampuriya vēṇṭiyatāṉa atu ceyyā toḻinta kuṟṟam avaṉ neñciṉi iṭittatu. "poṉceykollaṉ taṉcol kēṭṭa yāṉō aracaṉ; yāṉē kaḷvaṉ; maṉpati kākkum teṉpulam kāval, eṉ mutal piḻittatu; keṭuka eṉ āyuḷ' eṉṟu collik kīḻē vīḻntāṉ; avaṉ uyirum uṭaṉē nīṅkiṟṟu. arukil niṉṟa aracamātēvi, "kaṇavaṉi iḻantōrkkuk kāṭṭuvatu il" eṉṟu colli vīḻntu uyir tuṟantāḷ. kaṇṇaki,
"	kāviyuru nīrum kiyil taṉic cilampum
 	āvikuṭipōṉa avvaṭivam -pāviyēṉ
 	kāṭellām cūḻnta karuṅkuḻalum kaṇṭu añcik
 	kūṭalāṉ kūṭāyiṉāṉ"
eṉṟu moḻintu, kōpperuntēviyi nōkki,
"	allavi ceytārkku aṟam kūṟṟamām eṉṉum
 	pallaviyōr collum paḻutaṉṟē- pollā
 	viṭuviṉiyē ceyta vayavēntaṉ tēvi
 	kaṭuviṉiyēṉ ceyvatūum kāṇ"
eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ. ataṉōṭu avaḷuṭiya ciṉam taṇiyavilli. vīḻntu kiṭakkum tēviyip pārttu,
"	kōvēntaṉ tēvi koṭuviṉiyāṭṭiyēṉ
 	yāvum teriyā iyalpiṉēṉ; āyiṉum
 	muṟpakal ceytāṉ piṟaṉkēṭu taṉkēṭu
 	piṟpakal kāṇkuṟūum peṟṟiyakāṇ."
eṉṟu icittu, uḷḷat teḻunta vekuḷittī uruttuvara,
"	paṭṭāṅku yāṉum ōrpattiṉiyē yāmākil
 	oṭṭēṉ aracōṭu ōḻippēṉ maturiyum eṉ
 	paṭṭimiyum kāṇkuṟuvāy nī"
eṉṟu teḻittu, nakaramakkaḷi nōkki, "eṉ kātalaṉit tavaṟu kāṭṭik koliceyta ikkōnakaric cīṟiṉēṉ; ātalāl yāṉ tavaṟuṭiyēṉallēṉ" eṉa varunti yurittut taṉ iṭamārpit tiruki erintāḷ. nakariṭattē uṭaṉē tīyeḻuntatu. atu kaṇṭu, "tīyē, pārppār; aṟavōr, pacu, pattiṉip peṇṭir, mūttōr, kuḻavi eṉṟa ivarik kiviṭṭut tīttiṟattār pakkamē cērka" eṉṟu colli avviṭattiṉiṉṟum nīṅkiṉāḷ, avaḷ kūṟiya iṭamellām tīyeḻuntu cūḻntatu.
ivvāṟu eḻunta tī aracaṉ araṇmaṉi vāyili neruṅkiṟṟu. aracaṉum tēviyum iṟantati aṟiyātu aṟakkaḷattu antaṇarum kāviti mantirakkaṇakkarum, araṇmaṉi mākkaḷum makaḷirum maṉam kalaṅkic ceyalaṟṟoḻintaṉar. ceyvaki teriyātu kaṇṇaki kaṇavaṉ pirivāṟṟāmal pulampiyavaṇṇam terukkaḷil tiriyak kaṇṭa perumaṉi naṉmakaḷir, "ivaḷ ennāṭṭāḷō? yār makaḷō? innāṭṭu ivvūril taṉ kaṇavaṉi iḻantu vēntaṉiyum taṉ kāṟcilampāl veṉṟu ivvūril tīmūṭṭiṉāḷ" eṉṟu viyantu kūṟiṉar. appōtu kaṇṇakiyuṟṟa varuttatti, "maṟu kiṭimaṟukum ; kavaliyiṟ kavalum; iyaṅkalum iyaṅkum, mayaṅkalum mayaṅkum" eṉṟu iḷaṅ kōvaṭikaḷ iraṅkik kūṟuvar."
nakaravītiyil naḷḷiravil tiriyum kaṇṇakimuṉ maturi nakarat teyvam tōṉṟi, avaḷ ciṉam taṇiyattakka coṟkaḷiyum avaḷuṭiya muṟpiṟappu varalāṟṟiyum eṭutturittu,
"ummi viṉivantu urutta kālic
	cemmiyi lōrkkuc ceytavam utavātu" 
eṉṟu teḷivittu, "iṉi, patiṉāṉku nāḷ elli kaḻintapiṉ vāṉōr vaṭivil niṉ kaṇavaṉikkāṇ pāy" eṉṟu collavē, "eṉ karuttil uṟiyum kaṇavaṉik kaṇṭapiṉṉaṉṟi, yāṉ iruttalum illēṉ; niṟṟalum nillēṉ" eṉak kataṟik koṇṭē ceṉṟu maturik koṟṟaviyiṉ kōyilik kuṟuki, vāyilil taṉpoṟ ṟoṭiyi uṭitterintuviṭṭu, "kīḻttici
vāyil kaṇava ṉoṭu pukuntēṉ; mēṟṟicivāyil vaṟiyēṉ peyarkiṉṟēṉ'' eṉṟu colli viyik kariyit toṭarntu celvāḷāyiṉaḷ.
mēṭu eṉṟum paḷḷameṉṟum nōkkātu kaṇṇīrum kampaliyumāy ippōtu curuḷimali yeṉa vaḻaṅkum neṭuvēḷ kuṉṟam ēṟi "aṅkē oru vēṅkimara niḻaliyaṭintāḷ. inniliyil maturit teyvam kūṟiya patiṉāṉku nāḷ elli kaḻintatu. mālippoḻutu neruṅkavum "kaṇavaṉik kaṇṭu kitoḻum nāḷ itu" eṉa eṇṇi niṉṟapōtu, vāṉattiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiya tēvarkaḷiṭiyē kōvalaṉ varakkaṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭa kaṇṇaki avaṉōṭē vāṉavūrti ēṟi viṇṇulaka kaṭintāḷ.
innikaḻcciyi akkuṉṟattil vāḻum kuṉṟavar kaṇṭu kuraviyāṭi avaḷi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. avviṭattē cēra maṉṉaṉāṉa ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ malivaḷam kāṇum viruppāl taṉ tēviyuṭaṉ vantāṉāka, avaṟku avarkaḷ terivippa, avaṉōṭu irunta taṇṭamiḻ ācāṉāṉa cāttaṉār maturi nikaḻcci muṟṟum viriyak kūṟiṉār. pāṇṭiyaṉip pārāṭṭi, kaṇṇaki pāṇṭimātēvi eṉṟa iruvaruṭaṉ niṉṉāl viyakkappaṭum nalattōr yār?" eṉṟu avaṉ taṉ tēviyi nōkkik kēṭka, avaḷ, "kātalaṉ tuṉpam kāṇātu kaḻinta pāṇṭimātēvi vāṉattum peruntiruvuṟuka; nam nāṭu pōnta ippattiṉik kaṭavuḷip paracal vēṇṭum" eṉṟāḷ.
avaḷ colliyavāṟē ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭa nāṭu ceṉṟu cerukkiṉāl tamiḻari ikaḻntu pēciya vaṭavari veṉṟu imayattil kalleṭuttuk kaṅkiyāṟṟil nīrāṭṭi aṅkē pōrtoṭuttut tōṟṟa vēntar muṭimēl vittuk koṇarntu, kaṇṇakiyiṉ tiruvuruvatti ataṉ kaṇ poruntac camivittāṉ.
"	kiviṉi muṟṟiya teyvap paṭimattu
 	vittakar iyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kōlattu
 	muṟṟiḻi naṉkalam muḻutum pūṭṭip
 	pūppali ceytu kāppuk kaṭi niṟutti
 	vēḷviyum viḻāvum nāṭoṟum vakuttuk
 	kaṭavuḷ maṅkalam ceytaṉaṉ."
"	teyvam toḻā aḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuvāḷit
  	teyvam toḻum takimi tiṇṇitāl - teyvamāy
  	maṇṇaka mātark kaṇiyāya kaṇṇaki
  	viṇṇaka mātarkku viruntu."

29. tuṟavi maṇimēkali
kōvalaṉ maturi nakarkkaṇ kolikkaḷap paṭṭatum, kaṇṇaki pāṇṭivēntaṉpāl vaḻakkurittu ammaturiyit tīkkiriyākkiyatum, cēranāṭu ceṉṟu viṇṇulaku pukuntatum tamiḻakameṅkum paraviṉa. acceyti kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉatti eṭṭiyatum, kōvalaṉ kātali yākiya mātavi, annakarkaṇ irunta puttap paḷḷiyil aṟavaṇa aṭikaḷ eṉṟa putta cāṉṟōr pāl pōtiyaṟam kēṭṭut tuṟavu pūṇṭāḷ. avaṭkuk kōvalaṉ pāṟ piṟanta maṇimēkali eṉṉum makaḷ āṭal pāṭal aḻaku eṉṟa kalikaḷil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉāḷ.
āṇṭu tōṟum naṭipeṟuvatu pōl kāvirippūm paṭṭi ṉattil intira viḻā naṭipeṟṟatu. nakara vītikaḷ naṉku aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. pūraṇa kumpaṅkaḷum poṟpālikikaḷum pāviviḷakkukaḷum terukkaḷil ciṟappuṟṟaṉa. kulikkamukum, kulivāḻiyum, karumpum palvēṟu koṭi vakikaḷum maṉi vāyilkaḷil kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. terukkaḷilum maṉṟaṅkaḷilum paḻamaṇali nīkkip putumaṇal parappiṉar. ellākkōyilkaḷilum viḻāvum pūciyum iṉitu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. camaya vātikaḷum aṟivuriyāḷarkaḷum āṅkāṅkuk kūṭiyiruntu camayavātamum aṟivuriyum nikaḻttiṉar. icik kaḻakaṅkaḷil iṉṉiciyum nāṭaka araṅkukaḷil palvaki kūttum naṭipeṟṟaṉa. eṅkum yāvarum ceṟṟamum ciṉamum maṟantu iṉpa aṉpuṟavu koṇṭu makiḻtaṉar.
ici nāṭakac ciṟappukkaḷil talikkōṟ paṭṭam peṟṟa mātaviyum avaḷ makaḷ maṇimēkaliyum kalantu koḷḷavilli. ataṉāl nakarattavarkkup periyatōr ēmāṟṟam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. palarum palavēṟu vakiyiṟ pēcikkoṇṭaṉar.
ūravar kūṟuvatu kēṭṭu maṉam poṟāta mātaviyiṉ tāyāṉa cittirāpati vayantamāli yeṉṉum avaḷ tōḻiyi, nōkki "nī mātavipāṟ ceṉṟu avaḷ intira viḻā nikaḻcciyil kalantu koḷḷāmal oṭuṅkiyiruppatu paṟṟi ūravar alar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉa urittu varuka" eṉac colliviṭuttāḷ. avaḷ mātaviyum maṇimēkaliyum malar maṇṭapattil iruppataṟintu aṅkē ceṉṟu kaṇṭāḷ. aṅkē mātavi putta camayat tuṟavukkōlam pūṇṭu virataṅkaḷāl vāṭiya mēṉiyuṭaṉ iruppatu kaṇṭatum vayanta mālikku viyappuṇṭāyiṟṟu.
"	kālak kaṇitamum kalikaḷiṉ tuṇivum
 	nāṭaka makaḷirkku naṉkaṉam vakutta
 	ōviyac cennūl urinūṟ kiṭakkiyum
 	kaṟṟut tuṟi pōkiya poṟṟoṭi naṅki
 	naṟṟavam purintatu nāṇuṭitteṉṟu"
ūravar alar kūṟuvatu nayamuṭiyataṉṟu" eṉṟu avaḷ mātavikku eṭutturittāḷ
