aṟivuttuṟiyil mēṉmiyuṟṟirukkum nāṭukaḷil, makkaḷ, tam ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ iḷamiyiṉi mikakkaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyk kuṟittu, appōtē, avartam aṟivu cemmi neṟiyil vaḷara vēṇṭum eṉṟu muyalkiṉṟaṉar. ciṟārkaḷ ciṟitu pēcat toṭaṅkiyutam, pāṭṭicippatil peritum viruppuṟṟiruppati yuṇarntu. avarkaṭkēṟṟa pāṭṭukkaḷip pāṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. nam nāṭṭu makaḷirum tam ciṟu kuḻantikaṭkup pala pāṭṭukkaḷip pāṭi makiḻcci yūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa reṉiṉum avi tiruntiya muṟiyil varutal vēṇṭum. tāyar palarum kalviyaṟivu illātiruttaliṉ, appāṭṭukkaḷ cennili yeytāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa. mēlum, nam nāṭṭu makaḷir iṉiya tūya pāṭṭukkaḷ tāmē iyaṟṟuvatil ciṟantavarāvar. yāvarēṉum oruvar tam kuṭumpattil iṟantu pōvarēl, avarukkāka immakaḷir tāmē puṉintu pāṭum “oppārikaḷi” nuṉittu nōkkiṉ, ivarkaḷ iṉiya pāṭṭuk kaḷip puṉitalil taliciṟanta vaṉmiyuṭiyar eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. ivvaṉmiyi ivarkaḷ, ciṟuvarkaṭkuriya aṟivu vaḷarccit tuṟiyiṟ celuttippāṭat toṭaṅkuvarēl, tamiḻk kuḻantikaḷ mikka aṟivu nalam peṟuvar eṉṟu uṟuti kūṟalām. paṭitta peṇṇorutti tāṉ payanta kuḻantiyik kiyilēnti, taṉ vīṭṭiṉ tōṭṭattiliruntu pāṭikkoṇṭirunta pāṭṭoṉṟik kīḻē tarutum;
“	tōṭṭattilē mulli 
 	tōṉṟiyatuṉ pallik
 	kāṭṭiyatuṉ colli
 	kāṭṭiyatu kiḷḷi”
“	ammā(ḷ) koñcam pācci
 	aṭuppil (vittuk) kāycci
 	kummāḷamāyp pē(c)cik
 	koṭuttā(ḷ) paci pōcci”
ivvāṟu aḻakiya maḻalip pāṭṭukkaḷ (Nursery rhymes) palavaṟṟi ākkakkūṭiya tamiḻvaṉmiyi makaḷir peṟutal vēṇṭum. ippāṭṭukkaḷiyum immakaḷirē puṉital vēṇṭum. mēṉāṭṭavaruḷḷum iṉṉōraṉṉa pāṭṭukkaḷuḷ perum pālaṉavaṟṟi makaḷirē yāttirukkiṉṟaṉar. ippāṭṭukkaḷ tāmum iyaṟkip poruḷ, ceyaṟkip poruḷ, ciṟukatikaḷ eṉṟa ivaṟṟip poruḷākak koṇṭiruttalvēṇṭum. nīrāṭaṟkum, neṟkuṟṟutaṟkum, pūkkoytaṟkum, poṟcuṇṇa miṭittaṟkum pāṭṭicittup paṇpumēmpaṭṭirunta tamiḻ makaḷirkku ivi ariyavalla; ivaṟṟi ākkik kaṟpittuppāṭi makiḻtaṟkuriya tamiḻppayiṟci ivarpāl āra illāmiyē kāraṇam. iṉiyēṉum makaḷir ippaṇiyil iṟaṅkit tam kaṭamiyi āṟṟupavarātal vēṇṭum.
eḻuttaṟivikkum toṭakkap pāṭacālik kalvikku immakaḷir āṟṟakkūṭiya peruntoṇṭu oṉṟu uḷatu. attoṇṭu illāmiyāl, nam nāṭṭil toṭakkak kalvi vaḷampeṟātu cīrkulintu niṟkiṟatu.
iḷañciṟārkaḷ piṟantu moḻi payiṉṟu varuṅkālattu avarōṭu uṭaṉiruntu, uṭaṉ moḻintu, uṭaṉ payiṉṟu oḻukum oḻukkac ciṟappu makaḷirkkē peritum uṇṭu. ammakaḷir tamiḻaṟivu peruka vuṭiyarāyiṉ, acciṟuvarkaḷ moḻi payilat toṭaṅkuvatu mutal tam karuttukkaḷi oruvāṟu veḷiyiṭat toṭaṅkuvatu variyil avar colvaḻakkil varum coṟkaḷi avvappōtu kuṟittu, kālantōṟum avi avar vaḻakkil iṭampeṟum takutikkēṟpa, vakippaṭutti varuvatu oṉṟu. piṉṉar, accoṟ kaḷē vara, maḻalip pāṭṭum katiyum puṉintu pēcap payiṟci ceyvatu oṉṟu. iṉṉōraṉṉa paṇikaḷ makkaḷiṉ kalvip payiṟcikkup peruntuṇi nalkuvaṉavākum. makkaḷiṉ celva nilikkēṟpa,  ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ takutippāṭu vēṟupaṭum. ataṉāl avvav niliyiluḷḷa kuṭumpap peṇkaḷ ippaṇiyiṉi yāṟṟiṉ, kalvi vaḷarcci vaḷampeṟum.celva nalam mikkuḷḷa nāṭukaḷil, ivviḷañ ciṟārkaḷi oruṅku tokuttu ippaṇi purivittal eḷitil iyalum annalam kuṟintuḷḷa nam nāṭṭil, makaḷirē ipperum paṇiyiṉi āṟṟutal nalamākum. ittakiya toṇṭellām iṉitu  ceytaṟkuriya aṟivamiti, peruṅ kalviyaṟivu peṟṟavarkkē iṉitu kikūṭuvatākum. ātalāl, innilimiyiṉi iyalpākavē vāykkappeṟṟa makaḷir, nirampiya kalviyaṟivu peṟṟut tam makkaḷiṉ aṟivu vaḷarccip paṇiyākiya itaṟkut tuṇi ceyyavēṇṭumeṉa viḻikiṉṟēm.  pēraṟivum uṇmi yuḻippumuṭiya āciriyaṉmār ittuṟiyil uḻittut tuṇipuriya muṟpaṭuvarēl, “aḵtu avar yāppiṉuḷ aṭṭiya nīr” eṉpēm.
tamiḻ makaḷir taṅkaḷ kuṭumpap paṇṭi varalāṟukaḷiyum, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟukaḷiyum kaṟṟum kēṭṭum pulami mikkavarāy iruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉāl, avarkaḷ tam vayiṟṟiṟ piṟakkum ciṟārkaṭku aṟivum vīravuṇarcciyum, viṉittiṭpamum, avvaralāṟukaḷ vāyilāka vūṭṭi uṇmit tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷākac ceytal muṭiyum. ikkālat tamiḻc ciṟuvaṉ oruvaṉi viḷittu, “uṉ tantikkut tanti ceyta aruñceyal yātu? avaratu peṟṟōr peyar eṉṉi? uṅkaḷ muṉṉōruḷ mikkaciṟappuṟṟi runtavar evarēṉum uḷarō?” eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavaṟṟi viṉaviṉ, avaṉ aṟiyātu maruḷa viḻittalik kāṇkiṉṟōm. āṅkila naṉmakaṉ oruvaṉi viṉaviṉ, avaṉ taṉ muṉṉōr varalāṟṟiyum, ceyta ceyal vakikaḷiyum perumitattōṭu collak kēṭkiṉṟōm. avaṟṟik kūṟumpōtu avaṉ mukattilum, viḻiyilum, uṭalilum tōṉṟum oḷiyum, meyppāṭum, av vuṇarcci ettuṉip perum payaṉi avaṉatu vāḻviṟkum ākkattiṟkum utavukiṉṟateṉpataṉi iṉitu kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. mūtiṉmulli, ēṟāṇmulli, vallāṇmulli yeṉpaṉavākiya tuṟi vakuttu, āṭavar makaḷir eṉṟa irupālār vīramum, kuṭippiṟantār vīramum, avar tam ūravar vīramum uyarttip pēci maṟañcerukki viḷaṅkiyavar tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷ. “munti mutalvar kaltāṉ kāṭṭi, mūtil maṭavāḷ, taṉ putalvaṉic celka eṉṟāḷ pōrkku” eṉṟum, “kal niṉṟāṉ enti, kaṇavaṉ kaḷappaṭṭāṉ, - muṉ niṉṟu moyyavintār eṉṉiyar - piṉ niṉṟu, kipōyk kaṇiyutippak kāvalaṉ mēlōṭi, eypōl kiṭantāṉ eṉ ēṟu” eṉṟum varum paḻam pāṭalkaḷ, tamiḻ makaḷirkku ippayiṟci putuvataṉṟu; paṇṭēyuḷḷatu eṉṟu yāvarum aṟiya viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.

33. caṅkakālattuc centamiḻc celvi
tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu kāṇpavar, iv varalāṟṟi varalāṟṟu neṟipaṟṟiyum, ilakkiya neṟipaṟṟiyum ārāyvatuṇṭu. ivaṟṟuḷ varalāṟṟu neṟikkuriya karuvikaḷ palavum cevviyākak kiṭikkāmiyāl, varalāṟum ilakkiyamum orucērak koṇṭu kāṇpar. ammuṟiyē nōkkiṉ, tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu, caṅkakālam, ilakkiyakālam purāṇakālam, pirapanta kālam eṉap pakuttuk koḷḷappaṭum. caṅkakālattukkuk karuviyākac caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷik koḷvar. ilakkiya kālattukkuc cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali, cintāmaṇi, cūḷāmaṇi, nīlakēci, peruṅkati, irāmāyaṇam, makāpāratam mutaliyavaṟṟik koḷvar, purāṇam eṉṟatu tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam, kantapurāṇam, pākavata purāṇam mutalākat talapurāṇaṅkaḷ īṟākap palavakip purāṇaṅ kaḷiyumākum. pirapantaṅkaḷ, kumarakuruparar, civappirakācar mutaliya cāṉṟōr pāṭiya ciṟunūlkaḷum vēṟupalar eḻutiyuḷḷa antāti, māli, ulā kōvi mutaliyaṉavumākum. tēvāra tiruvācakaṅkaḷum, tirumuṟikaḷum, nālāyirat tivya pirapantam, tēcikar pirapantam mutaliyaṉavum ēṟṟampeṟṟi mēṟkoḷḷappaṭum.
ivāvaṟu palavakiyākat tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu ārāyap paṭumāyiṉum, kālattālum varalāṟṟu neṟiyālum piṟavaki yālum kāṇpārkkuc caṅkakālamē mikat toṉmi vāyntateṉpatu orutali. immuṭipiṉkaṇ karuttu vēṟupāṭu iruppatākat tōṉṟavilli. evvaki ārāycciyāḷarum caṅkakālattu ilakkiyaṅkaḷi mēṟkoḷvatil mikka ārvamē aṭikiṉṟaṉar. ikkālamum ēṟakkuṟiya iraṇṭāyiramāṇṭu kaṭku muṉṉateṉpatu ettiṟattārkkum oppamuṭinta uṇmiyākum.
iṉi, ivvilakkiya neṟiyil ārāyvārkku ikkāla ārāycciyāḷar cilar cila taṭikaḷi nikaḻttukiṉṟaṉar. ivar ārāycci yuṇmikaḷi viññāṉa neṟiyuṇmi  (Scientific truth) tattuvaneṟiyuṇmi (Philosophic truth), varalāṟṟuṇmi (Historie truth), pulavar vāyuṇmi (Poetic truth) eṉa nāṉkāka vakuttu ivaṟṟiṟku matippum ik kūṟiya muṟiyē tantoḻukuvar. ellā vaṟṟiṟkum iṟutiyākap pulavar vāyuṇmikaḷi niṟuttiyiruppatu kuṟikkattakkatākum. itaṟku avarkaḷ ciṟanta kāraṇamāka oṉṟu kūṟuvar. “pulavarkaḷ tām kāṇum poruḷi uḷḷavāṟu kūṟātu ciṟitu uyarttiyē kūṟuvar; akkūṟṟil ciṟitu uṇmi yallātatu kalantē irukkum; ataṉāl, ataṉi uḷḷataṉ uṇmi kūṟiyatākavē kōṭaṟkilli” eṉpar.
ivarkaḷ tam muṭipu muḻutum vaṭavar mēlināṭṭavar vaḻakkāṟupaṟṟiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu ceytirukkiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ illatu puṉintu kūṟum vaḻakkum atupaṟṟiya aṇivakiyum piṟavum malintirukkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟu, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷi ivarkaḷ naṉku ārāyntu kaṇṭavar allar. ivi muḻutum uḷḷatāṉ uṇmiyi uḷḷavāṟu kāṭṭum collōviyaṅkaḷ eṉpataṉi aṟintārilli. poyyum vaḻuvum puṉintu ōtupavarip pulavar eṉak koḷvatu tamiḻ marapaṉṟu eṉpatu ivarkaṭkut teriyātu. tam poṟi pulaṉkaṭkup pulaṉākum poruḷi aiyan tiripaṟa ārāyntuṇarttalum, uṇarntataṉi uḷḷavāṟu uṇarttalum uṭiyavarē pulavar eṉappaṭuvar. pulavar eṉṉum coṟporuḷiyum ivarkaḷ naṉku uṇarntiruppatāka ivarkaḷ urikkum urikaḷ kāṭṭavilli. ākavē, ivarkaḷ kūṟṟu nam caṅka ilakkiyattiṟku ēṟpaṉa alla eṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
pulavar vāyuṇmi yurikaḷil cila poyyum viraviyirukku meṉat tām koṇṭa muṭipukaḷi vaṟpuṟuttaṟku ivvārāycci yāḷar cintāmaṇi, irāmāyaṇam mutaliya ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu cila puṉivurikaḷik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. “nummōraṉṉōr māṭṭum, iṉṉa poyyoṭu miṭintavi tōṉṟiṉ meyyāṇṭuḷatō ivvulakattāṉē” (akam.286) eṉṟum, “ceyyā kūṟik kiḷattal, eyyātākiṉṟa em ciṟu cennāvē” (puṟam.148) eṉṟum, “māṅkuṭi marutaṉ talivaṉāka ulakamoṭu niliiya palar pukaḻ ciṟappiṉ, pulavar pāṭātu varika eṉ nilavari” (puṟam.72) yeṉṟum, “pulavar pāṭum pukaḻuṭiyōr vicumpiṉ valavaṉēvā vāṉavūrti yeytupa eṉpa tam ceyviṉi muṭittu” eṉṟum, “tollici nuṇaṅku nuṇ paṉuvaṟ pulavaṉ pāṭiya, iṉamaḻi tavaḻum ēḻiṟkuṉṟam” (akam.345) “ulakuṭaṉ tiritarum palar pukaḻ nallici, vāymoḻik kapilaṉ” (akam. 78) eṉṟum varuvaṉavum, piṟavum paṇṭip pulavaratu vāymiyum, atupaṟṟi avarpāl makkaḷ koṇṭirunta naṉmatippum teṟṟeṉa viḷakkum. ivaṟṟi avarkaḷ uṇmiyaṟivōṭu miṭintavi eṉak karuttil niṉittaṟkum kūṟit tammiyē aruvaruttuk koḷvar.
itaṉāṟ pōntateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, piṟanāṭṭu varalāṟu, annāṭṭut toṉmi,makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkippaṇpu mutaliya vaṟṟi ārāytaṟku vēṇṭumāyiṉ, ivvārāycciyaḷār koḷḷuṅ karuttu, poruntiṟ poruntumēyaṉṟip, paṇṭit tamiḻmakkaṭkuriya caṅka ilakkiyap pulavar vāymoḻi ārāyccikkuc ciṟitum poruntātu eṉpatu vaṟpuṟuttiyavāṟām. ip pulavarkaḷ vaḻaṅki yuḷḷavaṟṟiṉ meymmit taṉmikkuc cāṉṟu kāṭṭaluṟiṉ ik kaṭṭuri viriyumeṉṟañci mēṟ cellukiṉṟām. caṅka ilakkiya iṉkavit tiraṭṭu, kapilar, paraṇar, nakkīrar mutaliya nūlkaḷiyum,* caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum aṟiñar nērē tāmē kaṇṭu teḷintu koḷvatu ciṟappākum.
caṅkakālattuc centamiḻc celvi eṉṟu eṭuttuk koṇṭō māyiṉum, caṅkakālam caṅkanūṟ pulavar irunta kālattiyum avarkaṭku muṉpirunta tolkāppiyar kālattiyum uḷappaṭutti niṟkum. caṅka ilakkiyattuḷ tokukkappeṟṟuḷḷa pāṭṭukkaḷiṟ cila tolkāppiyattukku mūttaṉavākavum irukkalām eṉṟum, cila caṅkakālattukkup piṟpaṭṭaṉavākavum irukkalām eṉṟum kūṟum aṟiñar uṇṭu avar karuttum akappaṭu, tolkāppiyar kālattiyum īṇṭu aṭakkikkoḷḷalām. mēlum, tolkāppiyar tam kālattum tamakku muṉṉōr kālattum vaḻaṅkiya vaḻakka oḻukkaṅkaḷik tam nūlakattē vaṭittuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. avi palavum caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl vaṟpuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
caṅkakālattē nam tamiḻakam nilat takutipaṟṟik kuṟiñci mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli eṉap pakukkappeṟṟiruntatu tolkāppiyattuḷ pālikku nilam kūṟappaṭavilli. eṉiṉum ataṟkup piṉ vanta cilappatikāram pālikku nilam kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. iti nōkkiṉ, tolkāppiyar kālattukkup piṉṉum, cilappati kārattukku muṉṉum pālikku nilamuṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. caṅka ilakkiyam pālikkum ōrāṟṟāl nilam kuṟikkiṉṟatu. ākavē, caṅkakālattē tamiḻakattē aivakip pākupāṭum uṇṭeṉṟu tuṇiyalām.
innilap pākupāṭēpaṟṟi makkaṭcamūkamum pākupāṭu peṟṟiruntatu. kuṟiñci nilattavar kuṟavar eṉṟum, mulliyōr āyar eṉṟum, pāliyōr maṟavar eṉṟum, marutattōr uḻavar eṉṟum, neytalār paratavar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉar.
“āyar vēṭṭuvar āṭūuttiṉip peyar, āvayiṉ varūum kiḻavarum uḷarē” eṉṟu varuvatu tolkāppiyam. ik kuṟavar āyar mutaliyōri vēṭṭuvar, kuṉṟavar, veṟpar, iṭiyar, potuvar, kōvalar mutaliya pala vēṟu peyarkaḷālum cāṉṟōr aḻippar.
ivarkaḷ uyartoḻilum ciṟutoḻilum eṉa iruvakit toḻilum ceytaṉar. uyartoḻiṟkaṇ uyarkalvi kaṟṟu aṟam kūṟutalum, aracāḷalum, vāṇipam ceytalum, uḻavuttoḻil ceytalum, nirimēyttalum, vēṭṭiyāṭalum, tēṉīṭṭalum, mīṉ piṭittalum piṟavum aṭaṅkiṉa. uyar toḻiṟkuriya vāyppiṉ miyāl, attoḻilukkut tuṇipuritalum, uyartoḻilāḷarkku ēvalarātalum, aṭiyarātalum piṟavum ciṟu toḻilkaḷākak karutap paṭṭaṉa.
poruḷ īṭṭalum, ataṉāl aṟam ceytalum iṉpam nukartalum, vāḻkkiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. aṟamē poruḷ iṉpaṅkaḷip peṟutaṟku neṟiyāvatu eṉpatu, “ciṟappuṭi marapiṟ poruḷum iṉpamum, aṟattu vaḻippaṭūum” (puṟam.31) eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. “aṟaṉ kaṭippaṭāa vāḻkkiyum, eṉṟum piṟaṉkaṭic celāc celvamum” (akam. 155) avar vāḻvil mutaliṭam peṟṟiruntaṉa. curuṅkac colliṉ avarkaḷ, “nāḷatu ciṉmiyum, iḷamiya tarumiyum, tāḷāṇ pakkamum, takutiyatu amitiyum, iṉmiyatiḻivum, uṭimiyatu uyarcciyum, aṉpiṉṉa takalamum” (tol.poruḷ cū. 41) naṉku terintiruntaṉar; ītalum icipaṭa vāḻtalumē  uṭalōṭu kūṭi vāḻum uyirkku ūtiyamākak karutiṉar. ītal kuṟittē poruḷīṭṭiṉar; pukaḻpeṟuvatu kuṟittē īki mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. appukaḻiṉ aḻiyāmi yaṟintu, atu peṟuvatu kuṟittup pōr uṭaṟṟiṉar. “pukaḻeṉiṉ uyirum koṭukkuvar” eṉpatu puṟanāṉūṟu (182).
aṟaṅkūṟuvōr aṟavōr, cāṉṟōr, aiyar eṉappaṭuvar. aṟavōr evvuyirkkum aruḷpūṇṭu oḻukupavar ātalāl avari antaṇar eṉṟu uyarttuk kūṟiṉar. “antaṇar eṉpōr aṟavōr maṟṟevvuyirkkum centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻukalāṉ” eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar tamakku muṉpirunta cāṉṟōrmēl vittu “eṉpa” eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. “āṉṟavintaṭaṅkiya koḷkic cāṉṟōr palar yāṉ. vāḻumūrē” (puṟam.191) eṉa aṟavōr cāṉṟōrākap pēcappaṭutal kāṇka. “cālpum viyappum iyalpum kuṉṟiṉ, mācaṟak kaḻīi  vayaṅkupukaḻ niṟuttal, ācaṟu kāṭci yiyarkkum annili, eḷiya eṉṉār toṉmaruṅ kaṟiñar” (kuṟiñci) eṉa aṟavōr aiyarākavum vaḻaṅkappaṭumāṟu kāṇalām.
aracāṭci purintōr aracar, vēntar, maṉṉar eṉṟum vāṇipam purintōr vāṇikar eṉṟum, uḻavuttoḻil purintōr uḻavar, vēḷāḷar eṉṟum vaḻaṅki vantaṉar.
iṉi, ivarkaḷippōla nilampeṟātu, tamiḻakameṅkum tirintu, āṅkāṅku vāḻnta celvarkaḷiyum maṉṉarkaḷiyum avartam kuṇañceyalkaḷip pukaḻntu pāṭi paricil peṟṟu vāḻnta makkaḷum tamiḻnilattē uṇṭu. avarkaḷ kūttar, pāṇar, porunar, viṟaliyar mutalākap pala tiṟattiṉarkaḷum aṟamurikkum cāṉṟōrākiya pulavarkaḷum celvarkaḷum pāṭip paricil peṟṟaṉar; eṉiṉum, avaruṭiya pulami nalattāl, pāṇar mutalāyiṉār variciyil viyātu celvarkaṭku otta niliyilum uyarniliyilum appulavarkaḷ vittup pēṇappeṟṟaṉar. irupēraracarkaṭ kiṭiyē tūtu ceṉṟu oṟṟumi ceytu uyirk kēṭu nīṅkum uravōrātaliṉ, ivari nāṭōṭikaḷ eṉak karutuvatu kuṟṟam. “ikalmīk kaṭavum iruperuvēntar, viḷiyiṭi niṉṟa cāṉṟōr” (kuṟiñci) eṉṟu pōṟṟutal kāṇka.
ivvāṟu, caṅkakālat tamiḻmakkaḷ tammil ceyalvakiyāl palavakip peyar vēṟupāṭu peṟṟiruntaṉarē yaṉṟi makaṭkōṭal, uṇavukōṭal mutaliya tuṟikaḷil yātoru vēṟupāṭum koṇṭilar. aṟavōr mutal kūttar pāṇar īṟāka aṉivarum oruṅkiruntu uṇavu koḷvar; oruvarkkoruvar makaṭkoṭi purintukoḷvar. ik kālattuc cāti vēṟupāṭum, uṇavu vēṟupāṭum, maṇavēṟupāṭumākiya  ivaṟṟiṉ muṭināṟṟam ak kālattē avariṭiyē tōṉṟavē illi. neytal nilattavar piṟa nilattavarōṭum, piṟar piṟarōṭum koḷvaṉa koṭuppaṉa ceytu iṉitu vāḻntaṉar. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu nuḻintu avartam aṟavāḻkkiyiṉ cīrik kulikkavilli. ūṉuṇṭaṉar; mīṉuṇṭaṉar; pāluṇṭaṉar paḻamuṇṭaṉar; cōṟuṇṭaṉar; iṉiya cuviyuṭiya nallaṉavellām uṇṭaṉar.
paṭṭāṭiyum parutti yāṭiyum uṭuttiṉar. poṉṉum muttum maṇiyum piṟavum pūṇkaḷāy avar mēṉiyiṟ kiṭantu aḻaku ceytaṉa. āṭavarum makaḷirum uyariya aṇikaḷi aṇintu koḷvar. pūvum toṭuttaṇivar. āṭavar taliyil aṇintu koṇṭa pūvaṇi kaṇṇiyeṉavum, tōḷilaṇintatu tār eṉavum peyarpeṟum. makaḷir vaṇṭumoykkum pūkkaḷit tam kuḻalil aṇivar. iḷamakaḷir iṭiyil āmpalum piṟavum viravat toṭutta taḻiyi uṭimēl aṇintukoḷvar. avarkaḷ kiyil vaḷiyum toṭiyum peytukoḷvar. kālil cilampu pūṇpar. āṭavar tāḷil kaṭakamum, kālil kaḻalum puṉivar.
āṇ makkaḷiṉ niṉivu, col, ceyalkaḷil perumiyum uraṉum viḷaṅki niṟkum. kalvi kēḷvi mikuntu mēlum pala nūlkaḷi ōtutal, viṉipalavum ceytal, poruḷīṭṭal āṭavarkkup potuvāya ceykikaḷākum. āṭavaṉ taṉ vāḻkkit tuṇi viyittāṉē tēṭikkoḷvaṉ. peṟṟōr atu kuṟittu muyalum muyaṟci yoṉṟum caṅkakālattu nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭavilli. avaṉ tērntukoṇṭa peṇṇip piṉ maṇam ceytu koḷḷuṅ kālattil peṟṟōr mutaliya cāṉṟōrkaḷiṉ utaviyum vāḻttum peṟukiṉṟāṉ. “pōlakam eṉpatu tāṉ uḻan tuṇṭal” eṉṉum perumoḻiyē avaṉ uḷḷattil muṉ niṟkiṟatu. peṟṟōr īṭṭiya poruḷip peṟṟut tāṉ iṉitu vāḻalām eṉṟa karuttu avaṉ niṉivil nikaḻvatiyē kāṇōm.
avaṉ eññāṉṟum paḻipāvaṅkaṭku añcum iyalpiṉaṉākavē uḷḷāṉ. pukaḻpeṟuvatē avaṟkuk kuṟikkōḷ aṉpum aruḷum aṟamum avaṉuṭiya niṉivilum collilum ceyalilum nilavukiṉṟaṉa. iṟivaṉāṉa murukaṉip paravumpōtum, “yāam irappavi, poruḷum poṉṉupōkamumalla, niṉpāl, aruḷum aṉpum aṟaṉum mūṉṟum uruḷiṇark kaṭampiṉ olitārōyē” (paripā.5) eṉṟē vēṇṭukiṉṟāṉ. añcā neñcamum aṟivu nuṇmiyum paṭivaṉmiyum uṭiya it tamiḻmakaṉatu potuviyal iṉṉum virikkiṟ perukum; īṇṭu atu poruḷumaṉṟu.
iṉi, ic caṅka kālattuc centamiḻc celviyik kāṇpōm. ivaḷ pēti petumpi eṉṟa iruparuvattum taṉṉip peṟṟōr ātaravil aḻakiṉ koḻuntu vaḷarvateṉ vaḷarkiṉṟāḷ. ik kālattē ivaḷ peṟṟōrāl tiruntiya kalviyum poruntiya oḻukkamum peṟukiṉṟāḷ. kaṭimaṇamāṉa piṉ kaṇavaṉ kāvalil illiruntu nallaṟam puriyun tiṟattāl taṉṉi maṉiviyākap peṟṟavaṉukku uyirkkiṉiya uṟuti payakkun tuṇiviyāy avaṉuvappa makkaḷip payantu iṉpuṟukiṉṟāḷ. “kaṇavaruppap putalvarp payantu.... cuṟṟamoṭu vaḷamaṉi makaḷir kuḷanīr ayara” (maturik kāñci, 600-3) eṉa māṅkuṭimarutaṉār kūṟutal kāṇka. mutumi yeytiya vaḻi, kaṇavaṉōṭu tāṉum (mutumiyiṉ payaṉāka) maṟumikkuc ciṟanta nalviṉiyi āṟṟuvaḷ. “kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum ciṟantatu payiṟṟal iṟantataṉ payaṉē” eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. itaṉkaṇ “iṟanta” teṉṟatu, vayatu mutirntatu eṉṉum poruḷatākum, “iḷamiyum taruvatō iṟanta piṉṉē” (kali. 15) varutal kāṇka. orukāl, tām uyiruṭaṉ irukkum pōtē kaṇavaṉ iṟakka nēriṉ, tāmum avaṉuyiruṭaṉ uṭaṉ kaṭṭiyēṟi māytalum uṇṭu. iṉṟēl, maṟumiyil kaṇavaṉik kūṭal vēṇṭik kimmi nōṉpu nōṟṟiruttalum uṇṭu. irumaṇam puṇarum vaḻakkam paṇṭit tamiḻ makaḷirpāṟ kiṭiyātu. “alantata, virinīr uṭukki ulakam peṟiṉum, aruneṟiyāyar makaḷirkku, irumaṇam kūṭutal illiyal paṉṟē” (kali.114) eṉpataṉāl tuṇiyappaṭum.
im makaḷiratu iḷami, kāmañ cālā iḷamiyum, atu cāṉṟa iḷamiyum eṉa irutiṟamākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. kāmañcālā iḷamikkālattē im makaḷir puṟattē ceṉṟu naṉku viḷiyāṭuvar. ciṟṟil puṉitalum, ciṟucōṟaṭutalum, pāvi vittu viḷiyāṭalum, ammaṉi, kaḻaṅku, kaḻal, pantu mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu viḷiyāṭalum piṟavum viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷākum. pūkkoytu māli toṭuttalum taḻi puṉitalum ciṟitu mutirnta iḷamakaḷir ceyvar. kuṟiñci nilattavarāyiṉ kāmam cālum paruvameytum niliyiluḷḷār, cuṉikuṭitalum, tiṉippuṉam kāttalum; mulli nilatta varāyiṉ āṉiri piṉ ceṉṟu pāl kaṟantu koṇartalum; marutanilattavarāyiṉ, tāmariyiṉ piṟavum koytu māli toṭuttalum, neṟpōril oḷittu viḷiyāṭalum, ōriyāṭalum; neytal nilattavarāyiṉ, mīṉ kavara varum puḷḷōpputalum, kaṭaṟkāṉalil puṉṉi nīḻalil vaṇṭalayartalum piṟavum ceyvar. kāmam cāṉṟa paruvam eytiyavaḻi im makaḷir iṟceṟikkappaṭuvar.
“	.... muḷḷeyi ṟilaṅkiṉa;
 	talimuṭi cāṉṟa; taṇtaḻi yuṭiyi;
 	alamara lāyamoṭu yāṅkaṇum paṭāal;
 	mūppuṭi mutupati tākkaṇaṅku uṭiya;
 	kāppum pūṇṭiciṉ; kaṭiyum pōkali;
 	pēti yalli; mētiyaṅ kuṟumakaḷ,
 	petumpip paruvattu otuṅkiṉi puṟattu.” (akam.7)
eṉa varutalāl iṟceṟippiṉ iyalpu pulaṉākum.
ip petumpip paruvattē ivaḷ taṉakkuriya kātalaṉit tērntu koḷvaḷ. iṟceṟikkappaṭṭapōtum ivaḷ taṉ maṉiyakattē yāṇṭu vēṇṭiṉum iṉitu celvaḷ. ivaḷ ip paruvattēyē cīriya kuṇaṅkaḷ nirampi viṭukiṉṟaṉaḷ. accam, nāṇam, maṭaṉ eṉṟa mūṉṟu kuṇaṅkaḷ naṉku nilipeṟṟu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum immūṉṟiyē pala viṭattum vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟār. “accamum  nāṇum maṭaṉum muntuṟutta, niccayam peṇpāṟkuriya eṉpa” eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. “kāmattiṇiyiṟ kaṇṇiṉṟu varūum nāṇum maṭaṉum peṇmiya” eṉṟum, “uyiriṉum ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉum, ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpuc ciṟantaṉṟeṉat tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcam” eṉṟum, “uyirum nāṇum maṭaṉumeṉṟivi, ceyirtīr kaṟpiṉ nālvarkkum uriya” eṉṟum palaviṭattum tolkāppayir nāṇamum maṭaṉum kaṟpum iṉṟiyamiyātu amintiruttali eṭuttōtukiṉṟār. maṭaṉ eṉpatu tāṉ tērntukoṇṭataṉi ettaṉi iṭiyūṟum iṭiyīṭum eytiṉum viṭāmi. kaṟpeṉpatu taṉ meṉmit taṉmiyip peṟṟōrālum, cāṉṟōrālum nūlmukattālum aṟintu ekkālattum, taṉṉip pātukāttoḻukum aṟivuṭimi. nūlārāycciyum akappaṭa ik kaṟpeṉṉum col niṟpataṉi yaṟiyātār tām vēṇṭiyavāṟellām kūṟi, ataṉ uṇmip poruḷi maṟittu oḻintaṉar. “ulakan tōṉṟiya mēmpaṭu kaṟpiṉ villōr meymmaṟi” (patiṟ. 59) eṉak kākki pāṭiṉiyār eṉṟa peṇpāṟ pulavar, āṭukōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉip pāṭutalāl, kaṟpiṉik kalvimēl niṟuttip paṇṭiyōr vaḻaṅkiyavāṟu kāṇalām. ettuṇi yiṭukkaṇ variṉum tam kalviyaṟivu maḻuṅkātavariyē paṇṭit tamiḻaṟiñar, “toliyāk kaṟpu” (patiṟ.43,80) eṉṟu ōtiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. vētaṅkaḷi “eḻutāk kaṟpu” (kuṟun. 156) eṉpar. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum makaḷirkkuk kalvi yaṟiviṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyik kaṟpeṉa vāḷā ōtātu. “ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpu” eṉṟu kūṟiyaruḷiṉar. piṟitōriṭattē im makaḷir pāluḷḷa ciṟappuṭiya kuṇam kūṟaluṟṟu, uyiriṉum nāṇiṉum ciṟanta kaṟpiṉik kaṟpeṉṟōtātu aṟivu eṉṟu nāṭṭik koṇṭu, “ceṟivum niṟivum cemmiyum ceppum aṟivum arumiyum peṇpālāṉa” eṉṟu ōtiṉar. makaḷirkkut tavattiṉum kaṟpē ciṟantateṉpatu paṇṭiyōr muṭipu. itaṉi aṭiyārkku nallār, “puṉamayiṟ cāyaṟkum puṇṇiya mutalvikkum eṉ tiṟam uriyātēku” (cilap.11:199-200) eṉpataṉ uriyil urippatāl iṉitu uṇaralām.
kaṇavaṉoruvaṉik kaṭimaṇam ceytu koḷḷum kaṭappāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya ivaḷpāl “kaṟpum kāmamum naṟpāl oḻukkamum, melliyaṟ poṟiyum niṟiyum, vallitiṉ viruntu puṟantarutalum cuṟṟam ōmpalum” ivi pōluḷḷa nalaṅkaḷ palavum tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. taṉ maṉakkiṉiya kātalaṉ oruvaṉip peṟṟatum  “avaṉ varampu iṟattal” (tol) taṉakku aṟamaṉṟu eṉpatu ivaṭkut terintu viṭukiṟatu. “avaṉ cōrpu kāttal kaṭaṉ” (tol) ivaṭku, eṉṉum kaṟpu neṟi tāṉē pulaṉāki viṭukiṟatu. avaṉpāl tavaṟu uṇṭākiya vaḻi, “tāypōl kaḻaṟi” ataṉi uṇarvittut “taḻīikkōṭal” (tol) eṉṉum peruntaṉmi ivaṭkē urittāy niṟkiṟatu. ivvāṟē taṉ vayiṟṟiṟ piṟakkum makaṉukkum nallaṟivu koḷuttum naṉmakaḷākiṉṟāḷ.
“	īṉṟupuṟan tarutal eṉtalik kaṭaṉē,
 	cāṉṟō ṉākkutal tantikkuk kaṭaṉē
 	naṉṉaṭi nalkal vēntaṟkuk kaṭaṉē
 	vēlvaṭittuk koṭuttal kollaṟkuk kaṭaṉē
 	oḷiṟuvāṉ aruñcamam murukkik
 	kaḷiṟeṟintu peyartal kāḷikkuk kaṭaṉē.”	
(puṟam. 312)
eṉpatu avaḷ taṉ arumi makaṉit teruṭṭum cevvi naṉmoḻiyākum.
iṉṉa nalaṅkaṭku iṭaṉākiya tamiḻccelvi petumpip paruvam kaṭantu kaṇavaṉoruvaṉik kūṭik kaṭimaṇam ceytu koḷḷum tiṟamum, avaṉukkum taṉakkum uyiroṉṟiya kātalaṉpi vaḷarkkum tiṟamum ārāyuṅkāl mikka iṉpattit tarukiṉṟaṉa. ivi perumpālum peṟṟōrum piṟarum aṟiyāvakiyil nikaḻtaliṉ ivvoḻukkam kaḷavoḻukkam eṉappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉik kāntaruva maṇampōlum oru vaki maṇam eṉpar. kāntaruvattil maṇantavar, piṟaku vēṟē varivu ceytukōṭa liṉṟiyum makappeṟṟum amivar. iḵtu av viyalpiṉi yuṭiyataṉṟu. kaḷaviṉkaṇ kātal ciṟantu mutirntu oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāt toṭarpeyti mēmpaṭuvatum, ak kaḷavoḻukkam veḷippaṭṭa piṉpum ciṟitu ataṟku muṉpum, peṟṟōrum cāṉṟōrum aṟiya maṇam ceytu koḷvatum ciṟappāka uṭiyatākum. ātalāl, vaṭavar kūṟum kāntaruva maṇam vēṟu; paṇṭit tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷ mēṟkoṇṭirunta kaḷaviyal maṇam vēṟu. kātalar iruvar koṭuppārum aṭuppārum iṉṟit tāmē taṉittuk kūṭi maṉaṅkalantu aṉpu ciṟantu niṟkum av viyipu oṉṟiyē karuti āciriyar, “kāmakkūṭṭam kāṇum kālit tuṟiyami nalliyāḻt tuṇimiyōr iyalpē” eṉṟu ōtiṉar.
iv voḻukkamē perumpāṉmiyāka nikaḻiṉum ciṟu pāṉmi kollēṟu taḻuviyum, paṉṟiyeytum ivipōlum cila āṇmi tōṟṟuvikkum ceyalkaḷic ceytum mēmpaṭṭa āṭavaṉukku makaṭkoṭi ceytoḻukum maṇamuṟiyum nikaḻntirukkiṟatu. ataṉip piṉpu kāṇpām. iṉikkaḷavuk kāṭciyiṟ pukuvām.
potumpip paruvattiṉ muṭivilō ciṟitu iṭiyilō nam tamiḻc celvi taṉ uyirkkiṉiya talivaṉik kaṇṭu kātaluṟṟut tērntu koḷḷukiṉṟāḷ. ipparuvattē ivaḷ puṟattē ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭum iyalpiṉaḷ eṉpatu muṉpē kūṟappaṭṭatu. akkālattē avaḷi avvāṟu viḷiyāṭa viṭātu illiṉkaṇ ceṟittu vittal aṟamumaṉṟu; ataṉāl avaḷ uṭal nalamuṟā miyiṉ, avaḷatu etirkāla vāḻkkik kuriya ākkamum tēyum eṉpatu paṇṭiyōr naṉkaṟintiruntaṉar. “viḷiyā ṭāyamōṭu ōriyā ṭātu, iḷiyōr illiṭattiṟ ceṟin tiruttal, aṟaṉu maṉṟō ākkamum tēym eṉa.... vallitiṉ vaṇaṅkic cellunarp peṟiṉē” (naṟ.68) eṉac caṅkakālat tamiḻaṟiñar kūṟutal kāṇka.
ik karuttāl, nam celvi taṉ tōḻiyaruṭaṉ kuṟiñciyāyiṉ maliccārāl, maliccuṉi, aruvi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum, mulliyāyiṉ, mullikkoṭiyum pāṅkarkkoṭiyum piṟavum taḻitta tōṭṭaṅkaḷilum, marutamāyiṉ, nīrttuṟikaḷilum, neytalāyiṉ, kaḻimaruṅkaḷilum kāṉaṟ cōliyilum kaṭaṟkari yilum makaḷirkkuriya viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ palavum ayarvaḷ. ivviḷiyāṭṭiṭattiṟku oru tamiḻc celva makaṉ vantu yātāṉu mōrutaviyō, iṉiya col collutalō ceyvaṉ. atuvē vāyilāka avaṭku avaṉpāl kātal piṟakkum; avaṉukkum avaḷpāl kātalaṉpu tōṉṟip piṇikkum.
kuṟiñci nilattē, orukāl oru tamiḻc celvi taṉ tōḻiyar palaruṭaṉ maliccāralil niṉṟa vēṅki marattiṉ pūkkoytu viḷiyāṭaṟkuc ceṉṟāḷ. marattilēṟip pūkkaḷik koytu koṇṭirukkiyil oru cila makaḷir,. vēṅkiyiṉ pūvutirnta karumpāṟi pulipōlat tōṉṟak kaṇṭuviḷiyāṭṭākavē “puli puli” eṉṟu iriccaliṭṭaṉar. atu kēṭṭut tiṭukkiṭṭa makaḷirum uṇmi teḷintu tāmum at tōṟṟam viyantu “puli puli” eṉṟu pūcaliṭṭaṉar. avvaḻiyē vaṭiveṭutta kāmaṉpōla vaṭikkaṇiyum vari villu mēntiya tamiḻc celva makaṉoruvaṉ vantāṉ. avaṉ kātil im makaḷireḻuppiya pūcaloli viḻuntatu. avaṉ uṭaṉē villil ampu toṭuttu avarmuṉ pōntu, “eṅkē, eṅkē puli?” eṉa viṉavi niṉṟāṉ. avaṉik kaṇṭatum im makaḷir nāṇi oruvarpiṉ oruvarāka maṟintu koṇṭaṉar.  avar mukattōṉṟātu kūntalē taṉ kaṇṇiṟkup pulaṉākak kaṇṭa akkāḷi, “miyīrōti, maṭavīr, numvāyp poyyum uḷavō?” eṉṟāṉ. ivarkaḷ oṉṟum kūṟātu nāṇit taliyiṟiñci niṉṟoḻintaṉar. nam celviyiṉ malar mukamum oḷinutalum vāṭkaṇkaḷum paṉmuṟi maṉaṅkuḷirak kaṇṭu makiḻntāṉ. avaḷum avaṉ uyartōḷum virimārpum taṭakkiyum taṉ iru kaṇkaḷum ārak kaṇṭāḷ. iruvar karuttum oṉṟupaṭṭaṉa. piṉpu avaṉ ceṉṟuviṭṭāṉ. avaṉ maṟiyuṅkālum ivaḷ avaṉiyē nōkki niṉṟāḷ. avaṉ maṟinta piṉ, ivaḷ taṉ  tōḻiyi nōkki, “ivaṉ makaṉō, tōḻi” (akam.48) eṉṟāḷ. “atil taṭiyeṉṉi?” eṉat tōḻi kēṭpa, “illi; kēṭṭēṉ” eṉa muṟuvalittāḷ it tamiḻ makaḷ. iḵtoru tiṟam.
orukāl im makaḷir cuṉiyiṟ kuṭintu viḷiyāṭuṅkāl, ataṉkaṇ cēymiyil āḻattil pūttirunta pūvip paṟikka muyalkiṉṟaṉar; ak kāli it tamiḻmakaṉ pōntuataṉit tāṉ nīnticceṉṟu paṟittut tarukiṉṟāṉ; avvutavi vāyilākak kātal tōṉṟukiṟatu. piṟitorukāl, im makaḷīr paṟitta pūkkaḷi mika aḻakāka virivil māliyākat toṭuttutavu kiṉṟāṉ; avarkaḷ vittu viḷiyāṭum pāviyiṉ poruṭṭut tāṉ aṇintirunta kaṇṇiyi eṭuttu at tamiḻ makaṉ ic celva makaṭku utavukiṉṟāṉ. (naḷ.173) itaṉālum kātalaṉpu piṟantu viṭukiṟatu.
maṟṟorukāl, ic celvi taṉ tōḻiyaruṭaṉ tiṉip puṉattē yiruntu, at tiṉik katirik kavaravarum kiḷikaḷi ōppikkoṇṭirukkiṟāḷ. arukē maliccārāl uḷatu; ataṉkaṇ mūṅkil vaḷarntu taḻittu nīṇṭirukkiṟatu. kōṭikkāṟṟu vīcukiṟatu. am mūṅkil cilavaṟṟuḷ tuḷitōṉṟi yirukkiṟatu. attuḷivaḻi vīcum kāṟṟuk kuḻalōci yeḻuppukiṉṟatu.  ataṉarukē maliyaruvi vīḻvatu muḻavu oli pōlkiṉṟatu. āṅkāṅkup pūttirukkum pūkkaḷil tēṉuṇṇum vaṇṭiṉam yāḻpōla icikkiṉṟatu. mantikaḷ pāṟimēl iruntu ivvici kēṭṭu maruṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. mūṅkil niṟinta cāralil mayil taṉ tōkiyi virittuk kūttāṭum viṟalipōl āṭukiṉṟatu. ik kāṭci, viḻākkaḷattē kuḻaliyampa muḻavu muḻaṅka, yāḻici iṉiticippa makkaḷ iruntu kāṇa viṟaliyar kūttāṭum iṉiya kāṭciyi vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. itu kaṇṭa nam tamiḻc celvamakaḷ akkāṭci yiṉpatit tuyttirukkiṉṟāḷ. akkālattē tamiḻ makaṉoruvaṉ villum ampum koṇṭu, yāṉi vēṭṭam  varuvāṉ ivaḷpāl vantu, “yāṉi ceṉṟatu kaṇṭīrō? aḵtu evvaḻic ceṉṟatu, kūṟumiṉ,” eṉṟu at tiṉip puṉattiṉ oru maruṅkē niṟkiṉṟāṉ. avaṉi it tamiḻ makaḷ kaṇṭu kātaluṟu kiṉṟāḷ (akam.82). iḵtoru tiṟam.
iṉi, iv vaṇṇamēyaṉṟi vēṟu vakiyālum, kātalaṉpu tōṉṟaṟku vāyiluṇṭu. centamiḻc celvi yoruttiyatu kātaloḻukkatti yuṇarttum vāyilāka āciriyar kapilar āriyavaracaṉ pirakattaṉ eṉpāṉukkut tamiḻaṟivikkiṉṟār. atu pattup pāṭṭiṉuḷ oṉṟākiya kuṟiñcippāṭṭu eṉappaṭum. ataṉkaṇ, centamiḻc celvi oruttiyi avaḷ tāy,
“	tuyttali vāṅkiya puṉiṟutīr peruṅkural
 	naṟkōḷ ciṟutiṉip paṭupuḷ ōppi
	eṟpaṭa varutiyar”          	        (37-9) eṉa viṭuvikkiṟāḷ.)
avaḷum taṉ tōḻiyaruṭaṉ puṉam ceṉṟu, āṅku amittirunta paraṇmītu iruntukoṇṭu,
“	cāral cūral takipeṟa valanta
 	taḻalum taṭṭiyum kuḷirum piṟavum
 	kiḷikaṭi marapiṉa ūḻūḻ vāṅki”		     (42-4)
puṉaṅ kāval purintu varukiṉṟāḷ. varuṅkāl veyil vemmi mikka varuttumpōtu, aruviyilum cuṉiyilum nīrāṭi, naṉinta kūntalip pularttikkoṇṭu, āṅkup pūttirukkum kāntaḷ, āmpal, aṉiccam, kuvaḷi, kuṟiñci, veṭci mutaliya palavakip pūkkaḷip paṟittut tokuttut taḻiyum kōtiyum toṭuttup puṉintukoṇṭu acōka maramoṉṟiṉ niḻalil irukkiṉṟaṉaḷ. iṭiyiṭiyē kiḷiyōppuvati maṟakka villi. appōtu tamiḻ makaṉoruvaṉ, taṉ taṭakkiyil,
“	vaṇṇa varivil ēnti ampu terintu
 	nuṇviṉik kaccit tayakkaṟak kaṭṭi
 	iyalaṇip polinta vīki vāṉkaḻal
 	tuyalvarun tōṟum tiruntaṭik kalāva
 	muṉipāḻ paṭukkun tuṉṉarum tuppiṉ
 	pakipuṟaṅ kaṇṭa palvēl iḷiñaroṭu”	(124-29)
avvaḻiyē varukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉuṭaṉ vēṭṭi nāykaḷ. vantavi im makaḷic cūḻntu koḷkiṉṟaṉa. atu kaṇṭu immakaḷ añci naṭuṅkukiṉṟaṉaḷ. uṭaṉē avaṉ pōntu, meṉmoḻi cila kūṟi, ivaḷit tēṟṟi, an nāykaḷiyum pōkkiviṭṭu,
“	maṭamatar maḻikkaṇ iḷiyīr, iṟanta
 	keṭutiyum uṭiyēṉ”			(141-2)
eṉkiṉṟāṉ. avaṟku ivaḷ oru viṭiyum pakarkiṉṟāḷ illi. avaṉ maṉam tikittu,
“	keṭutiyum viṭirāyiṉ emmoṭu
 	collalum paḻiyō? melliyalīr”		(144-5)
eṉṟu kēṭṭu niṟkiṉṟāṉ. iruntāṟpōliruntu kāṉam kalleṉṉu māṟu oru perumuḻakkam kēṭkiṟatu. ciṟitu pōtiṟkuḷḷē,
“	kārppeyal urumiṉ piḷiṟic cīrttaka
 	irumpiṇart taṭakki yirunilañ cērttic
 	ciṉamtikaḻ kaṭāam cerukki maramkolpu
 	miyal vēḻam,”				(162-5()
oṉṟu maṭaṅkalpōl etirvarukiṉṟatu. uṭaṉē im makaḷ añci alamantu, vaḷiyolippa nāṇam maṟantu virintōṭi at tamiḻmakaṉi yaṭintu naṭuṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉaḷ. tamiḻ makaḷit taḻīiniṉṟa at tamiḻk kāḷi,
“	uṭuvuṟum pakaḻi vāṅkik kaṭuvici
 	aṇṇal yāṉi aṇimukat taḻuttaliṉ
 	puṇṇumiḻ kuruti mukampāyn tiḻitarap
 	puḷḷi varinutal citiya nillātu”		(170-3)
ayarntu puṟaṅkoṭuttu ōṭi viṭukiṉṟatu. celviyum avaṉit taḻuvi niṉṟa ki viṭātu ciṟitupōtu niṟkiṉṟāḷ. apperun takiyum,
“	añci lōti! aciyal, yāvatum;
 	añca lōmpu niṉ aṇinalam nukarkueṉa
 	mācaṟu cuṭarnutal nīvi nīṭuniṉintu”		(180-2)
avaḷ mukam nōkkik kuṟunaki mukiḻkkiṉṟāṉ. appōtu avaṭku nāṇum uṭkum tōṉṟa, avaḷ ciṟitu piriyakkaruta avaṉ avaḷi viṭātu taḻī ik koṇṭu niṟkiṉṟāṉ. piṉpu avaṉ tāṉ koṇarnta aṭicili,
“	niṉṉōṭu uṇṭalum purivatu eṉṟāṅku
 	aṟampuṇi yākat tēṟṟip piṟaṅkumali
 	mīmicik kaṭavuḷ vāḻttik kitoḻutū
 	ēmuṟu vañciṉam vāymiyiṉ tēṟṟi
 	antīn teṇṇīr kuṭittaliṉ neñcamarntu”	(207-11)
iṉitu irukkiṉṟāṉ. piṉpu poḻutu cāya, antimāli neruṅkiṟatu. ataṉik kaṇṭatum avaṉ, tamiḻ makaḷiṉ,
“	nēriṟi muṉki paṟṟi numartara
 	nāṭaṟi naṉmaṇam ayarkam, ciṉṉāḷ
 	kalaṅka lōmpumiṉ, ilaṅkiḻi yīr” eṉa	(231-3)
īramoḻikaḷ cila kūṟi avaḷuṭaṉ avaḷirukkum ūrkku vāyilil uḷḷa nīrttuṟikkaṇ viṭuttuviṭṭut tāṉ ceṉṟu viṭukiṉṟāṉ. iḵtoru tiṟamākum.
iṉi, mēlē kūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi, it tamiḻmakaḷ viḷiyāṭṭi ṭattō piṟarṇṭō, avaḷ taṉittirukkuṅkāl oru tamiḻ makaṉ avaḷik kāṇṭalum avaḷ avaṉik kāṇṭalum iruvarum karuttorumittu aṉpu koḷvatum uṇṭu. avvāṟu uṇṭākiya aṉpu, piṟitoruṅkāl avarkaḷ tāmē tamittuk kaṇṭu kūṭalāl perukik kātalāy oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāta nilimi payantuviṭukiṟatu. in nilimik kaṇ avaḷ, tāṉ avaṉiyiṉṟi amiyamāṭṭāt taṉmiyi uṇarntukoḷkiṉṟāḷ; āṉāl ataṉip piṟarkku eṭuttōtum peṟṟi peṇmikku illāmi yāl, pacumaṭkalattuḷ nīrpeytu vittavaḻi, atu puṟattē pocintu kāṭṭuvatu pōlat taṉ vēṭki tāṉē taṉ meyppaṭṭu aritu tōṉṟumāṟu oḻukuvaḷ; collāl kūṟāḷ eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟuvar; iḷantiriyaṉ eṉpār,
“	kāmañ ceppal āṇmakaṟ kamiyum, yāṉē, peṇmi
 	taṭpa tuṇṇitil tāṅki” 			  (naṟ. 94)
eṉat tamiḻc celvamakaḷ kūṟṟil vittup peṇmiyē avaḷi av vēṭkiyik kūṟamāṭṭātu taṭukkumeṉṟu ōtukiṉṟār.
itaṉāl, avaḷi avaṉ kaṇṭu kātalāl taḻī ikkoṇṭu, akkātaliṉ tōṟṟam kūṟuvāṉ,
“	yāyum yāyum yārā kiyarō?
 	entiyum nuntiyum emmuṟik kēḷīr?
 	yāṉum nīyum evvaḻi yaṟitum?
 	cempulap peyalnīr pōla
 	aṉpuṭi neñcam tāmkalan taṉavē” 		(kuṟun. 40)
eṉṟu avaṭkuk kūṟakiṉṟāṉ. piṉpu avaḷiṉ nīṅkit taṉittiruk kuṅkāl avaḷ niṉivē mikuntu melintu ammelivu kēṭṭa naṉpaṉukkut taṉ kātaṉmiyi eṭuttōtukiṉṟāṉ.
“	kēḷir vāḻiyō kēḷir, nāḷumeṉ
 	neñcupiṇik koṇṭa añci lōtip
 	peruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷ ciṟumel lākam
  	orunāḷ puṇarap puṇariṉ
 	arināḷ vāḻkkiyum vēṇṭaleṉ yāṉē”
(kuṟun.280)
eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. piṟāṇṭum avaḷatu “katuppayal viḷaṅkum tirunutal, putukkōḷ yāṉiyiṟ piṇittaṟṟāl emmē” (kuṟun. 129) eṉṟum, “koṭicci kiyakat tatuvē piṟar viṭuttaṟ kākātu piṇittaveṉ neñcē” (naṟ.95) eṉṟum palappala kūṟi avaṉtāṉ avaḷi iṉṟiyamiyāk kātalaṉātalip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟāṉ.
ivvaṇṇam,it tamiḻc celva makaṉum tamiḻc celva makaḷum tammil tāmē tamittuk kūṭalē perumpāṉmi; ciṟupāṉmiyē tamiḻ makaḷ mutaṟkaṇ tōḻiyar kūṭṭattiṭiyē niṉṟu tamiḻ makaṉik kāṇṭalum kātalurutalum nikaḻvatu. taṉittuk kaṇṭu kātaleytum māṇpuṭiya ivaḷ taṉ ceykiyiṉ arumip pāṭṭiṉiyum, tirumaṇattiṉ ciṟappiṉiyum uṇarāta vaḷallaḷ.
“	muttiṉum maṇiyiṉum poṉṉiṉum attuṇi
 	nērvaruṅ kuriya kalamkeṭiṟ puṇarum;
 	cālpum viyappum iyalpum kuṉṟiṉ
 	mācaṟak kaḻīi vayaṅkupukaḻ niṟuttal
 	ācaṟu kāṭci aiyarkkum annili
 	eḷiya eṉṉār toṉmaruṅ kaṟiñar.”	     (kuṟiñci.13-8)
eṉṟum,
“	koṭuppiṉ naṉku uṭimiyum kuṭiniral uṭimiyum
 	vaṇṇamum tuṇiyum porīi eṇṇātu
 	emiyēm tuṇinta ēmamcāl aruviṉi”	(kuṟi.30-2)
eṉṟum im makaḷiriṭi nikaḻum pēccukkaḷi nōkkiṉ ivarkaḷ kātal vāḻviṉ ciṟappum kaṭimaṇattiṉ kuṟippum piṟavum naṉkuṇarn tiruttal iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
tamittu niṉṟu oruvari yoruvar kaṇṭu uḷam kalantu uṇarvoṉṟa eytiya kātal piṉpu evvakiyil vaḷarkiṉṟatu; ivarkaḷāl evvaṇṇam vaḷarkkap peṟukiṉṟatu eṉpaṉavaṟṟi iṉik kāṇalām. centamiḻc celviyiṉ uḷḷattil muḷit tirukkum kātali, avaḷ, perunāṇum pēraccamum uṭiyaḷā taliṉ,piṟarkkuc ciṟitum pulaṉākātu maṟintoḻukuvāḷ, avaḷōṭu uṭaṉiruntu uṭaṉuṟintu uṭaṉpayiṉṟu uṭaṉoḻukum uyirttōḻikkum vāyviṭṭu uriyāḷ. ceṟivum niṟivum cemmiyum ceppum aṟivum piṟa nalaṅkaḷum mikavuṭiya ittamiḻ makaṭku uyirttōḻiyāka ilakupavaḷ aṟivilum oḻukkattilum colnalattilum ettuṇiyum kuṟivillātavaḷ. aṟivu, aṉpu, aṟam eṉṟa mūṉṟum kalantu peṟṟa peṇ vaṭivamoṉṟu uḷatāyiṉ ataṉit tamiḻ makaḷiṉ uyirttōḻi eṉṟukūṟalām. aṟivē avaḷatu uḷḷam; aṉpē avaḷatu col; aṟamē avaḷatu ceyal. ittōḻiyiṉ tuṇimi iruntālaṉṟi, tamiḻ makkaḷ iruvar pālum piṟanta kātal ciṟakkumāṟu illi yeṉpatit tamiḻ makaḷ naṉku uṇarntu cila kuṟippukkaḷāl avaḷ taṉ kātalaṉukku uṇartti viṭukiṉṟāḷ. avaḷtāṉ tōḻiyariṭiyē irukkuṅkāl, kātalaṉ variṉ avaṉ uṇarumāṟu, taṉ uyirttōḻiyuṭaṉ aṭikkaṭi collāṭiyum kaṭkaṭiyāl kuṟippāy nōkkiyum uṇarttuvaḷ. avaṉ ataṉip piḻiyaṟa uṇarntu koḷvaṉ. ituvēyaṉṟi avaṉum tamiḻ makaḷit taṉittuk kāṇumiṭattu, “iṉivaruṅkāl nīruṉ tōḻiyuṭaṉ varuka” (aiṅ. 175) eṉac collāl colluvaṉ. tōḻiyiṉ ceykikaḷik kaṇṭum avaṉ avaḷi aṟintu kōṭalum uṇṭu.
ikkālattē avaḷ peṇmi nalam ciṟantu kātal māṭci yeytum mutukkuṟivu uṭiyaḷāyiṉum, icceṟikkappeṟum attuṇi yarumip pāṭṭiṉi eytilaḷātaliṉ, avaḷ tōḻiyuṭaṉ puṟattuc ceṉṟu payilum iṭamē tamiḻ makaṉ tōḻiyiṉ matiyiṉi uṭampaṭuvikkum muyaṟcikkum ēṟṟa iṭamākiṟatu. ataṟkuriya cevviyip peṟuvataṟku avaṉ kālam nōkkiyirukkiṉṟāṉ. tamiḻmakaḷ tiṉippuṉam kāttaṟkuc cellum kālatti yaṟintu ataṉi naṉku payaṉpaṭuttik koḷkiṉṟāṉ.
tamiḻmakaḷ puṉaṅkāttu oḻukumiṭattu, avaḷuṭaṉ uyirt tōḻiyum irukkiṉṟāḷ. iruvarum oruṅkirukkum cevvi yaṟintu, avaṉ vēṭṭam celvāṉpōṟ ceṉṟu, “iṅku yāṉ eyta kaḷiṟu varakkaṇṭīrō? māṉ varakkaṇṭīrō? paṉṟi varakkaṇṭīrō?” eṉṟu viṉavi niṟpaṉ. allatu, putuvōṉpōla niṉṟu, avarkaḷuṭiya ūrum peyarum ūrkkuc cellum vaḻiyum urimiṉ eṉṟu irantu niṟpaṉ. mētakka taḻiyoṉṟu puṉintukoṇṭu pōntu, “itaṉi ēṟṟukkoṇmiṉ” eṉa vēṇṭuvaṉ. ivvāṟu palavakiyālum tōḻipāl collāṭum toṭarpu ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭu avaḷpāl, tāṉ tamiḻmakaḷpāl koṇṭa kātaluṟaviṉik kuṟippāl terivittuviṭuvaṉ.
tamiḻmakaṉatu collum ceyalum avaṉ kātaṟkuṟippi naṉku pulapṭuttakkāṇum tōḻi, avaḷpāl parivu koṇṭu, tamiḻmakaḷiṉ maṉakkarutti yaṟintukoṇṭu avaṉi ittamiḻ makaḷ karuttukku icivikkum kaṭappāṭṭiṉip peṟukiṉṟāḷ. tamiḻmakaḷiṉ, perumiyum tōḻiyiṉ tōḻamiyum ceyaliṉ arumiyum piṟavum oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu icivuṟātu piṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. inniliyil,avarkaḷiṭiyē nikaḻum collāṭṭukkaḷic ciṟitu kāṇpām. ac colliṭiyē oḷirum nuṭpamum aṟivoṭpamum paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ cīriya nākarikatti iṉitu viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.
centamiḻc celviyit taṉittuk kaṇṭu kātaluṟṟuk karuttoṉṟiya tamiḻmakaṉ, avaḷtaṉ tōḻiyuṭaṉ irukkum cevvi nōkkiyirukkiṉṟāṉ. avaḷ tōḻiyaruṭaṉ tiṉippuṉam kāttaṟku varukiṉṟāḷ. avaṉum ap puṉattiṟkup pōtaru
kiṉṟāṉ. avaḷ taṉ tōḻiyaruṭaṉ kūṭit tiṉikkural kavarum kiḷikaḷi oppukiṉṟāḷ. akkiḷikaḷiṉ kūṭṭam ōppuntōṟum nīṅki, ōppi ōyuntōṟum vantu puṉattē paṭikiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl, avaḷ puṉattē iruttali attamiḻ celvamakaṉ uṇarntu, “centamiḻc celvi puṉaṅkāval purivati ik kiḷḷikaḷ namakkuk kāṭṭiṉa;” “veḷḷavarampiṉ ūḻipōkiyum kiḷḷi vāḻiya palavē” (aiṅ.281) eṉṟu vāḻtti, “naṉṟē ceyta utavi; naṉṟu terintu yām evaṉ ceykuvam (aiṅ.288) neñcē!” eṉṟu taṉakkuḷḷa naṉṟiyaṟivip pulappaṭutti makiḻntu koḷkiṉṟāṉ.
piṉpu, avaṉ accelviyum uyirttōḻiyum piṟarum kūṭi nīrāṭac cellutal kaṇṭu mella maṟiviruntu pārttu “talip puṇik koḷiṉē talippuṇik koḷḷum, kaṭippuṇik koḷiṉē kaṭippuṇik koḷḷum, puṇiki viṭṭup puṉalō ṭoḻukiṉ, āṇṭum varukuvaḷ pōlum” (kuṟun.222) eṉat tōḻiyiṉ perumiyum tuṇimiyum teḷintu koḷ kiṉṟāṉ. itupōlum  neṟikaḷ pala uṇṭu. tōḻiyit tērntu koṇṭatum avaḷi yaṇmi “kaḷiṟu, māṉ, paṉṟi mutaliyaṉa varakkaṇṭa tuṇṭō” eṉṟu viṉavuvaṉ; takka viṭi peṟātu nīṅkuvaṉ. piṟitorukāl, tōḻiyum celviyum oruṅkiruppap pōntu, ūrum pērum piṟavum viṉavi “peruṅkal vēlic ciṟukuṭiyātu eṉac collavum collirāyiṉ................ ceṅkēḻāṭiya ceḻuṅkural ciṟutiṉik koypuṉaṅ kāvalum numatō? (naṟ.213) eṉṟu  viṉavi, avarkaḷ mukantarāmi kaṇṭu nīṅkuvaṉ. inniliyil, avaṉ varavum, atukaṇṭu celvi oḻukum oḻukkamum nōkkamum pārviyum tōḻiyāl kuṟikkoṇṭu nōkkappaṭum.”
 nam celviyō at tamiḻt tōṉṟali muṉpē kaṇṭu kātal ciṟantu niṟkiṉṟāḷātaliṉ, avaṉ varuntōṟum tōḻiyōṭu collāṭuntōṟum, kaṇṭu uḷḷattē uvakimikiṉum ataṉi maṟittu oṉṟum aṟiyāḷpōṉṟu oḻukukiṉṟāḷ. tōḻiyum pēraṟiviṉaḷātaliṉ, ac celvikkum celvaṉukkum muṉpē kātaluṟavu uṇṭākiyiruttali oruvāru uṇarntu koḷkiṉṟāḷ. ataṉi naṉku aṟintukōṭal kuṟittu mika nuṭpamāka avaḷuṭaṉ pēci ārāyat toṭaṅkukiṉṟāḷ.
puṉaṅkāt toḻukum nāḷil, orunāḷ tōḻiyum celviyum oru puṭiyē irukkiṉṟaṉar. ēṉi makaḷir puṉattiṉ pala maruṅkilum iruntu puḷḷiṉaṅkaḷi ōppikkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. taṉimi naṉku amiyak kaṇṭa nam aṟivuṭit tōḻi, celviyiṉ mukatti nōkkiṉāḷ; piṉpu ētō kūṟuvāḷ pōlac col leṭuttāḷ. celviyum avaḷuṭaṉ nōkkiṉaḷ. atukaṇṭa tōḻi, “uṇkaṇṇāy! pārttaṉiyā, oruvaṉ pulivēṭṭam mēṟkoṇṭu ataṉ aṭiccuvaṭupaṟṟi varuvāṉpōla villum ampum ēntit taliyil kaṇṇi cūṭip paṉṉāḷum varukiṉṟāṉ. varupavaṉ, eṉṉi nōkki cila kuṟippukkaḷik kāṭṭuvaṉē yaṉṟit taṉ maṉattē uṟṟa varuttatti vāyāl collāmalē pōyviṭuvaṉ. eṉmaṉattē avaṉ vaṭivē patintuviṭṭatu. avaṉ niṉivē mikuntatu. ataṉāl yāṉum iravellām kaṇṇuṟakkam iṉṟi varuntāniṟpē ṉāyiṉēṉ. ivvāṟē cila nāṭkaḷ kaḻintaṉa. avaṉum taṉ karutti eṉ muṉniṉṟu urittal āṟṟāṉ; namakkō nam kātaṟkuṟippi vāyviṭṭurippatum peṇtaṉmi yaṉṟu. itaṉāl, cila nāṭkaḷil avaṉ eṉṉik kāṇāmalē pōyviṭutalum uṇṭu. eṉṉālum iṉip poṟuttal muṭiyātu eṉṟoru niliyuṇṭāyiṟṟu. ākavē, yāṉum aritil tuṇintu oru nāṇiṉmi ceytēṉ” eṉkiṉṟāḷ. itu kēṭṭatum, celvi mukam civappa, uḷḷatteḻunta aruvaruppāl nutaloḷi māṟa, vāyil puṉṉaki tavaḻa, “naṭantatu eṉṉi” eṉkiṟāḷ. avaḷ mukavēṟu pāṭṭit tōḻi kuṟikkiṉṟāḷ.
ataṉmēl tōḻi, mēlum kūṟaluṟṟu,
“	.............naṟunutāl! ēṉal
 	iṉakkiḷi yāmkaṭin tōmpum puṉattayal
 	ūcal ūrntāṭa, oruñāṉṟu vantāṉi
	‘aiya, ciṟitu eṉṉi ūkki’ eṉa (yāṉ) kūṟat
	‘tiyāl! naṉṟu’ eṉṟu avaṉ ūkkak, kinekiḻpu
 	poyyāka vīḻntēṉ avaṉ mārpil; vāyāc cettu
 	oyyeṉa āṅkē eṭuttaṉaṉ koṇṭāṉmēl,”
eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷō illiyō, celvi mikac ciṉantu vāy tuṭippa naṉittu, “cīccī” eṉṟu colli, “piṉpu naṭantatu eṉṉi? col col” eṉkiṉṟāḷ. avaṭkut tōḻi, muṟuvalittu,
“mey aṟiyātēṉ pōl kiṭantēṉ maṉ”
eṉkiṉṟāḷ. celvi, poṟāmi mītūra, ciṉattī kiḷaṟa, “iṉi, iti eṉṉiṭam collātē; pō; av vaṉkaṇaṉyāvaṉ” eṉṟu collavum, tōḻi, avaṉi viṭātē, “itik kēḷ” eṉap paṟṟi niṟutti,
“	...................................................āyiṭi
	meyyaṟintu ēṟṟeḻuvēṉāyiṉ, maṟṟu, oyyeṉa
	‘oṇkuḻāy! celka’ eṉakkūṟi viṭam paṇpiṉ
	aṅkaṇ uṭiyaṉ avaṉ” 			 (kali.37)
eṉṟu muṭikkiṉṟāṉ. celviyum, “vāḻntēṉ” eṉa vāy veruvukiṉṟāḷ.
ic ceyalkaḷāl, tamiḻc celvikku ac celvaṉpāl muṉṉuṟavu uṇṭeṉpatit tōḻi yaṟintukoṇṭu, avaṉi nōkki, “nerunalvanta avaṉ iṉṟum varuvaṉ;”
“iṉṟum, tōlāvāṟilli, tōḻi! nāmceṉmō
cāyiṟip paṇittōṭ kiḻami taṉakkē
māciṉ ṟātalum aṟiyāṉ; ēcaṟṟu
eṉkuṟip puṟanili muyalum
aṇka ṇāḷaṉi nakukam yāmē”	          (akam.32)
eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟāḷ. ivaṟṟik kēṭṭu ac celvi paṭum pāṭum, pārkkum pārviyum kuṟippum tōḻiyiṉ aiyatti aṟavē nīkkiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iḵtivvāṟāka, muṉṉi nāḷ cevvipeṟātu vāṭic ceṉṟa tamiḻ makaṉ maṟunāḷ makaḷirkkuriya aḻakiya taḻiyoṉṟu puṉintukoṇṭu tōḻipāl varukiṉṟāṉ. attaḻiyiṉkaṇ mulli, marutam, neytal mutaliya nilattup palvakip  pūkkaḷ viraviyirukkiṉṟaṉa. atukaṇṭu, “nī koṇarnta ittaḻiyiṉkaṇ, vēṟunilattuppūkkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa; eṅkaḷ ‘kuṉṟam kurutippūviṉ kulikkāntaṭṭu’ (kuṟun 1); ātaliṉ vēṇṭēm; vēṇṭi yaṇiyiṉ vēṟu pala aiyattukku iṭaṉām” eṉa maṟuttu viṭukiṉṟāḷ. ac celvaṉ collāṭun tiṟaṉiṉṟi ēṅki nīṅkukiṉṟāṉ. celviyum ataṉit tāṉ ēṟṟu aṇintu avaḷi makiḻvittaṟkuk kūṭāmiyāl maṉam maṟuki varuntum varuttatti maṟittuk koṇṭu oḻukukiṉṟāḷ. ic ceyalālum iruvar pālum muṉṉuṟavu iruttalit tōḻi teḷikiṉṟāḷ.
piṉṉoru nāḷ, accelvaṉ ic celviyum tōḻiyarum kūṭit toṭukkum taḻipōlum taḻiyuṭi yoṉṟu toṭuttuk koṇṭu pōntu,  tōḻiyi irantu niṉṟu “itaṉi ēṟṟuk koṇmiṉ” eṉkiṉṟāṉ. iṉi, maṟuttaṟku iyalāmiyiṉ ēṟṟukkoṇṭu celviyi yaṭintu,
“	...............................kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ
 	uṭukkum taḻitan taṉaṉē; yām aḵtu
 	uṭuppiṉ yāy añcutumē; koṭuppiṉ
	kēḷuṭik kēṭu añcutumē; āyiṭi
	vāṭala kollō tāmē, avaṉmalip
	pōruṭi varuṭiyum pāyāc
	cūruṭi yaṭukkattu koyaṟkarun taḻiyē”	
(naṟi.359)
eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟāḷ. itaṟkuḷ, avaṉ taṉ vēṇṭukōḷi, ittōḻiyum celviyum kēḷārāyiṉ, taṉ kātalip palaraṟiyat tōṟṟuvikkum neṟiyi mēṟkoḷvatā? yātu ceyvatu? eṉṟu palapaṭa niṉintu tōḻiyiṉ kātil viḻumāṟu, “māveṉa maṭaloṭu maṟukil tōṉṟit teṟṟeṉat tūṟṟalum paḻiyē, vāḻtalum paḻiyē pirivutali variṉē” (kuṟun. 12) eṉak kūṟi iṉikiṉṟāṉ.
uṭaṉē tōḻi avaṉ muṉṉē niṉṟu, iruvar kuṟippum naṉku terintum avaḷi maṟuttu,
ivaḷē,
kāṉal naṇṇiya kāmar ciṟukuṭi
nīṉiṟap peruṅkaṭal kalaṅka uḷpukku
mīṉeṟi paratavar makaḷē.
nīyē
neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum niyama mūtūrk
kaṭuntērc celvar kātal makaṉē
niṇaccuṉā aṟutta uṇakkal vēṇṭi
iṉappuḷ oppum emakkunalaṉ evaṉō?
pulavu nāṟutum; celaniṉ ṟīmō
perunīr viḷiyuḷem ciṟunal vāḻkki
nummoṭu purivatō aṉṟē;
emma ṉōril cemmalu muṭittē”		   (naṟ.45)
eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟāḷ. iccoṟkaḷāl accelvaṉ ciṟitum taḷarātu mēṉmēlum vantu evvāṟēṉum tōḻiyiṉ tuṇi peṟṟuc celviyik kūṭum karuttiṉaṉākiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ neñcil, celviyik kāṇuntōṟum vēṭki mikukiṉṟatu (kuṟun.165). maṟumuṟiyum tōḻiyi irantu kēṭkavē viḻikiṉṟāṉ.
“	aruḷiṉum aruḷā ḷāyiṉum peritaḻintu
 	piṉṉili muṉiyalmā neñcē eṉṉatūum
 	aruntuya ravalam tīrkkum,
 	maruntupiṟi tilli yāṉ uṟṟa nōykkē”	(naṟ.140)
eṉṟu paṉmuṟiyum muyaṉṟu muṭivil tōḻi taṉ kuṟiyi muṭittut tara uṭaṉpaṭumāṟu ceytukoḷkiṉṟāṉ.
iṉi, tōḻi celviyōṭu collāṭi ac celvaṉ kuṟi muṭittaṟku muyalum muyaṟci mikka arumi vāyntatākum. celvi, ac celvaṉ taṉ kātalaṉ eṉpati oru pōtum vāyviṭṭat tōḻipāl urittatilli. ākavē, avaḷit taṉ karuttiṟku uṭaṉpaṭuvittalil tōḻi taṉ aṟivi mikka nayamum nuṭpamum amiyak kiyāḷum kaṭamiyaḷākiṉṟāḷ.
taṉ ceykiyāl celvaṉukkum celvikkum taṉpāl mikka varutta muṇṭāyiṉum, avartam, vemmiyit tāṅki aṟattiṉ vaḻuvā toḻukuvikkum aṟivuttiṇmi avaḷ pāl nirampavuṇṭu. “uṟukaṇ ōmpal taṉ iyalpākaliṉ, uriyatākum tōḻikaṇ uraṉē” eṉa āciriyar naṉku vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟār. mēlē, tāṉ accelvaṉōṭu kūṭiyāṭiyatu pōlvaṉavum, accelvi kēṭṭu nāṇat takuvaṉavum tōḻi kūṟiyatellām, accelviyiṉ oḻukkamum aṟavāḻvum kuṟittēyākum. “meyyiṉum poyyiṉum vaḻinili piḻiyātu, palvēṟu kavarporuḷ nāṭṭattāṉum” (tol) tōḻi celviyi ārāytaṟkuriyaḷ eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟiṉar. ituvēyaṉṟi, celvaṉpāl celvik kuriya kātali, avaḷuṭiya “nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum oḻukkamum uṇṭiyum, ceyviṉi maṟippiṉum celaviṉum payilviṉum” takuvaṉa kaṇṭu teḷikuvaṉ eṉṟum kūṟiyaruḷiṉar.
ivvakiyālellām tōḻi, accelviyiṉ kātaṉ māṇpi uṇarntukoṇṭapōtiṉum, tāṉ aṟiya avaḷ taṉ kātalaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi yoḻukappaṇṇum kaṭami mēṟkoḷkiṉṟāḷ. celviyō accamum maṭaṉum nāṇum mikavuṭiyaḷ. ivaṟṟuṭ ciṟitu vaḻuviṉum uyirviṭum attuṇi māṉa muṭiyaḷ. taṉ uyirttōḻi yāyiṉum, avaḷ taṉ kaḷaviṉi yaṟivaḷō eṉum accamum, atu kāraṇamāka niṟkum nāṇamum celvipāl ciṟitu keṭṭālaṉṟi, tōḻi avaḷik kaḷaviṉkaṇ iṉitu oḻuku vikkum aṟattic ceyya ilayātu. nittamum peṇpāṟkuriya eṉappaṭum accattiyum nāṇattiyum mikac ciṟitaḷavu keṭukka muyalum tōḻiyiṉ oṭpam mika viyattaṟkariyatu. payiṟcikkaṇ nīṅkum accattiṉum nāṇam makaḷirkku uyiriṉum ciṟantatākum. “uyiriṟ ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē” eṉat tolkāppayiṉārum, “uyiriṉum ciṟanta taṉ nāṇ yātum ilaḷāki nakutalum nakūum” (kali.147) eṉa nallantuvaṉārum kūṟutal kāṇka. ittuṟiyil pāṭṭiyaṟṟuvatil pulavaṉatu pulamittiṟam ōraḷavu kālkoṇṭu niṟkiṟateṉṟukūṭa urikkālam. ēṉeṉiṉ, nāṇaḻivu cuṭṭiya kuṟippukkaḷik kēṭpōr aruvarātu nayakkumāṟu kūṟal vēṇṭum.
ac celvaṉukkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi taṉpāl vantu avaṉ kuṟiyi nayakkumāṟu kūṟavarukiṉṟāḷ eṉpataṉi at tamiḻc celviyum tōḻiyiṉ kuṟippukkaḷāl oruvāṟu uṇarntu koḷkiṉṟāḷ eṉṟālum ataṉip puṟattē kāṭṭātu maṟit toḻukukiṉṟāḷ. inniliyil, tōḻi avaḷiṭam vantu, “aṉṉāy, orunāḷ vāralaṉ irunāḷ vāralaṉ, paṉṉāḷ vantu paṇimoḻi payiṟṟi, eṉ naṉṉar neñcam nekiḻnta piṉṟi, vari mutirtēṉiṉ pōkiyōṉē (kuṟun.176) eṉ neñcu kaluḻkiṉṟatu” eṉkiṉṟāḷ. oru muṟikkup paṉmuṟi avaṉ kuṟi yirantu niṉṟatu kuṟippittal kāṇka, piṟitorukāl celviyi yaṇmi, “akkuṉṟa nāṭaṉ, yām taṟpaṭantami aṟiyāṉ, tāṉum vēṉil āṉēṟupōlac cāyiṉaṉ eṉpa nam māṇnalam paṭarntē” (kuṟun.74) eṉkiṉṟāḷ.  avaṉ kuṟiyi nayattaṟkuriya paṭarcci ac celvipāl uḷatākat tāṉē uḷappaṭuttik koṇṭu tōḻi kūṟum tiṟam kāṇka. celvi nayavāḷāyiṉ, tōḻi taṉṉi yuḷappaṭuttuk kūṟutali maṟuppaḷeṉpatu karuttu.
ivvāṟṟāl celvi kuṟinayakkum karuttiṉaḷātalik kuṟippalāy uṇarntu koṇṭa tōḻi, maṟṟorukāl ceviliyi nōkki, “iti niṉittāl nakikku iṭaṉākiṉṟatu, kāṇ” eṉkiṟāḷ. cevili, ārvamuṭaṉ “eṉṉiyatu?” eṉṟalum,
“	nakiyā kiṉṟē tōḻi; ‘takiya
 	aṇimalar muṇṭakattu āypūṅ kōti
 	maṇimaruḷ aimpāl vaṇṭupaṭat tiit
 	tuṇinīrp puvam tuṇiyō ṭāṭi.......
	teḷitīṅka kiḷavi! yāri yōeṉ
	aritupuṇar iṉṉuyir vuviya nī?’eṉap
	pūṇmali neṭuntērp puravi tāṅkit
	tāṉnam aṇaṅkutal aṟiyāṉ, nammil
	tāaṇaṅ kuṟṟami kūṟik kāṉal
	Rmpuimir cuṭarnutal nōkkip
	peruṅkaṭal cērppaṉ toḻutuniṉ ṟatuvē”	(naṟ.245)
eṉṟu collukiṉṟāḷ. itaṉkaṇ avaṉ, eṉ iṉṉuyir vuviya nī yāriyō? eṉṟu viṉavi, cuṭarnutal nōkkit toḻutu niṉṟatu nakiyāka irukkiṉṟatu eṉpavaḷ, avaṉpāl celvikkuriya kātali. “tāṉ nam aṇaṅkutalaṟiyāṉ” eṉṟum, avaḷatu āṟṟāk kātali “nammil tāṉ aṇaṅ kuṟṟatu kūṟi... toḻutu niṉṟatu” eṉṟum kūṟum tiṟam kāṇka.
piṟitorukāl, vantu tōḻipāl viṭipeṟṟu nīṅkum ac celvat tamiḻ makaṉ ciṟitu cēymikkaṇ ceṉṟu koṇṭiruk kiṉṟāṉ. avaṉit tōḻi nam centamiḻ celvikkuk kāṭṭi, “cellumaṉṉō mellam pulampaṉ” eṉkiṉṟāḷ. uṭaṉē, celvi oṉṟum aṟiyāḷpōl, “yār avaṉ? ēṉ cel
kiṉṟāṉ?” eṉṟu viṉava, tōḻi, aṉṉāy,
“	kaḻippūk kuṟṟum kāṉal alkiyum
 	vaṇṭaṟ pāvi varimaṇal ayarntum
 	iṉpuṟap puṇarntum iḷivarap puṇarntum”
nammōṭu viḷiyāṭṭayarnta celvamakaḷē avaṉ. avaṉ, ippōtu,
“	taṉtuyar veḷippaṭat tavaṟi, namtuyar
 	aṟiyā miyiṉ ayarnta neñcamoṭu
 	cellu maṉṉō, mellam pulampaṉ”
eṉṟu urikkiṉṟāḷ. “ceṉṟāl cellaṭṭumē” eṉpāḷ
pōlac celvi tōḻiyip pārkkiṉṟāḷ. uṭaṉē ivvaṟivuṭit tōḻi,
“	celvōṉ peyarpuṟattu iraṅki muṉniṉṟu
 	takiiya ceṉṟeṉ niṟiyil neñcam
 	eytiṉṟu kollō tāṉē; eytiyum
 	kāmam ceppa nāṇiṉṟu kollō?”
eṉṟa taṉ neñcu kaluḻntatu pōlak kūṟi, mēlum,
“	utuva kāṇavar ūrnta tērē
 	kuppi veṇmaṇaṟ kuvavumici yāṉum
 	ekkart tāḻi maṭalvayi ṉāṉum
 	āykoṭip pācaṭumpu pariya ūrpuiḻipu
 	ciṟukuṭip paratavar peruṅkaṭal maṭutta
 	kaṭuñcelal koṭuntimil pōla
 	nivantupaṭu tōṟṟamoṭu ikantumā yummē”	
(akam. 330)
eṉṟu colli muṭikkiṉṟāḷ. itu kēṭṭa celvi, taṉṉip pōlavē taṉ tōḻiyum celvaṉpāl meyyē aṉpu koṇṭoḻukukiṉṟāḷ eṉat teḷintu makiḻkiṉṟāḷ. itaṉāl, uyiriṉum ciṟanta avaḷatu nāṇam ciṟitu nekiḻkiṉṟatu.
piṉporukāl, at tōḻi, nam celviyi nōkki, “aṉṉāy, ac celvanāṭaṉ, nerunal nammoṭu kūṭi nam puṉattē kiḷikaṭintu viḷiyāṭiṉaṉ aṉṟō? avaṉ eṉpāl ētō collutaṟku muyaṉṟāṉ” eṉkiṟāḷ. “coṉṉāṉā?” eṉṟu celvi viṉavalum, tōḻi,
“	colli ṭampeṟāṉ peyarntaṉaṉ, peyarntatu
 	alla laṉṟu, atu kātalan tōḻi.
	tātuṇ vēṭkiyiṉ pōtuterin tūtā
 	vaṇṭō raṉṉaavaṉ taṇṭāk kāṭci”
eṉṟatum, celvi, avaḷik kūrttu nōkkukiṉṟāḷ. tōḻiyō,
“	..................................avaṉ taṇṭāk kāṭci
 	kaṇṭum kaḻaltoṭi valitteṉ
 	paṇpil ceyti niṉippā kiṉṟē”		   (naṟ.25)
eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟāḷ. itaṉkaṇ, “vaṇṭōraṉṉa avaṉ taṇṭāk kāṭci” yeṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiyatu, niṉpāl nalaṉ nukartaṟku eṉpāl collāṭamuyaṉṟāṉ eṉṟavāṟu. “kaṇṭum kaḻaltoṭi valitta eṉ paṇpil ceyti” eṉṟataṉāl, celvi, taṉ uyirkkātalaṉāṉa accelvaṉip palakāl kāṇappeṟṟum kūṭi makiḻappeṟāmiyiṉ uṭampunaṉi curuṅkiyatum, pāṭaṟintu *kuṟinayakkum kuṟi viḷaṅkak kāṭṭāmiyum viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ivvāṟu celviyiṉ uḷḷatti accelvaṉpāl kūṭṭuvittaṟkēṟpat tōḻi paṇpaṭuttik koṇṭapiṉ, avaḷi nōkki,
“	aṟiyāy vāri tōḻi.........................
 	māmali nāṭaṉoṭu maṟuviṉ ṟākiya
 	kāmam kalanta kātal uṇṭeṉiṉ,
 	naṉṟu maṉ;atu nāṭāy kūṟi;
 	nāṇum naṭpum illōrt tēriṉ
 	yāṉalatu illiiv vulakat tāṉē;
	iṉṉuyi raṉṉa niṉṉoṭum cūḻātu.......
 	perumpeyar enti aruṅkaṭi nīvic
 	ceytupiṉ iraṅkā viṉiyoṭum
 	eyyala perumpaḻi eyti ṉēṉē;”	         (akam.268)
eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟāḷ. itaṉkaṇ, nam celvi, muṉpu ac celvaṉpāl taṉittut kūṭippeṟṟa naṭpiṉit tōḻitāṉ uṇarntirukku māṟṟiṉiyum oruvāṟu terivittuk kuṟinayap pittuk koṇṭa tiṟam kāṇalām.
maṟupaṭiyum, at tōḻiyē, nam celviyi nōkki,
“	ammali kiḻavōṉ namnayantu eṉṟum
 	varuntiṉaṉ eṉpatōr vāyccol tēṟāy;
 	nīyum kaṇṭu numaroṭum eṇṇi
 	aṟivaṟin taḷaval vēṇṭum; maṟuttaraṟku
 	ariya vāḻi tōḻi; periyōr
 	nāṭi naṭpiṉ allatu
 	nāṭṭunā ṭārtam oṭṭiyōr tiṟattē”		   (naṟ.32)
eṉa aṟivuṭiya naṉmoḻi kūṟi avaḷik kuṟinayappikkiṉṟāḷ. iṉi ivvakiyēyaṉṟi, vēṟu vakiyil, “celvaṉ pōntu,”
“	arumpalit tiyaṟṟiya curumpār kaṇṇi
 	piṉṉuppuṟan tāḻak koṉṉē cūṭṭi
 	nalvaral iḷamuli nōkki neṭiniṉintu
 	nillātu peyarntaṉaṉ oruvaṉ; ataṟkē......
 	paṭappi niṉṟa muṭattāṉ puṉṉip
 	poṉṉēr nuṇtātu nōkki
 	eṉṉum nōkkumiv vaḻuṅka lūrē”        	         (akam.180)
eṉṟum,
“	koṇkaṉ ūrnta koṭiñci neṭuntēr
 	teṇkaṭal aṭikarit tērmaṇi yolippak
 	kāṇa vantu nāṇap peyarum;
 	aḷitō tāṉē kāmam
 	viḷivatu maṉṟa nōkō yāṉē”		(kuṟun.212)
eṉṟum tōḻi, nam celvi kuṟinayakkumāṟu colvaṉa palavākum. aviyellām virippiṉ perukum. (kali.60,61)
ivvaṇṇam tōḻi kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, nam centamiḻc celvi, accam nīṅki, nāṇum oru ciṟitu nekiḻntu, taṉakkum ac celvaṉukkum uḷatākiya toṭarpiṉik kuṟippāy urikkaluṟuvaḷ.
“	cuṭarttoṭīi! kēḷāy: teruvilnām āṭum
 	maṇaṟciṟṟil kāliṉ citiyā, aṭicciya
 	kōti parintu, varippatu koṇṭōṭi
 	nōtakka ceyyum ciṟupaṭṭi (celvaṉ), mēlōrnāḷ,
 	aṉṉiyum yāṉum iruntēmā, ‘illirē!
 	uṇṇunīr vēṭṭēṉ’ eṉavantāṟku, aṉṉi 
 	‘aṭarpoṟ cirakattāl vākkic cuṭariḻāy!
 	uṇṇumnīr ūṭṭivā’ eṉṟāḷ; eṉa, yāṉum
 	taṉṉi yaṟiyātu ceṉṟēṉ; maṟṟueṉṉi,
 	vaḷimuṉki paṟṟi naliyat, terumantiṭṭu,
	‘aṉṉāy! ivaṉoruvaṉ ceytatukāṇ,’ eṉṟēṉā;
	aṉṉi alaṟip paṭartarat, taṉṉi, yāṉ,
	‘uṇṇunīr vikkiṉāṉ’ eṉṟēṉā; aṉṉiyum
	taṉṉip puṟampaḻittu nīva, maṟṟu, eṉṉik
	kaṭikkaṇṇāl kolvāṉpōl nōkki naṭikkūṭṭam
	ceytāṉak kaḷvaṉ makaṉ”			   
(kali.51)
eṉṟu ac celvi urikkiṉṟāḷ. itaṉik kēṭṭa tōḻi, “aṉṉāy, ivvāṟu muṉpē uṟavuṇṭākavum, eṉṉi itu kāṟum maṟittoḻukiṉīraṉṟō! mēlum, nī kūṟiyavakiyē nōkkiṉ. avaṉ attuṇi vaḻipāṭoṉṟum taṅkaṭkuc ceytilaṉpōla irukkiṟatē; paṭṭimiyuṭiyāri naṭṭal kūṭātē” eṉṟu cuṭuvatupōla urikkiṉṟāḷ. atu poṟāta celvi, avaṉ taṉṉi vaḻipaṭṭa neṟiyum, tāṉ taṉ perumi kuṉṟātu ataṉi ēṟṟa niliyum kūṟaluṟṟu,
“	iṉṉaki ilaṅkeyiṟṟut tēmoḻin tuvarccevvāy
 	naṉṉutāl niṉakkoṉṟu kūṟuvām; kēḷi; iṉi:
 	nileṉa niṟuttāṉ, niṟuttē vantu
 	nutalum mukamum tōḷum kaṇṇum
	iyalum collum nōkkupu niṉii,
 	aitēyn taṉṟu, piṟiyu maṉṟu;
 	mitīrk taṉṟu matiya maṉṟu;
 	vēyamaṉ ṟaṉṟu maliyu maṉṟu;
 	pūvamaṉ ṟaṉṟu cuṉiyu maṉṟu;
 	mella iyalum mayilu maṉṟu;
 	collat taḷarum kiḷiyu maṉṟu; eṉavāṅku,
	aṉiyaṉa palapā rāṭṭip, piyaṉe
 	valivar pōlac cōrpataṉ oṟṟip,
 	puliyar pōlap puṇkaḷ nōkkit
 	toḻalum toḻutāṉ, toṭalum toṭṭāṉ,
 	kāḻvari nillāk kaṭuṅkaḷiṟu aṉṉōṉ
 	toḻūum toṭūum avaṉtaṉmi
 	ēḻit taṉmiyō illi tōḻi”	(kali.55)
eṉṟu kūṟi muṭikkiṉṟāḷ. tōḻi, makiḻntu, nam celviyi ‘varuka’ eṉat taṉiyē aḻittucceṉṟu, “nī īṅkē iru; ceṉṟu pūkkaḷ nallaṉa koṇarkiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟu yāṉ colli nīṅkukiṉṟāḷ. āṅkē ciṟitu pōtil, accelvaṉ pōntu nam celviyik kaṇṭu kaḻiyuvaki eytukiṉṟāṉ. nam celviyum mutalil peritum nāṇi, tōḻiyiṉ ceyal nuṭpam teḷintu, celvaṉatu kūṭṭattāl iṉpam ciṟakkiṉṟāḷ.
ivvāṟu, nam tamiḻccelviyum celvaṉum kaṭimaṇam puṇartaṟkiṭiyē palamuṟi oruvariyoruvar kaṇṭu kūṭi makiḻvar; āṉāl, ivarkaṭkiṭiyē makappēṟṟukkuriya meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻvatu kiṭiyātu. piṟkālak kōvi nūlkaḷil aḵtu uḷatu pōṉṟa kuṟippukkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. āyiṉum, avi caṅka kālattu oḻukkamiṉmiyiṉ koḷḷappaṭā.
34. ilakkiya vuṇmi
makkaḷ vaḻakkil, avaruṭiya niṉivu col ceyal eṉṟa mūṉṟiyum nilikkaḷamākak koṇṭē uṇmiyum poym miyum nilavukiṉṟaṉa. niṉivu mutaliya mūṉṟum ulakiyaṟ poruḷ kaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu niṟpaṉa. ātalāl uṇmiyum poymmiyum ulakiyalōṭu toṭarpuṟṟu iruppatu teḷivākum. oruporuḷ uḷatākuṅkāl uṇmiyum, avvāṟu ākātu maṟiyuṅkāl poymmiyum tōṟṟam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. uḷḷatu oṉṟaṉ uṇmiyip piṟar collak kēṭpavaṉ ataṉi nēriṟ ceṉṟu kāṇum pōtu apporuḷ maṟintoḻiyiṉ ataṉ iṉmiyāl muṉṉark kēḷviyuṟṟa uṇmi poyyāki avaṉi mayakkukiṟatu. ataṉi mēṟkoṇṭu ārāyntu kāṇāvaḻi avaṉukku atu poyyākavē amintu viṭukiṟatu. ivvaṇṇamē niṉivum nikaḻcciyum oṉṟāviṭattum, collum ceyalum ovvāviṭattum, ceyalum poruḷum (payaṉum) ciṟavāviṭattum poymmi urukkoḷḷukiṟatu. ārāycciyāl poymmi nīṅkuvatum, uṇmi oḷiviṭṭut tōṉṟi iṉpam ceyvatum makkaḷulakil nāṭōṟum nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷākum.
uṇmiyārāycciyil talippaṭṭōr uṇmikaḷi viññāṉa vuṇmi (Scientific Truth), varalāṟṟu uṇmi  (Historical) meynneṟiyuṇmi, (Philosophical Truth), pulaṉeṟiyuṇmi (Poetic Truth) eṉa nāṉku vakiyākak kaṇṭu urittuḷḷaṉar.
iraṇṭōṭu mūṉṟik kūṭṭiṉāl aintākum eṉpatu pōlvaṉavum, irupaṅku nīrk kāṟṟum oru paṅku neruppuk kāṟṟum cērntāl nīr uṇṭākum eṉpatu pōlvaṉavum viññāṉa vuṇmi; namatu tamiḻakattic cila āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar mēṉāṭṭu āṅkilarum, avarkkumuṉ nāyakka mukamatiya maṉṉar kaḷum, avarkaṭku muṉṉē pāṇṭiya cōḻa maṉṉarkaḷum, avarkku muṉpu pallavarum āṭcipurintaṉar eṉpatu pōlvaṉa varalāṟṟuṇmi; ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷik kaṇ mutaliya karuvi kaḷāl aṟintapiṉ aṟintavaṟṟi ārāyntu muṭipu ceyvataṟku maṉa mutaliya karuvikaḷ namakku uṇṭu eṉpatum avaṟṟi neṟippaṭuttum karuviyāka aṟivu eṉpatu uṇṭu eṉpatum ivi pōlvaṉa piṟavum meynneṟi uṇmikaḷākum. “cuvi yoḷi ūṟu ōci nāṟ nāṟṟam eṉṟintiṉ vakiterivāṉ kaṭṭē ulaku” eṉpatum atu. kaṇ mutaliya  karuvikaḷāl puṟattē kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷi maṉattiṉkaṇ niṟutti avaṟṟik kūṭṭiyum kaḻittum vakuttum mīṭṭum colvaṭivil veḷippaṭuttik kēṭpōr uḷḷattil taṉ maṉattiṟkaṇṭa kāṭci yeḻumāṟu ceyyum pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkil kāṇappaṭum poruḷum ceykiyum pulaṉeṟi yuṇmiyākum.
mūttu naritta mutiyavaḷ orutti taṉmakaṉ yāṉiyōṭu pōrāṭi ataṉik koṉṟuviṭṭut tāṉum maṭintāṉ eṉṟu kēḷvippaṭṭu, yāṉiyik koṉṟatupaṟṟi uvakiyum maṭintatu paṟṟit tuyarum koṇṭu kaṇṇīr vaṭikkiṉṟāḷ. ataṉip pulavaṉoruvaṉ namakku urikkiṉṟāṉ. mutiyavaḷatu mutumi niliyi “mīṉuṇ kokkiṉ tūviyaṉṉa, vālnarik kūntal mutiyōḷ” eṉṟum, avaḷ makaṉ yāṉiyik koṉṟu tāṉum iṟantāṉeṉak kēḷviyuṟṟati “mutiyōḷ ciṟuvaṉ kaḷiṟu eṟintu paṭṭaṉaṉ eṉṉum” eṉṟum, avaḷ corinta kaṇṇīrik “kaṇṇīr, nōṉkaḻit tuyalvarum vetirattu, vāṉ peyat tūṅkiya citariṉum palavē” eṉṟum urittu, yāṉiyoṭu pōrāṭiya makaṉ ap pōril yāṉiyik koṉṟu veṉṟi eytiyataṉāl uvakik kaṇṇīr corintati, “kaḷiṟu eṟintu paṭṭaṉaṉ eṉṉum uvaki, īṉṟa ñāṉṟiṉum peritē” eṉṟum, “kaṇṇīr vetirattuc citariṉum palavē” eṉṟum kūṟiyatupōl vaṉa pulaṉeṟi yuṇmikaḷām. “vāṉattiṟ paṟakkalām, entiravūrti iyaṟṟumiṉ eṉṟāṉ”, kaṭaṟkuḷ mūḻkic ceṉṟāṉ. nīr mēl naṭakkalām eṉṟellām pulamik kaṇṇil tōṉṟiya kāṭcikaḷ innāḷil nam kaṇmuṉṉē nikaḻtalāl, aviyum etiratu pōṟṟi makkaḷulakkiṉ māṭci terikkum pulaṉeṟi yuṇmi vakiyēyām.
iv vuṇmikaḷi ārāyvatu nāṭṭu nalaṉukkum makkaḷ vāḻvukkum perutta naṉmiyākum. viññāṉa vuṇmi ārāycciyāl nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal vāḻvu ākkam peṟukiṟatu mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ palavum amarikka nāṭum iṉṟu poruḷ vāḻvu ciṟantu polivatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟu. varalāṟ ṟarāycciyāl araciyal vāḻvu tiṭpamum cemmiyum aṭikiṟatu. āṅkila nāṭṭu araciyaṟ pārāḷumaṉṟa muṟi ulakiluḷḷa ēṉi nāṭukaṭku eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka ilaṅkuvatu āṅkila makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟā rāycciyāl viḷinta perum payaṉ. meynneṟi yārāycci, vitivilakku, nīti, nērmi mutaliya kūṟukaḷ vaḷampeṟṟu nāṭṭil amitiyum aṟamum poruntiya vāḻvu nilavutaṟkut tuṇi ceykiṉṟatu. muṉṉāḷil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya yavaṉanāṭṭu meynneṟip pulavarkaḷ ceyta aṟivuppaṇiyāl iṉṟiya araciyal caṭṭaṅkaḷ uruvāy amintiruppati itaṟku eṭuttuk kūṟalām. iṉṟum caṭṭakkalvi kaṟpikkum kalviniliyaṅkaḷ yavaṉanāṭṭuc caṭṭaṅkaḷi namnāṭṭu iḷiñarkaṭku kaṟpittu varuvatu itaṉi naṉku vaṟpuṟuttukiṟatu iṟutiyāka niṟkum pulaṉeṟi yuṇmi ārāycci makkaḷi ēṉiya vilaṅku mutaliya mākkaḷiṉ iṉattiṉiṉṟu pirittu makkaṭpaṇpi vaḷarttu, aṟivu, aṟam, aṉpu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vāyilākak kaṇṇukku aḻakum karuttukku iṉpamum perukum immi vāḻvum ammiyiṟ peṟalākum antamil iṉpattu aḻiyāp pēriṉpa vāḻvum kivaracceyyum kaviṉ vāyntatākum. nāḷum vaḷarntu varum makkaḷiṉattukku vēṇṭuvaṉa vaṟṟi uṇartti vēṇṭātavaṟṟi vilakki, nēriya niṉivum cīriya naṭiyum uṭiyatāka āṅkila makkaṭ camutāyattic ceytatu ippulaṉeṟi yuṇmi ārāycciyē, ittuṇi nalaṅkaḷ inta uṇmiyārāycciyil iruppatu kaṇṭē nākarikam paṭitta nāṭṭiṉar aṉivarum ivaṟṟi mikavirumpik kiyāṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. namnāṭṭu araciyalum ittuṟiyil karuttuc celutti varuvati nām nāḷum kaṇṭu varukiṉṟōm.
iv vuṇmiyārāycci potuvakiyil nāṉku perum pirivākak kāṇappaṭṭatāyiṉum av vakikaḷ oṉṟu koṉṟu toṭarpiṉṟit taṉittu iyaṅkuvatilli. oṉṟiyoṉṟu iṉṟiyamiyātu cārntuvarum muṟimi ivaṟṟiṭiyē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṟatu. viññaṉattuṟiyil orukalattiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa vuṇmi piṟitoru kālattil toṭarpāka ārāycci ceyyap peṟṟu viḷakkam peṟuvatōṭu mēlum pala uṇmikaḷik kāṭṭi yamikiṟatu. pukivaṇṭi mutal vēṟṟumi peritum koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. viññāṉa uṇmi kāṇum ārāycci vaḻivaḻiyākat toṭarntu naṭantatē itaṟkuk kāraṇam, iṉṟiya tōṟṟtatil kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumik kūṟukaḷ, tōṉṟiya kālattuk kūṟukaḷōṭu oppa vittu nōkkiṉ, ataṉkaṇ varalāṟṟuṇmi uḷḷīṭāka niṟpatik kāṇalām. ēṉi meyn neṟit tuṟiyilum piṟavaṟṟilum varalāṟu uḷḷuṟa niṟpatum, varalāṟṟuṇmi ārāycciyil viññāṉam, meynneṟi mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ uṇmi kalantiruppatum ārāypavarkkut teḷivākat terikiṉṟaṉa.
iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyumākiya ellāp poruṭkum akak kūṟum puṟakkūṟum nāṇpaṟa uṇmi yārāyccikku uruvākum. ak kūṟukaḷil tōyntirukkum uṇmikaḷi mēlē kūṟiya viññāṉam varalāṟu meynneṟi ākiya tuṟikaḷ ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttā niṟpa, pulāṉeṟi yuṇmiyārāycci av viruvakik kūṟukaḷiṉ amippiṭiyē nilavum aḻakiyum poṟpiyum veḷippaṭutti uḷḷattukku iṉpattiyum amiti yiyum nalkukiṟatu. ulakiluḷḷa makkaḷiṉam ellāvaṟṟiṟkum amitiyiyum nalkukiṟatu. ulakiluḷḷa makkaḷiṉam ellā vaṟṟirukkum akak kūṟukaḷiṉ amippiṭiyē nilavum aḻakiyum poṟpiyum veḷippaṭutti uḷḷattukku iṉpattiyum amitiyiyum nalkakiṟatu. ulakiluḷḷa makkaḷiṉam ellā vaṟṟiṟkum akak kūṟukaḷil oṟṟumi kāṇappaṭiṉum puṟakkūṟu kaḷākiya tōṟṟam, niṟam, vaṭivam mutaliyavaṟṟil pala vēṟuvaki vēṟṟumikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avvēṟṟumi yiṭiyē ovvoṉṟiṉum oṉṟiyoṉṟi īrttu makiḻ vikkum aḻakum poṟpum amintiruppatu uṇmi, iyaṟkip poruḷiṭattē kāṇappaṭum eḻilum aḻakum makkaḷ maṉattiṟ paṭintu avar ceyyum ceyaṟkip poruḷiṭattum veḷippaṭa amintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. nām kāṇum mōṭṭārkārkaḷiṉ akakkūṟukaḷil orumi kāṇappaṭuvatu pōlap puṟakkūṟākiya tōṟṟam, amippu,  niṟam mutaliyavaṟṟil kāṇappaṭātu vēṟṟumiyuṟṟālum, namkaṇṇiyum karuttiyum īrttu niṟutti iṉpuṟuttum vaṉappum aḻakum taṉittu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. akamum puṟamumākiya iruvakik kūṟukaḷiyum nōkkip puṟat tōṟṟattil pūttu viḷaṅkum poṟpilum vaṉappilum aminta aḻakiyum kaviṉiyum kāṇpatilum piṟarkkuk kāṭṭuvatilum pulaṉeṟi yuṇmi yārāycci peritum īṭupaṭṭu niṟkiṟatu. itu makkaḷuyiriṉ ceyaṟṟiṟatti ūkkutaṟkum aṟivut tiṟatti ātarippataṟkum peruntuṇiyāy niṟṟalāl, ārāycciyāḷar pulaṉeṟit tuṟikkuc ciṟanta iṭatti nalkit tiruvuṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ip pulaṉeṟit tuṟiyip paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlār pulattuṟi yeṉṟum pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkeṉṟum pukaṉṟu urittaṉar. piṟkālat tār ilakkiyaneṟi eṉṟum, pulaṉeṟiyuṇmi yārāycciyi ilakkiya ārāycci yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkalāyiṉar. pulaṉeṟik kuriyavaṉ pulavaṉ. pulam eṉpatu aṟivu; aṟivuṭiyavaṉ pulavaṉā kiṉṟāṉ. uyir uṇarum taṉmiyatu. eṉavē uyiruṭiyavar aṉivarum uṇarvuṭiyavarē; āyiṉum avaruḷḷum vaki potu kiyālaṉṟic ciṟappu vakiyāl mēṉmiyuṟupavariyē “uṇarvuṭi māntar” eṉa āṉṟōr uyarttuk kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āciriyar tolkāppayiṉār,
“	kaṇṇiṉum ceviyiṉum tiṇṇitiṉ uṇarum
 	uṇarvuṭi māntark kallatu teriyiṉ
 	naṉṉayap poruḷkōḷ eṇṇaruṅ kurittē”
eṉṟu urippataṉāl itaṉi aṟiyalām. avvāṟē aṟivuṭiyōr aṉivarum aṟiñar eṉappaṭuvatilli. cuvi oḷi ūṟu ōci nāṟṟam eṉṟa pulaṉkaḷ aintālum ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷik kāṇpavar pulavarāyiṉum, ap pulaṉkaḷi āḷum aṟiviṉāl ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇṇikaḻ kūṟukaḷi uḷḷattē vittu āyntu koḷvatil pulavaṉ  ciṟappuṟu kiṉṟāṉ. avaṉuṭiya pulamiyil viññāṉa mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ uṇmikaḷum uṇmik kūṟukaḷum amintuḷḷaṉa. avaṉē pulattuṟi muṟṟiyavaṉ; avaṉēpulavaṉ eṉṟa coṟkup poruḷā kiṉṟāṉ.
nam nāṭṭil pulattuṟi muṟṟiya pulavaṉatu pulaṉeṟi ciṟanta vakiyil itukāṟum iyaṉṟu vantuḷḷatu. uḷḷataṉ uṇmiyum illataṉ iṉmiyum uyttaṟiyum avaṉuṭiya pulamiyuḷḷam av vuṇmikaḷi ulakiṟku urikka muṟpaṭukiṟatu. eḻuttum iciyum naṭippum ōviyamum ciṟpamum avaṉ uḷḷattukkuk karuviyākiṉṟaṉa. piṟaruṭiya kaṇṇum kātumākiya ciṟappuṭiya aṟikaruvikaḷil kaṇṇi maṭṭum naṭippum ōviyamum ciṟpamum vāṅkik koḷkiṉṟaṉa; ici ceviyip paṟṟikkoḷḷukiṟatu. eḻuttu oṉṟutāṉ kaṇṇiyum ceviyiyum paṟṟi aṟivum iṉpamum aḷikkiṉṟatu. aṟivum iṉpamum uṭalōṭu iyaṅkum makkaṭku ilakkamātalāl avaṟṟi nalkum eḻuttut tuṟi ilakkiyattuṟi eṉappaṭutaṟkum poruttamāka vuḷatu.
ilakkiyattuṟiyil paṇiceyta tamiḻppulavarkaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷi nōkkiṉ, nālvakip pulaṉeṟikaḷ avaṟṟil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi uḷḷatu puṇarttal, illatu puṇarttal eṉa iraṇṭāy niṉṟu ovvoṉṟum iv viraṇṭāy virikiṟatu. uḷḷatu puṇarppu, uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka uḷḷatu puṇarttal, eṉavum, illatu puṇarppu, uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka puṇarttal. illōṉ talivaṉāka uḷḷatu illatu puṇarttalum, illōṉ talivaṉāka illatu puṇarttalum eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟuḷ uḷḷatu eṉṟatu ulakiyalōṭu oṭṭiniṟpatu; illatu ulakiyalil eṅkum kāṇappaṭātatu. uḷḷatu puṇartta ikkiyaṅkaḷ ekkālattum payaṉ taruvaṉavāy ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉ; iṉṟum yāvarum virumpip payilkiṉṟaṉar. iv vakiyil caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ muṉṉaṇiyil niṟkiṉṟaṉa. cilappatikāram maṇimēkali cīvakacintāmaṇi ākiya mūṉṟum ciṟitaḷavu illatu puṇarkkappaṭṭiruppiṉum avaṟṟi vilakki ciṟitaḷavu illatu puṇarkkappaṭ ṭiruppiṉum avaṟṟi vilakki nōkkiyavaḻi uḷḷatu puṇartta ilakkiyappaṇpu ciṟitum kuṉṟāmiyāl uḷḷatu puṇartta vakiyil aṭakkik kōṭaṟku iṭamuṇṭu. illatu puṇartta ilakkiya puṇartta ilakkiya vakiyil, kōvi, ulā, tūtu, maṭal mutaliyaṉa vantu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ik kōvi mutaliya ilakkiyaṅkaḷuḷ mēlēkaṇṭa viññāṉa mutaliya uṇmikaṭkuc cirtta iṭam illāmiyāl, viññāṉa mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aṟivu peṟṟuḷḷa in nāḷil avi ciṟṟilakkiya meṉṟa peyar pūṇṭu pōtiya celvākkiṉṟi nūl vaṭivil paḻiyōr pēṇit tanta paḻan tamiḻp poruḷāyt tammi virumpip payilvōr kuṉṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṟum eṉṟum iṉpam taruma vakiyil, uḷḷatu puṇartta ikkiyaṅkaḷuḷ aḻakum iṉpak kūṟum poruntumāṟu puṉin turippakutikaḷ kāṇappaṭuvatuṇṭu. puṉinturikkum poyyu rikkum vēṟupāṭu aṟiyāta cilar aḻaku tarum puṉin turiyiyum payaṉillāta poyyuriyākak karuti mayaṅkavā rāyiṉar. “poy piṟantatu pulavaṉ vāyilē” eṉṟa potumoḻi im mayakkattāl viḷinta payaṉ.
ceyyappaṭum evvakic ceyaṟkip poruḷiṭattum kāṇpār kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum īrkkum vaṉappum aḻakum amippatu iyaṟkineṟi, ilakkiyap pulavaṉ inta iyaṟki neṟikku uṭpaṭṭavaṉātalāl avaṉ ceyyum ilakkiyamākiya poruḷiṉ kaṇ puṉinturi vaḻaṅkum vaṉappum pulattuṟi yiṉpam tarum aḻakum amiyātu oḻiyāvaṉṟō? ēṉip poruṭku aḻaku ceytal ūtiyam karutiyatu; ilakkiyap poruṭku atu vēṇṭāvē eṉṟoru kēḷvi eḻuvatum uṇṭu. uṇmi yuṇarnta uyarntōr uḷḷattē iḵtu eḻātāyiṉum ēṉōr itaṉi eḻuppuvatu iyalpu. cumār munnūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉ mēṉāṭṭil ik kēḷvi eḻuntatu. ataṟku akkālattē cerumaṉi nāṭṭil vāḻnta ṣōpaṉavā eṉṟa nallicip palavar muṉvantu, “nām uyir vāḻvataṟku uyirkkāṟṟē Oxygen vēṇṭuvatu; aḵtu illiyāyiṉ nām maṭintu pōvōm eṉpatu viññāṉa vuṇmi anta uyirk kāṟṟip piṟa kāṟṟu vakikaḷiṉiṉṟum pirittut tūyaniliyil vaṭittu uṭkoṇṭō māyiṉ, cila viṉāṭikaḷil nuriyīral ventu iṟantu viṭuvōm. itaṉāl uyirkku iṉṟiyamiyāta uyirkkāṟṟum piṟa kāṟṟuk kaḷōṭu kalantu puṉinturuvāy varutal vēṇṭum eṉpatu iyaṟkiyiṉ vitiyeṉa uṇartal vēṇṭum. mēlum, uṇmiyi uḷḷavāṟē oppaṉiyum oḷiyumiṉṟi urittāl atu kēṭpōr uḷḷatti yuṟṟu oḷiceyyātoḻiyum. uṭaṟkuṭ pukuntu uyirkku nalam ceyvataṟku uyirk kāṟṟōṭu piṟa kāṟṟuvakikaḷik kalantu uyirk kāṟṟākap puṉintu iyaṟki vaḻaṅkuvatu pōla uṇmiyum oppaṇiyāl (puṉinturiyāl) oḷiyūṭṭappaṭa vēṇṭum” eṉa viṭi yiṟuttār.
uḷḷataṉ uṇmiyi yurikkumiṭattup puṉinturi vakiyāl poy ciṟitu kalakkumāyiṉum appoymmi vāymiyāy uṇmi tarum uṟutippayaṉip peṟuvittalāl atu vilakkuṇṇātu. iṉṟēl koḷḷattakka uṇmiyum kāṇpōr karuttik kavaramāṭṭāmal poyyāy vilakip pōm. “poymmiyum vāymi yiṭatta puritīrnta naṉmi payakkum eṉiṉ” eṉṟa iyaṟkiyaṟamum īṇṭu niṉivukūrat takkatu. iyaṟkiyi aṭippaṭuttit taṉakku ēval ceyvikkum aḷavukku makkaḷuyir uyarntu niṟkiṟatu. itaṟku inta ēṟṟattit tantatu viññāṉa vaḷarcci. ataṉ aṭippaṭik kūṟukaḷuḷ nilattiyal kaṇakku (geometry) eṉpatu oṉṟu. antak kaṇakkiyal puḷḷiyum kōṭum eṉṟa iraṇṭiṉmēl niṟkiṟatu. puḷḷiyāvatu uṭparappillāta vaṭṭam eṉṟum aḵtu iyaṅkumiṭattu uṇṭākum cuvaṭē (locus) kōṭu eṉṟum, akkōṭṭukku nīḷamuṇṭē yaṉṟi akalameṉpatē kiṭiyātu eṉṟum, ak kaṇakkiyal kūṟukiṟatu. itu veṟum puṉinturiyē yaṉṟi uṇmi yākātu. ivvāṟē nōkkiṉ puṉinturi kalavāta varalāṟṟu nūlē inta nilavu lakattil eṅkum kiṭiyātu. ākavē, puṉinturi uṇmikku vēṇṭāta taṉṟu eṉpatu teḷiya uṇaravēṇṭuva toṉṟām.

3. “These wise sayings; sid Bacon, the auther of some of the wisest of them, are not only for ornament but for action andbusiness, having a point or edge, whereby knots in business are pierced and discovered, And he applands Cicero’s description of such sayings as saltpits, that you may extract salt out of them and sprinkle it where you will” - John Morley.
1. It has been sid that our knowledge is this; that we know best, first, what we have divined by native instinct; second, what we have learned by experience of men and things; third, what we have learned not in books, but by books - that is, by the reflections that they suggest; fourth, last and least what we have learned in books or with masters. The virtue of an aphorism comes under the third of these heads; it conveys a portin of a truth with such point as to set us thinking on what remins.         - J. Morley.
